## ח (id: BDB02756)

ח, *Ḥêth,* 8th letter; \= numeral 8 in post BHeb.

> \[Str2243] \[חֹב], חֻבִּי v. sub חבנ. below



## חָבָא (id: BDB02758)

\[Str2244] † \[חָבָא] **vb.** withdraw, hide (not in **Qal**); cf. also חבה (NH *id.;* Ar. خَبَأَ; Eth. ኀብአ As. *ḫabû* DlPr 175; Targum Ethpa. *hide oneself*)—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. נֶחְבָּא Ju [9:5](https://ref.ly/Judg9:5) \+ [1 S 10:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:22), [2 S 17:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:9) (both may be *pt.*); נַחְבֵּאתָ [Gn 31:37](https://ref.ly/Gen31:37); ´וְנַחְ consec. [1 S 19:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:2); נֶעחבְּא־וּ [Jos 10:27](https://ref.ly/Josh10:27); נֶחְבָּאוּ [Jb 29:8](https://ref.ly/Job29:8), [29:10](https://ref.ly/Job29:10); *Impf.* 2 ms. תֵּחָבֵא [Jb 5:21](https://ref.ly/Job5:21); *Inf. cstr.* הֵחָבֵא [2 Ch 18:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:24), [Dn 10:7](https://ref.ly/Dan10:7); *Pt.* pl. (cf. also *supr.*) נֶחְבְּאִים [Jos 10:17](https://ref.ly/Josh10:17), etc.—*hide oneself* in fear [Gn 3:10](https://ref.ly/Gen3:10) (J), [Jos 10:16](https://ref.ly/Josh10:16), [10:17](https://ref.ly/Josh10:17), [10:27](https://ref.ly/Josh10:27) (all JE), Ju [9:5](https://ref.ly/Judg9:5), [1 S 19:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:2), [2 S 17:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:9), [Am 9:3](https://ref.ly/Amos9:3), [2 Ch 18:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:24), [Dn 10:7](https://ref.ly/Dan10:7); from modesty [1 S 10:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:22); cf. [Jb 29:8](https://ref.ly/Job29:8); also לָ֫מָּה נַחְבֵּאתָ לִבְרֹתַ [Gn 31:27](https://ref.ly/Gen31:27) (E), i.e. why didst thou flee secretly? pass., *be hidden* [Jb 29:10](https://ref.ly/Job29:10) קול נְגִידִים נְחְבָאוּ (‖ולשׁונם לְחִכָּם דָּבֵָקָה, cf. also v. 9\) i.e. in reverence, \= *hushed* (so we speak of a *veiled* voice); be hidden for protection בִּשׁוֺט לָשׁוֺן [Jb 5:21](https://ref.ly/Job5:21). **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 pl. חֻבְּאוּ *have been hidden* (\= are made to hide themselves, Di *ad loc.*) [Jb 24:4](https://ref.ly/Job24:4). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. הֶחְבִּיאָנִי [Is 49:2](https://ref.ly/Isa49:2); 3 fs. הֶחְבְּאַ֔תָֹה [Jos 6:17](https://ref.ly/Josh6:17), cf. Ges§ 75, Rem. 21 *a* Köi. 624 ff. etc.; *Impf.* וַיַּחְבִּיאֵם [1 K 18:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:4); 3 fs. וַתַּחְבִּא [2 K 6:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:29); וָאַחְבִּא [1 K 18:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:13); —*hide,* trans., c. acc. [Jos 6:17](https://ref.ly/Josh6:17), [6:25](https://ref.ly/Josh6:25), [1 K 18:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:4), [18:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:13), [2 K 6:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:29); metaph., of divine protection [Is 49:2](https://ref.ly/Isa49:2) ´בְּצֵל יָדוֺ ה. **Hoph.** *Pf. be hidden* הָחְבָּאוּ [Is 42:22](https://ref.ly/Isa42:22) (בְּבָתֵּי כלאים), i.e. imprisoned. **Hithp.** *Pf.* הִתְחַבְּאוּ [1 S 14:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:11); *Impf.* יִתְחַבֵּא [1 S 23:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:23); ´וַיִּתְ [Gn 3:8](https://ref.ly/Gen3:8); יִתְחַבָּאוּ [Jb 38:30](https://ref.ly/Job38:30); וַיִּתְחַבְּאוּ [1 S 13:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:6); *Pt.* מִתְחַבֵּא [2 K 11:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:3) \+ 2 t.; מִתְחַבְּאִים [1 S 14:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:22), [1 Ch 21:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:20); —**1\.** *draw back, hide oneself,* usu. c. בְּ loc.; [Gn 3:8](https://ref.ly/Gen3:8) (J), [1 S 13:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:6), [14:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:11), [14:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:22), [23:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:23), [2 K 11:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:3), [1 Ch 21:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:20), [2 Ch 22:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:9), [22:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:12). **2\.** *draw together, thicken, harden,* of water becoming ice [Jb 38:30](https://ref.ly/Job38:30) כאבן ´מים יִתְחַ.

> \[Str4224] † מַחֲבֵא **n.\[m.]** hiding\-place, only cstr. כְּמַחֲבֵא רוּחַ וְסֵתֶר זֶרֶם [Is 32:2](https://ref.ly/Isa32:2) *hiding\-place from wind.*

> \[Str4224] † \[מַחֲבֹא] **n.\[m.]** id., pl. abs. מִכֹּל הַמַּחֲבֹאִים אשׁר יתחבא שׁם [1 S 23:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:23).

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Chronicles 22:12; Job 29:10; 2 Kings 6:29; Isaiah 32:2; Joshua 10:16; 1 Samuel 23:23; 1 Kings 18:13; 2 Samuel 17:9; 2 Chronicles 22:9; Job 24:4; Genesis 31:27; 1 Chronicles 21:20; 1 Kings 18:4; Joshua 10:17; Amos 9:3; 2 Kings 11:3; Job 38:30; Joshua 6:17; 1 Samuel 14:11; Judges 9:5; Genesis 3:10; 1 Samuel 10:22; Genesis 31:37; 2 Chronicles 18:24; Isaiah 42:22; Joshua 6:25; Genesis 3:8; Joshua 10:27; Daniel 10:7; Isaiah 49:2; 1 Samuel 19:2; 1 Samuel 13:6; 1 Samuel 14:22; Job 29:8; Job 5:21


## חָבַב (id: BDB02761)

\[Str2245] † \[חָבַב] **vb.** love (NH Pi. *id.;* Ar. حَبَّ *be loved;* Eth. ተሐበበ፡ *amore alicujus incendi* Di; Aram. ܚܰܒ *kindle, be set on fire,* ܚܰܒܶܒ, חַבֵּב *love, embrace,* חוּבָּא *bosom*)—**Qal** *Pt.* אַף חֹבֵב עַמִּים [Dt 33:3](https://ref.ly/Deut33:3) (Di rds. עַמּוֺ, cf. LXX).

> \[Str2243] † \[חֹב] **n.\[m.]** bosom (Aram.) לִטְמוֺן בְּחֻבִּי עֲוִֺ\+ני [Jb 31:33](https://ref.ly/Job31:33) (cf. חֵיק).

> \[Str2246] † חֹבָב **n.pr.m.** (cf. Ph. n.pr. חב; WeSkizzen iii, 171\. 217 suggests compar. with حُباب *serpent*)—son of Reuel, חֹתֵן of Moses (v. חתן), [Nu 10:29](https://ref.ly/Num10:29) (J E), Ju [4:11](https://ref.ly/Judg4:11); ins. ´ח prob. also Ju [1:16](https://ref.ly/Judg1:16), v. GFM.

> \[Str3160] † חֻבָּה **n.pr.m.** a descendant of Asher, [1 Ch 7:34](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:34) (rd. וְחֻבָּה, so Qr; \> Kt יְחֻבָּה).

* **Associated Passages:** Job 31:33; Judges 1:16; 1 Chronicles 7:34; Judges 4:11; Numbers 10:29; Deuteronomy 33:3
* **Associated Articles:** I חתן (ID: `BDB03591@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## חָבָה (id: BDB02765)

\[Str2247] † \[חָבָה] **vb.** withdraw, hide, cf. חבא;—**Qal** *Imv.* ms. חֲבִי [Is 26:20](https://ref.ly/Isa26:20) (Aram. form, Ol:§ 235, 3 Ew§ 224 c Du; Di rds. חֲבֵּי \= חֲבֵה), *withdraw* into privacy (‖בּאֹ בַחֲדָרֶיךָ). **Niph.** *Pf.* וְנֶחְבָּה consec. [Je 49:10](https://ref.ly/Jer49:10); לְנַחְבֵּתֶם consec. [Jos 2:16](https://ref.ly/Josh2:16) (JE); *Inf.* לְהֵחָבֵה (as א“ל) [1 K 22:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:25), [2 K 7:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:12); *withdraw, hide oneself,* lit. Jos 216 (JE), sq. שָׁמָּה; [2 K 7:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:12) c. בְּ loc.; [1 K 22:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:25); of Esau \= Edom, *hiding* from judgment of ´י, וְנֶחְבָּה לאֹ יוּכָּל [Je 49:10](https://ref.ly/Jer49:10), as pointed, meant no doubt as pf., but constr. then hard; rd. rather inf. abs. נַחְבֹּה; so Gf Gie al. (v. Köi. 624\).

> \[Str2253] † חֶבְיוֺן **n.\[m.]** hiding, hiding\-place, or \< veil, envelope, only cstr. עֻזּהֹ ´וְשָׁם ח Hb [3:4](https://ref.ly/Hab3:4) (\< LXX SyrVer Hi\-St Gr וְשָם *and he maketh,* cf. [Psalm 18:12](https://ref.ly/Ps18:12)).

> \[Str2252] † חֲבָיָה **n.pr.m.** head of a priestly family [Ezr 2:61](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:61) \= [Ne 7:63](https://ref.ly/Neh7:63) (van d. H. Ezr חֲבַיָּה; Ne חֳבָיָה, marg. חֳבַיָה; cf. Baer's note on [Ezr 2:61](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:61); LXX Ezr Λαβεια, LXXL Ωδουια, A Οβαια, LXX Ne Εβεια, LXXL Αβια, א Αβεια).

> \[Str2252] חֲבַיָּה, חֳבָיָה v. foregoing. P. 285

> \[Str3160] יְחֻבָּה v. חֻבָּה sub חבב

> \[Str5147] † נַחְבִּי **n.pr.m.** a Naphtalite, one of the Hebrew spies [Nu 13:4](https://ref.ly/Num13:4) (P).

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Kings 7:12; 1 Kings 22:25; Habakkuk 3:4; Isaiah 26:20; Joshua 2:16; Jeremiah 49:10; Psalms 18:12; Nehemiah 7:63; Numbers 13:4; Ezra 2:61
* **Associated Articles:** חָבַב (ID: `BDB02761@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## חָבַט (id: BDB02771)

\[Str2251] † \[חָבַט] **vb.** beat off, beat out (NH *id.; strike, beat,* Ar. خَبَط, Aram. ܚܒܰܛ, חֲבַט)—**Qal** *Impf.* יַחְבֹּט [Is 27:12](https://ref.ly/Isa27:12); 3 fs. וַתַּחְבֹּט [Ru 2:17](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:17); 2 ms. תַּחְבֹּט [Dt 24:20](https://ref.ly/Deut24:20); *Pt.* חֹבֵט Ju [6:11](https://ref.ly/Judg6:11); —**1\.** *beat off,* זְיתְךָ [Dt 24:20](https://ref.ly/Deut24:20). **2\.** *beat out* (grain), Gideon חֹבֵט חִטִּים בַּגַּת Ju [6:11](https://ref.ly/Judg6:11), so [Ru 2:17](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:17), of small quantities;—distinct fr. threshing on open floor, with cattle; fig. of ´י's judgment, and subsequent gathering of Isr. [Is 27:12](https://ref.ly/Isa27:12) (no obj. expr.), so Che Di Du; Ges Hi Kn Brd place sub **1;** VB undecided. **Niph.** *Impf.* יֵחָ֫בֶט [Is 28:27](https://ref.ly/Isa28:27) fennel (קֵצַךְ) beaten out בַּמַּטֶּה.

> \[Str2252] חֲבָיָה, חֲבִיָּה, חֳבָיָה v. sub חבה. p. 285

> \[Str2253] חֶבְיוֺן v. sub חבה. p. 285

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 27:12; Ruth 2:17; Deuteronomy 24:20; Isaiah 28:27; Judges 6:11


## I חָבַל (id: BDB02774)

\[Str2254] † I. \[חָבַל] **vb.** bind, pledge (Ar. حَبَلَ *bind;* cf. Aram. ܚܒܰܠ, ܚܰܒܶܠ *travail;* חֲבַל *pledge* (but rare and dub.); esp. ܚܰܒܠܴܐ *cord, field,* חַבְלָא *cord,* حَبْلَا, חֶבְלָא *pain,* esp. of travail; Sab. חבל *border\-territory,* or *field,* Sab. Denkm.:48\.49; Eth. ሐበለ፡ ZimBP 93 n., comp. As. *naḫbalu, rope, snare* (but *ḫ* \= خ))—**Qal** *Pf.* חָבָל Ez [18:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:16) *Impf.* יַחֲבֹל [Dt 24:6](https://ref.ly/Deut24:6); 2 ms. תַּחֲבֹל [Dt 24:17](https://ref.ly/Deut24:17); תַּחְבֹּל Ez [22:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:25), [Jb 22:6](https://ref.ly/Job22:6); יַחְבְּלוּ [Jb 24:3](https://ref.ly/Job24:3); יַחְבָֹּלוּ [Jb 24:9](https://ref.ly/Job24:9); *Imv.* sf. חַבְלֵהוּ [Pr 20:16](https://ref.ly/Prov20:16), [27:13](https://ref.ly/Prov27:13); *Inf. abs.* חָבֹל [Ex 22:25](https://ref.ly/Exod22:25); *Pt. act.* חֹבֵל [Dt 24:6](https://ref.ly/Deut24:6), cf. חֹבְלִים [Zc 11:7](https://ref.ly/Zech11:7), [11:14](https://ref.ly/Zech11:14) (name of מַקֵּל); *pass.* חֲבֻלִים [Am 2:8](https://ref.ly/Amos2:8); —**1\.** *bind,* only in חֹבְלִים, name of one of Zec.'s symbolic staves, [Zc 11:7](https://ref.ly/Zech11:7), [11:14](https://ref.ly/Zech11:14) symbol. of fraternity. Esp. **2\.** *bind* by taking a pledge of, *hold by a pledge,* sq. acc. Pers. [Pr 20:16](https://ref.ly/Prov20:16), [27:13](https://ref.ly/Prov27:13), [Jb 22:6](https://ref.ly/Job22:6); *take* or *hold in pledge,* sq. acc. rei [Ex 22:25](https://ref.ly/Exod22:25) ( JE), [Dt 24:6](https://ref.ly/Deut24:6), [24:6](https://ref.ly/Deut24:6), [24:17](https://ref.ly/Deut24:17), [Jb 24:3](https://ref.ly/Job24:3); sq. acc. cogn. חֲבֹל Ez [18:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:16); עַל־עָנִי יַחְבָֹּלוּ [Jb 24:9](https://ref.ly/Job24:9) prob. pregn. (v. Di) *take pledges* (getting power) *over the poor;* בְּגָדִים חֲבֻלִים [Am 2:8](https://ref.ly/Amos2:8) *garments taken in pledge.* **Niph.** *Impf.* יֵחָ֫בֶל לוֺ [Pr 13:13](https://ref.ly/Prov13:13) *becometh pledged to it* (i.e. pledged to pay the penalty, opp. יְשֻׁלָּם), so Ew Hi De Now Str RVm VB. \> sub II. חָבַל q. v. **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 fs. חִבְּלָה Ct [8:5](https://ref.ly/Song8:5); חִבְּלַתְךָ Ct [8:5](https://ref.ly/Song8:5); *Impf.* יְחַבֶּ־ל [Psalm 7:15](https://ref.ly/Ps7:15); —*writhe, twist,* hence *travail;* of mother Ct [8:5](https://ref.ly/Song8:5), [8:5](https://ref.ly/Song8:5) (c. acc. of child; ‖יְלָדָתְךָ); metaph. of wicked man :יְחַבֶּל־אָוֶן וְהָרָה עָמָל וְיָלַד שָֽׁקֶר [Psalm 7:15](https://ref.ly/Ps7:15).

> \[Str2256] † I. חֶ֫בֶל **n.m.**:[Jos 2:14](https://ref.ly/Josh2:14) **f.**:† [Zp 2:6](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:6) (but v. infr.) cord, territory, band; ´ח abs. [Jos 2:15](https://ref.ly/Josh2:15) \+; cstr. [Dt 3:4](https://ref.ly/Deut3:4) \+, חֵ֫בֶל [Jos 19:29](https://ref.ly/Josh19:29); sf. חַבְלוֺ [Jb 18:10](https://ref.ly/Job18:10); pl. חֲבָלִים [2 S 8:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:2) \+ 10 t. \+ Ez [47:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:13), [Jb 21:7](https://ref.ly/Job21:7) v. infr.; cstr. חַבְלֵי [Jos 17:5](https://ref.ly/Josh17:5) \+ 5 t.; חֶבְלֵי [Psalm 116:3](https://ref.ly/Ps116:3) \+ 2 t. \+ [Psalm 18:5](https://ref.ly/Ps18:5) (rd. משׁברי v. infr.); sf. הֲבָלָיִךְ [Is 33:23](https://ref.ly/Isa33:23); חֲבָלָיו [Is 33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20); —**1\.** *cord, rope,* by which men were lowered [Jos 2:15](https://ref.ly/Josh2:15) (JE), [Je 38:6](https://ref.ly/Jer38:6), [38:11](https://ref.ly/Jer38:11), [38:12](https://ref.ly/Jer38:12), [38:13](https://ref.ly/Jer38:13), or stones dragged [2 S 17:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:13); of tents [Is 33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20); tackling of ship v:[23](https://ref.ly/Isa33:23); for binding Ez [27:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:24), [Jb 40:25](https://ref.ly/Job40:25), [Est 1:6](https://ref.ly/Esth1:6); symbol of captivity or subjection [1 K 20:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:31), [20:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:32); fig. of snare for wicked [Jb 18:10](https://ref.ly/Job18:10) (‖מַלְכֻּדְתּוֺ), set by wicked [Psalm 140:6](https://ref.ly/Ps140:6) (‖פַּה); [Psalm 119:61](https://ref.ly/Ps119:61) *cords of wicked men have surrounded me;* of wicked held בחבלי חַטָּאתוֺ [Pr 5:22](https://ref.ly/Prov5:22); ´ח הָשָּׁוְא [Is 5:18](https://ref.ly/Isa5:18) *cords* (*ropes*) *of wickedness* (‖כַּעֲבוֺת הָעֲגָלָה); of cords of distress (עָֹנִי) [Jb 36:8](https://ref.ly/Job36:8) (‖וִקִּים); so חֶבְלֵי מָוֶת (prob.) [Psalm 116:3](https://ref.ly/Ps116:3), חֶבְלֵי שׁאוֺל [Psalm 18:6](https://ref.ly/Ps18:6) \= [2 S 22:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:6) (‖מוֺקְשֵׁי מָוֶת);—for חֶבְלֵי מות [Psalm 18:5](https://ref.ly/Ps18:5) rd. מִשְׁבְּרֵי, v. [2 S 22:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:5) and ‖נַחֲלֵי בְלִיַּעַל, De Che Bae; to draw בְּחַכְלֵי אָדָם [Ho 11:4](https://ref.ly/Hos11:4) (i.e. *humanely,* kindly); of cord of life חֶבֶל הַכֶּסֶף [Ec 12:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:6). **2\.** *measuring\-cord, line* מִדָּה ´ח [Zc 2:5](https://ref.ly/Zech2:5); so ´ח alone [2 S 8:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:2), [8:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:2), [8:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:2), [Am 7:17](https://ref.ly/Amos7:17), [Mi 2:5](https://ref.ly/Mic2:5), [Psalm 78:55](https://ref.ly/Ps78:55); fig. (of favoured life) [Psalm 16:5](https://ref.ly/Ps16:5); in gen. of one's portion, fortune [Jb 21:17](https://ref.ly/Job21:17) acc. to Targum Thes Add. Ew Di al., but \< v. חֵבֶל. Thence **3\.** *measured portion, lot, part, region,* נַחֲלָה ´ח *inherited portion* [Dt 32:9](https://ref.ly/Deut32:9), [1 Ch 16:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:18) \= [Psalm 105:11](https://ref.ly/Ps105:11); also ´ח alone [Jos 17:5](https://ref.ly/Josh17:5), [17:14](https://ref.ly/Josh17:14), [19:9](https://ref.ly/Josh19:9), Ez [47:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:13) (חבלים; Vulgate Targum rd. as dual, v. ## II חָבַל (id: BDB02783)

\[Str2254] II. חָבַל **vb.** act (ruinously) corruptly (NH *id. wound, injure;* Ar. خَبَلَ *corrupt* or *render unsound* (the mind, a limb, etc.), خَبِلَ *be deranged* (in mind); As. *ḫabâlu, injure,* VR62, 11; Aram. ܚܰܒܶܠ, חַבֵּל *corrupt, ruin;* Eth. ተኀበለ፡ *be arrogant, audacious*; Sab. חַבל DHMEpigr. Denkim. 48; cf. Palm. חבל *mortuus* VogNo. 161 and oft.)—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 pl. חָבַלְנוּ [Ne 1:7](https://ref.ly/Neh1:7); *Impf.* 1 s. אֶחְבֹּל [Jb 34:31](https://ref.ly/Job34:31); *Inf. cstr.* (used as *abs.*) [Ne 1:7](https://ref.ly/Neh1:7); —*act corruptly* against, חֲרֹל חבלנו לְ [Ne 1:7](https://ref.ly/Neh1:7); abs. [Jb 34:31](https://ref.ly/Job34:31). **Niph.** *Impf.* יֵחָ֫בֶל לוֺ [Pr 13:13](https://ref.ly/Prov13:13) *shall be ruined* (לְ reflex.), Thes RobGes AV RV, but \< I. חָבַל (q. v.) **Pi.** *ruin, destroy: Pf.* 3 ms. consec. וְחִבֵּל את מעשה ידיך [Ec 5:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:5), subj. God. *Impf.* 3 fs. תְּחַבֵּל וְחֶבֶל נִמְרָץ [Mi 2:10](https://ref.ly/Mic2:10) *that destroyeth,* even with sore destruction; but rd. rather ´נ ´תְּחֻבְּלוּ ח *ye shall be destroyed with* etc., so LXX Roorda Che. *Inf. cstr.* לְחַבֵּל c. acc., the poor [Is 32:7](https://ref.ly/Isa32:7), the land [13:5](https://ref.ly/Isa13:5); abs. [54:16](https://ref.ly/Isa54:16); cf. *Pt.* pl. מְחַבְּלִים Ct [2:15](https://ref.ly/Song2:15) c. acc. of foxes ruining vineyards. **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 ms. וְחֻבַּל consec. [Is 10:27](https://ref.ly/Isa10:27) of yoke, *be ruined, broken,* si vera l.; RSJPh xiii. 1885, p. 62 prop. יחדל, cf. also Checrit. n.; and rem. by Di Du; 3 fs. חֻבָּלָה [Jb 17:1](https://ref.ly/Job17:1) *my spirit is broken* (‖יָמַי נִזְעָכוּ); *Impf.* 2 mpl. תְּחֻבְּלוּ [Mi 2:10](https://ref.ly/Mic2:10) (emend.: v. supr.)

## חָבַק (id: BDB02787)

\[Str2263] † \[חָבַק] **vb.** clasp, embrace (NH *id.*; Targum חַבֵּק; Mand. ܚܒܰܩ, NSyr. حپق; Ar. حَبَقَ II. *collect* one's possessions, etc., Frey)—**Qal** *Inf.* לַחֲבוֺק [Ec 3:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:5); *Pt.* חֹבֵּק [Ec 4:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:5); חֹבֶקֶת [2 K 4:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:16); —*embrace,* c. acc. בֵּן [2 K 4:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:16); יָדָיו [Ec 4:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:5) (said of הַכְּסִיל \= fold his arms, i.e. be idle; cf. חִבֻּק); no obj. expr. [Ec 3:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:5) (‖ **Pi** ). **Pi.** *Pf.* חִבְּקוּ [Jb 24:8](https://ref.ly/Job24:8), [La 4:5](https://ref.ly/Lam4:5); *Impf.* וַיְחַבֵּק [Gn 48:10](https://ref.ly/Gen48:10); וַיְחַבֶּ־ק [Gn 29:13](https://ref.ly/Gen29:13); וַיְחַבְּקֵהוּ [Gn 33:4](https://ref.ly/Gen33:4); 3 fs. sf. תְּחַבְּקֵ֫נִי Ct [2:6](https://ref.ly/Song2:6), [8:3](https://ref.ly/Song8:3); 2 ms. וּתְחַבֵּק [Pr 5:20](https://ref.ly/Prov5:20), sf. תְּחַבְּקֶ֫נָּה [Pr 4:8](https://ref.ly/Prov4:8); *Inf.* חַבֵּק [Ec 3:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:5); —*embrace* of Pers., no obj. עת לִרְחֹק מֵחַבֵּק [Ec 3:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:5) (‖ **Qal**); sq. לְ [Gn 29:13](https://ref.ly/Gen29:13) (J), [48:10](https://ref.ly/Gen48:10) (E); sq. sf. [33:4](https://ref.ly/Gen33:4) (J), [Pr 4:8](https://ref.ly/Prov4:8), Ct [2:6](https://ref.ly/Song2:6), [8:3](https://ref.ly/Song8:3); c. acc. [Pr 5:20](https://ref.ly/Prov5:20); embrace צוּר for want of shelter [Jb 24:8](https://ref.ly/Job24:8) (of poor); embrace refuse\-heaps אשׁפתות [La 4:5](https://ref.ly/Lam4:5), i.e. are outcasts.

> \[Str2265] † חֲבַקּוּק **n.pr.m** the prophet Habakkuk Hb [1:1](https://ref.ly/Hab1:1), [3:1](https://ref.ly/Hab3:1); LXX Ἀμβακουμ (As. *ḫambaḳûḳu* is name of a plant, DlHA 36, Pr 84, so حبقبق inYemen (Schweinfurth) v. HomAufsätze u. Abh. 1892, 27, 28\).

## חָבַר (id: BDB02790)

\[Str2266] † \[חָבַר] **vb.** unite (usu. intr.), be joined, tie a magic knot or spell, charm (NH *id.*; Eth. ኅበሪ፡ yet As. \[*abâru*], *ubburu* bind, ban (of spells), *êbru*, friend, and many deriv., DlW 51 ff.; Aram. ܚܰܒܰܪ חַבֵּר and many deriv.; cf. Ph. n. חבר associate)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 mpl. חָֽבְרוּ [Gn 14:3](https://ref.ly/Gen14:3) *Pt. act.* m. חֹבֵר [Dt 18:11](https://ref.ly/Deut18:11), חוֺבֵר [Psalm 58:6](https://ref.ly/Ps58:6); fpl. חֹבְרֹת [Ex 26:3](https://ref.ly/Exod26:3), [26:3](https://ref.ly/Exod26:3) \+ 2t. \+ Ez [1:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:9) (cf. infr.), חוֺבְרוֺת Ez [1:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:11); *Pt. pass.* cstr. חֲבוּר [Ho 4:17](https://ref.ly/Hos4:17) (yet. v. infr.);—**1\.** *unite, be joined:* **a.** of allies, sq. אֶ־לֹ loc. [Gn 14:3](https://ref.ly/Gen14:3), constr. praegn. \= *came as allies unto*; pt. pass. fig. חֲבוּר עֲצַבִּים אֶפְרָ֭יִם [Ho 4:17](https://ref.ly/Hos4:17) *Ephr. is joined to idols* (but We rds. חבר, v. חבֵר **2 d,** and cf. חֲבֵרָיו [Is 44:11](https://ref.ly/Isa44:11), חֲבֶרְתְּךָ [Mal 2:14](https://ref.ly/Mal2:14)). **b.** of one thing reaching to, touching another; wings of Ezek.'s living creatures sq. אֶ־ל rei, Ez [1:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:9) (del. LXX B Co, but v. Sm); cf. אישׁ ´ת, v:[11](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:11), i.e. *joining each one* (trans.), \< LXX SyrVer Co אשׁה ´ח אל־אחותה (as v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:9)), *united each to the other;* so of curtains of tabernacle, sq. אֶ־ל rei [Ex 26:3](https://ref.ly/Exod26:3), [26:3](https://ref.ly/Exod26:3); abs. *joined together,* of shoulderpieces of ephod [Ex 28:7](https://ref.ly/Exod28:7) (all P; cf. also **Pu**). **2\.** *tie magic knots, charm*(RSJPh xiv.1885,123 thinks mng. *charm* is der. fr. *nectere verba,* and comp. Ar. خَبَر, *narrative*); only c. acc. cogn. חָבֶר ´ה [Dt 18:11](https://ref.ly/Deut18:11) (in a long series of kindred phrases), specif. of charming serpents [Psalm 58:6](https://ref.ly/Ps58:6) (‖מְלַחֲשִׁים). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 ms. חִבַּר [Ex 36:10](https://ref.ly/Exod36:10); 2 ms. וְחִבַּרְתָּ֫ [Ex 26:6](https://ref.ly/Exod26:6) \+ 2 t.; *Impf.* וַיְחַבֵּר [Ex 36:10](https://ref.ly/Exod36:10) \+ 2 t.; sf. וַיְחַבְּרֵהוּ [2 Ch 20:36](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:36); *Inf. cstr.* לְחַבֵּר Ex 36:l8;—**1\.** *make an ally of unite* one with, only ויחברהו עִמּוֺ [2 Ch 20:36](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:36) *and he united him with himself,* sq. Inf. purpose. **2\.** *unite, join* (trans.), only Ex (P), of tabern., obj. the curtains, sq. acc. \+ אֶ־ל rei [Ex 26:6](https://ref.ly/Exod26:6), [36:10](https://ref.ly/Exod36:10), [36:10](https://ref.ly/Exod36:10), [36:13](https://ref.ly/Exod36:13); . sq. acc. only [26:9](https://ref.ly/Exod26:9), [36:16](https://ref.ly/Exod36:16); obj. את־האהל *join the tent together,* [26:11](https://ref.ly/Exod26:11), [36:18](https://ref.ly/Exod36:18). **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 ms חֻבָּר [Ex 39:4](https://ref.ly/Exod39:4), וְחֻבָּר consec [Ex 28:7](https://ref.ly/Exod28:7) (but v. infr.) 3 fs. שֶׁחֻבְּרָ־ה [Psalm 122:3](https://ref.ly/Ps122:3); *Impf.* 3 ms. יְחֻבַּר [Ec 9:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:4) Qr (Kt יבחרv. infr.); sf. הַיְחָבְרְךָ [Psalm 94:20](https://ref.ly/Ps94:20) (Köi. 257 f.; Ges Ew Bö De al. as **Qal**;—**1\.a.** *be allied with* thee (sf. ref. to ´י) [Psalm 94:20](https://ref.ly/Ps94:20); **b.** *be united to* \= be one of, אל כ־ל ´מי אשׁר יְח הַחַיִּם *whoever is united to all the living* (Kt יבחר is meaningless). **2\.** *be joined together,* of ephod ´עַל־שְׁנֵי קְצוֺותָו ח [Ex 39:4](https://ref.ly/Exod39:4) *by its two edges was it joined together;* cf. ‖ [28:7](https://ref.ly/Exod28:7) (where MT אֶל־שְׁנֵי קְצוֺתיו וְחֻבָּר \< LXX Sam which rd. ´ק ´עַל־שׁ יְחֻבָּר); ־לָתּ´עִיר שֶׁח [Psalm 122:3](https://ref.ly/Ps122:3) *joined together for itself* i.e. compactly built (of Jerusalem). **Hiph.** *Impf* 1 s. אַחְבִּ֫ירָה עליכם בְּמִלִּים [Jb 16:4](https://ref.ly/Job16:4) *I could make a joining with words* (i.e. *join words together* RV VB) *against you.* **Hithp.** (late) *Pf.* אֶתְחַבֵּר (Ges§ 54 1 n.) [2 Ch 20:35](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:35); *Impf.* יִתְחַבָּרוּ [Dn 11:6](https://ref.ly/Dan11:6); *Inf.* sf. הִתְחַבֶּרְךָ [2 Ch 20:37](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:37); Aram. form הִתְחַבְּרוּת [Dn 11:23](https://ref.ly/Dan11:23) (Ges§ 54, 3 R. 1\)—*join oneself to, make an alliance* with, sq. עִם Pers. [2 Ch 20:35](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:35), [20:37](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:37), sq. אֶ־ל Pers. [Dn 11:23](https://ref.ly/Dan11:23); recipr. *league together* (abs.) [Dn 11:6](https://ref.ly/Dan11:6).

> \[Str2267] † חֶ֫בֶר **n.\[m.]** company, association, spell—abs. חָבֶר [Dt 18:11](https://ref.ly/Deut18:11) \+ 2 t.; cstr. חֶבֶר [Ho 6:9](https://ref.ly/Hos6:9); pl. חֲבָרִים [Psalm 58:6](https://ref.ly/Ps58:6), sf. חֲבָרַיִךְ [Is 47:9](https://ref.ly/Isa47:9), [47:11](https://ref.ly/Isa47:11)—**1\.** *company, band* of (bad) priests [Ho 6:9](https://ref.ly/Hos6:9). **2\.** *association, society* (abstr.), only in בֵּית חָבֶר [Pr 21:9](https://ref.ly/Prov21:9) \= [25:24](https://ref.ly/Prov25:24) *house of association,* i. e. shared with a contentious woman (house in common, v.RVm VB). **3\.** spell : **a.** as acc. cogn. c. חָבַר (q. v.) [Dt 18:11](https://ref.ly/Deut18:11), [Psalm 58:6](https://ref.ly/Ps58:6). **b.** alone, of Babylonian magic [Is 47:9](https://ref.ly/Isa47:9), [47:11](https://ref.ly/Isa47:11) (v. Len Chald. Magic \[l878]; Magie u. Wahrsage\-kunst 1878; ScholzGötzendienst u. Zauberwesen, 1877\. 80 ff. SayTBA iii. 145 ff and Rel,. Bab 396 ff., 440 ff.).

## חָבַשׁ (id: BDB02808)

\[Str2280] † \[חָבַשׁ] **vb.** bind, bind on, bind up (NH *id.;* As. *abâšu,* acc. to DlW 70, Pr 174; Ar. حَبَسَ *confine, restrict;* yet on As. *ḫibšu ‘Kopfbinde’* (c. חַ), etc., v. ZehnpfundBAS i. 499, 526; Aram. ܚܒܰܫ; cf. ܚܒܽܘܫܝܳܐ, חֲבוּשְׁיָא *imprisonment,* etc.)—**Qal** *Pf.* 2 ms. וְחָבַשְׁתָּ֫ [Ex 29:9](https://ref.ly/Exod29:9); 2 mpl. חֲבַשְׁתֶּם Ez [34:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:4); *Impf.* יַחֲבוֺשׁ [Jb 34:17](https://ref.ly/Job34:17); וַיַּחֲבשׁ [Gn 22:3](https://ref.ly/Gen22:3) \+ 4 t.; וַיַּחֲבָ־שׁ [1 K 13:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:23); וְיֶחְבָּשׁ [Jb 5:18](https://ref.ly/Job5:18); וַיַּחְבְּשֵׁנוּ [Ho 6:1](https://ref.ly/Hos6:1); 3 fs. וַתַחֲבשׁ [2 K 4:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:24); אֶחֱבשׁ Ez [34:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:16); cohort. אֶחְבְּשָׁ־ה [2 S 19:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:27) (but cf. infr.); וָאֶחְבְּשֵׁח Ez [16:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:10); וַיַּחְבְּשׁ־ו [1 K 13:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:13); וִיַּחֲבָשׁו [1 K 13:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:27); *Imv.* חֲבשׁ [Jb 40:13](https://ref.ly/Job40:13); חֲבוֺשׁ Ez [24:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:17); חִבשׁוּ [1 K 13:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:13), [13:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:27); *Inf. cstr.* חֲבשׁ [Is 30:26](https://ref.ly/Isa30:26), [61:1](https://ref.ly/Isa61:1); לְחָבְּשָׁהּ Ez [30:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:21) (del. Co, cf. LXX). *Pt. act.* חֹבֵשׁ [Is 3:7](https://ref.ly/Isa3:7); *pass.* חָבוּשׁ [Jon 2:6](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:6); חֲבוּשִׁים Ju [19:10](https://ref.ly/Judg19:10); חֲבֻשִׁים [2 S 16:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:1), Ez [27:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:24); —**1\.** *bind, bind on:* **a.** headgear, c. acc. rei, לְ Pers. [Ex 29:9](https://ref.ly/Exod29:9), [Lv 8:13](https://ref.ly/Lev8:13) (both P); c. לְ Pers. Ez [24:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:17) (P); *pass.* of seaweeds clinging about head סוּף חָבוּשׁ לְראֹשִׁי [Jon 2:6](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:6); c. sf. Pers. et בְּ rei וָאֶחְבְּשֵׁח Ez [16:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:10) *and I bound* (or *wound*) *about thee* (i.e. thy head, Sm VB) שֵׁשׁ (in metaph. of ´י's care for Jerus.; ‖אַלְבִּישֵׁח, אֶנְעֲלֵח); fig. of punishment for wicked פְּנֵיהֶם חֲבשׁ בממון [Jb 40:13](https://ref.ly/Job40:13) *bind their faces in darkness* (‖טָמְנֵם בֶּעָפָר) v. Di. **b.** pass. prob. *twined, twisted* in חֲבָלִים חֲבֻשִׁים וַאֲרֻוִים Ez [27:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:24), *cords twisted and strong,* v. Sm Da. Esp. **c.** of equipping a beast for riding; ass, in acc. [Gn 22:3](https://ref.ly/Gen22:3), [Nu 22:21](https://ref.ly/Num22:21) (both E), [2 S 17:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:23) \& [19:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:27) (where however rd. לוֺ חָבְשָׁה־לִּי, so LXX SyrVer Vulgate Th We Dr), [1 K 2:40](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:40), [13:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:13), [13:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:13), [13:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:23), [13:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:27), [13:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:27) (v:[27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:27) b no dir. obj. expressed) [2 K 4:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:24); pass., also of asses, Ju [19:10](https://ref.ly/Judg19:10), [2 S 16:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:1). **d.** fig. \= *restrain, control* (Germ. *bändigen*), abs. [Jb 34:17](https://ref.ly/Job34:17). **2\.** *bind up,* viz. a wound, usually in fig., of comforting the distressed, etc.; c. sf. Pers. [Ho 6:1](https://ref.ly/Hos6:1) (‖ רָפָא, opp. הִכָּה); sq. לְ Pers. לַחֲבשׁ לְנִשְׁבְּרֵי־לֵב [Is 61:1](https://ref.ly/Isa61:1); cf. Ez [34:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:4) (sq. לַנִּשְׁבֶּרֶת; ‖חִזֵּק, רִפֵּא), v:[16](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:16) (sq. *id.;* ‖חִזֵּק); sq. acc. rei בְּיוֺם אֶת־שֶׁבֶר עַמּוֺ ´חֲבשׁ י [Is 30:26](https://ref.ly/Isa30:26) (‖רָפָא); c. acc. of Pharaoh's broken arm Ez [30:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:21) (si vera l., v. supr.; cf. **Pu.**); abs. [Jb 5:18](https://ref.ly/Job5:18); also [Is 3:7](https://ref.ly/Isa3:7) of repairing fortunes of people (opp. הִכְאִיב; ‖רָפָה \= רָפָא). **Pi.** *Pf.* חִבֵּשׁ *bind, restrain* מִבְּכִי נהרות [Jb 28:11](https://ref.ly/Job28:11); *Pt.* מְחַבֵּשׁ *bind up* לְעַצְבוֺתָם [Psalm 147:3](https://ref.ly/Ps147:3). **Pu.** *Pf. be bound up* חֻבְּשָׁה Ez [30:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:21) (metaph.) Pharaoh's broken arm; חֻבָּשׁוּ [Is 1:6](https://ref.ly/Isa1:6) (wounds of land of Judah).

* **Associated Passages:** Job 40:13; Isaiah 30:26; Psalms 147:3; Ezekiel 34:16; Ezekiel 34:4; Leviticus 8:13; Ezekiel 30:21; 2 Samuel 16:1; Exodus 29:9; Genesis 22:3; Ezekiel 24:17; 1 Kings 2:40; Ezekiel 27:24; Isaiah 1:6; Jonah 2:6; 2 Kings 4:24; Isaiah 61:1; Ezekiel 16:10; 2 Samuel 17:23; 2 Samuel 19:27; Job 34:17; Hosea 6:1; Judges 19:10; Numbers 22:21; Isaiah 3:7; 1 Kings 13:23; 1 Kings 13:27; Job 5:18; 1 Kings 13:13; Job 28:11


## חבת (id: BDB02809)

חבת (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. خَبَتَ *be obscure,* IV. *be lowly, submissive,* خَبْتٌ *low ground*).

> \[Str2281] † חֲבִתִּים **n.\[m.] pl.** (NH *id.*)—some kind of flat cakes, or bread\-wafers, only ´מַעֲשֵה הַח [1 Ch 9:31](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:31).

> \[Str4227] † מַחֲבַת **n.f.** flat plate, pan, or griddle for baking, only P and late; abs. ´מ [Lv 2:5](https://ref.ly/Lev2:5) \+ 2 t.; מַחֲבַָת [Lv 7:9](https://ref.ly/Lev7:9); cstr. מַחֲבַת Ez [4:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:3); —**1\.** used in preparing the (baken) *mincha,* preceded by עַ־ל [Lv 2:5](https://ref.ly/Lev2:5), [6:14](https://ref.ly/Lev6:14), [7:9](https://ref.ly/Lev7:9) (all P), cf. [1 Ch 23:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:29). **2\.** בַּרְזֶל ´מ Ez [4:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:3) *a plate of iron,* signifying an iron wall, in prediction of siege of city.

> \[Str2282–83] חָג, חָגָּא v. sub חגּגּ. below, p. 291

* **Associated Passages:** Leviticus 7:9; Leviticus 6:14; Leviticus 2:5; 1 Chronicles 9:31; Ezekiel 4:3; 1 Chronicles 23:29


## חגּב (id: BDB02813)

חגּב (√ of foll.; mng. dub.; poss. cf. Ar. حَجَبَ *prevent, intervene, hide,* حِجَابٌ *that which veils, conceals, hides;* Syr. ܚܽܘܓܒܳܐ *shrine;* hence *locusts* as *concealing* the sky?).

> \[Str2284] † I. חָגָב **n.m.**:[Ec 12:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:5) locust, grasshopper (NH *id.;* cf. Targum), prob. a non\-flying species, v. Kn in Di:[Lv 11:22](https://ref.ly/Lev11:22); הֶחָגָב allowed as food [Lv 11:22](https://ref.ly/Lev11:22) (P; ‖אַרְבֶּה, סָלְעָם, חַרְגֹּל), כַּחֲגָבִיס [Nu 13:33](https://ref.ly/Num13:33) (JE; sim. of insignificance), so [Is 40:22](https://ref.ly/Isa40:22); as agent of Yahweh's wrath חָגָב לָאֱכוֺל חארץ [2 Ch 7:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:13); וְיִסְתַּבֵּל הֶחָגָב [Ec 12:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:5) *and the grasshopper shall be a burden;* better *shall drag itself along,* De Hi Now RVm; De thinks ´ח here fig. of the back part of the pelvic cavity (Ar. حَجَبَةٌ is *point of hip\-bone,* Bo, cf. Lane), and refers to stiffness of aged joints, so Now; but the meaning is dubious; v. other views of Comm.

> \[Str2285] † II. חָגָּב **n.pr.m.** head of a family of returning exiles [Ezr 2:46](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:46) LXX Ἁγαβ (no ‖ in Ne 7 H; but LXXL [Ne 7:48](https://ref.ly/Neh7:48) has both Αγαβ and Αγαβα, cf. חֲגָבָא).

> \[Str2286] † חֲגָבָא **n.pr.m.** head of family of returning exiles [Ne 7:48](https://ref.ly/Neh7:48), LXX Ἁγαβα (cf. also foregoing) prob. \= following.

> \[Str2286] † חֲגָבָה **n.pr.m.** head of a family of returning exiles [Ezr 2:47](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:47), prob. \= foregoing; LXX Ἁγαβα.

* **Associated Passages:** Leviticus 11:22; Ecclesiastes 12:5; Isaiah 40:22; Numbers 13:33; Nehemiah 7:48; Ezra 2:47; 2 Chronicles 7:13; Ezra 2:46


## חָגַג (id: BDB02818)

\[Str2287] † \[חָגַג] **vb.** make pilgrimage, keep a pilgrim\-feast (Ar. حَجَّ *betake oneself to or towards an object of reverence; make a pilgrimage to Mecaa;* Sab. חגג *make pilgrimage* Sab. Denkm.:86\. cf. 85; Syr. ܚܰܓܺܝ *celebrate a feast.* In Palm. חגגו is n.pr. VogNo. 61; cf. also Ph. n.pr. חגי, חנת);—**Qal** *Pf.* 2 mpl. חַגֹּתֶם [Ex 12:14](https://ref.ly/Exod12:14) \+ 2 t.; *Impf.* 2 ms. תָּחֹג [Ex 23:14](https://ref.ly/Exod23:14), [Dt 16:15](https://ref.ly/Deut16:15); 3 mpl. יָח֫וֺגּוּ [Psalm 107:27](https://ref.ly/Ps107:27) (Bö§ 1118 (2\)); וְיָחֹ֫גּוּ [Ex 5:1](https://ref.ly/Exod5:1); 2 mpl. תָּחֹ֫גּוּ [Lv 23:39](https://ref.ly/Lev23:39), [23:41](https://ref.ly/Lev23:41); *sf.* תְּחָגֻּ֫הוּ [Ex 12:14](https://ref.ly/Exod12:14); *Imv.* f. חָגִּי [Na 2:1](https://ref.ly/Nah2:1); *Inf.* cstr. חֹג [Zc 14:18](https://ref.ly/Zech14:18) \+ 2t., *Pt.* חוֺגֵג [Psalm 42:5](https://ref.ly/Ps42:5); pl. חֹגְַגִים [1 S 30:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:16); —**1\.** *keep a pilgrim\-feast,* abs. [Ex 5:1](https://ref.ly/Exod5:1) (JE) of one proposed by Moses; שׁלשׁ רגלים תחג לי *three times shalt thou make pilgrimage unto me* (in the year) [Ex 23:14](https://ref.ly/Exod23:14) (covt. code); of pilgrim\-feasts in general [Na 2:1](https://ref.ly/Nah2:1), the Passover [Ex 12:14](https://ref.ly/Exod12:14) (P); elsewh. feast of Booths; all celebrated in part by sacred processions and dances; המון חוגג [Psalm 42:5](https://ref.ly/Ps42:5) *multitude keeping festival* (led by the Psalmist in procession); חגג חג [Lv 23:39](https://ref.ly/Lev23:39) (H), [Nu 29:12](https://ref.ly/Num29:12) (P), [Na 2:1](https://ref.ly/Nah2:1), [Zc 14:16](https://ref.ly/Zech14:16), [14:18](https://ref.ly/Zech14:18), [14:19](https://ref.ly/Zech14:19); חגג אתו חנ [Ex 12:14](https://ref.ly/Exod12:14) (P), [Lv 23:41](https://ref.ly/Lev23:41) (H); אתוֺ [Lv 23:41](https://ref.ly/Lev23:41) (H); sf. [Ex 12:14](https://ref.ly/Exod12:14) (P). **2\.** behave as at a חָג [1 S 30:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:16) (of the Amalekites when surprised by David i.e. *enjoying themselves merrily,* Dr; others, *circling in the sacred dance*). **3\.** *reel,* in giddiness on the sea [Psalm 107:27](https://ref.ly/Ps107:27) (cf. חָגָּא [Is 19:17](https://ref.ly/Isa19:17)); this either points to an earlier meaning of √; or better, the Psalm being late, means *reel* as from festival excesses (‖וְיָנוּעוּ כַשִּׁכּוֺר).

> \[Str2282] † חַג **n.m.**:[Is 29:1](https://ref.ly/Isa29:1) festival\-gathering, feast, pilgrim\-feast (NH *id.;* Aram. ܚܰܓܳܐ, חַגָּא; Ar. حَجٌّ *pilgrimage;* Sab. חג DHMEpigr. Denkm. 31; NöZMG 1887\. 719; RSProph. Lect. ii. n. 6; \> WeSkizzen iii. 106, 165 *sacred dance*)—חַג abs. and cstr. [Ex 10:9](https://ref.ly/Exod10:9) \+ 36 t.; חָג [Nu 28:17](https://ref.ly/Num28:17) \+ 13 t.; sf. חַגִּי [Ex 23:18](https://ref.ly/Exod23:18); חֶַ\+גּךָ [Dt 16:14](https://ref.ly/Deut16:14); חַגָּהּ [Ho 2:13](https://ref.ly/Hos2:13); חַגֵּנוּ [Psalm 81:4](https://ref.ly/Ps81:4); pl. חַגִּים [Is 29:1](https://ref.ly/Isa29:1) \+ 2 t.; sf. חַגַּיִח [Na 2:1](https://ref.ly/Nah2:1); חַגֵּיכֶם [Am 5:21](https://ref.ly/Amos5:21) \+ 2 t.;—**1\.** *feast,* esp. one observed by a *pilgrimage* ([Ex 23:14](https://ref.ly/Exod23:14), [23:17](https://ref.ly/Exod23:17)): **a.** *special feast* to the golden calf [Ex 32:5](https://ref.ly/Exod32:5) (J; where there was a sacred dance v:[19](https://ref.ly/Exod23:19)); a feast in the 8th month observed by Jeroboam in place of the feast of the 7th month in Judah [1 K 12:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:32), [12:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:33); pilgrim feast proposed by Moses [Ex 10:9](https://ref.ly/Exod10:9) (JE); feast at Shiloh Ju [21:19](https://ref.ly/Judg21:19). Elsewhere **b.** apparently always of the three great pilgrim feasts, celebrated by processions and dancing: (1\) *in general* [Is 30:29](https://ref.ly/Isa30:29), [Am 8:10](https://ref.ly/Amos8:10), [Na 2:1](https://ref.ly/Nah2:1); חגים ינקפו *let feasts come round* [Is 29:1](https://ref.ly/Isa29:1); ‖עצרות [Am 5:21](https://ref.ly/Amos5:21); ´חַג לי [Lv 23:41](https://ref.ly/Lev23:41) (H); disting. fr. the more gen. מועדים (sacred seasons) Ez [45:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:17), [46:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:11), [Ho 9:5](https://ref.ly/Hos9:5) (יום חג), and fr. specif. חדשׁים, שׁבתות as well, [Ho 2:13](https://ref.ly/Hos2:13); (2\) in particular: unleavened cakes and Passover מצות(ה)חג [Ex 23:15](https://ref.ly/Exod23:15) \= [34:18](https://ref.ly/Exod34:18) (JE); [Lv 23:6](https://ref.ly/Lev23:6) (P), [Dt 16:16](https://ref.ly/Deut16:16), [2 Ch 8:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:13), [30:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:13), [30:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:21), [35:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:17), [Ezr 6:22](https://ref.ly/Ezra6:22); חג [Ex 12:14](https://ref.ly/Exod12:14) (P); first day of the seven [Nu 28:17](https://ref.ly/Num28:17) (P); the last day [Ex 13:6](https://ref.ly/Exod13:6) (J); the seven Ez [45:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:21), [45:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:23); חג הפסח [Ex 34:25](https://ref.ly/Exod34:25), חגי [Ex 23:18](https://ref.ly/Exod23:18) (both JE); בַּכֶּסֶה לְיּוֺם חַגֵּנוּ [Psalm 81:4](https://ref.ly/Ps81:4) (probably the full moon of Passover); חג הקציר [Ex 23:16](https://ref.ly/Exod23:16) \= חג שׁבעת [Ex 34:22](https://ref.ly/Exod34:22) (JE), cf. [Dt 16:10](https://ref.ly/Deut16:10), [16:16](https://ref.ly/Deut16:16), [2 Ch 8:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:13); ף(י)חג האס [Ex 23:16](https://ref.ly/Exod23:16) \= [34:22](https://ref.ly/Exod34:22) (JE) \= ת(ו)חג הסכ [Lv 23:34](https://ref.ly/Lev23:34) (P), [Dt 16:13](https://ref.ly/Deut16:13), [16:16](https://ref.ly/Deut16:16), [31:10](https://ref.ly/Deut31:10), [2 Ch 8:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:13), [Ezr 3:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:4), [Zc 14:16](https://ref.ly/Zech14:16), [14:18](https://ref.ly/Zech14:18), [14:19](https://ref.ly/Zech14:19); also ´חג י [Lv 23:39](https://ref.ly/Lev23:39) (H), הֶחָג [1 K 8:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:2), [8:65](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:65) \= [2 Ch 5:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:3), [7:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:8), [7:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:9), Ez 45, [Ne 8:14](https://ref.ly/Neh8:14); הַגֶּךָ (i.e. of people) [Dt 16:14](https://ref.ly/Deut16:14); 7 days [Nu 29:12](https://ref.ly/Num29:12) (P), [Ne 8:18](https://ref.ly/Neh8:18).—Vbs. c. חג are: חגג [Lv 23:39](https://ref.ly/Lev23:39); [23:41](https://ref.ly/Lev23:41) (H), [Ex 12:14](https://ref.ly/Exod12:14), [Nu 29:12](https://ref.ly/Num29:12) (P), [Na 2:1](https://ref.ly/Nah2:1), [Zc 14:16](https://ref.ly/Zech14:16), [14:18](https://ref.ly/Zech14:18), [14:19](https://ref.ly/Zech14:19); שׁמר [Ex 23:15](https://ref.ly/Exod23:15) \= [34:18](https://ref.ly/Exod34:18) (JE); התקדשׁ [Is 30:29](https://ref.ly/Isa30:29); but usually עשה [Ex 34:22](https://ref.ly/Exod34:22) (JE), [Dt 16:10](https://ref.ly/Deut16:10), [16:13](https://ref.ly/Deut16:13), [1 K 8:65](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:65), [12:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:32), [12:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:33), [2 Ch 7:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:8), [7:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:9), [30:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:13), [30:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:21), [35:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:17), [Ezr 3:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:4), [6:22](https://ref.ly/Ezra6:22), [Ne 8:18](https://ref.ly/Neh8:18). **2\.** *festival sacrifice* (cf. ## חָדַד (id: BDB02836)

\[Str2300] † \[חָדַד] **vb.** be sharp, keen (Aram. חֲדַד; NH חִדֵּד *sharpen;* Ar. حَدَّ *be sharp* and *sharpen;* As. Pi. *uddudu, sharpen*, DlPr 174, W 121\)—**Qal** *Pf*. וְחַדּ֫וּ מִזְּאֵבֵי עֶרֶב consec. Hb [1:8](https://ref.ly/Hab1:8) *are keener than evening wolves* (of impetuous Chaldean horses). **Hiph.** הֵחֵד (Che Or Brd) or יַחֵד (Näg cf. Dr§ 123 a, R), ins. [Is 44:12](https://ref.ly/Isa44:12) after LXX SyrVer: the smith *sharpeneth* an axe. **Hoph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. הוּחַדָּה Ez [21:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:14), [21:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:15), [21:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:16) *be sharpened* (in all, subj. חֶרֶב).—Vid. also 1\. חדה.

> \[Str2299] † II. \[חַד] **adj.** sharp—only fs. חַדָּה, of חֶרֶב Ez [5:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:1); in fig. of tongue [Psalm 57:5](https://ref.ly/Ps57:5); sim. of mouth [Is 49:2](https://ref.ly/Isa49:2); fig. of end of (i.e. final experience with) strange woman כְּחֶרֶב פִיּוֺת ´ח [Pr 5:4](https://ref.ly/Prov5:4) (‖מָרָ֯ה כַלַּעֲנָה).—1\. חַד v. sub אֶחָד.

> \[Str5876] חַדָּה v. עֵין חַדָּה

> \[Str2303] † \[חַדּוּד] **adj.** sharpened, sharp, pointed, only cstr., with superlat. sense \= subst.: תַּחְתָּיו חַדּוּדֵי חָרֶשׁ [Jb 41:22](https://ref.ly/Job41:22) *beneath him the sharpest of potsherds*, of scales of crocodile, cf. Di VB.

> \[Str2301] † חֲדַד **n.pr.m.** (on this and foll. cf. Palm. n.pr. חדודן VogNo. 96, Sab. חדד Hal27);—a son of Ishmael [Gn 25:15](https://ref.ly/Gen25:15) (cf. Baer's n.; van d. H. חֲדַר) LXX Χοδδαν, LXXL Χοδδαδ; \= [1 Ch 1:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:30) LXX Χονδαν, A Χοδδαδ, LXXL Αδαδ.

> \[Str2307] † חָדִיד **n.pr.loc.** in Benj., mentioned with לֽד and אוֺנוֺ [Ezr 2:33](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:33) \= [Ne 7:37](https://ref.ly/Neh7:37); with these and other places [Ne 11:34](https://ref.ly/Neh11:34); prob. \= Ἀδιδά 1 Macc 12:38, Ἀδίδοις 13:13; mod. *El\-Ḥadite* c. 3 1/4 miles N. of E. from Lydda, GuêrinJudêe i. 320 (cf. RobRR iii. 143\).

## I חָדָה (id: BDB02842)

\[Str2300] † I. \[חָדָה] **vb.** be or grow sharp;—**Qal** *grow sharp, Impf*. apoc. \= juss. יָ֫חַד [Pr 27:17](https://ref.ly/Prov27:17) a; **Hiph.** *sharpen* (fig.), *Impf. apoc*. יַ֫חַד [Pr 27:17](https://ref.ly/Prov27:17) b; בַּרְזֶל בְּבֶרְזֶל ָ\+יחַד וְאִישׁ יַחְד פְּנֵי־רֵעֵהוּ *let iron by means of iron grow sharp, and let a man sharpen the countenance* (presence, bearing) *of his friend;*—so KiMichl. 126 a De Now KöLgb i, 373 f., cf. Str. (who however makes both forms Hiph.)

* **Associated Passages:** Proverbs 27:17


## II חָדָה (id: BDB02843)

\[Str2302] † II. \[חָדָה] **vb.** rejoice (As. *ḫadû* DlPr 153; Aram. ܚܕܳܐ, חֲרָא)—**Qal** *Impf*. 3 ms. וַיִּ֫חַדְּ [Ex 18:9](https://ref.ly/Exod18:9) (E), and Jethro *rejoiced* over (עַל rei); juss. אַלאיִ֫חַדְּ [Jb 3:6](https://ref.ly/Job3:6) *let it not rejoice* among (בְּ) the days of the year, i.e. not take its place joyfully among them (fig. of day of Job's birth).—K1o prop. בְּ…וְלֽא חָדוּ [1 S 6:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:19) for בְּ…וַיַּח cf. also Dr **Pi.** *Impf*. sf. (causat.) תְּחַדּהוּ בְשִמְחְה [Psalm 21:7](https://ref.ly/Ps21:7) *thou makest him joyful* (Che *cheerest him*) *with gladness* (‖תְּשִׁיתֵהוּ בְרָכוֺת).

> \[Str2304] † חֶדְוָה **n.f.** (late) joy (Aram. חדוא).—´ח [1 Ch 16:27](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:27) (in the sanctuary); ´חֶדְוַת י [Ne 8:10](https://ref.ly/Neh8:10) (‖אַל־תֵּעָצֵָבוּ).

> \[Str3164] † יַחְדִּיאֵל **n.pr.m.** (*God giveth joy*) a chief man in Manasseh [1 Ch 5:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:24), LXX Ιελιηλ, A LXXL Ιεδιηλ.

> \[Str3165] † יֶחְדְּיָ֫הוּ **n.pr.m.** (*May* ´י *give joy!*) **1\.** a Levite [1 Ch 24:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:20), LXX Ιεδεια, LXXL Ιαδαια. **2\.** officer of David [1 Ch 27:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:30) LXX Ιαδ(α)ιας.

## חָדַל (id: BDB02847)

\[Str2308] † חָדַל **vb.** cease (Sab. חדל *be negligent*, Sab. Denkm.:76\)—**Qal** *Pf*. חָרַל [Gn 18:11](https://ref.ly/Gen18:11) \+ 4 t.; וְחָדַל consec. [Nu 9:13](https://ref.ly/Num9:13), [Psalm 49:9](https://ref.ly/Ps49:9); 2 ms. וְחָדַלְתָּ֫ [Ex 23:5](https://ref.ly/Exod23:5); pl. הָֽדְּלוּ Ju [5:6](https://ref.ly/Judg5:6) \+ 4 t.; חָדָלּוּ (cf. Köi, 243\) Ju [5:7](https://ref.ly/Judg5:7), [1 S 2:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:5); 1 pl. חָדַלְנוּ [Je 44:18](https://ref.ly/Jer44:18); *Impf.* יֶחְדַּל [Dt 15:11](https://ref.ly/Deut15:11) \+ ([Jb 10:20](https://ref.ly/Job10:20) Kt יחדל jussive \> Qr וַחֲדָל *Imv.*); אֶחְדָּל Ju [15:7](https://ref.ly/Judg15:7) \+ 3 t.; וְאַחְדְּלָה [Jb 16:6](https://ref.ly/Job16:6); pl. יֶחְדָּלוּ Ez [2:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek2:5) \+ 2 t.; יֶחְדָּלוּ֔ן [Ex 9:29](https://ref.ly/Exod9:29); וַיַּחְדְּלוּ [Gn 11:8](https://ref.ly/Gen11:8), [Ex 9:33](https://ref.ly/Exod9:33), נֶחְדָּל [1 K 22:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:15), etc.; *Inf. cstr.* חֲדֹל [1 S 12:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:23); *Imv.* חֲדַל [Ex 14:12](https://ref.ly/Exod14:12) \+, etc.; חִדְלוּ [Is 1:16](https://ref.ly/Isa1:16), [2:22](https://ref.ly/Isa2:22); חֲדָלוּ [Zc 11:22](https://ref.ly/Zech11:22); —**1\.** *cease, come to an end* [Ex 9:29](https://ref.ly/Exod9:29), [9:33](https://ref.ly/Exod9:33), [9:34](https://ref.ly/Exod9:34) (J), [Is 24:8](https://ref.ly/Isa24:8); impers. חָדַל לִהְיוֺת לְשָרָה [Gn 18:11](https://ref.ly/Gen18:11) (J); *cease to be* [Dt 15:11](https://ref.ly/Deut15:11) (sq. מִן), and poet. Ju [5:6](https://ref.ly/Judg5:6), [5:7](https://ref.ly/Judg5:7), [5:7](https://ref.ly/Judg5:7), [1 S 2:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:5), [Jb 14:7](https://ref.ly/Job14:7); \= *be lacking, fail* [Jb 19:14](https://ref.ly/Job19:14), [Pr 10:19](https://ref.ly/Prov10:19). **2\.** *cease, leave off,* sq. inf. c. לְ [Gn 11:8](https://ref.ly/Gen11:8) (J), [41:49](https://ref.ly/Gen41:49) (E), [Nu 9:13](https://ref.ly/Num9:13) (P), [1 S 12:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:23), [23:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:13), [Je 44:18](https://ref.ly/Jer44:18), [51:30](https://ref.ly/Jer51:30), [Dt 23:23](https://ref.ly/Deut23:23), [Psalm 36:4](https://ref.ly/Ps36:4), [Pr 19:27](https://ref.ly/Prov19:27), [Ru 1:18](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:18); inf. alone [Is 1:16](https://ref.ly/Isa1:16); so also [Ho 8:10](https://ref.ly/Hos8:10), where read וְחָֽדְלוּ for וַיָּחֵלּוּ LXX Che We; sq. inf. c. מִן [Ex 23:5](https://ref.ly/Exod23:5) (JE), [1 K 15:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:21) \= [2 Ch 16:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:5); sq. subst. c. מִן (*cease regarding*) [Is 2:22](https://ref.ly/Isa2:22) (but Che Du al. treat v. as interpol.; om. LXX), [1 S 9:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:5), [Pr 23:4](https://ref.ly/Prov23:4); sq. מֵאלהים [2 Ch 35:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:21), i.e. leave off provoking God; [Ex 14:12](https://ref.ly/Exod14:12) (J), sq. מִמֶּנּו *desist from us,* let us alone; [Jb 7:16](https://ref.ly/Job7:16); sq. acc. [Jb 3:17](https://ref.ly/Job3:17) רֹגֶז ´ח; [Jb 14:6](https://ref.ly/Job14:6) *have rest* (i.e. cease from being troubled, see Di; cf. v:[1](https://ref.ly/Job14:1)); no complem. expressed, *cease, forbear* Ju [15:7](https://ref.ly/Judg15:7), [20:28](https://ref.ly/Judg20:28), [Am 7:5](https://ref.ly/Amos7:5), [1 K 22:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:6), [22:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:15) \= [2 Ch 18:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:5), [18:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:14), [Je 40:4](https://ref.ly/Jer40:4), [41:8](https://ref.ly/Jer41:8), [Zc 11:12](https://ref.ly/Zech11:12), [Psalm 49:9](https://ref.ly/Ps49:9), [Jb 16:6](https://ref.ly/Job16:6), [2 Ch 25:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:16); specif. of refusal to hear Ez [2:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek2:5), [2:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek2:7), [3:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:11), [3:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:27) (all opp. שָׁמַע). **Hoph.** *Pf.* (contracted) c. interrog. part. הֶחֳדַלְתִּי Ju [9:9](https://ref.ly/Judg9:9), [9:11](https://ref.ly/Judg9:11), [9:13](https://ref.ly/Judg9:13) *should I be made to leave,* sq. acc. (cf. Köi, 240 ff.); but perh. rd. הֶחָדַלְתִּי, **Qal** *Pf.* Stu Be, cf., for acc. sq., [Jb 3:17](https://ref.ly/Job3:17).

> \[Str2310] † חָדֵל **adj.** forbearing, lacking, הֶחָדֵל as subst. Ez [3:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:27) *he that forbeareth* (opp. שׁמע, cf. חָדֵל **Qal** ad fin.); cstr. חֲדַל אִישִׁים [Is 53:3](https://ref.ly/Isa53:3) *lacking men* (i.e. forsaken by them);—*ceasing, transient* מֶה־חָדֵל אָנִי [Psalm 39:5](https://ref.ly/Ps39:5), but rd. rather חֶלֶד v. Checrit. n.

> \[Str2311] † חַדְלָי **n.pr.m.** an Ephraimite, [2 Ch 28:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:12), LXX Χοαδ, A Αδδι, LXXL Αδλι.

## חדק (id: BDB02851)

חדק (√ of foll.; NH צִדֵּק *press* or *thrust in,* Aram. צַדֵּק *id.* (denom. ?)).

> \[Str2312] † חֵָדֶק **n.\[m.]** brier (NH *id.;* Aram. חִדְקָא Löwp. 147, No. 104; cf. Ar. حَدَقٌ a kind of night\-shade, v. Lane532);—טוֺבָם כַּחֵדֶק [Mi 7:4](https://ref.ly/Mic7:4) *the best of them* (si vera l.) *is like the brier* (‖יָשָׁר מִמְּסוּכָה); דֶּרֶח עָצֵל כִּמְשֻכַת חָדֶק [Pr 15:19](https://ref.ly/Prov15:19) *the way of a sluggard is like a brier\-hedge.*

> \[Str2313] † חִדֶּ֫קֶל **n.pr.fl. Tigris** (As. *Idiḳlat, Diḳlat,* Old Pers. *Tigrâ* SpiegAPK, Gloss., etc.), the famous Assyr. river; v. esp. DlPa 110 f. 170 ff.; ´ח [Gn 2:14](https://ref.ly/Gen2:14); חִדָּקֶל [Dn 10:4](https://ref.ly/Dan10:4).

* **Associated Passages:** Daniel 10:4; Micah 7:4; Proverbs 15:19; Genesis 2:14


## חָדַר (id: BDB02854)

\[Str2314] † \[חָדַר] **vb.** surround, enclose (Ph. in deriv.; Ar. خَدَرَ II. *conceal,* خَدِرَ *curtain* concealing a person, *chamber, house, or tent* as concealing some one; Eth. ኀደረ፡ *dwell;* v. also foll.)—only **Qal** **Pt.** fs. הַחֹדֶ֫רֶת לָהֶם Ez [21:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:19) *that which surroundeth them* (of the sword),—so SyrVer Thes Sm Co Da VB.

293:b. \[חָדַר]: Ph. is only חדר, חדרת (temple or sepulchral) *chamber* (Lzb271 Cooke126); Ez [21:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:19) is best explained from Syr. ܚܕܰܪ *go about, surround*, ܚܕܳܪ *around.* It seems uncertain whether חֶדֶר, Ph. חדר *chamber*, Ar. خِدْرٌ *curtain, chamber, tent*, etc., are (ultimately) fr. this √ (as something *surrounding*); or (Buhl) from II. חדר \= خَدِرَ II. *conceal behind curtain, conceal, confine*, IV. *conceal oneself*, also *abide, stay, remain behind* (Land707 f.), Eth. ኀደረ፡ *abide, dwell* (Dr, privately, Nov. 1905\).

> \[Str2315–16] † חֶ֫דֶר **n.m.**:[1 Ch 28:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:11) chamber, room (NH *id.;* Ph. חדר, חדרת; Sab. חַדר MordtZMG, 1876, 24; Eth. ምኣኅደር፡)—abs. חֶדֶר [2 S 13:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:10) \+ 11 t.; הַחַ֫דְרָה [Gn 43:20](https://ref.ly/Gen43:20), [1 K 1:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:15); הֶחָדְרָה Ju [15:1](https://ref.ly/Judg15:1), [2 S 13:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:10); cstr. חֶ֫דֶר Ct [3:4](https://ref.ly/Song3:4); חֲדֶר [Ex 7:28](https://ref.ly/Exod7:28) \+ 5 t.; sf. חֶדְרוֺ [Jo 2:16](https://ref.ly/Joel2:16); pl. חֲדָרִים [Dt 32:25](https://ref.ly/Deut32:25), [Pr 24:4](https://ref.ly/Prov24:4); cstr. חַדְרֵי [Pr 7:27](https://ref.ly/Prov7:27) \+ 8 t.; sf. חֲדָרֶיךָ [Is 26:20](https://ref.ly/Isa26:20); חֲדָרָיו Ct [1:4](https://ref.ly/Song1:4), [1 Ch 28:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:11); —*room, chamber,* usually private, as bedroom [Gn 43:30](https://ref.ly/Gen43:30) (J), Ju [15:1](https://ref.ly/Judg15:1), [16:9](https://ref.ly/Judg16:9), [16:12](https://ref.ly/Judg16:12), [2 S 13:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:10), [13:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:10), [1 K 1:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:15), Ct [1:4](https://ref.ly/Song1:4), [Is 26:20](https://ref.ly/Isa26:20); Ju [14:18](https://ref.ly/Judg14:18) rd. prob. הַחַדְרָה *bridal\-chamber* for הַחַרְסָה StaZAW 1884, 253, cf. [Jo 2:16](https://ref.ly/Joel2:16) (‖חֻפָּתָהּ of bride), הוֺרָתִי ´ח Ct [3:4](https://ref.ly/Song3:4) (‖בֵּית אִמִּי); of kings (of Egypt) [Psalm 105:30](https://ref.ly/Ps105:30); specif.: מִשְׁכָּב ´ח *bed\-chamber* [Ex 7:28](https://ref.ly/Exod7:28) (J), [2 S 4:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:7), [2 K 6:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:12), [Ec 10:20](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:20); other combinations:—הַמְּקֵרָה ´ח Ju [3:24](https://ref.ly/Judg3:24) \= *cool chamber;* הַפְּנִימִים ´ח [1 Ch 28:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:11) *inner chamber;* חדרי מַשְכִּיתוֺ Ez [8:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:12) *his chambers of imagery* (del. Hi Co Sgfr.); ´בְּח ´ח *a chamber within a chamber,* i.e. an innermost chamber [1 K 20:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:30), [22:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:25) \= [2 Ch 18:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:24), [2 K 9:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:2); pl. \= *store\-rooms* [Pr 24:4](https://ref.ly/Prov24:4); so הַמִּטּוֺת ´ח [2 K 11:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:2) \= [2 Ch 22:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:11), i.e. room where beds were stored (Ke Th); metaph. ´ח מָוֶת [Pr 7:27](https://ref.ly/Prov7:27) (‖דַּרְכֵי שְׁאוֺל); חדרי תֵּמָן [Jb 9:9](https://ref.ly/Job9:9) *chambers of south* (where constellations are treasured up, v. Di; Hoffm. thinks of n.pr. of star or constell.); cf. [Jb 37:9](https://ref.ly/Job37:9) a *chamber* whence comes storm\-wind (סוּפָה); חַדְרֵי בֶטֶן i.e. inner parts of body, only fig. [Pr 18:8](https://ref.ly/Prov18:8) \= [26:22](https://ref.ly/Prov26:22), [20:27](https://ref.ly/Prov20:27), [20:30](https://ref.ly/Prov20:30); in [Dt 32:25](https://ref.ly/Deut32:25) מֵחֲדָרִים \= *within,* poet. for מִבַּיִת, ‖מִחוּץ *without.*
> 
> 293:b. חֶדֶר, l. 3: ኀደረ፡ምኣኅደር፡ is not ‘chamber,’ but (fr. √ *dwell*) *dwelling\-place, abode, tent*.

## חָדַשׁ (id: BDB02857)

\[Str2318] † \[חָדַשׁ] **vb.** only **Pi.** (and **Hithp.**) renew, repair (poet. and late) (NH *id.,* Ph. חדש; n. חדש *new moon,* also in קרתחדשת *new\-city* \= *Carthage;* Aram. حدت and חַדֵּשׁ; Ar. حدَثَ *be new,* Eth. ሐደሰ፡ 1\. 2\. *renew;* Sab. החדתַ, v. Os (Levy):ZMG 1865, 204 MordtZMG 1876, 30; As. *\[adâšu], uddiš, renew, eššu, new,* etc. DlW 199 ff.)—**Pi.** *Pf.* וְחִדְּשׁוּ consec. [Is 61:4](https://ref.ly/Isa61:4); *Impf.* וַיְחַדֵּשׁ [2 Ch 15:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:8); 2 ms. תְּחַדֵּשׁ [Jb 10:17](https://ref.ly/Job10:17), [Psalm 104:30](https://ref.ly/Ps104:30); וּנְחַדֵּשׁ [1 S 11:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:14); *Imv.* חַדֵּשׁ [Psalm 51:21](https://ref.ly/Ps51:21); *Inf.* לְחַדֵּשׁ [2 Ch 24:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:4), [24:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:12);—**1\.** *renew, make anew* הַמְּלוּכָה [1 S 11:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:14) (editorial); פני אדמה [Psalm 104:30](https://ref.ly/Ps104:30), רוּחַ [51:12](https://ref.ly/Ps51:12) (‖בּרא); \= bring back יָמֵינוּ כְּקֶדֶם [La 5:21](https://ref.ly/Lam5:21); עדיך ´ח [Jb 10:17](https://ref.ly/Job10:17) i.e. *bringest fresh (new) witnesses.* **2\.** *repair,* cities [Is 61:4](https://ref.ly/Isa61:4), temple [2 Ch 24:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:4), [24:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:12), altar [2 Ch 15:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:8). **Hithp.** *Impf.* 3 fs. תִּתְחַדֵּשׁ c. pl. subj. כַּנֶּשֶׁר נְעוּרָיְכִת ´ת [Psalm 103:5](https://ref.ly/Ps103:5) *thy youth reneweth itself, like the eagle.*

## חוּב (id: BDB02863)

\[Str2325] † \[חוּב] **vb.** be guilty (NH *id., be under obligation, guilty;* Aram. ܚܳܒ, חוּב, *be defeated, guilty;* Ar. خَابَ *be disappointed, fail;*—Ar. حُوبٌ, حَوْبٌ *sin,* is loan\-wd. acc. to Bev [Dn 1:10](https://ref.ly/Dan1:10));—**Qal** not in MT; but rd. perh. חַבְתִּי (for סַבֹּתִי) [1 S 22:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:22) *I am guilty,* so SyrVer Th We Dr. **Pi.** *Pf.* *inculpate* וְחִיַּבְתֶּם את־ראֹשִׁי לַמֶָּלֶח consesc. [Dn 1:10](https://ref.ly/Dan1:10) *and inculpate my head with the king*.

> \[Str2326] † חוֺב **n.\[m.]** debt (NH *id.;* Aram. ܚܰܘܒܳܐ, חוֺבָא);—יָשִׁיב ´חֲבֹלָתוֺ ח Ez [18:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:7) *his pledge as to indebtedness he restores,* v. Hi\-Sm Ew§ 291 b Ges§ 132 R 5, b KöLgb. i. 497 f. and cf. Dr§ 193 (but constr. hard: Co שׁוֺב; dittogr. fr. ´חבל Bev:[Dn 1:10](https://ref.ly/Dan1:10), cf. v:[12](https://ref.ly/Dan1:12), v: [16](https://ref.ly/Dan1:16)).

> \[Str2327] † חוֺבָה **n.pr.loc.** [Gn 14:15](https://ref.ly/Gen14:15), north of Damascus; mod. *Ḫoba,* 20 hours N. of Dam., acc. to WetzstDel Gn. ed. 4, 561 ff. so Di.

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 18:7; Daniel 1:12; Daniel 1:16; 1 Samuel 22:22; Daniel 1:10; Genesis 14:15


## חוּג (id: BDB02866)

\[Str2328] † \[חוּג] **vb.** draw round, make a circle (Aram. ܚܳܓ *circumivit;* n. حْوغتْء, חוּגְתָּא, *circle, vault of heavens*)—**Qal** *Pf.* חֹק חָג על־פני מים [Jb 26:10](https://ref.ly/Job26:10) (cf. [Pr 8:27](https://ref.ly/Prov8:27)), *hath drawn as a circle* a bound, etc., of the horizon\-line.

> \[Str2329] † חוּג **n.\[m.]** vault;—only of vault of the heavens חוּג שׁמים התהלך [Jb 22:14](https://ref.ly/Job22:14); בְּחֻקַוֺ חוּג ע־ל תהום ´פנ [Pr 8:27](https://ref.ly/Prov8:27); הישֵׁב עַל־חוּג הארץ [Is 40:22](https://ref.ly/Isa40:22).

> \[Str4230] † מְחוּגָה **n.f.** circle\-instrument, compass, used by carvers of idols וּבַמְּחוּגָה יְתָֽאֳרֵָהוּ [Is 44:13](https://ref.ly/Isa44:13).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 44:13; Isaiah 40:22; Job 26:10; Proverbs 8:27; Job 22:14


## I חוד (id: BDB02869)

I. חוד (√ of foll.; Ar. حَادَ is *decline, turn aside, avoid,* hence perh. *riddle,* as *indirect, obscure*).

> \[Str2420] † חִידָה **n.f.** riddle, enigmatic, perplexing saying or question;—abs. ´ח Ju [14:12](https://ref.ly/Judg14:12) \+ 6 t.; sf. חִידָתִי Ju [14:18](https://ref.ly/Judg14:18), [Psalm 49:5](https://ref.ly/Ps49:5); חִידָֽתְךָ Ju [14:13](https://ref.ly/Judg14:13); pl. abs. חִידוֺת [1 K 10:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:1) \+ 4 t.; חִידֹת [Nu 12:8](https://ref.ly/Num12:8); sf. חִידֹתָם [Pr 1:6](https://ref.ly/Prov1:6); —**1\.** *riddle* \= dark, obscure utterance [Nu 12:8](https://ref.ly/Num12:8) (JE; opp. פֶּה־אֶל־פֶּה and מַרְאֶה); of something put indirectly and needing interpretation;—an allegory ´חוד ח Ez [17:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:2) (‖מְשֹׁל מָשָׁל); allegor. and fig. sayings [Pr 1:6](https://ref.ly/Prov1:6) (‖מְלִיצָה מָשָׁל and דִּבְרֵי חֲכָמִים); enigmat. sentences and comparisons, declaring fate of Chaldeans Hb [2:6](https://ref.ly/Hab2:6) (\+ מְלִיצָה; ‖מָשָׁל), perplexing moral problem [Psalm 49:5](https://ref.ly/Ps49:5) (c. פָּתַח *open, propound;* ‖מָשָׁל), a lesson taught indirectly [Psalm 78:2](https://ref.ly/Ps78:2) (מִנִּי קֶדֶם ´ח, c. הִבִּיעַ; ‖מָשָׁל). **2\.** *riddle, enigma,* to be guessed, in Ju 14: **a.** with vb. חוּד Ju [14:12](https://ref.ly/Judg14:12), [14:13](https://ref.ly/Judg14:13), [14:16](https://ref.ly/Judg14:16) *propound a riddle*. **b.** with vb. הִגִּיד *tell* (give answer to) *a riddle* Ju [14:14](https://ref.ly/Judg14:14), [14:15](https://ref.ly/Judg14:15), [14:17](https://ref.ly/Judg14:17), [14:19](https://ref.ly/Judg14:19). **c.** with vb. מצא *find out a riddle* Ju [14:18](https://ref.ly/Judg14:18). **3\.** *perplexing questions* by which queen of Sheba put Sol. to the test (נִסָּה) [1 K 10:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:1) \= [2 Ch 9:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:1). **4\.** with הֵבִין skilled in *double\-dealing* [Dn 8:23](https://ref.ly/Dan8:23) (Bev).

> \[Str2330] † II. \[חוּד] **vb. denom.** propound a riddle;—**Qal** *Pf.* 2 ms. חַדְתָּה Ju [14:16](https://ref.ly/Judg14:16); *Impf.* אָח֫וּדָה Ju [14:12](https://ref.ly/Judg14:12); *Imv.* (חִידָה וּמְשֹׁל מָשָׁל) חוּד Ez [17:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:2); חוּ֫דָה Ju [14:13](https://ref.ly/Judg14:13); always c. חידה acc. cogn.

* **Associated Passages:** Judges 14:19; Habakkuk 2:6; 2 Chronicles 9:1; Ezekiel 17:2; Judges 14:18; 1 Kings 10:1; Judges 14:13; Numbers 12:8; Psalms 49:5; Judges 14:14; Judges 14:12; Proverbs 1:6; Daniel 8:23; Judges 14:15; Judges 14:17; Judges 14:16; Psalms 78:2

## II חוה (id: BDB02875)

II. חוה (√ of foll.; Ar. حَوَى *collect, gather;* حِوَاءٌ *circle of tents* cf. II. חַי sub חיה, Ar. حَيَّ).

> \[Str2333,2334] † II. \[חַוָּה] **n.f.** tent\-village—Pl. חַוֹּתֵיהֶם [Nu 32:41](https://ref.ly/Num32:41) (JE), cf. Di. Elsewh. in cpd. appellative, almost \= n.pr. חַוֹּת יָאִיר [Dt 3:14](https://ref.ly/Deut3:14), [Jos 13:30](https://ref.ly/Josh13:30) (D), Ju [10:4](https://ref.ly/Judg10:4), [1 K 4:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:13), [1 Ch 2:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:23).—I. חַוָּה n.pr.f. v. supr.

> \[Str2340] † חִוִּי **adj. gent.** ( \= *villagers* ?)—**1\.** שְׁכֶם ´בֶּן־חֲמוֺר הַח [Gn 34:2](https://ref.ly/Gen34:2) (P), cf. ´צִבְעוֺן הַח [36:2](https://ref.ly/Gen36:2) (but rd. rather הַחֹרִי Ol Di cf. LXX [Jos 9:7](https://ref.ly/Josh9:7)). **2\.** c. art. as n.pr.coll. הַחִוִּי *the Hivvites* ‘begotten’ by Canaan [Gn 10:17](https://ref.ly/Gen10:17) \= [1 Ch 1:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:15); esp. in list of Canaanit. peoples displaced by Hebrews [Ex 3:8](https://ref.ly/Exod3:8), [3:17](https://ref.ly/Exod3:17), [13:5](https://ref.ly/Exod13:5), [23:23](https://ref.ly/Exod23:23), [23:28](https://ref.ly/Exod23:28), [33:2](https://ref.ly/Exod33:2), [34:11](https://ref.ly/Exod34:11), [Dt 7:1](https://ref.ly/Deut7:1), [20:17](https://ref.ly/Deut20:17), [Jos 3:10](https://ref.ly/Josh3:10), [9:1](https://ref.ly/Josh9:1), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Josh12:8), [24:11](https://ref.ly/Josh24:11) (all JED), Ju [3:5](https://ref.ly/Judg3:5), [1 K 9:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:20) \= [2 Ch 8:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:7); cf. וְהַכְּנַעֲנִי ´עָרֵי הַח [2 S 24:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:7) (note מִבְצַר־חֹר, צִידוֺן just preceding); applied specif. to Gibeonites [Jos 9:7](https://ref.ly/Josh9:7) (JE), [11:19](https://ref.ly/Josh11:19) (D).—In [Jos 11:3](https://ref.ly/Josh11:3), Ju [3:3](https://ref.ly/Judg3:3) rd. prob. הַחִתִּי for הַחִוִּי We Mey BuUrg 350 v. (partly) LXX, Dr on [Dt 7:1](https://ref.ly/Deut7:1).

> \[Str4233] † מַחֲוִים appar. **adj. gent.,** only ´אֱלִיאֵל הַמּ [1 Ch 11:46](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:46), but rd. prob. הַמַּחֲנִי (Be), v. מַחֲנַיִם n.pr.loc. sub; חנה; Öt suggests also הַמְּעוֺנִי; LXX ὁ Μιει, A ὁ Μαωειν, LXXL ὁ Μαωθε.

## III חָוָה (id: BDB02879)

\[Str2331] † III. \[חָוָה] **vb.** only **Pi.** (poet. \& late), tell, declare (Aram. ܚܰܘܺܝ, חַוֵּי)—**Pi.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יְחַוֶּ־ה [Psalm 19:3](https://ref.ly/Ps19:3); 1 s. אֲחַוֶּה [Jb 32:10](https://ref.ly/Job32:10), [32:17](https://ref.ly/Job32:17); sf. אֲחַוְךָ [Jb 15:17](https://ref.ly/Job15:17), אֲחַוֶּךָּ [Jb 36:2](https://ref.ly/Job36:2); *Inf. cstr.* חַוֹּת;—*declare, make known,* c. acc. דָּעַת [Psalm 19:3](https://ref.ly/Ps19:3) (subj. לַיְלָה, sq. לְלַיְלָה; ‖יַבִּיעַ אֹמֶר); c. acc. דֵּעִי [Jb 32:10](https://ref.ly/Job32:10), [32:17](https://ref.ly/Job32:17); c. 2 acc. וָאִירָא מֵחַוֹּת דֵּעִי אֶתְכֶם [32:6](https://ref.ly/Job32:6), sq. sf. Pers. \+ obj. cl., :וַאֲחֶַ\+וּדָּ כִּי־עוֺד לֶאֱלוֺהַּ מִלִּֽים [36:2](https://ref.ly/Job36:2) *and I will shew thee that* (there are) *yet words for God;* sq. sf. Pers. only [Jb 15:17](https://ref.ly/Job15:17); —rd. prob. also וַאֲחַוֶּה for וַאֲקַוֶּה [Psalm 52:11](https://ref.ly/Ps52:11) Hi Che al.

> \[Str262] † \[אַחְוָה] **n.f.** declaration (in form, an Aram. Aphel Inf.)—only sf. :וְאַחְוָתִי כְּאָזְנֵיכֶֽם [Jb 13:17](https://ref.ly/Job13:17) (in ‖ line שִׁמְעוּ שָׁמוֺעַ מִלָּתָי).

> \[Str2335] חוָֺ\+זי [2 Ch 33:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:19) v. sub חזה. p. 302

## חוח (id: BDB02882)

חוח (√ of foll., meaning dubious).

> \[Str2336–37] † חוֺחַ **n.m.**:[2 K 14:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:9) **1\.** brier, bramble. **2\.** hook, ring, fetter;—´ח abs. [Ho 9:6](https://ref.ly/Hos9:6) \+ 8 t; pl. חֲוָחִים [1 S 13:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:6) (but v. infr.), חוֺחִים Ct [2:2](https://ref.ly/Song2:2); חֹחִים [2 Ch 33:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:11); —**1\. a.** *brier, bramble* [2 K 14:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:9), [14:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:9) \= [2 Ch 25:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:18), [25:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:18) (allegory of Jehoash); coll., sign of desolation [Ho 9:6](https://ref.ly/Hos9:6) (‖קִמּוֺשׁ), [Is 34:13](https://ref.ly/Isa34:13) (‖קִמּוֺשׁ, סִירִים), [Jb 31:40](https://ref.ly/Job31:40) (opp. חִטִּים); in sim. of fool's parable עָלָה בְּיַד־שִׁכּוֺר ´ח [Pr 26:9](https://ref.ly/Prov26:9) *a brier cometh into the hand of a drunken man* (De Now Str); כְּשׁוֺשַׁנָּה בֵּין הַחוֺחִים; Ct [2:2](https://ref.ly/Song2:2); *briers* \= *thickets* as hiding\-places [1 S 13:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:6) (but Ew We Dr חוֺרִים *holes,* cf. [14:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:11); v. sub III. חרר; ‖מְעָרוֺת, סְלָעִים, צְרִחִים, בֹּרוֺת; LXX here ἐν τ. μάνδραις̀ [14:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:11) ἐκ τ. τρωγλῶν. **2\.** late, **a.** *hook* or *ring,* in jaw of crocodile c. תִּקֹּב [Jb 40:26](https://ref.ly/Job40:26) (‖הֲתָשִים אַגְמֹן בְּאַפּוֺ); of captive וַיִּלְכְּדוּ אֶת־מְנַשֶּׁה בַּחֹחִים [2 Ch 33:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:11) (cf. חַח).

> \[Str2397] † חַח **n.m.** hook, ring, fetter;—חַח abs. [Ex 35:22](https://ref.ly/Exod35:22); sf. חַחִי [Is 37:29](https://ref.ly/Isa37:29), [2 K 19:28](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:28); pl. חַחִים Ez [19:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:4) \+ 2 t. \+ Ez [29:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:4) Qr (Kt erron. חחיים, v. infr.);—**1\.** *hook, ring,* in nose of captive וְשַמְתִּי חַחִי בְּאַפֶָּךָ [Is 37:29](https://ref.ly/Isa37:29) \= [2 K 19:28](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:28) (cf. בַּחֹחִים [2 Ch 33:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:11), v. חוֺחַ supr.); so of princes of Judah, under fig. of young lions Ez [19:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:4), [19:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:9) (in v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:9) transp:, so that בחחים foll. הביא as v:[4](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:4); so Co); in jaws of crocodile (metaph. of Pharaoh), c. נתן Ez [29:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:4), cf. [38:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:4) (but LXX om. and sense opposes, cf. Co and—dub.—Da). **2\.** *hook, ring* as ornament, perhaps *nose\-ring* (others *brooch,* see VB), [Ex 35:22](https://ref.ly/Exod35:22) (P; ‖נֶזֶם, טַבַּעַת, כּוּמָז).

## חוט (id: BDB02885)

חוט (of foll.; Ar. خَاط, Aram. ܚܳܛ, חַיֵּט *sew*).

> \[Str2339] † חוּט **n.m.**:[Jos 2:18](https://ref.ly/Josh2:18) thread, cord, line (NH *id.;* Ar. خَيْطٌ, Aram. ܚܽܘܛܳܐ, חוּטָא)—´ח abs. [Gn 14:23](https://ref.ly/Gen14:23) \+ 2 t.; cstr. [Jos 2:18](https://ref.ly/Josh2:18)\+ 3 t.;—**1\.** *thread,* as easily broken Ju [16:12](https://ref.ly/Judg16:12) (sim.); as composing a rope or cord; הַשָּׁנִי הַזֶּה ´תִּקְוַת ח [Jos 2:18](https://ref.ly/Josh2:18) (JE) *this cord of scarlet thread* (lit. the cord of this thread of scarlet); prob. also מִחוּט וְעַד שְרוֺךְ נַעַל [Gn 14:23](https://ref.ly/Gen14:23) *from a thread to a sandal\-thong;* in sim. ´כְּח הַשָּׁנִי Ct [4:3](https://ref.ly/Song4:3) *like a thread of scarlet* are thy lips. **2\.** cord, הַמְּשֻׁלָּשׁ ´הַח [Ec 4:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:12) *a three\-fold cord.* **3\.** *line,* as measure of length [1 K 7:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:15) *and a line of twelve cubits surrounded* (i.e. would surround) *it* (cf. קָו v:[23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:23)), so [Je 52:21](https://ref.ly/Jer52:21).

> \[Str2340] חִוִּי v. sub II. חוה. p. 295

> \[Str2341] † חֲוִילָה **n.pr.terr.** (√ dub. DlPa 12 suggests חוֺל *sand\-land, downs,* as Heb. popular etym., MV11 give it as actual etym.; StaThLZ Apr. 28, 1894, 235 compares this, as well as חוֺל *sand,* with Ar. وَحَلٌ *soft mud,* \[*dampsand*])—אֶרֶץ הַחֲוִילָה [Gn 2:11](https://ref.ly/Gen2:11) (surrounded or bordered—סבב—by river Pishon; noted for excellent gold, bdellium and *shoham\-stone*); elsewhere without art. חֲוִילָה [10:7](https://ref.ly/Gen10:7) \= [1 Ch 1:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:9) as a ‘son’ of Cush (between סְבָא and סַבְתָּה); but also as a ‘son’ of יָקְטָן, a descendant of Shem [Gn 10:29](https://ref.ly/Gen10:29) \= [1 Ch 1:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:23) (between אוֺפִיר and יוֺבָב); עַד־שׁוּר ´מֵח [Gn 25:18](https://ref.ly/Gen25:18) said of limits of Ishmaelitish territory; cf. same limits of Amalek [1 S 15:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:7) (but dub.; We rds. טֵילָם, cf. v:[4](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:4) and vid. Dr; GlaserSkizze ii, 326 rds. חֲכִילָה as [23:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:19), [26:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:1), [26:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:3)).—Most have supposed several regions named ´ח to be indicated in these passages:—e.g. Thes
> 
>  1\. Arabian shore of Pers. Gulf [Gn 10:29](https://ref.ly/Gen10:29) etc. 2\. Ethiopian coast [Gn 10:7](https://ref.ly/Gen10:7) etc. 3\. India [Gn 2:11](https://ref.ly/Gen2:11) (regarded as indef. extension of 1\). Di:[Gn 10:7](https://ref.ly/Gen10:7) distinguishes 1 and 2 either as quite distinct, or as different settlements of one great people, and (on [Gn 2:14](https://ref.ly/Gen2:14)) thinks that ´כל ארץ ח [Gn 2:11](https://ref.ly/Gen2:11) implies vague extension eastward. DlPa 12 ff. 57 ff. identif. ´ח in all passages with NE. part of Syrian desert, so E. MeyerGesch. Alterth. i. 224; GlaserSkizze ii. 323 ff. with central and NE. Arabia. The question is still undecided.

* **Associated Passages:** Genesis 14:23; 1 Chronicles 1:9; 1 Kings 7:15; Joshua 2:18; 1 Samuel 23:19; Genesis 2:11; Genesis 25:18; 1 Samuel 26:1; Genesis 2:14; Song of Songs 4:3; Judges 16:12; Genesis 10:29; Genesis 10:7; 1 Samuel 15:4; 1 Chronicles 1:23; 1 Kings 7:23; 1 Samuel 15:7; Jeremiah 52:21; Ecclesiastes 4:12; 1 Samuel 26:3


## I חוּל (id: BDB02889)

DHMZK i. 357\); on form of Heb. **vb.** v. NöZMG. 1883, 536\)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. חָ֫לָה [Mi 1:12](https://ref.ly/Mic1:12) \+ 2 t.; וְחָלָה֫ consec. II. חול); וַיָּ֫חֶל [1 S 31:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:3) \= [1 Ch 10:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:3) \+ [Gn 8:10](https://ref.ly/Gen8:10) (where rd. prob. וַיִּיָּ֫חֶל as v:[12](https://ref.ly/Gen8:12); \= וַיְיַחֵל Ol Di); 3 fs. תָּחִיל [Is 26:17](https://ref.ly/Isa26:17) \+ 2 t. \+ Ez [30:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:16) Kt (cf. *supr.*); וַתָּחֶל [Psalm 97:4](https://ref.ly/Ps97:4); 2 fs. תְּחִלִין [Is 45:10](https://ref.ly/Isa45:10); וָחִילוּ [Psalm 77:17](https://ref.ly/Ps77:17)\+ 3 t. ([Psalm 10:5](https://ref.ly/Ps10:5) v. II. חול); וַיָּחִ֫ילוּ Ju [3:25](https://ref.ly/Judg3:25); יְחִילוּן [Is 13:8](https://ref.ly/Isa13:8); תָּחִ֫ילוּ [Je 5:22](https://ref.ly/Jer5:22); *Imv.* fs. חוּ֫לִי [Mi 4:10](https://ref.ly/Mic4:10), [Psalm 114:7](https://ref.ly/Ps114:7); mpl. חִילוּ [Psalm 96:9](https://ref.ly/Ps96:9), [1 Ch 16:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:30); *Inf. cstr.* חוּל(לָ) Ju [21:21](https://ref.ly/Judg21:21), Ez [30:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:16) ( \= Inf. abs.); *Pt.* fs. חוֺלָה [Je 4:31](https://ref.ly/Jer4:31) vid. Gf Gie and Ges§ 72 R. 1; —**1\.** *dance* Ju [21:21](https://ref.ly/Judg21:21) (לָחוּל בַּמְּחֹלוֺת). **2\.** *twist, writhe:* **a.** in pain, esp. childbirth [Is 26:17](https://ref.ly/Isa26:17), [45:10](https://ref.ly/Isa45:10) ´מַה־תח (‖מה־תוליד; metaph., of sea [Is 23:4](https://ref.ly/Isa23:4) (‖ילד); Israel [26:18](https://ref.ly/Isa26:18) (‖חרה), [54:1](https://ref.ly/Isa54:1) (‖ילד); Zion [66:7](https://ref.ly/Isa66:7), [66:8](https://ref.ly/Isa66:8) (‖ *id.*), [Mi 4:10](https://ref.ly/Mic4:10) (‖גֹּחִי, sim. כיולדה; cf. v. [9](https://ref.ly/Mic4:9)), [Je 4:31](https://ref.ly/Jer4:31). **b.** fig., *be in severe pain*, or *anguish* (mostly poet. and elevated prose), sq. מפני [Dt 2:25](https://ref.ly/Deut2:25) (‖רגז, cf. also פַּחַד and יִרְאָה va); Ez [30:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:16), [Psalm 96:9](https://ref.ly/Ps96:9), [Je 5:22](https://ref.ly/Jer5:22) (‖ירא); sq. מִלִּפְנֵי [1 Ch 16:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:30), [Psalm 114:7](https://ref.ly/Ps114:7); abs. [Psalm 55:5](https://ref.ly/Ps55:5) (‖אימות מָוֶת נפלו עלי), [Je 4:19](https://ref.ly/Jer4:19) Kt, [Psalm 77:17](https://ref.ly/Ps77:17) (‖רגז) [97:4](https://ref.ly/Ps97:4), [Is 13:8](https://ref.ly/Isa13:8), [23:5](https://ref.ly/Isa23:5), [Je 51:29](https://ref.ly/Jer51:29) (‖רָעַשׁ), [Jo 2:6](https://ref.ly/Joel2:6), [Zc 9:5](https://ref.ly/Zech9:5) (‖ירא), prob. also [1 S 31:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:3) (cf. [28:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:5)) \= [1 Ch 10:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:3), cf. Öt; fig. of mts. Hb [3:10](https://ref.ly/Hab3:10). **c.** in contrition [Je 5:3](https://ref.ly/Jer5:3), חָ֫לוּ der. fr. חול Ew Gf Gie ; \< SyrVer Ra Hi Ke al. fr. חלה (wrongly accented; vid. חלה **Qal 2**). **d.** in anxious longing עַד בּוֺשׁ Ju [3:25](https://ref.ly/Judg3:25), sq. לְ [Mi 1:12](https://ref.ly/Mic1:12) (on [Gn 8:10](https://ref.ly/Gen8:10), cf. supr.) **3\.** *whirl, whirl about* עַל־ראֹשׁ יוֺאָב (i.e. blood—דמים—of Abner, from sword) [2 S 3:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:29); of attack of sword itself [Ho 11:6](https://ref.ly/Hos11:6) (‖כִּלָּה, אכל), cf. [La 4:6](https://ref.ly/Lam4:6) לא חָ֫לוּ בה ידים; of tempest על ראשׁ רשׁעים יחול [Je 23:19](https://ref.ly/Jer23:19) (va סער מִתְחוֺלֵל) \= [30:23](https://ref.ly/Jer30:23) (va סַעַר מִתְגּוֺרֵר). **Poïlel.** *Impf.* יְחוֺלֵל [Psalm 29:9](https://ref.ly/Ps29:9); 3 fs. תְּחוֺלֵל [Pr 25:23](https://ref.ly/Prov25:23); sf. תְּחוֺלֶלְכֶם [Is 51:2](https://ref.ly/Isa51:2); 2 ms. תְּחוֺלֵל [Jb 35:14](https://ref.ly/Job35:14); וַתְּחוֺלֵל [Psalm 90:2](https://ref.ly/Ps90:2); *Inf.* cstr. חֹלֵל [Jb 39:1](https://ref.ly/Job39:1); *Pt.* מְחוֺלֵל [Pr 26:10](https://ref.ly/Prov26:10); מְחֹלֶלְךָ [Dt 32:18](https://ref.ly/Deut32:18); מְחוֺלֶלֶת [Is 51:9](https://ref.ly/Isa51:9); מְחֹלְלֶוֺת Ju [21:23](https://ref.ly/Judg21:23); —[1 S 18:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:6), [Psalm 87:7](https://ref.ly/Ps87:7) v. infr.; on חֹלְלָה [Jb 26:13](https://ref.ly/Job26:13) v. חלל;—**1\.** *dance* Ju [21:23](https://ref.ly/Judg21:23), so perh. also [1 S 18:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:6) (rd. הַמְּחֹלְלוֺת LXX We Dr; and חֹלֲלִים pt. (מ om.) [Psalm 87:7](https://ref.ly/Ps87:7) RV Pe De Che Bae; cf. **Qal 1**). **2\.** *writhe* in travail *with, bear, bring forth* (poet.), of Sarah, mother of Isr., [Is 51:2](https://ref.ly/Isa51:2) (‖אַבְרָהָם אֲבִיכֶם); of hinds [Jb 39:1](https://ref.ly/Job39:1) [Psalm 29:9](https://ref.ly/Ps29:9) (caus.); fig. of ´י's producing earth [Psalm 90:2](https://ref.ly/Ps90:2) (‖יֻלָּדָוּ); of his bringing forth Isr. (with difficulty, v. > \[Str2344] † חוֺל **n.m.**:[Ho 2:1](https://ref.ly/Hos2:1) sand (perh. as turning or *whirling;* otherwise explained by Sta, vid. sub חֲוִילָה; NH *id.*, Aram. חָלָא, ܚܳܠܴܐ)—´ח abs. [Gn 32:13](https://ref.ly/Gen32:13) \+ 6 t.;—*sand* [Ex 2:12](https://ref.ly/Exod2:12) (E); set as bound for sea [Je 5:22](https://ref.ly/Jer5:22); ´שְפֻנֵי טמוני ח [Dt 33:19](https://ref.ly/Deut33:19) *hidden treasures of sand*, poet. for *glass* (regarded as mysteriously produced out of sand, v. Di and reff.); **a.** usu. sand of seashore, sim. of numberlessness, vastness, so of Abraham's seed [Gn 22:17](https://ref.ly/Gen22:17) (J; cf. [15:5](https://ref.ly/Gen15:5)); Jacob's [32:13](https://ref.ly/Gen32:13) (E) of a host [Jos 11:4](https://ref.ly/Josh11:4) (JE), Ju [7:12](https://ref.ly/Judg7:12), [1 S 13:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:5), [2 S 17:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:11); a people [1 K 4:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:20), [Ho 2:1](https://ref.ly/Hos2:1), [Is 10:22](https://ref.ly/Isa10:22), [48:19](https://ref.ly/Isa48:19) (‖וְצֶאֱצָאֵי מֶעֶיךָ כִּמְעֹתָיו), [Je 33:22](https://ref.ly/Jer33:22), Hb [1:9](https://ref.ly/Hab1:9); of corn gathered by Joseph [Gn 41:49](https://ref.ly/Gen41:49); of days of Job [Jb 29:18](https://ref.ly/Job29:18) (on interpr. \= *phoenix*, vid. Di); of birds עוף כנף (i.e. quails) [Psalm 78:27](https://ref.ly/Ps78:27) (‖כֶּעָפָר); of vast extent of Sol.'s wisdom [1 K 5:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:9). **b.** in comp., *more than the sand* [Je 15:8](https://ref.ly/Jer15:8) (widows of Judah); thoughts of God [Psalm 139:18](https://ref.ly/Ps139:18). **c.** of weight (in balances), metaph. of Job's vexation (כַּעַש) [Jb 6:3](https://ref.ly/Job6:3) *heavier than the* sand, cf. וְנֵטֶל הַחוֺל [Pr 27:3](https://ref.ly/Prov27:3) (‖כֹּבֶד אֶבֶן), said of כַּעַס אֱוִיל.—Combinations are: ´כח אשׁר על שפת הים [Gn 22:17](https://ref.ly/Gen22:17), [Jos 11:4](https://ref.ly/Josh11:4), [1 S 13:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:5), [1 K 5:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:9); הַיָּם ´כח [Gn 32:13](https://ref.ly/Gen32:13), [41:49](https://ref.ly/Gen41:49), [Is 10:22](https://ref.ly/Isa10:22), [Ho 2:1](https://ref.ly/Hos2:1) (\+ אשׁר לא יִמַּד ולא יִסָּפֵר); הַיָּם ´ח [Je 33:22](https://ref.ly/Jer33:22); שֶׁעַל־שפת הים לרב ´כח Ju [7:12](https://ref.ly/Judg7:12); אשׁר־על־הים לרב ´כח [2 S 17:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:11), [1 K 4:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:20); חול יַמִּים(מ) [Jb 6:3](https://ref.ly/Job6:3), [Psalm 78:27](https://ref.ly/Ps78:27), [Je 15:8](https://ref.ly/Jer15:8).

> \[Str2426,2430] † חֵל, חֵיל **n.m.**:[Ob 20](https://ref.ly/Obad1:20) rampart, fortress (perh. orig. *surrounding wall*, cf. Sab. Denkm.:91 n. 2\)—abs. חֵיל [Na 3:8](https://ref.ly/Nah3:8), חֵל [La 2:8](https://ref.ly/Lam2:8) \+ 3 t.;(\+ [Psalm 10:10](https://ref.ly/Ps10:10) Qr v. חלכה); cstr. חֵל [1 K 21:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:23); sf. חֵילֵךְ [Psalm 122:7](https://ref.ly/Ps122:7), חֵילָהּ [Zc 9:4](https://ref.ly/Zech9:4); חֵילָה \= חֵילָהּ [Psalm 48:14](https://ref.ly/Ps48:14), Ol:§ 40 c. 75g. 96 e Sta§ 347 c;—**1\.** *rampart* (defined by Jews as בֶּן חוֺמָה, i.e. a little wall, cf. Ki s. v.), of an outer fortification [2 S 20:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:15) (others, by meton., of space between outer and inner fortif., incl. moat, v. Dr Kit); [Is 26:1](https://ref.ly/Isa26:1), [La 2:8](https://ref.ly/Lam2:8) (both ‖חוֺמוֺת); in gen. of defences, or sea\-power of Tyre וְהִכָּה בַיָּם חֵילָהּ [Zc 9:4](https://ref.ly/Zech9:4) (v. StaZAW i.1881, 15\), of No\-Amon (Thebes) אֲשֶׁר חֵיל יָם מִיָּם חוֺמָתָהּ [Na 3:8](https://ref.ly/Nah3:8); of Zion [Psalm 122:7](https://ref.ly/Ps122:7), also [48:14](https://ref.ly/Ps48:14) (חילה \= חֵילָהּ, cf. supr.; both ‖ארמנותיך).—For חֵל [1 K 21:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:23) rd. prob. חֵלֶק, q. v. **2\.** *fortress* גָּלֻת הַחֵל הַזֶּה Ob:[20](https://ref.ly/Obad1:20) *the exiles of this fortress* (Hi\-St; Or, AVRV *this army*, חֵל \= חַיִל).

> \[Str2430] † חֵילָה [Psalm 48:14](https://ref.ly/Ps48:14), v. חֵל supr.

> \[Str2431] † חֵילָם **n.pr.loc.** E. of Jordan [2 S 10:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:16) (Th *their army*, but LXX SyrVer Targum EwGesch. iii. 211 Anm. 1 \= H iii. 155 n. 2 We Dr) \= חֵלָא֯מָה v:[17](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:17) (LXX Αιλαμ); \+ חילם Ez [47:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:16) (LXX Ηλιαμ) Co; acc. to this it lay on the border between Damascus and Hamath.

> \[Str2432] † חִילֵן **n.pr.loc.** in Judah [1 Ch 6:43](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:43) \= חֹלֽן **2\.**

> \[Str2473] † חֹלוֺן, חֹלֽן **n.pr.loc. 1\.** in Moab חֹלוֺן [Je 48:21](https://ref.ly/Jer48:21) (LXX Χελων), Χαιλων). **2\.** in Judah חֹלֽן [Jos 15:51](https://ref.ly/Josh15:51) (LXX Χαλου, LXXL Χειλου); priestly city [21:15](https://ref.ly/Josh21:15) (LXX Γελλα, LXXL Ιλων) \= חֹילֵן [1 Ch 6:43](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:43) (LXXL Χελων).

> \[Str2497] † חֵלֽן **n.pr.m.** a man of Zebulon [Nu 1:9](https://ref.ly/Num1:9), [2:7](https://ref.ly/Num2:7), [7:24](https://ref.ly/Num7:24), [7:29](https://ref.ly/Num7:29), [10:16](https://ref.ly/Num10:16) (LXX Χαιλων, LXXL Χελων).

> \[Str2479] † חַלְחָלָה **n.f.** anguish, ´מָֽלְאוּ מָתְנַי ח [Is 21:3](https://ref.ly/Isa21:3), בכל מתנים ´ח [Na 2:11](https://ref.ly/Nah2:11), Ez [30:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:4), [30:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:9) (‖החריד).

> \[Str4234] † מָחוֺל **n.m.**:[La 5:15](https://ref.ly/Lam5:15) dance;—abs. ´מ [Psalm 30:12](https://ref.ly/Ps30:12) \+ 3 t.; cstr. מְחוֺל [Je 31:4](https://ref.ly/Jer31:4); מְחוֺלֵנוּ [La 5:15](https://ref.ly/Lam5:15); —*dance*, token of joyousness [Psalm 30:12](https://ref.ly/Ps30:12) (opp. מספד), [Je 31:4](https://ref.ly/Jer31:4), [31:13](https://ref.ly/Jer31:13) (cf. vb), [La 5:15](https://ref.ly/Lam5:15) (opp. אֵבֶּל); act of praise to ´י [Psalm 149:3](https://ref.ly/Ps149:3), [150:4](https://ref.ly/Ps150:4); accompanied by timbrel (תֹּף) and sometimes other instruments [Psalm 149:3](https://ref.ly/Ps149:3), [149:4](https://ref.ly/Ps149:4), [Je 31:13](https://ref.ly/Jer31:13).

> \[Str4246] † \[מְחוֺלָה] **n.f.** id.;—cstr. מְחֹלַת Ct [7:1](https://ref.ly/Song7:1); pl. מְחֹלֽת [Ex 32:19](https://ref.ly/Exod32:19); מְחֹלוֺת [Ex 15:20](https://ref.ly/Exod15:20) \+ 5 t.; *dancing*, token of joyousness after victory, Ju [11:34](https://ref.ly/Judg11:34), [1 S 18:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:16), [21:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:12), [29:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:5); in worship, at feast Ju [21:21](https://ref.ly/Judg21:21); act of praise to ´י [Ex 15:20](https://ref.ly/Exod15:20) (song); in idolatrous worship [Ex 32:19](https://ref.ly/Exod32:19) (E); as graceful and beautiful Ct [7:1](https://ref.ly/Song7:1); accomp. by timbre1 [Ex 15:20](https://ref.ly/Exod15:20), Ju [11:34](https://ref.ly/Judg11:34), and by singing [Ex 15:20](https://ref.ly/Exod15:20), [1 S 18:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:6) (where however rd. prob. Poïlel Pt. הַמְּחֹלְלוֺת, cf. vb. חוּל supr.; so LXX We Dr), [21:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:12), [29:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:5).

## II חול (id: BDB02902)

\[Str2342] † II. \[חול, חִיל] **vb.** befirm, strong (Aram. Pa. *strengthen*, חֵילָא, ܚܰܝܠܴܐ *strength;* Palm. רב חילא רבא *general\-in\-chief* VogNo. 28 al.; Sab. חַיל Sab. Denkm.:91 (on חַל in Sab.:n. pr. v. HalJAS Juin, 1872, 533\); Eth. ኄለ፡ ኀየለ፡ *be strong*, ኀይል፡ *strength, army*, ኀይኣል፡ *strong;* As. *ḫâ(i)ltu, army* Flood Tabl.:iii. 22 (*ḫaiialtu* JägerBAS i. 461\); poss. comp. Ar. خَيْلٌ *horses* (as *strong ones*? cf. [Psalm 33:17](https://ref.ly/Ps33:17)))—only **Qal** *Impf.* in לאֹ יָחִיל טוּבוֺ [Jb 20:21](https://ref.ly/Job20:21) *his prosperity is not firm* (does not endure); יָחִילוּ דְרָכָוּ֯ בְּכָל־עֵת [Psalm 10:5](https://ref.ly/Ps10:5) *his ways are always firm* (stable, prosperous), so Ol De Pe Che al.

[1 S 2:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:4), [Psalm 18:33](https://ref.ly/Ps18:33), [18:40](https://ref.ly/Ps18:40) \= [2 S 22:33](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:33), [22:40](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:40); also ´נָּֽברוּ ח [Jb 21:7](https://ref.ly/Job21:7), cf. Jonah, על הקיקיון [Jon 4:10](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:10).—Vid. also חמל.
