## I חוץ (id: BDB02912)

I. חוץ (√ of foll., meaning unknown).

> \[Str2351] חוּץ **n.\[m.]** the outside, sts., esp. in pl., spec. a street, never with sf. in sg., with ה—ָ *loc.* ח֫וּצָה, חֻ֫צָה, pl. חוּצוֺת, חֻצוֺת, with sf. חוּצֹתָיו etc., [Is 15:3](https://ref.ly/Isa15:3) etc.;—**1\.** *the outside,* of a house, tent, city, camp, etc., oft. used, esp. with preps., to express the adv. idea of *outside, abroad* (Aram. syn., exc. in sense **2 a,** is בַּר, ܒܰܪ): **a.** as accus. after vb. of motion [Dt 23:13](https://ref.ly/Deut23:13) יָצָא חוּץ to go forth outside (sc. a camp), v:[14](https://ref.ly/Deut23:14) (as adv. accus.) בְּשִׁבְתְּךָ חוּץ *outside,* הוֺצִיא הַחוּץ to bring forth *outside* (a house or chamber) Ju [19:25](https://ref.ly/Judg19:25), [2 S 13:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:18) cf. [Ne 13:8](https://ref.ly/Neh13:8), Ju [12:9](https://ref.ly/Judg12:9) b בָּנוֺת הֵבִיא לְבָנָיו מִן־הַחוּץ from *outside,* from *abroad,* [2 K 4:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:3) שַׁאֲלִי־לָח כֵּלִים מִן־הַחוּץ from *outside* (opp. בַּבַּיִת v:[2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:2)); as genit. [Lv 18:9](https://ref.ly/Lev18:9) מוֺלֶדֶת חוּץ one born *abroad* (i.e. by another father or mother, of a half\-sister: opp. מוֺלֶדֶת בַּיִת) Ez [47:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:2), [47:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:2). With ה—ָ *loc.* חוּצָה, after a vb. of motion [Ex 12:46](https://ref.ly/Exod12:46) חוּצָה…מִן־הַבַּיִת, [Pr 5:16](https://ref.ly/Prov5:16), [2 Ch 29:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:16), [33:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:15) חוּצָה לָעִיר *outside of* the city, [Nu 35:4](https://ref.ly/Num35:4) מִקִּיר הָעִיר וָחוּצָה from the wall of the city *and outwards*; \= *on the outside, without* [1 K 6:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:6), [Is 33:7](https://ref.ly/Isa33:7) צָֽעֲקוּ חוּצָה cry *without,* [2 Ch 24:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:8): so הַחוּצָה [Gn 15:5](https://ref.ly/Gen15:5) ´וַיּוֺצֵא אֹתוֺ הח and brought him *outside,* [19:17](https://ref.ly/Gen19:17), [24:29](https://ref.ly/Gen24:29), [Jos 2:19](https://ref.ly/Josh2:19) \+, [1 K 8:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:8) לאֹ יֵרָאוּ הַחוּצָה were not seen *towards the outside,* [Dt 25:5](https://ref.ly/Deut25:5) לאֹ תִהְיֶה אֵשֶׁת הַמֵּת הַחוּצָה לְאִישׁ זָרi.e. shall not be married into another family, cf. Ju [12:9](https://ref.ly/Judg12:9) a. **b.** with preps.: *a.* † אל־החוץ *towards the outside* Ez [41:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:9), † [34:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:21) pleon. אל־החוצה. *b.* בחוץ [Gn 9:22](https://ref.ly/Gen9:22) *outside* (opp. בְּתוֺח אָהֳלֽה v:[21](https://ref.ly/Gen9:21)), [24:31](https://ref.ly/Gen24:31) לָמָּה תַעֲמֹד בַּחוץ, [Dt 24:11](https://ref.ly/Deut24:11), [Ex 21:19](https://ref.ly/Exod21:19) אם יקום והתהלך בחוץ (after leaving a sick couch), [2 K 10:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:24), [Psalm 31:12](https://ref.ly/Ps31:12), Ez [7:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:15) (opp. מִבַּיִת), [Ho 7:1](https://ref.ly/Hos7:1) \+. *c.* † לחוץ [Psalm 41:7](https://ref.ly/Ps41:7) יצא לחוץ; \= *on the outside* Ez [41:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:17), [42:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:7), † לַחוּצָה [2 Ch 32:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:5) *towards the outside*. *d.* מִחוּץ *on* (מִן **3 c**) *the outside* Ez [40:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:19), [46:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:2), מִבַיִת וּמִחוּץ within and *without* (of an ark, building, etc.) [Gn 6:14](https://ref.ly/Gen6:14), [Ex 25:11](https://ref.ly/Exod25:11) \= [37:2](https://ref.ly/Exod37:2), [1 K 7:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:9), [Dt 32:25](https://ref.ly/Deut32:25) מחוץ תְּשַׁכֶּל־חֶרֶב (opp. יּמֵחֲדָרִים אֵימָה), [La 1:20](https://ref.ly/Lam1:20) (cf. Ez [7:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:15) supr. *b*); so מֵהַחוּץ † Ez [41:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:25). מִחוּץ לְ *on the outside of,* [Gn 19:16](https://ref.ly/Gen19:16) וַיַּנִּיחֵהוּ מִחוּץ לָעִיר, [24:11](https://ref.ly/Gen24:11), [Ex 26:35](https://ref.ly/Exod26:35), [37:7](https://ref.ly/Exod37:7), [37:7](https://ref.ly/Exod37:7), [Lv 8:17](https://ref.ly/Lev8:17), [14:8](https://ref.ly/Lev14:8), [Nu 35:5](https://ref.ly/Num35:5), [Jos 6:23](https://ref.ly/Josh6:23), [Je 21:4](https://ref.ly/Jer21:4) \+, even after a vb. of motion [1 K 21:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:13) וַיּוֺצִיאוּהוּ מִחוּץ לְעִיר, [2 K 23:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:6), though in this case אל מחוץ ל is more common, [Lv 4:12](https://ref.ly/Lev4:12), [4:21](https://ref.ly/Lev4:21) אל מחוץ למחנה, [6:4](https://ref.ly/Lev6:4), [10:4](https://ref.ly/Lev10:4), [14:40](https://ref.ly/Lev14:40), [14:41](https://ref.ly/Lev14:41) \+ oft. P, [Dt 23:11](https://ref.ly/Deut23:11). *e.* † מִחוּצָה Ez [40:40](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:40) *on the outside,* מִחוּצָה לְ v:[44](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:44). *f.* (late) † חוּץ מִן *outside of,* [Ec 2:25](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:25) who can eat, and who enjoy, חוּץ מִמֶּנִּיִ *outside of* me, i.e. *except* me? (but rd. rather, with LXX SyrVer De Now al., חוּץ מִמֶּנּוּ *apart from, without* him, i.e. God). (So NH חוּץ מִן *except, without,* v. De:[Ec 2:25](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:25). Cf. The Aram. syn. בַּר מִן *except* [Ex 20:3](https://ref.ly/Exod20:3) Targum; *apart from, without* [Is 36:10](https://ref.ly/Isa36:10) Targum; Syr. ܠܒܰܪ ܡܶܢ *id*.)
> 
> **2\.** Of a definite locality, viz. **a.** that which is outside the houses of a town, i.e. *a street* [Is 51:23](https://ref.ly/Isa51:23) כַּחוּץ לַעֹבְרִים, [Je 37:21](https://ref.ly/Jer37:21) חוּץ הָאֹפִים the Bakers' *street,* [Pr 1:20](https://ref.ly/Prov1:20) כַּחוּץ, (‖כָּֽרְחֹבוֺת: so [7:12](https://ref.ly/Prov7:12), [22:13](https://ref.ly/Prov22:13), [Je 9:20](https://ref.ly/Jer9:20)); oft. in pl., as חוּצֹת אַשְׁקְלוֹן the *streets* of Ashkelon [2 S 1:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:20), ירושׁלם ´ח [Je 5:1](https://ref.ly/Jer5:1), [7:17](https://ref.ly/Jer7:17), [7:34](https://ref.ly/Jer7:34) \+ oft. Jer., [1 K 20:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:34) וְחֻצוֺת תָּשִים־לְךָ בְּדַמֶּשֶק and *streets* (i.e. bazaars) shalt thou make thee in D., as my father made in Samaria (a concession involving the right of trading), [Am 5:16](https://ref.ly/Amos5:16), [Is 15:3](https://ref.ly/Isa15:3), [24:11](https://ref.ly/Isa24:11) (as scene of mourning), [Is 5:25](https://ref.ly/Isa5:25), [Je 51:4](https://ref.ly/Jer51:4), Ez [11:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:6), [28:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:23) ( as filled with corpses of slain), בְּראֹשׁ כָּל־חוּצוֺת i.e. in conspicuous places, [Na 3:10](https://ref.ly/Nah3:10), [La 2:19](https://ref.ly/Lam2:19), [4:1](https://ref.ly/Lam4:1), [Is 51:20](https://ref.ly/Isa51:20), טִיט חוּצוֺת mire of *streets* [Zc 10:5](https://ref.ly/Zech10:5) (trampled on by warriors), in simile, of foes trodden under foot, [Psalm 18:43](https://ref.ly/Ps18:43), [Mi 7:10](https://ref.ly/Mic7:10) ( so ´כְּחֹמֶר ח [Is 10:6](https://ref.ly/Isa10:6)), metaph. of cheapness and abundance [Zc 9:3](https://ref.ly/Zech9:3). **b.** more gen., that which is outside enclosed cities, the *open country,* [Jb 18:17](https://ref.ly/Job18:17) וְלאֹ שֵׁם לוֺ עַל־פְּנֵי־חוּץ (v. De), cf. [Pr 24:27](https://ref.ly/Prov24:27); in the pl. [Jb 5:10](https://ref.ly/Job5:10) וְשֹׁלֵחַ מַיִם עַל פְנֵי חוּצוֺת, [Pr 8:26](https://ref.ly/Prov8:26), [Psalm 144:13](https://ref.ly/Ps144:13) (as place where flocks abound).

By accident in Baer, cf. Strad loc.) [17:23](https://ref.ly/Prov17:23) sf. חֵיקִי [1 K 3:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:20), [Psalm 89:51](https://ref.ly/Ps89:51); חֵקִי [Jb 19:27](https://ref.ly/Job19:27) etc.; elsewh. alw. plene c. sf.; חוקף Kt [Psalm 74:11](https://ref.ly/Ps74:11) (Qr חֵיקְךָ);—*bosom,* specif. **1\.** of fold of garment, at breast [Ex 4:6](https://ref.ly/Exod4:6), [4:6](https://ref.ly/Exod4:6), [4:7](https://ref.ly/Exod4:7), [4:7](https://ref.ly/Exod4:7), [4:7](https://ref.ly/Exod4:7) (all J), [Pr 6:27](https://ref.ly/Prov6:27), [16:33](https://ref.ly/Prov16:33), metaph. of ´י [Psalm 74:11](https://ref.ly/Ps74:11); ´שֹׁחַד בח [Pr 21:14](https://ref.ly/Prov21:14) is a *hidden reward* (concealed in bosom; ‖מַתָּן בַּסֵּתֶר); שֹׁחַד טֵחֵק [17:23](https://ref.ly/Prov17:23) is a *gift secretly given*; fig., *requite into bosom* [Is 65:6](https://ref.ly/Isa65:6), [65:7](https://ref.ly/Isa65:7), [Psalm 35:13](https://ref.ly/Ps35:13), [79:12](https://ref.ly/Ps79:12), [Je 32:18](https://ref.ly/Jer32:18). **2\.** *carry* (נָשָא) *in bosom,* Moses the people [Nu 11:12](https://ref.ly/Num11:12) (JE); Naomi, Ruth's child [Ru 4:16](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:16), cf. ## I חָוַר (id: BDB02921)

\[Str2357] † I. \[חָוַר] **vb.** be or grow white, pale (Ar. حَوِرَ *be white,* حَوَرٌ *whiteness,* Aram. ܚܘܰܪ, חֲוַר *be white*)—only **Qal** *Impf.* 3 mpl. וְלֽא עַתָּה פָּנָיו יֶחֱוָרָוּ [Is 29:22](https://ref.ly/Isa29:22) (‖לֽא עַתָּה יֵבוֺשׁ יַעֲקֹב).

> \[Str2353] † I. חוּר **n.\[m.]** white stuff (dub. whether cotton or linen, cf. כַּרְפַּס), ‖תְּכֵלֶת:—of Mordecai's garments בִּלְבוּשׁ מַלְכוּת תְּכֵלֶת וָחוּר [Est 8:15a](https://ref.ly/Esth8:15) *in a royal garment of violet and white stuff* (cf. וְתַכְרִיךְ בּוּץ וְאַרְגָּמָן vb); of furnishings of royal palace of Susa כַּרְפַּס וּתְכֵלֶת אֶחוּז בְּחַבְלֵי־בוּץ ´ח וְאַרְגָּמָן [1:6](https://ref.ly/Esth1:6) *white stuff of fine linen (?) held fast by cords of byssus and purple.*

> \[Str2355] † חוֺרָי **n.m.** white stuff, cf. I. חוּר; וְאֹרְגִים חוֺרָי [Is 19:9](https://ref.ly/Isa19:9) *and* (those) *weaving white stuff*.

> \[Str2751] † I. חֹרִי **n.\[m.]** white bread or cake שְׁלשָׁה סַלֵּי חֹרִי [Gn 40:16](https://ref.ly/Gen40:16) *three baskets of white bread*.—II. חֹרִי v. III. חרר

> \[Str2354,1133,2352] † II. חוּר **n.pr.m.** (mng. dub.; cf. Nab. n.pr. חורו Nö in Eutp. 45\)—**1\.** LXX Ωρ, named with Aaron as compan. of Moses [Ex 17:10](https://ref.ly/Exod17:10), [17:12](https://ref.ly/Exod17:12), [24:14](https://ref.ly/Exod24:14) (all E). **2\.** LXX Ωρ, a Judahite [Ex 31:2](https://ref.ly/Exod31:2) \= [35:30](https://ref.ly/Exod35:30), cf. [38:22](https://ref.ly/Exod38:22) (all P) [1 Ch 2:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:19), [2:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:20), [2:50](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:50) (on div. of verse v. Be VB); also [4:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:1), [4:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:4), [2 Ch 1:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:5). **3\.** LXX Ουρ, a king of Midian [Nu 31:8](https://ref.ly/Num31:8), [Jos 13:21](https://ref.ly/Josh13:21) (both P). **4\.** LXXL Εουρ (B om.) head of fam. of returned exiles [Ne 3:9](https://ref.ly/Neh3:9). **5\.** בֶּן־חוּר officer of Sol. [1 K 4:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:8), v. sub בֵּן p. 122\.

> \[Str2360,1914] † חוּרַי **n.pr.m.** a hero of David מִנַּחֲלֵי ´ח גָעַשׁ [1 Ch 11:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:32) LXX Ουρ(ε)ι, LXXL Ουριὰ \= הִדֶּי [2 S 23:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:30) (om. B; A. Αθθαι, LXXL Αδδαι). p. 213

> \[Str2437] † הִירָה **n.pr.m.** (√ חור but mng. dub.) an Adullamite, friend of Judah [Gn 38:1](https://ref.ly/Gen38:1), [38:12](https://ref.ly/Gen38:12) (both J), LXX Ειρας.

## II חור (id: BDB02931)

II. חור (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. خَارَ *bend, turn, incline,* of man *be weak,* Lane820 c, خَوْرٌ *hollow, depressed ground between hills*).

> \[Str2735] † I. חֹר **n.\[m.]** hollow, only in חֹר הַגִּדְָּ\+גּד [Nu 33:32](https://ref.ly/Num33:32)  *hollow of Gidgad* (v. Di).

> \[Str2715] II., III. חוֺר, חֹר, (חוֺרִים) v. sub II., III. חרר. p. 359

> \[Str2362] † חַוְרָן **n.pr.terr.** (meaning unknown; conjectures are:—*black\-land* (as basaltic region); supported by حَوْر *black,* dial. of Yemen, MaltzanZMG 1874, 230, and tokens of immigration from Yemen into Ḥaurân, Wetzst in De:[Job 2:598](https://ref.ly/Job2:598); ZKW 1884, 120; *land of caves,* Thes Hi\-Sm, PorterDict. Bib., and *hollow,* GASmGeogr. 552, who comp. Heb. הור *hole;* but this prob. fr. III. חרר, and Ar. خَوْر, *hollow,* diff. fr. حَوْرَان)—district SE. fr. Mt. Hermon, extending between Jaulan and Lejah, toward Syr. desert; only גְּבוּל חַוְרָן Ez [47:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:16) cf. מִבֵּין חַוְשָרן וּמִבֵּין דַּמֶּשֶק v:[18](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:18), v:[18](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:18); \= As. *Ḫaurani* DlPa 294; LXX Αυρανιτις, and so JosAnt. xv. 10, 1 etc., Ar. حَوْرَان. On this district v. WetzstHauran, (1860\) and in De:Job 2. 597ff. BDPal 195 ff. SchumacherAcross the Jordan, 1889; ZPV xii. 1889, 225 ff. (with map) GASmGeogr. 552 f. 609 ff.

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 33:32; Ezekiel 47:16; Job 2:598; Ezekiel 47:18


## I חוּשׁ (id: BDB02935)

\[Str2363,2439] † I. חוּשׁ **vb.** haste, make haste (As. *ḫâšu,* DlPr 180, Eth ሖሰ፡; on this vb. V. NöZMG 1883, 538\)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. חָשׁ [Dt 32:35](https://ref.ly/Deut32:35) (or *Pt.,* but v. Di), 1 S חַשְׁתִּי [Psalm 119:60](https://ref.ly/Ps119:60); *Impf.* 3 fs. וַתַּ֫חַשׁ (sic!) [Jb 31:5](https://ref.ly/Job31:5) Ges§ 72 R. 9; *Imv.* חוּ֫שָׁה [1 S 20:38](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:38) \+ 6 t. Psalm, \+ [Psalm 71:12](https://ref.ly/Ps71:12) Qr (Kt חישׁה); *Inf.* sf. חוּשִׁי [Jb 20:2](https://ref.ly/Job20:2); *Pt. act.* חָשׁ Hb [1:8](https://ref.ly/Hab1:8) \+ [Is 8:1](https://ref.ly/Isa8:1), [8:3](https://ref.ly/Isa8:3) in n.pr.; *pass.* חֻשִׁיּס [Nu 32:17](https://ref.ly/Num32:17); but v. infr.:—*make haste* [Dt 32:35](https://ref.ly/Deut32:35) (‖קרוב), [Psalm 119:60](https://ref.ly/Ps119:60); in prophetic n.pr. מַהֵר שָׁלָל חָשׁ בַּ\+ז [Is 8:1](https://ref.ly/Isa8:1), [8:3](https://ref.ly/Isa8:3); of eagle (in sim. of Chald. army) נשׁר חשׁ לאכול Hb [1:8](https://ref.ly/Hab1:8); [Jb 31:5](https://ref.ly/Job31:5) subj. רנל, sq. על־מרמה; חוּֽשִׁי בּי [Jb 20:2](https://ref.ly/Job20:2) *my haste in me,* i.e. my inward excitement—esp. *Imv.* [1 S 20:38](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:38) (‖מהרה), and in Psalm Psalm: sq. לְעַזְרָתִי [22:20](https://ref.ly/Ps22:20), [38:23](https://ref.ly/Ps38:23), [40:14](https://ref.ly/Ps40:14), [70:2](https://ref.ly/Ps70:2), [71:12](https://ref.ly/Ps71:12) (Qr); sq. לִי [70:6](https://ref.ly/Ps70:5), [141:1](https://ref.ly/Ps141:1); *pt. pass.* נֵחָלֵץ חֻשִׁים [Nu 32:17](https://ref.ly/Num32:17), but prob. for חֲמֻשִׁים (Kn Di comp. [Ex 13:18](https://ref.ly/Exod13:18) etc.); BaNB 180 retains חוּשִׁים and regards it as act. (intrans.) \= *hastening*. **Hiph.** *Pf.* הֵחִישׁוּ Ju [20:27](https://ref.ly/Judg20:27); *Impf.* יָחִישׁ [Is 28:16](https://ref.ly/Isa28:16); יָחִ֫ישָׁה coh. [5:19](https://ref.ly/Isa5:19) (Ges§ 48\. 3\); אָחִ֫ישָׁה coh. [Psalm 55:9](https://ref.ly/Ps55:9); *sf.* אֲחִישֶׁנָּה [Is 60:22](https://ref.ly/Isa60:22); —**1\.** *shew haste, act quickly* Ju [20:37](https://ref.ly/Judg20:37); *hasten, come quickly* [Is 5:19](https://ref.ly/Isa5:19) (subj. מַעֲשֵהוּ, ‖יְמַהֵר; others: *let him hasten on his work*), יָחִישׁ [Is 28:16](https://ref.ly/Isa28:16) *hasten away* (flee), or *hasten about* distractedly (si vera l.; Che Guthe rd. ימישׁ *yield, give way*). **2\.** transit. c. sf. [Is 60:22](https://ref.ly/Isa60:22) *I will hasten it*; c. acc. [Psalm 55:9](https://ref.ly/Ps55:9) *I would hasten* (\= secure quickly) *my escape.*

> \[Str2440] † חִישׁ **adv.** quickly, כי נז חישׁ [Psalm 90:10](https://ref.ly/Ps90:10) of passing away of human life.

## II חוּשׁ (id: BDB02937)

\[Str2363] † II. \[חוּשׁ] **vb.** feel, enjoy (with the senses) (Now comp. Ar. حَسَّ *feel, perceive* by senses; NH חוּשׁ *feel pain*; Aram. ܚܳܫ, חוּשׁ, *feel pain*; Eth. حوشس:: *perception*)—only **Qal** *Impf*. 3 ms. מִּי יאֹכַל וּמִי יָחוּשׁ [Ec 2:25](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:25) *who can eat and who can feel* (i.e. *enjoy pleasure*)?

> \[Str2364] † חוּשָׁה **n.pr.m.** a ‘son’ of חוּר \= place in Judah [1 Ch 4:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:4).

> \[Str2843] † חֻשָׁתִי **adj.gent.** of foregoing:—**1\.** of individ., c. art. ´הַח [2 S 21:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:18) \= [1 Ch 20:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:4), [2 S 23:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:27) \= [1 Ch 11:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:29), v. also [1 Ch 27:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:11).

> \[Str2365] † חוּשַׁי **n.pr.m. 1\.** הָאַרְכִּי ´ח [2 S 15:32](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:32) so [17:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:5), [17:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:14), called רֵעֶה דור [15:37](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:37) *friend of David*, so [16:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:16) a; cf. [16:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:16) b, [16:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:17), [16:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:18), [1 Ch 27:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:33); also [1 S 17:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:6), [17:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:7), [17:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:8), [17:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:15). **2\.** [1 K 4:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:16) father of one of Solomon's officers.

> \[Str2366] † I. חוּשִׁים, חֻשִׁים **n.pr.f.** wife of Shaharaim of Benjamin, חוּשִׁים [1 Ch 8:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:8), חֻשִׁים v:[11](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:11).

> \[Str2366] † II. חֻשִׁים **n.pr.m.** of Dan, ´וּבְנֵי דּן ח [Gn 46:23](https://ref.ly/Gen46:23) cf. חֻשִַׁם בְּנֵי אַחֵר [1 Ch 7:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:12) (v. Be VB); \= שׁוּחָם (q.v.) [Nu 26:42](https://ref.ly/Num26:42) cf. שׁוּחָתִי v:[42](https://ref.ly/Num26:42), v:[43](https://ref.ly/Num26:43).

> \[Str2367] † חֻשַָׁם, חוּשָׁם **n.pr.m.** a king of Edom: חֻשַָׁם [Gn 36:34](https://ref.ly/Gen36:34), [36:35](https://ref.ly/Gen36:35) \= חוּשָׁם [1 Ch 1:45](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:45), [1:46](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:46).

## I חָזָה (id: BDB02946)

\[Str2372] † I. חָזָה **vb.** (almost wholly poet.) see, behold (Aram. ܚܙܳܐ, חֲוָא, *see, perceive with the eyes;* Palm. Ethp. אתחזי \= ἔδοξεν ReckendorfZMG, 1888, 397; Ar. حَزَى, *perceive with the inner vision*, only; حَازٍ *astronomer, astrologer*)—**Qal** *Pf*. ´ח [Psalm 58:11](https://ref.ly/Ps58:11) \+ 6 t.; חָזִיתָ [Pr 22:29](https://ref.ly/Prov22:29) 29:30; חָזִית [Is 57:8](https://ref.ly/Isa57:8); חֶזִיתִי [Jb 15:17](https://ref.ly/Job15:17); חֲזִיתִךָ [Psalm 63:3](https://ref.ly/Ps63:3); שָחזוּ [Jb 24:1](https://ref.ly/Job24:1) \+ 5 t.; חֲוִיתֶם [Jb 27:12](https://ref.ly/Job27:12), Ez [13:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:7), [13:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:8); *Impf*. יֶחֱוֶה [Nu 24:4](https://ref.ly/Num24:4) \+ 2 t.; 3 fs. וְתַ֫חַז [Mi 4:11](https://ref.ly/Mic4:11); 2 ms. תֶּחֱוֶה [Ex 18:21](https://ref.ly/Exod18:21); 1 S. אֶחֱוֶה [Psalm 17:15](https://ref.ly/Ps17:15) \+ 4 t.; אָ֫חַז [Jb 23:9](https://ref.ly/Job23:9); pl. יֶחֱזוּ [Is 26:11](https://ref.ly/Isa26:11) \+ 4 t.; יֶחֱזָ\+יוּן [Is 26:11](https://ref.ly/Isa26:11); תֶּחְוֶ֫ינָה [Is 33:17](https://ref.ly/Isa33:17) \+ 2 t.; תֶּתֱזוּ [Is 30:10](https://ref.ly/Isa30:10), Ct [7:1](https://ref.ly/Song7:1); 1 pl. וְנֶחֱוֶה Ct [7:1](https://ref.ly/Song7:1); *Imv*. חֲוֵה [Is 33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20), [48:6](https://ref.ly/Isa48:6); חֲווּ [Psalm 46:9](https://ref.ly/Ps46:9), [Is 30:10](https://ref.ly/Isa30:10); *Inf. cstr*. חֲזוֺת [Psalm 27:4](https://ref.ly/Ps27:4), Ez [21:34](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:29); *Pt*. חֹוֶה Ez [12:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:27); pl. חֹזִים [Is 47:13](https://ref.ly/Isa47:13), Ez [13:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:9), [13:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:16), [22:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:28). (All other ptc. forms are used as nouns, v. חֹוֶה.) **1\.** *see, behold:* **a.** *with the eye*, acc. rei [Psalm 58:9](https://ref.ly/Ps58:9), [58:11](https://ref.ly/Ps58:11), [Pr 22:29](https://ref.ly/Prov22:29), [29:20](https://ref.ly/Prov29:20), [Is 33:20](https://ref.ly/Isa33:20), [57:8](https://ref.ly/Isa57:8); God in theophany [Ex 24:11](https://ref.ly/Exod24:11) (JE); God, after death, apart from the flesh [Jb 19:26](https://ref.ly/Job19:26), [19:27](https://ref.ly/Job19:27); the face of God [Psalm 11:7](https://ref.ly/Ps11:7), [17:15](https://ref.ly/Ps17:15) (after death). **b.** subj*. the eyes themselves;* man's eyes: תֶּחֱוֶינָה עֵינֶיךָ *thine eyes will behold* [Is 33:17](https://ref.ly/Isa33:17); also of God's eyes: [Psalm 11:4](https://ref.ly/Ps11:4), [17:2](https://ref.ly/Ps17:2). **c.** with בְּ *look on* (intensely, with gratification); a damsel Ct [7:1](https://ref.ly/Song7:1), [7:1](https://ref.ly/Song7:1); Zion [Mi 4:11](https://ref.ly/Mic4:11); ´נֹעַם י in the temple [Psalm 27:4](https://ref.ly/Ps27:4); the work of God [Jb 36:25](https://ref.ly/Job36:25); with investigation בַּכּוֺכָבִים on the stars, as stargazers, in astrology [Is 47:13](https://ref.ly/Isa47:13). **2\.** *see as a seer in the ecstatic state*, with acc. of the vision seen: חזון [Is 1:1](https://ref.ly/Isa1:1), Ez [12:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:27), [13:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:16); מחזה [Nu 24:4](https://ref.ly/Num24:4), [24:16](https://ref.ly/Num24:16) (JE; poet., Balaam); מחזה שׁוא Ez [13:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:7); משּא [Is 13:1](https://ref.ly/Isa13:1), Hb [1:1](https://ref.ly/Hab1:1); משאות שׁוא [La 2:14](https://ref.ly/Lam2:14); שׁוא Ez [13:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:6), [13:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:9), [13:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:23), [21:34](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:34), [22:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:28), [La 2:14](https://ref.ly/Lam2:14); כזב Ez [13:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:8); שֶׁקֶר [Zc 10:2](https://ref.ly/Zech10:2); obj. *deceitful things* [Is 30:10](https://ref.ly/Isa30:10); (יס)דבר [Is 2:1](https://ref.ly/Isa2:1), [Am 1:1](https://ref.ly/Amos1:1), [Mi 1:1](https://ref.ly/Mic1:1); with לְ *be a seer, for* any one [Is 30:10](https://ref.ly/Isa30:10); *see a vision for* one [La 2:14](https://ref.ly/Lam2:14), [2:14](https://ref.ly/Lam2:14). **3\.** *see, perceive*: **a.** with the intelligence, abs. [Jb 34:32](https://ref.ly/Job34:32), [Pr 24:32](https://ref.ly/Prov24:32); acc. rei [Is 48:6](https://ref.ly/Isa48:6); Yahweh in his temple [Psalm 63:3](https://ref.ly/Ps63:3); in his providential workings [Jb 23:9](https://ref.ly/Job23:9), [Psalm 46:9](https://ref.ly/Ps46:9), [Is 26:11](https://ref.ly/Isa26:11), [26:11](https://ref.ly/Isa26:11). **b.** *see*, by experience abs. [Jb 27:12](https://ref.ly/Job27:12); acc. rei [Jb 15:17](https://ref.ly/Job15:17), [24:1](https://ref.ly/Job24:1). **c.** *provide*, תחזה מכל העם אנשׁי חיל [Ex 18:21](https://ref.ly/Exod18:21) *provide from all the people men of ability* (E).

 *Note.*—בֵּית אֲבָנִים יֶתֱוֶה [Jb 8:17](https://ref.ly/Job8:17) is difficult: RV renders *he* (*it*) *beholdeth the place of stones* (the root of the plant personified) Hengst, cf. Reuss al. after SerVer Targum. Other renderings are: *pierceth* (cf. חָזַז) Bö Ew Hi (יְחַוֶּה), Di De MV; *between* (v. p. 108\) *the stones it liveth* (יִחְיֶה) LXX Siegf. 

> \[Str2335] חוָֺ\+זי [2 Ch 33:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:19), v. following.

> \[Str2374,2335] † חֹזֶה **n.m.** seer;—abs. ´ח [Am 7:12](https://ref.ly/Amos7:12) \+ 7 t.; cstr. חֹוֵה [1 Ch 21:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:9) \+ 3 t.; חוֺוֵה [2 Ch 35:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:15); pl. חֹוִים [Is 29:10](https://ref.ly/Isa29:10) \+ 4 t.; חוֺזִים [2 Ch 33:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:19) (so rd. with LXX; חוֺזָ\+י MT scribal error)—**1\.** *seer:* **a.** of unnamed persons, syn. (ים)נבא [2 K 17:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:13), [Is 29:10](https://ref.ly/Isa29:10); רֹאִים [Is 30:10](https://ref.ly/Isa30:10); קֹסְמִים [Mi 3:7](https://ref.ly/Mic3:7); historical writers דִּבְרֵי הַחֹוִים [2 Ch 33:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:18), דברי חזזים (LXX) [2 Ch 33:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:19) (MT appar. n. pr.) **b.** named prophets; Gad [2 S 24:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:11) \= [1 Ch 21:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:9), [1 Ch 29:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:29), [2 Ch 29:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:25); Amos [Am 7:12](https://ref.ly/Amos7:12); Iddo [2 Ch 9:29](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:29), [12:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr12:15); Jehu [2 Ch 19:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:2). **c.** the singers: Heman [1 Ch 25:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:5); Asaph [2 Ch 29:30](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:30); Jeduthun [2 Ch 35:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:15). *Note*—It seems best to add **2\.** *vision* (cf. רֹאֶה [Is 28:7](https://ref.ly/Isa28:7)) עִם שְׁאוֺל עָשִינוּ חֹוֶה [Is 28:15](https://ref.ly/Isa28:15) *with She’ôl we made a vision* (had a vision, in connexion with She'ôl—i.e. by necromancy, v. Du—which makes us secure; ‖ כרת ברית אֶת־מָוֶת; Ew§ 156 e trans. *oracle* here and [2 K 17:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:13); HoffmZAW iii. 95 puts this under **1:** ‘we have appointed a prophet with She’ôl (SS \&ld;Ein bei der Scheol be\-stellter Seher\&rd;), who is answerable to us for it;’ LXX Vulgate Targum AV RV Che Di al., *are at* (or *have made an) agreement* (prophetic advice of seers being taken at time of making treaties; v. חָזוּת **1**),—Vid. also כָּל־חֹזֶה **n.pr.m.** sub כּל.

> \[Str2377] † חָזוֺן **n.m.**:[Dn 8:1](https://ref.ly/Dan8:1) vision (on format. cf. LgBN 204\)—abs. ´ח [Ho 12:11](https://ref.ly/Hos12:11) \+ 25 t.; cstr. חֲזוֺן [Is 1:1](https://ref.ly/Isa1:1) \+ 8 t. **1\.** *vision*, as seen in the ecstatic state ‖קסם [Mi 3:6](https://ref.ly/Mic3:6); ‖מקסם Ez [12:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:24); ‖חלמות [Dn 1:17](https://ref.ly/Dan1:17); ‖נביא [Dn 9:24](https://ref.ly/Dan9:24); ´חזה ח Ez [12:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:27), [13:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:16); ´ראה ח [Dn 8:15](https://ref.ly/Dan8:15); ´ראה בח [Dn 8:2](https://ref.ly/Dan8:2), [8:2](https://ref.ly/Dan8:2), [9:21](https://ref.ly/Dan9:21); ´דבּר בח [Psalm 89:20](https://ref.ly/Ps89:20); חזון נראה אל [Dn 8:1](https://ref.ly/Dan8:1) *vision appeared unto;* ´מצא ח מיהוה [La 2:9](https://ref.ly/Lam2:9) *find a vision from Yahweh;* also Ez [12:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:22), [12:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:23), [Dn 8:13](https://ref.ly/Dan8:13), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Dan8:17), [8:26](https://ref.ly/Dan8:26), [10:14](https://ref.ly/Dan10:14), [11:14](https://ref.ly/Dan11:14). **2\.** *vision*, in the night חֲזוֺן לַיְלָה [Is 29:7](https://ref.ly/Isa29:7). **3\.** *divine communication in a vision, oracle, prophecy* ´בקשׁ ח מנביא *seek a vision (prophecy) from prophet* Ez [7:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:26); ‖(ים)דבר [1 S 3:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:1), [1 Ch 17:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:15); חֲווֺן לִבָּס יְדַבֵָּרוּ [Je 23:16](https://ref.ly/Jer23:16) *a prophecy of their own hearts (minds)* they speak; חֲזוֺן שֶׁקֶר וְקֶסֶם אֱלִיל וְתַרְמִית לִבָּם הֵמָּה מִתְנַבְּאִים [Je 14:14](https://ref.ly/Jer14:14) *false vision and* (v. אֱלִיל) *worthless divination \& deceit of their own hearts they are prophesying;* ´כתב ח *write the vision* Hb [2:2](https://ref.ly/Hab2:2); also [Ho 12:11](https://ref.ly/Hos12:11), Hb [2:3](https://ref.ly/Hab2:3), [Pr 29:18](https://ref.ly/Prov29:18), Ez [7:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:13) (del. Co). **4\.** *vision,* as title of book of prophecy, ספר חזון נחוּם [Na 1:1](https://ref.ly/Nah1:1); ישׁעיהו ´ח [Is 1:1](https://ref.ly/Isa1:1); עבדיה ´ח Ob:[1](https://ref.ly/Obad1:1); of other writings of prophets [2 Ch 32:32](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:32).

> \[Str2378] † \[חָזוֺת] **n.\[f.]**, only cstr. בַּחֲזוֺת יֶעְדּוֺ *in the visions* (prophecies) *of Iddo* [2 Ch 9:29](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:29), the title of a collection of prophetic history, v. חָזוֺן **4\.**

> \[Str2380] † חָזוּת **n.f.** vision, conspicuousness (on format. cf. LgBN 202\)—abs. ´ח [Is 21:2](https://ref.ly/Isa21:2), [Dn 8:5](https://ref.ly/Dan8:5), [8:8](https://ref.ly/Dan8:8); cstr. חָזוּת [Is 29:11](https://ref.ly/Isa29:11); sf. חָזוּחְכֶס [Is 28:18](https://ref.ly/Isa28:18); —**1\.** *vision, oracle of a prophet* חָווּת קָשָׁה הֻגַּד־לִי [Is 21:2](https://ref.ly/Isa21:2) *a hard vision is declared to me;* וַרְּהִי לָכֶם תָזוּת הַּכֹּל כְּדִבְרֵי הַסֵּפֶר הֶחָתוּם [Is 29:11](https://ref.ly/Isa29:11) *the vision* (prophecy) *of the whole is become to you as the words of a sealed writing;* חָזוּתְכֶם אֶת־שְׁאוֺל לאֹ תָקוּם [Is 28:18](https://ref.ly/Isa28:18) *your agreement with Sheol will not stand*, so Thes MV De Che Di al. after Vrss, but Ew§ 156 e renders *oracle*, and Siegf. *Hellseherei*, v. חֹוֶה **2\. 2\.** *conspicuousness in appearance:* קֶרֶן הָזוּת *a conspicuous horn* [Dn 8:5](https://ref.ly/Dan8:5) \= the great horn v:[8](https://ref.ly/Dan8:8), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Dan8:21); so חָזוּת אַרְבַּע [Dn 8:8](https://ref.ly/Dan8:8), but \< rd. אֲחֵרוֺת *others*, as LXX Gr Bev.

> \[Str2384] חִזָּיוֺן **n.m.**: [2 S 7:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:17) vision (on format. cf. LgBN 200\)—abs. ´ח [2 S 7:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:17), [Is 22:1](https://ref.ly/Isa22:1), [22:5](https://ref.ly/Isa22:5); cstr. חֶזְיוֺן [Jb 20:8](https://ref.ly/Job20:8), [33:15](https://ref.ly/Job33:15); sf. חֶוְיֹנוֺ [Zc 13:4](https://ref.ly/Zech13:4); pl. abs. חֶוְיֹנוֺת [Jb 7:14](https://ref.ly/Job7:14), [Jo 3:1](https://ref.ly/Joel3:1), cstr. *id*. [Jb 4:13](https://ref.ly/Job4:13); —**1\.** *vision*, in the ecstatic state ´ראה ח [Jo 3:1](https://ref.ly/Joel3:1); ´ח (א)גּי *valley of vision* [Is 22:1](https://ref.ly/Isa22:1), [22:5](https://ref.ly/Isa22:5). **2\.** *vision*, in the night לַיְלָה(וֺת)חֶזְיוֺנ [Jb 4:13](https://ref.ly/Job4:13), [20:8](https://ref.ly/Job20:8), [33:15](https://ref.ly/Job33:15); ‖חֲלֽמוֺת [Jb 7:14](https://ref.ly/Job7:14). **3\.** *divine communication in a vision, oracle, prophecy:* ‖דברים [2 S 7:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:17) ( \= חזון [1 Ch 17:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:15)); יֵבשׁוּ אִישׁ מֵחֶזְיֹנוֺ בְּהִנָּכְֽאֹתוֺ [Zc 13:4](https://ref.ly/Zech13:4) *they will be ashamed, every one of his vision when he prophesieth*.

> \[Str2383] † חֶזְיוֺן **n.pr.m.** (*vision*) ancestor of Benhadad king of Aram [1 K 15:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:18) (Klo prop. חֶזְרוֺן and comp. LXX [1 K 11:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:23) f.).

> \[Str2371] † חֲזָהאֵל **n.pr.m.** (*EĒl sees;* As. *Ḫaza'ilu* COTGloss) king of Aram [2 K 8:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:8), [8:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:13), [8:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:15), v:[29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:29) \= [2 Ch 22:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:6); contr. חֲזָאֵל 1 K [19:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:15), [19:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:17), [2 K 8:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:9), [8:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:12), v:[28](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:28) \= [2 Ch 22:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:5), [2 K 9:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:14), [9:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:15), [10:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:32), [12:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:18), [12:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:18), [12:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:19), [13:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:3), [13:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:3), [13:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:22), [13:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:24), [13:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:25); בֵּית חֲזָאֵל [Am 1:4](https://ref.ly/Amos1:4).

> \[Str2381] † חֲזִיאֵל **n.pr.m.** (*vision of EĒl*) Levite of the line of Gershon, of the time of Solomon [1 Ch 23:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:9).

> \[Str2382] † חֲזָיָה **n.pr.m.** (*Yah hath seen*) one of the chiefs of the בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה [Ne 11:5](https://ref.ly/Neh11:5).

> \[Str3166] † יַחֲזִיאֵל **n.pr.m.** (*EĒl sees*)—**1\.** Benjamite warrior of David [1 Ch 12:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:4). **2\.** priest of David [1 Ch 16:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:6). **3\.** a Levite [1 Ch 23:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:19) [24:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:23). **4\.** a Levite of the sons of Asaph [2 Ch 20:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:14). **5\.** ancestor of one of the families of the restoration [Ezr 8:5](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:5).

> \[Str3167] † יַחְזְיָה **n.pr.m.** (*Yah sees*) a prince of the restoration Ez [10:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:15).

> \[Str4236] † מַחֲזֶה **n.\[m.]** vision—abs. ´מ [Gn 15:1](https://ref.ly/Gen15:1); cstr. מַחֲזֵה [Nu 24:4](https://ref.ly/Num24:4), [24:16](https://ref.ly/Num24:16), Ez [13:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:7); —*vision, in the ecstatic state* מַצֲוֵה שָׁוְא (‖מִקְסַם כָּזָב) Ez [13:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:7) of the false prophets; מַחֲזֵה שַׁדַּי [Nu 24:4](https://ref.ly/Num24:4), [24:16](https://ref.ly/Num24:16) (JE; poems of Balaam); אֶל־אַבְרָם בַּמַּחֲזֶה ´הָיָה דְבַר י [Gn 15:1](https://ref.ly/Gen15:1) (R) *the word of ´י came unto Abram in a vision*.

> \[Str4237] † מֶחֱזָה **n.f.** light, place of seeing, window, מֶחֱזָה אֶל־מֶחֱזָה *light over against light* [1 K 7:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:4), [7:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:5).

> \[Str4238] † מַחֲזִיאֹת **n.pr.m.** (*visions*) [1 Ch 25:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:4) (but on this cf. RSOTJC 422; ed. 2\. 143\), \= מַהֲוִיאוֺת [1 Ch 25:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:30) a Hemanite chief of a course of singers.

## II חזה (id: BDB02962)

II. חזה (√ of foll.; Ar. حِذَاءٌ III., حَذَا *be opposite*, حِدَأٌ *front*, Lane537 b; v. LgBN 50 Dr§ 178, cf. Sab. חדַית prep. *opposite* DHM in MV).

> \[Str2373] † חָזֶה **n.m.**:[Lv 7:31](https://ref.ly/Lev7:31) breast of animals (Aram. ܚܰܕܝܳܐ, חַדְיָא)—abs. ´ח [Ex 29:26](https://ref.ly/Exod29:26) \+ 4 t.; cstr. חֲוֵה [Ex 29:27](https://ref.ly/Exod29:27) \+ 5 t.; pl. חָזוֺת [Lv 9:20](https://ref.ly/Lev9:20), [9:21](https://ref.ly/Lev9:21)—*breast*, only P, and only of sacrificial animals; always as wave\-offering; of ram of installation [Ex 29:26](https://ref.ly/Exod29:26), [29:27](https://ref.ly/Exod29:27), [Lv 8:29](https://ref.ly/Lev8:29), of peace\-offering [Lv 7:30](https://ref.ly/Lev7:30), [7:30](https://ref.ly/Lev7:30), [9:20](https://ref.ly/Lev9:20), [9:21](https://ref.ly/Lev9:21), of Nazirite sacrif. [Nu 6:20](https://ref.ly/Num6:20); perquisite of Aaron and his sons [Lv 7:31](https://ref.ly/Lev7:31), [7:34](https://ref.ly/Lev7:34), [10:14](https://ref.ly/Lev10:14), [10:15](https://ref.ly/Lev10:15), cf. [Nu 18:18](https://ref.ly/Num18:18).

> \[Str2375] † חֲזוֺ **n.pr.m.** son of Nahor [Gn 22:22](https://ref.ly/Gen22:22), LXX Αζαυ (cf. As. n.pr.loc. *Hazû*, mentioned with *Bazû* (v. III. בּוּז) by Esar. DlPa 307; ZK 1885, 93 f. COT on [Gn 22:21](https://ref.ly/Gen22:21); also Di).

## חָזַק (id: BDB02967)

\[Str2388] חָזַק:291 **vb.** be or grow firm, strong, strengthen (NH *id.;* Aram. ܚܙܰܩ חֲזַק *bind on* or *about, gird on,* cf. Ar. حَزَقَ *bind, squeeze*)—† **Qal** *Pf.* ´ח [Gn 41:57](https://ref.ly/Gen41:57) \+ 4 t.; חָזָקָ [2 Ch 26:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:15); sf. חֲזָקוֺ [2 Ch 28:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:20), etc.; *Impf.* יֶהֱ\+זק [2 Ch 28:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:7) (Baer) \+ 2 t.; וַיֶּחֱזַק [Gn 41:56](https://ref.ly/Gen41:56) \+; יֶחֶזְקוּ [2 S 10:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:11) \+ 2 t.; יֶחְזְקוּ [Is 28:22](https://ref.ly/Isa28:22); *Imv.* חֲוֵק [Dt 12:23](https://ref.ly/Deut12:23) \+; חִזְקוּ [Dt 31:6](https://ref.ly/Deut31:6) \+; *Inf. cstr.* לְחָזְקָה [2 K 12:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:13); sf. לְחָזְקָהּ Ez [30:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:21); *Pt.* חָזֵק [Ex 19:19](https://ref.ly/Exod19:19), [2 S 3:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:1), etc.;—I. intrans. *be* or *grow strong:*—**1\. a.** of physical strength of hands Ju [7:11](https://ref.ly/Judg7:11), [2 S 2:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:7), [16:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:21), Ez [22:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:14), [Zc 8:9](https://ref.ly/Zech8:9), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Zech8:13) (on Ez [3:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:14) v. infr.); of arm, sq. inf. Ez [30:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:21); used of recovery fr. illness [Is 39:1](https://ref.ly/Isa39:1); sq. מִן compar. *over power* [1 S 17:50](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:50) (c. בְּ instr.), [2 S 13:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:14). **b.** of people, army [Dt 11:8](https://ref.ly/Deut11:8), [Jos 17:13](https://ref.ly/Josh17:13) (JE), \= Ju [1:28](https://ref.ly/Judg1:28), [Ezr 9:12](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:12); sq. מִן compar. *be stronger than, too strong for, prevail against* [2 S 10:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:11), [10:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:11) \= [1 Ch 19:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:12), [19:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:12), [1 K 20:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:23), [20:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:25); *id.,* sq. acc. [1 K 16:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:22), cf. of ´י as prevailing over man [Je 20:7](https://ref.ly/Jer20:7) (‖יָכֹל); sq. על [2 Ch 8:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:3), [27:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr27:5). **c.** of royal power וְדָוִד הֹלֵךְ וְהָוֵק [2 S 3:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:1) (opp. הֹלְכִים וְדַלִּים); cf. [2 Ch 26:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:15) (‖חֶוְקָה v:[16](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:16)), [Dn 11:5a](https://ref.ly/Dan11:5). **d.** *prevail over, upon,* of word of king [2 S 24:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:4) (sq. אֶ־ל) \= [1 Ch 21:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:4) (sq. עַל). **e.** of bonds [Is 28:22](https://ref.ly/Isa28:22). **f.** of sound of trumpet הוֺלֵךְ וְהָוֵק מְאֹד [Ex 19:19](https://ref.ly/Exod19:19) (E, cf. חָזָק v:[16](https://ref.ly/Exod19:16)) it *grew much louder and louder.* **2\.** *be firm, fast:*—**a.** lit. *be caught fast,* of Absalom's head [2 S 18:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:9) (sq. בְּ). **b.** *be firm, secure,* of a kingdom [2 K 14:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:5) ( \+ בְּיָדוֺ) \= [2 Ch 25:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:3) ( \+ עָלָיו). Oftener **c.** *be firm* \= courageous, confident, esp. Imv., usually ‖ אמץ (q.v.), [Dt 31:6](https://ref.ly/Deut31:6), [31:7](https://ref.ly/Deut31:7), [31:23](https://ref.ly/Deut31:23), [Jos 1:6](https://ref.ly/Josh1:6), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Josh1:7), [1:9](https://ref.ly/Josh1:9), [1:18](https://ref.ly/Josh1:18), [10:25](https://ref.ly/Josh10:25), [23:6](https://ref.ly/Josh23:6) (all D), [2 S 10:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:12), [13:28](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:28), [1 K 2:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:2), [1 Ch 19:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:13) (‖התחזק), [22:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:13), [28:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:10), [28:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:20), [2 Ch 15:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:7), [19:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:11), [32:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:7), [Ezr 10:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:4), [Psalm 27:14](https://ref.ly/Ps27:14), [31:25](https://ref.ly/Ps31:25), [Is 35:4](https://ref.ly/Isa35:4), [41:6](https://ref.ly/Isa41:6), [Hg 2:4](https://ref.ly/Hag2:4), [2:4](https://ref.ly/Hag2:4), [2:4](https://ref.ly/Hag2:4), [Dn 10:19](https://ref.ly/Dan10:19), [10:19](https://ref.ly/Dan10:19); sq. לַמִּלְחָמָה [2 Ch 25:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:8). **d.** *hold firmly to, devote oneself to,* sq. בְּ [2 Ch 31:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:4). **e.** sq. Inf. *be firm* not to eat blood, i.e. *firmly refrain* from it [Dt 12:23](https://ref.ly/Deut12:23), cf. [1 Ch 28:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:7). **3\.** *press, be urgent,* sq. עַל \+ Inf. [Ex 12:33](https://ref.ly/Exod12:33) (E); sq. עַל of hand of ´י in prophetic ecstasy Ez [3:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:14) (LXX Co rd. חֲזָקָה, adj., v. חָזָק). **4\.** in bad sense: **a.** *grow stout, rigid, hard,* with idea of perversity, of Pharaoh's heart [Ex 7:13](https://ref.ly/Exod7:13), [7:22](https://ref.ly/Exod7:22), [8:15](https://ref.ly/Exod8:15) (all P), [9:35](https://ref.ly/Exod9:35) (R); cf. חזקו עָלַי דבריכם [Mal 3:13](https://ref.ly/Mal3:13). **b.** *be severe, grievous,* of battle [2 K 3:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:26); of famine [Gn 41:56](https://ref.ly/Gen41:56), [41:57](https://ref.ly/Gen41:57) (E), [47:20](https://ref.ly/Gen47:20) (J; sq. על), [2 K 25:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:3) \= [Je 52:6](https://ref.ly/Jer52:6). **II.** transit. only לאֹ חֲזֶקוֺ [2 Ch 28:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:20) *strengthened him not,* but txt. very dub. (‖וַיָצַר לוֺ). † **Pi.** *Pf.* חִזַּק [Psalm 147:13](https://ref.ly/Ps147:13) \+, etc.; *Impf.* וַיְתַזֵּק [Ex 9:12](https://ref.ly/Exod9:12) \+ 10 t., etc.; *Imv.* חַוֵּק [Ne 6:9](https://ref.ly/Neh6:9) \+ 2 t.; fs. חַזְּקִי [Na 3:14](https://ref.ly/Nah3:14); חֵַ\+זּקִי [Is 54:2](https://ref.ly/Isa54:2) etc.; *Inf. cstr.* חַוֵּק [2 K 12:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:9) \+ 12 t.; *Pt.* מְחַזֵּק [Ex 14:17](https://ref.ly/Exod14:17); מְחַזְּקִים [2 K 12:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:9); —**1\.** *make strong* (physically): **a.** sq. acc. Pers. Ju [16:28](https://ref.ly/Judg16:28); sq. על [3:12](https://ref.ly/Judg3:12); sq. מָתְנַיִם [Na 2:2](https://ref.ly/Nah2:2) (‖אַמֵּץ כֹּחַ; of personif. people). **b.** \= *restore to strength* [Dn 10:18](https://ref.ly/Dan10:18), [10:19](https://ref.ly/Dan10:19); *to health* Ez [34:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:4), [34:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:16); c. לְ *give strength to* [1 Ch 29:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:12); so, sq. זְרוֺעֹתָם, [Ho 7:15](https://ref.ly/Hos7:15), Ez [30:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:24) (and v:[25](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:25) Co, v. **Hiph.**) **c.** sq. acc. rei [Is 41:7](https://ref.ly/Isa41:7), [Je 10:4](https://ref.ly/Jer10:4) (both c. בְּ instr.; of manufacture of idols); of strengthening tent\-pegs, in metaph. [Is 54:4](https://ref.ly/Isa54:4), cf. ## חזר (id: BDB02978)

חזר (√ of foll.; meaning dub.; perh. cf. Ar. خَزَرَ *the eye was* or *became narrow, small,* Lane731 c; hence *narrow\-eyed* animal).

> \[Str2386] † חֲזִיר **n.m.**:[Psalm 80:14](https://ref.ly/Ps80:14) swine, boar (NH *id.;* Aram. חֲוִירָא, ܚܙܺܝܪܳܐ Ar. خِنْزِيرٌ (Aram. Loan\-wd. acc. to Frä110, yet v. LagBN 113\); Eth. ኀንዚር፡: (only once; \= wild boar), v. HomNS 319 f. 358, 385\)—alw. st. abs., 4 t. c. art. ´הַח;—**1\.** *swine,* forbidden as food [Lv 11:7](https://ref.ly/Lev11:7) (P), [Dt 14:8](https://ref.ly/Deut14:8), cf. ´בְּשַר הַח [Is 65:4](https://ref.ly/Isa65:4), [66:17](https://ref.ly/Isa66:17), and ´דַּס־ח [66:3](https://ref.ly/Isa66:3) as heathen offering (RS Sem. 1, 272, 325, 338, 392\); with implication of repul siveness ´נֶזֶם זָהָב בְּאַף ח [Pr 11:22](https://ref.ly/Prov11:22) sim. of fair woman with dub. character. **2\.** *wild boar* מִָ\+יּעַר ´ח [Psalm 80:14](https://ref.ly/Ps80:14) fig. of foes of Isr. (in fig. of vineyard).

> \[Str2387] † חֵזִיר **n.pr.m.** (cf. inscr. in Ra1867, pl. 7, DrSm xxiii; NöZMG 1886, 162 ‘pointing —ֵ to avoid offence;’ but NbrAcad. 1887, Dec. 17 comp. Talm. \& Targum (חַזּוּרָא, חֵיזוּר, חֲזוּרָא etc.) *pomegranate* (and *apple;* Syr. ܚܰܙܘܽܪ, ܚܙܪܘܐ), cf. בְּנֵי רִמּוֺן [2 S 4:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:2), [4:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:5), [4:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:9))—**1\.** a priest of 17th course (assigned to David's time) [1 Ch 24:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:15); LXX Ξηξειν (16th, v:[14](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:14)), but A Ιεξειρ, LXXL Ξηξειρ. **2\.** one of those sealed [Ne 10:21](https://ref.ly/Neh10:21); LXX Ηξειρ, LXXL Αξηρ.

> \[Str274,3170] † יַחְזֵרָה **n.pr.m.** [1 Ch 9:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:12) cf. אֲחַוְיָהוּ p. 28 b. p. 403

God, abs. [Is 31:7](https://ref.ly/Isa31:7), [Ho 12:9](https://ref.ly/Hos12:9); חָטָא ´ח [La 1:8](https://ref.ly/Lam1:8) cf. [Dt 19:15](https://ref.ly/Deut19:15), [2 K 10:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:29); משׁפט מות ´ח *sin* worthy of death [Dt 21:22](https://ref.ly/Deut21:22) \= מות ´ח [22:26](https://ref.ly/Deut22:26); with בְּ, *because of,* [Dn 9:16](https://ref.ly/Dan9:16); God is entreated: *hide thy face from my sins* [Psalm 51:11](https://ref.ly/Ps51:11); and it is said in faith, *thou wilt cast behind thy back all my sins* [Is 38:17](https://ref.ly/Isa38:17). **2\.** *guilt of sin:* היה חטא ב *sin* (the guilt of sin) *come on one* [Dt 15:9](https://ref.ly/Deut15:9), [21:22](https://ref.ly/Deut21:22), [23:22](https://ref.ly/Deut23:22), [23:23](https://ref.ly/Deut23:23), [24:15](https://ref.ly/Deut24:15); בחטאו מת *in his sin* (guilt) *die* [Nu 27:3](https://ref.ly/Num27:3) (P); אישׁ בחטאו *each for his own sin* [Dt 24:16](https://ref.ly/Deut24:16) \= [2 K 14:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:16) \= [2 Ch 25:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:4); בחטא יחמתני אמי [Psalm 51:7](https://ref.ly/Ps51:7) *in sin* (condition of sin and guilt) *my mother conceived me*; אם חטאיכם כשׁנים [Is 1:18](https://ref.ly/Isa1:18) *though your sins be like scarlet* (in guilt); לא כחטאינו עשה לנו [Psalm 103:10](https://ref.ly/Ps103:10) *not according to our sins* (their guilt) *hath he done to us*. **3\.** *punishment for sin:* ´נשא ח *bear sin* (its punishment) [Lv 20:20](https://ref.ly/Lev20:20), [24:15](https://ref.ly/Lev24:15) (H), [Nu 9:13](https://ref.ly/Num9:13), [18:22](https://ref.ly/Num18:22) (P); with עַל, *bear sin because of* [Lv 19:17](https://ref.ly/Lev19:17), [22:9](https://ref.ly/Lev22:9) (H) [Nu 18:32](https://ref.ly/Num18:32) (P); bear sins of idols (in worshipping idols) Ez [23:49](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:49); the ideal servant of Yahweh *bore the sins of many* [Is 53:12](https://ref.ly/Isa53:12); without נשא, only [La 3:39](https://ref.ly/Lam3:39) גבר על חטאו (wherefore doth) *a man* (complain) *for the punishment of his sin ?*

Sin\-offering elsewh. only in codes of H Ez and P.—On [Ho 4:8](https://ref.ly/Hos4:8) v. **1\.** supr. In H, [Lv 23:19](https://ref.ly/Lev23:19) a שעיר עזים, for feast of weeks. In Ez ´ח in gen. ‖ other sacrifices, Ez [40:39](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:39), [45:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:17), [45:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:25), [46:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:20); and priests eat them Ez [42:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:13), [44:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:29). At dedication of altar, the blood of a young bullock was to be applied to horns of altar, the corners of its settle and its border, the bullock itself burnt without the sanctuary; for each of the 7 days following a שעיר עזים was to be offered Ez [43:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:19), [43:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:21), [43:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:22), [43:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:25). On first day of first month a young bullock was to offered to cleanse the sanctuary: its blood put on the door posts of the house and of the gate of the inner court an on the four corners of the settle of the altar; also on the seventh day (LXX first day of seventh month) Ez [45:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:17), [45:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:19) (cf. v:[18](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:18), v:[20](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:20)). On 14th day of first month the prince was to offer a bullock for himself and the people and on each of the 7 days of the Passover week a שעיר עזים Ez [45:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:22), [45:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:23). When a priest entered the sanctuary to minister after his cleaning he was to offer his sin\-offering Ez [44:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:27). P gives several grades: **a.** a ruler should offer a he\-goat [Lv 4:24](https://ref.ly/Lev4:24), [4:25](https://ref.ly/Lev4:25), [Nu 7:16](https://ref.ly/Num7:16), [7:22](https://ref.ly/Num7:22), [7:28](https://ref.ly/Num7:28), [7:34](https://ref.ly/Num7:34), [7:40](https://ref.ly/Num7:40), [7:46](https://ref.ly/Num7:46), [7:52](https://ref.ly/Num7:52), [7:58](https://ref.ly/Num7:58), [7:64](https://ref.ly/Num7:64), [7:70](https://ref.ly/Num7:70), [7:76](https://ref.ly/Num7:76), [7:82](https://ref.ly/Num7:82), [7:87](https://ref.ly/Num7:87); an ordinary person a she\-goat [Lv 4:29](https://ref.ly/Lev4:29) (cf. v:[28](https://ref.ly/Lev4:28)) [5:6](https://ref.ly/Lev5:6), [Nu 15:27](https://ref.ly/Num15:27), ewe lamb [Lv 4:32](https://ref.ly/Lev4:32), [4:33](https://ref.ly/Lev4:33), [4:33](https://ref.ly/Lev4:33), [4:34](https://ref.ly/Lev4:34), [14:19](https://ref.ly/Lev14:19), [Nu 6:14](https://ref.ly/Num6:14), [6:16](https://ref.ly/Num6:16), a turtle dove or young pigeon [Lv 5:7](https://ref.ly/Lev5:7), [5:8](https://ref.ly/Lev5:8), [5:9](https://ref.ly/Lev5:9), [5:9](https://ref.ly/Lev5:9), [12:6](https://ref.ly/Lev12:6), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Lev12:8), [14:22](https://ref.ly/Lev14:22), [14:31](https://ref.ly/Lev14:31), [15:15](https://ref.ly/Lev15:15), [15:30](https://ref.ly/Lev15:30), [Nu 6:11](https://ref.ly/Num6:11), or one\-tenth of an ephah of fine flour [Lv 5:11](https://ref.ly/Lev5:11), [5:11](https://ref.ly/Lev5:11), [5:12](https://ref.ly/Lev5:12); according to ability of the person, and nature of offence. The victim was brought unto the tent of meeting, the hands of the offerer laid on its head, it was slaughtered by the offerer, the priest took some of the blood and put it on horns of altar of burnt\-offering and the rest of the blood he poured out at the base of it, to cover over the sin, or cleanse the altar from the sin defiling it. All the fat pieces (cf. [Lv 4:22](https://ref.ly/Lev4:22), [4:35](https://ref.ly/Lev4:35)) and a handful of the flour (cf. [Lv 5:12](https://ref.ly/Lev5:12)) were burned on altar. The rest of the flesh and the flour were eaten by priests in court of Holy place [Lv 5:13](https://ref.ly/Lev5:13), [6:10](https://ref.ly/Lev6:10), [6:18](https://ref.ly/Lev6:18), [6:18](https://ref.ly/Lev6:18), [6:23](https://ref.ly/Lev6:23) (cf. v:[11](https://ref.ly/Lev6:11), v:[19](https://ref.ly/Lev6:19)), [7:7](https://ref.ly/Lev7:7), [10:16](https://ref.ly/Lev10:16), [10:17](https://ref.ly/Lev10:17), [10:19](https://ref.ly/Lev10:19), [10:19](https://ref.ly/Lev10:19), [14:13](https://ref.ly/Lev14:13), [Nu 18:9](https://ref.ly/Num18:9). **b.** a bullock was offered for priests [Ex 29:14](https://ref.ly/Exod29:14), [29:36](https://ref.ly/Exod29:36), [Lv 4:3](https://ref.ly/Lev4:3), [4:8](https://ref.ly/Lev4:8), [4:20](https://ref.ly/Lev4:20), [8:2](https://ref.ly/Lev8:2), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Lev8:14), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Lev8:14), [9:2](https://ref.ly/Lev9:2), [9:7](https://ref.ly/Lev9:7), [9:8](https://ref.ly/Lev9:8), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Lev9:10); for Levites at their installation [Nu 8:8](https://ref.ly/Num8:8), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Num8:12) and for whole congregation [Lv 4:14](https://ref.ly/Lev4:14), [4:21](https://ref.ly/Lev4:21); but usu. offering for congregation was he\-goat [Nu 15:24](https://ref.ly/Num15:24), [15:25](https://ref.ly/Num15:25), esp. at dedication of altar [Lv 9:3](https://ref.ly/Lev9:3), [9:15](https://ref.ly/Lev9:15), [9:22](https://ref.ly/Lev9:22). and in ritual of feasts [Nu 28:15](https://ref.ly/Num28:15), [28:22](https://ref.ly/Num28:22), [28:30](https://ref.ly/Num28:30), [29:5](https://ref.ly/Num29:5), [29:11](https://ref.ly/Num29:11), [29:16](https://ref.ly/Num29:16), [29:19](https://ref.ly/Num29:19), [29:22](https://ref.ly/Num29:22), [29:25](https://ref.ly/Num29:25), [29:28](https://ref.ly/Num29:28), [29:31](https://ref.ly/Num29:31), [29:34](https://ref.ly/Num29:34), [29:38](https://ref.ly/Num29:38). Before consecr. of the tabernacle the blood went to altar of burnt\-offering (Lv [9:9](https://ref.ly/Lev9:9)), but subsequ. some of it was sprinkled seven times before the vail and some of it put on horns of altar of incense to cleanse this higher altar; the fast pieces were burned on altar of burnt\-offering, and the rest of the victim burned without the camp ([Lv 4:3–21](https://ref.ly/Lev4:3-Lev4:21), [6:23](https://ref.ly/Lev6:23), [8:16](https://ref.ly/Lev8:16), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Lev8:17), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Lev9:10), [9:11](https://ref.ly/Lev9:11)). **c.** on day of atonement, sin\-offering for high priest was a bullock [Lv 16:3](https://ref.ly/Lev16:3), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Lev16:6); and for congregation two he\-goats, one לַעֲזָאזֵל (v. עזאזל) [Lv 16:5](https://ref.ly/Lev16:5) (cf. v:[8](https://ref.ly/Lev16:8), v:[10](https://ref.ly/Lev16:10), v:[20](https://ref.ly/Lev16:20), v:[22](https://ref.ly/Lev16:22)), the other for sacrifice ליהוה [Lv 16:9](https://ref.ly/Lev16:9) (cf. v:[8](https://ref.ly/Lev16:8)). Some of the blood of Aaron's bullock was first taken into innermost sanctuary and sprinkled on the כפרת and seven times before it; so also blood of the he\-goat [Lv 16:11](https://ref.ly/Lev16:11), [16:11](https://ref.ly/Lev16:11), [16:15](https://ref.ly/Lev16:15) (cf. v:[12–14](https://ref.ly/Lev16:12-Lev16:14), v:[16](https://ref.ly/Lev16:16), v:[17](https://ref.ly/Lev16:17)) to cleanse highest altar: then the blood of the two victims was applied to lower altars as in **a** and **b** and so sin was covered over at the three altars [Ex 30:10](https://ref.ly/Exod30:10) (cf. [Lv 16:16–19](https://ref.ly/Lev16:16-Lev16:19)). The fat pieces went to altar of burnt\-offering and the rest of the victims was burnt without the camp [Lv 16:25](https://ref.ly/Lev16:25), [16:27](https://ref.ly/Lev16:27), [16:27](https://ref.ly/Lev16:27). (Sins which might be covered over were limited to those committed בשׁגגה [Lv 4:2](https://ref.ly/Lev4:2), [4:22](https://ref.ly/Lev4:22), [4:27](https://ref.ly/Lev4:27), [Nu 15:27](https://ref.ly/Num15:27), לשׁגגה [Nu 15:24](https://ref.ly/Num15:24), minor offences [Lv 5:1–6](https://ref.ly/Lev5:1-Lev5:6); and ceremonial uncleanness [Lv 12:6](https://ref.ly/Lev12:6), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Lev12:8), [14:13](https://ref.ly/Lev14:13), [15:15](https://ref.ly/Lev15:15), [Nu 6:11](https://ref.ly/Num6:11), [6:14](https://ref.ly/Num6:14).)—Pharses for sacrificing are: ´עשה ח [Lv 9:7](https://ref.ly/Lev9:7), [9:22](https://ref.ly/Lev9:22), [14:19](https://ref.ly/Lev14:19), [Nu 6:16](https://ref.ly/Num6:16), Ez [45:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:17), ´הקריב ח [Lv 10:19](https://ref.ly/Lev10:19), Ez [44:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:27), ´שׁחט ח [Lv 14:13](https://ref.ly/Lev14:13), Ez [40:39](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:39). **5\.** *purification* from sins of ceremonial uncleanness, all P: ´ח ´מֵ [Nu 8:7](https://ref.ly/Num8:7) *water of purification from sin;* הוּא ´נִדָּה ח ´מֵ *water of (cleansing from) impurity, it is a purification from sin* [Nu 19:9](https://ref.ly/Num19:9); ´עֲפַר שְרֵפַת הח [Nu 19:17](https://ref.ly/Num19:17) *ashes of the burning of the purification from sin* (RV renders *sin\-offering;* but there was no offering made, only water of purification was used).

## I חָטַב (id: BDB02989)

\[Str2404] † I. \[חָטַב] **vb.** cut or gather wood, usu. *firewood* (NH *id.;* Ar. حَطَبٌ *firewood*, حَطَبَ *collect firewood* (v. Wetzst in De:[Psalm 144:12](https://ref.ly/Ps144:12)); Eth. ሐጠበ፡ *cut* (or gather) *firewood*)—**Qal** *Impf.* יַחְטְבוּ Ez [39:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:10); *Inf.* לַחְטֹב [Dt 19:5](https://ref.ly/Deut19:5); *Pt.* חֹטֵב [Dt 29:10](https://ref.ly/Deut29:10); pl. חֹטְבִים [2 Ch 2:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:9); cstr. חֹטְבֵי [Jos 9:21](https://ref.ly/Josh9:21) \+ 3 t.; pass. pl. f. חֲטֻבוֺת [Pr 7:16](https://ref.ly/Prov7:16)—*cut or gather* wood, sq. acc. [Dt 19:5](https://ref.ly/Deut19:5), [29:10](https://ref.ly/Deut29:10) (‖שׁאב מים); cf. Ez [39:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:10) מן־היערים (obj. in preceding cl.); [2 Ch 2:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:9) (‖כֹּרְתֵי הָעֵצִים); *Pt.* cstr. עצים ´ח [Je 46:22](https://ref.ly/Jer46:22), and (‖שֹׁאֲבֵי מַיִם) [Jos 9:21](https://ref.ly/Josh9:21), [9:23](https://ref.ly/Josh9:23), [9:27](https://ref.ly/Josh9:27). **Pu.** *Pt.* מְחֻטָּבוֺת [Psalm 144:12](https://ref.ly/Ps144:12) meaning dub.; perhaps *hewn* (so, in substance, Thes MV SS Ew Che Bae; v. however II. חטב), of corner\-pillars (זָוִיּוֺת v. זָוִית p. 265 supr.), sim. of beautiful, stately daughters.

* **Associated Passages:** Joshua 9:23; 2 Chronicles 2:9; Deuteronomy 19:5; Deuteronomy 29:10; Ezekiel 39:10; Joshua 9:21; Psalms 144:12; Proverbs 7:16; Joshua 9:27; Jeremiah 46:22


## II חטב (id: BDB02990)

II. חטב (√of foll.; cf. Ar. خَطِبَ be of the colour خُطْبَةٌ, i.e. *turbid, dusky, mixed with yellowish red*, etc.; Aram. *Pt.* ܡܚܰܛܒܬܳܐ *variegated* SyrVer [2 S 13:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:19)).

> \[Str2405] † חֲטֻבוֺת **n.f.pl.** dark\-hued stuffs, only in חֲטֻםבוֺת אֵטוּן מִצְרַיִם [Pr 7:16](https://ref.ly/Prov7:16) *dark\-hued stuffs* (of) *yarn of Egypt* (‖מַרְבַדִּים), v. De Now; מְחֻטָּבוֺת [Psalm 144:12](https://ref.ly/Ps144:12) der. fr. this √ by De (q.v., and Wetzt.'s n.), but v. I. חטב.

> \[Str2406] חִטָּה *wheat*, v. sub חנט. p. 334

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 144:12; Proverbs 7:16; 2 Samuel 13:19
* **Associated Articles:** II חטב (ID: `BDB02990@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## חטט (id: BDB02993)

חטט (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. خَطَّ *make lines, marks*, خَطٌّ *line, streak, stripe;* Aram. ܚܰܛ, חֲטַט *dig*, ܚܛܘܽܛܳܐ *furrow, trench*).

> \[Str2410] † חֲטִיטָא **n.pr.m.** head of a Levit. family, returned exiles [Ezr 2:42](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:42) LXX Ατητα, \= [Ne 7:45](https://ref.ly/Neh7:45) LXX Ατειτα; in both A Ατιτα, LXXL Αζιζα.

* **Associated Passages:** Nehemiah 7:45; Ezra 2:42


## חטל (id: BDB02995)

חטל (√of foll.; Ar. خَطِلَ \= *be flabby* (of ear), *be tall, long, quivering, be light, quick*).

> \[Str2411] † חַטִּיל **n.pr.m.** head of a family of ‘the children of Sol.'s servants’ [Ezr 2:57](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:57) LXX Αττεια, A Αττιλ \= [Ne 7:59](https://ref.ly/Neh7:59) LXX Εγηλ, A Εττηλ, LXXL Αττιλ in both.

* **Associated Passages:** Nehemiah 7:59; Ezra 2:57


## חָטַם (id: BDB02997)

\[Str2413] † חָטַם **vb.** hold in, restrain (Ar. خَطَمَ *strike the nose, attach the* خِطَامٌ \= *camel\-halter*, خَطْمٌ n. *muzzle, nose of beast;* NH חֲטָם *nose\-ring of camel*, חוֺטָם; Aram. חוּטְמָא *nose;* cf. חטם, Inscr. Zinj.; acc. to DHMSendsch. 34, of taming wild beasts)—**Qal** *Impf.* אֶחֱטָם־לָךְ :לְבִלְתִּי הַכְרִיתֶֽךָ [Is 48:9](https://ref.ly/Isa48:9) *I will restrain for thee* (mine anger), *not to cut thee off* (‖אַאֲרִיח אַפִּי).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 48:9


## חָטַף (id: BDB02998)

\[Str2414] † חָטַף **vb.** catch, seize (Ar. خَطِفَ; cf. As. *taḫṭîpu, oppression*, DlPr 181; NH חָטַף, Aram. ܚܛܰܦ *seize*, חֲטַף *do hurriedly*)—**Qal** *Pf.* וַחֲטַפְתֶּם consec. Ju [21:21](https://ref.ly/Judg21:21) lit., *seize* wives; *Impf.* יַחֲטֹף עָנִי [Psalm 10:9](https://ref.ly/Ps10:9); *Inf.* לַחֲטוֺף עָנִי [Psalm 10:9](https://ref.ly/Ps10:9) both fig. of oppressor *catching* the distressed; all sq. acc.

> \[Str2412] † חֲטִיפָא **n.pr.m.** head of a family of Nethinim, post\-exil., [Ezr 2:54](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:54) LXX Ατουφα, A Ατιφα \= [Ne 7:56](https://ref.ly/Neh7:56) LXX Ατειφα; LXXL both Ατουφα.

## חטר (id: BDB03000)

> \[Str2416] † II. \[חַי] **n.\[m.]** kinsfolk (Ar. حَيَّ a group of families united by vital ties RS: K 36–40 Dr: Sm 119\), pl. sf. חַיַּי [1 S 18:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:18), incorrectly pointed with the interpretation *my life* RV, but rd. חַיִּי *my kins\-flok*, We Ki Dr RS SS RVm VB. It is explained by the gloss משׁפחת אבי We (Klo rds. אַחַי וּ).

> \[Str2416] † II. חַיָּה **n.f.** (si veral.) community ( \= fem. of II. חַי acc. to Nö: ZMG, 1886, 176\)—וְחַיַּת פְּלִשְׁתִּים חֹנָה *and a community of Philistines* (i.e. a group of allied families, making a raid together) *was encamping* [2 S 23:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:13) ( \= מַחֲנֵה [1 Ch 11:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:15)); perh. also [Psalm 68:11](https://ref.ly/Ps68:11) (cf. infr.)
> 
>  *Note.*—Several other passages are dub.: וַיֵּאָסְפוּ פְלִשְׁתִּים לַחַיָּה [2 S 23:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:11) *assembled into a troop*, or *by bands* (RV, but neither rend. juatif.: Bö Ew We Klo Dr rightly rd. לֶ֫חְיָה to *Leḥi*, cf. Ju [15:9](https://ref.ly/Judg15:9) wd. om. by accident in ‖ [1 Ch 11:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:13) Dr); חַיָּתְֽךָ יָֽשְׁבוּ־בָהּ [Psalm 68:11](https://ref.ly/Ps68:11) *thy troop dwelt in it* (Thes SS RVm Hi Ri Che, but LXX SyrVer Jer Pe *thy* (*living*) *creatures*, of the people; Hup *id.,* ref. to quails; improb.); אַל־תִּתֵּן לְחַיַּת נֶפֶשׁ תּוֺרֶךָ חַיַּת עֲנִיֶּיךָ אַל־תִּשְׁלכּח לָנֶ֫צַח [Psalm 74:19](https://ref.ly/Ps74:19) *give not the soul of thy turtle\-dove to the wild beasts, the life of thine afflicted do not forget for ever:* RV Hi Pe Bae Sch render thus, giving חית diff. meanings in the two clauses, the former being archaic fem. abs. Ges§ 80, 2\. R. 2; text doubtless corrupt, rd. poss. למות Schr Ri, for לחית, or לשׁחת Kroch; Gr either of these; Che either, or better לחרב.

> \[Str2422] † \[חָיֶה] **adj.** having the vigour of life, lively (on format. v. LagBN 49\)—fpl. חָיוֺת [Ex 1:19](https://ref.ly/Exod1:19) (E), of Hebrew women in childbirth, bearing quickly easily.

> \[Str2424] † חַיּוּת **n.f.abstr.** אַלְמְנוּת חַיּוּת [2 S 20:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:3), lit. ‘widowhood of livingness,’ Dr; We rds. אַלְמָנוֺת חַיּוֺת *living widows* (‘grass\-widows,’ who were separated from their husbands), after LXX χῆραι ζῶσαι, so SS.

> \[Str4241] † מִחְיָה **n.f.** preservation of life, sustenance, ´מ [Gn 45:5](https://ref.ly/Gen45:5) \+ 4 t.; cstr. מִחְיַת [Lv 13:10](https://ref.ly/Lev13:10), [13:24](https://ref.ly/Lev13:24); sf. מִחְיָתֶָךָ Ju [17:10](https://ref.ly/Judg17:10); —**1\.** *preservation of life* [Gn 45:5](https://ref.ly/Gen45:5) (E), [2 Ch 14:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:12). **2\.** *sustenance* Ju [6:4](https://ref.ly/Judg6:4), [17:10](https://ref.ly/Judg17:10). **3\.** *reviving*, [Ezr 9:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:8), [9:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:9). **4\.** *the quick* (בָּשָר) מִחְיַת *the quick* (or raw spot) *of the flesh* [Lv 13:10](https://ref.ly/Lev13:10), [13:24](https://ref.ly/Lev13:24) (P).

> \[Str2419] † חִיאֵל **n.pr.m.** *(Ēl lives* (or abbrev. for יחיאל); Sab. חיאל, name of coin, after n.pr. of king DHMZMG 1875, 613, 1883, 15; cp. also foll., and Ph. מריחי, כמשיחי)—name of the rebuilder of Jericho [1 K 16:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:34); LXX Αχ(ε)ιηλ, om. LXXL. p. 27

> \[Str3171] † יְחִיאֵל **n.pr.m.**(*may Ēl live;* Ph. יחואלן, יחומלך v. EutZMG 1876, 136\)—LXX usu. Ιειηλ, Ιαιηλ;—**1\.** one of David's chief musicians [1 Ch 15:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:18) (LXX Ιειηλ, LXXL Ιαιηλ), v:[20](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:20) (LXX Ειθηλ, LXXL Ιειηλ), [16:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:5) (LXX Ειειηλ, LXXL Ιαιηλ). **2\.** one of David's chiefs of the Levites [1 Ch 23:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:8) (LXX Ι(ει)ηλ), [29:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:8) (LXX Βεσιηλ, A LXXL Ιειηλ). **3\.** tutor of David's sons [1 Ch 27:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:32) (LXX Ιε(ρι)ηλ, LXXL Ιωηλ). **4\.** son of Jehoshaphat [2 Ch 21:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:2). **5\.** יְחִו֯אֵל Hemanite of Hezekiah's reign [2 Ch 29:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:14). **6\.** over\-seer in Hezekiah's reign [2 Ch 31:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:13). **7\.** ruler of the temple in Josiah's reign [2 Ch 35:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:8). **8\.** contemporaries of Ezra, [Ezr 8:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:9) (LXX Ιεμα A Ιεειηλ, LXXL Ιειηλ), [10:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:2), [10:21](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:21), [10:6](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:6).

> \[Str3172] † יְחִיאֵלִי **n.pr.m.** (patronym.), [1 Ch 26:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:21), [26:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:22) (LXX Ιειηλ̀ LXXL om. in v:[22](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:22)).

> \[Str3174] † יְחִיָּה **n.pr.m.** (*may Yah live*) keeper of the ark [1 Ch 15:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:24) (LXX Ιε(α)ια, LXXL Ιειηλ).
