## חָכָה (id: BDB03028)

\[Str2442] † \[חָכָה] **vb.** wait, await—**Qal** *Pt. pl* cstr. חוֺכֵי [Is 30:18](https://ref.ly/Isa30:18); with לְ *waiting for him* (´י). **Pi.** חִכָּה [Jb 32:4](https://ref.ly/Job32:4); חִכְּתָה [Psalm 33:20](https://ref.ly/Ps33:20); חִכִּיתִי [Is 8:17](https://ref.ly/Isa8:17); חִכּוּ [Psalm 106:13](https://ref.ly/Ps106:13); חִכִּינוּ [2 K 7:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:9); *Impf.* יְחַכֶּה [Is 30:18](https://ref.ly/Isa30:18); תְּחַכֶּה [2 K 9:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:3); *Imv.* חַכֵּ־ה Hb [2:3](https://ref.ly/Hab2:3); חִכּוּ [Zp 3:8](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:8); *Inf. cstr.* כְּחַכֵּי [Ho 6:9](https://ref.ly/Hos6:9) (Ephraimit. for חַכֵּה?); *Pt.* מְחַכֶּה [Dn 12:12](https://ref.ly/Dan12:12); cstr. מְחַכֵּה [Is 64:3](https://ref.ly/Isa64:3); pl. מְחַכִּים [Jb 3:21](https://ref.ly/Job3:21); —**1\.** *wait, tarry*, abs. [2 K 9:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:3) *tarry not;* with עַד till morning light [2 K 7:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:9); c. acc. את איוב בדברים [Jb 32:4](https://ref.ly/Job32:4) *tarry for Job with words.* **2\.** *wait* (in ambush), כְּחַכֵּי אִישׁ גְּדוּדִים [Ho 6:9](https://ref.ly/Hos6:9) *as marauding bands wait for a man* AV RV De, or *as marauders lie in wait* Hi Ew (taking אישׁ as cstr.) **3** *wait for, long for*, with לְ for ´י [Is 8:17](https://ref.ly/Isa8:17), [64:3](https://ref.ly/Isa64:3), [Zp 3:8](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:8), [Psalm 33:20](https://ref.ly/Ps33:20); for his counsel [Psalm 106:13](https://ref.ly/Ps106:13); for his vision Hb [2:3](https://ref.ly/Hab2:3); for death [Jb 3:21](https://ref.ly/Job3:21); יחכה יהוה לחננכם *Yahweh waiteth to be gracious unto you* [Is 30:18](https://ref.ly/Isa30:18) (Di regards this as threatening and refers it to **1**); abs. *waiteth* (and cometh to the days of blessedness) [Dn 12:12](https://ref.ly/Dan12:12).

> \[Str2443] † חַכָּה **v.** sub חנך. p. 335

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 64:3; Isaiah 30:18; 2 Kings 7:9; Daniel 12:12; Job 32:4; Psalms 33:20; Job 3:21; Hosea 6:9; Zephaniah 3:8; Psalms 106:13; 2 Kings 9:3; Isaiah 8:17; Habakkuk 2:3


## חכל (id: BDB03030)

חכל (√of foll.; cf. Ar. حَكَلَ IV. *be confused, vague;* حُكْلَةٌ *barbarousness*, or *impediment in speech;* As. *akâlu, be gloomy, êklu, dark, eklitu, darkness*, Dl in ZimBP 115, w. 385 ff., cf. JägerBAS ii, 282\).

> \[Str2444] † חֲכִילָה **n.pr.loc** (*dark*) a hill in southern Judah, on edge of wilderness of Ziph [1 S 23:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:19), [26:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:1), [26:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:3) (Glaserskizze ii, 326 rds. also for חוילה, q.v., in [1 S 15:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:7)); LXX Εχελα [26:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:1) B Ξελμαθ, A Αχιλα.
> 
> 314:a. חֲכִילָה n.pr.loc.; alw. c. art.

> \[Str2447] † חַכְלִילִי **adj.** (dark) dull, from wine, ´ח עינים מִָ\+יּיִן [Gn 49:12](https://ref.ly/Gen49:12).

> \[Str2448] † חַכְלִילוּת **n.f** dulness, of eyes in drunk\-enness, :עֵינָֽיִם´לְמִי חַכ [Pr 23:29](https://ref.ly/Prov23:29) (v:[30](https://ref.ly/Prov23:30) לַמְאַחֲרִים עַל־הַָ\+יּיִן).

> \[Str2446] † חֲכַלְיָה **n.pr.m.** (mng. dub.; perhaps חַכֵּה לְיָהּ *wait for Yahweh,* √ חכה, So We?—father of Nehemiah [Ne 1:1](https://ref.ly/Neh1:1), LXX Ξελκ(ε)ια, LXXL Ξελκιας(ου), א; A Αχαλια; [10:2](https://ref.ly/Neh10:2) LXX Αχελια, A LXXL Αχαλια.

* **Associated Passages:** Genesis 49:12; Proverbs 23:29; 1 Samuel 23:19; 1 Samuel 15:7; Proverbs 23:30; 1 Samuel 26:1; Nehemiah 10:2; 1 Samuel 26:3; Nehemiah 1:1


## חָכַם (id: BDB03035)

\[Str2449] † חָכַם **vb.** be wise (NH *id.;* Aram. ܚܟܰܡ, חֲכַם *id.;* Zinj. חכמה *wisdom*, DHMsendsch.57; As. *ḫakâmu, know* (with exceptional חַ v. DlPr 178 f.); Ar.حَكَمَ *restrain from acting in an evil manner, judge, govern;* IV.*make firm, sound, free from defect* by the exercise of skill)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ח [Pr 23:15](https://ref.ly/Prov23:15); 3 fs. חָֽכְמָה [Zc 9:2](https://ref.ly/Zech9:2); הָכַמְתָּ [Pr 9:12](https://ref.ly/Prov9:12), [9:12](https://ref.ly/Prov9:12), etc. \+ 3 t. Pf.; *Impf.* יֶחְכַּם [Pr 9:9](https://ref.ly/Prov9:9), [21:11](https://ref.ly/Prov21:11); וַיֶּחְכַּם [1 K 5:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:11); יֶחְבָּם [Pr 20:1](https://ref.ly/Prov20:1)\+; וֶ֯חְכָּם [Pr 13:20](https://ref.ly/Prov13:20); תֶּחְכַּם [Pr 19:20](https://ref.ly/Prov19:20); אֶחְכָּמָה [Ec 7:23](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:23); pl. יֶחְכָּמוּ [Jb 32:9](https://ref.ly/Job32:9); *Imv.* חֲכַם [Pr 27:11](https://ref.ly/Prov27:11); חֲכָם [Pr 6:6](https://ref.ly/Prov6:6), [23:19](https://ref.ly/Prov23:19) \+ [13:20](https://ref.ly/Prov13:20) Kt; חֲכָמוּ [Pr 8:33](https://ref.ly/Prov8:33); —*be or become wise, act wisely* [Dt 32:29](https://ref.ly/Deut32:29) (poet.) [1 K 5:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:11), [Jb 32:9](https://ref.ly/Job32:9), [Pr 6:6](https://ref.ly/Prov6:6), [8:33](https://ref.ly/Prov8:33), [9:9](https://ref.ly/Prov9:9), [9:12](https://ref.ly/Prov9:12), [9:12](https://ref.ly/Prov9:12), [13:20](https://ref.ly/Prov13:20), [19:20](https://ref.ly/Prov19:20), [20:1](https://ref.ly/Prov20:1), [21:11](https://ref.ly/Prov21:11), [23:19](https://ref.ly/Prov23:19), [27:11](https://ref.ly/Prov27:11), [Ec 2:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:15), [7:23](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:23), [Zc 9:2](https://ref.ly/Zech9:2); אם חכם לבך *if thy mind be wise* [Pr 23:15](https://ref.ly/Prov23:15); c. acc. שׁחכמתי [Ec 2:19](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:19) *wherein I have acted wisely.* **Pi.** *Impf.* יְחַכֵּם [Psalm 105:22](https://ref.ly/Ps105:22); sf. יְחַכְּמֵנוּ [Jb 35:11](https://ref.ly/Job35:11); תְּחַכְּמֵנִי [Psalm 119:98](https://ref.ly/Ps119:98); all *make wise, teach wisdom*, c. acc **Pu.** *Pt.* *made wise*, intensive, as it were the embodiment of wisdom חוֺבֵר חֲבָרִים מְחֻכָּם [Psalm 58:6](https://ref.ly/Ps58:6), pl. הֵמָּה חֲכָמִים מְחֻכָּמִים [Pr 30:24](https://ref.ly/Prov30:24) (of intelligent animals). **Hiph.** *Pt.* cstr. מַחְכִּימַת פֶָּתִי [Psalm 19:8](https://ref.ly/Ps19:8) *making wise the simple.* **Hithp.** *Impf.* נִתְחַכְּמָה לוֺ [Ex 1:10](https://ref.ly/Exod1:10) (E) *let us deal wisely (shrewdly) toward it* (the people); 2 ms. תִּתְחַכַּם [Ec 7:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:16) *make or shew thyself wise.*

> \[Str2450] † חָכָם **adj.** wise, ´ח [Dt 4:6](https://ref.ly/Deut4:6) \+ 67 t.; cstr. חֲכַם [Is 3:3](https://ref.ly/Isa3:3) \+ 9 t.; pl. חֲכָמִים [Dt 1:13](https://ref.ly/Deut1:13) \+ 39 t.; cstr. חַכְמֵי [Ex 28:3](https://ref.ly/Exod28:3) \+ 4 t.; sf. חֲכָמֶיךָ [Is 19:12](https://ref.ly/Isa19:12) etc. \+ 8 t.; **f.** חֲכָמָה [2 S 14:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:2), [20:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:16); cstr. חַכְמַת [Ex 35:25](https://ref.ly/Exod35:25); pl. חֲכָמוֺת [Je 9:16](https://ref.ly/Jer9:16); cstr. חַכְמוֺת Ju [5:29](https://ref.ly/Judg5:29), [Pr 14:1](https://ref.ly/Prov14:1)(but rd. חָכְמוֺת De)—**1\.** *skilful* in technical work; artificers [Is 3:3](https://ref.ly/Isa3:3); sailors Ez [27:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:8), so (prob.) v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:9); mourning women [Je 9:16](https://ref.ly/Jer9:16); artisans of tabern. and temple and their furniture [Ex 28:3](https://ref.ly/Exod28:3), [31:6](https://ref.ly/Exod31:6), [35:10](https://ref.ly/Exod35:10), [36:1](https://ref.ly/Exod36:1), [36:2](https://ref.ly/Exod36:2), [36:4](https://ref.ly/Exod36:4), [36:8](https://ref.ly/Exod36:8) (P), [1 Ch 22:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:15), [2 Ch 2:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:6), [2:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:12), [2:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:13), [2:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:13); women in spinning [Ex 35:25](https://ref.ly/Exod35:25) (P); goldsmiths and other artisans [Je 10:9](https://ref.ly/Jer10:9); makers of idol\-images [Is 40:20](https://ref.ly/Isa40:20). **2\.** *wise in the administration of affairs:* Joseph in Egypt [Gn 41:33](https://ref.ly/Gen41:33), [41:39](https://ref.ly/Gen41:39) (E); heads of tribes [Dt 1:13](https://ref.ly/Deut1:13), [1:15](https://ref.ly/Deut1:15), judges [16:19](https://ref.ly/Deut16:19); David [2 S 14:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:20), of Solomon [1 K 2:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:9), [3:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:12), [5:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:7) \= [2 Ch 2:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:11); the prince of Tyre, in satire Ez [28:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:3); of kings in general [Pr 20:26](https://ref.ly/Prov20:26); class of political advisers of Judah [Is 29:14](https://ref.ly/Isa29:14), [Je 18:18](https://ref.ly/Jer18:18), appar. also [Is 5:21](https://ref.ly/Isa5:21); of Egypt [Is 19:11](https://ref.ly/Isa19:11), [19:11](https://ref.ly/Isa19:11), [19:12](https://ref.ly/Isa19:12), Edom Ob:[8](https://ref.ly/Obad1:8), the nations [Je 10:7](https://ref.ly/Jer10:7); so of God [Is 31:2](https://ref.ly/Isa31:2). **3\.** *shrewd, crafty, cunning:* Jonadab [2 S 13:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:3); the princesses Ju [5:29](https://ref.ly/Judg5:29); אשׁה חכמה *wise woman* [2 S 14:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:2), [20:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:16); gen., [Je 9:22](https://ref.ly/Jer9:22); intelligent animals [Pr 30:24](https://ref.ly/Prov30:24); בערמם ´לכד ח *taking the cunning in their craft* [Jb 5:13](https://ref.ly/Job5:13); חכם בעיני *wise in one's own eyes* [Pr 3:7](https://ref.ly/Prov3:7), [26:5](https://ref.ly/Prov26:5), [26:12](https://ref.ly/Prov26:12), [26:16](https://ref.ly/Prov26:16), [28:11](https://ref.ly/Prov28:11); חכמי לב *wise of mind* (in their own mind) [Jb 37:24](https://ref.ly/Job37:24). **4\.** pl. class of *learned* and *shrewd* men, incl. astrologers, magicians and the like, of Egypt [Gn 41:8](https://ref.ly/Gen41:8) (E), [Ex 7:11](https://ref.ly/Exod7:11) (P ; cf. [Is 19:11](https://ref.ly/Isa19:11), [19:11](https://ref.ly/Isa19:11), [19:12](https://ref.ly/Isa19:12)); Babylon [Is 44:25](https://ref.ly/Isa44:25), [Je 50:35](https://ref.ly/Jer50:35), [51:57](https://ref.ly/Jer51:57); Persia [Est 1:13](https://ref.ly/Esth1:13), [6:13](https://ref.ly/Esth6:13). **5\.** *prudent*, towards king [Pr 16:14](https://ref.ly/Prov16:14); in controversies [Pr 11:29](https://ref.ly/Prov11:29), [29:8](https://ref.ly/Prov29:8), [29:9](https://ref.ly/Prov29:9), [29:11](https://ref.ly/Prov29:11); religious affairs [Ho 13:13](https://ref.ly/Hos13:13), [14:10](https://ref.ly/Hos14:10), [Je 4:22](https://ref.ly/Jer4:22), [8:8](https://ref.ly/Jer8:8), [8:9](https://ref.ly/Jer8:9), [Dt 4:6](https://ref.ly/Deut4:6), [32:6](https://ref.ly/Deut32:6), [Psalm 107:43](https://ref.ly/Ps107:43); אישׁ ´ח [Je 9:11](https://ref.ly/Jer9:11). **6\.** *wise*, ethically and religiously,. in WisdLt: **a.** as adj., חֲכַם לֵב *wise of mind* [Pr 10:8](https://ref.ly/Prov10:8), [16:21](https://ref.ly/Prov16:21); לֵב חָכָם [Ec 8:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:5); of the son [Pr 10:1](https://ref.ly/Prov10:1) \= [15:20](https://ref.ly/Prov15:20), [13:1](https://ref.ly/Prov13:1), [23:24](https://ref.ly/Prov23:24), [Ec 2:19](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:19); ´ילד ח [Ec 4:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:13) *wise boy;* ´גבר ח [Jb 34:34](https://ref.ly/Job34:34) *wise man;* ´מוכיח ח *wise reprover* [Pr 25:12](https://ref.ly/Prov25:12). **b.** \= subst.: (1\) as a *wise learner* in school of wisdom, he fears God and departs from evil [Pr 14:16](https://ref.ly/Prov14:16); is silent [Pr 17:28](https://ref.ly/Prov17:28); hearkens to counsel [Pr 12:15](https://ref.ly/Prov12:15); hears and increases in learning [Pr 1:5](https://ref.ly/Prov1:5); his ear seeketh knowledge [Pr 18:15](https://ref.ly/Prov18:15); he receives it [Pr 21:11](https://ref.ly/Prov21:11); and stores it up [Pr 10:14](https://ref.ly/Prov10:14); his ears hearken to the reproof which giveth life [Pr 15:31](https://ref.ly/Prov15:31); and he becomes wiser through it [Pr 9:8](https://ref.ly/Prov9:8), [9:9](https://ref.ly/Prov9:9). (2\) as a *wise teacher, a sage,* he does not answer with windy knowledge [Jb 15:2](https://ref.ly/Job15:2); he tells the experience of the past [Jb 15:48](https://ref.ly/Job15:48); has knowledge [Jb 34:2](https://ref.ly/Job34:2); teaches it [Ec 12:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:9); disperses it [Pr 15:7](https://ref.ly/Prov15:7); his tongue is health [Pr 12:18](https://ref.ly/Prov12:18); it utters knowledge aright [Pr 15:2](https://ref.ly/Prov15:2); his mind instructs his mouth and adds learning to his lips [Pr 16:23](https://ref.ly/Prov16:23); his words are gracious [Ec 10:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:12); it is good to hear his rebuke [Ec 7:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:5); his instruction is a fountain of life [Pr 13:14](https://ref.ly/Prov13:14); one walking with him becomes wiser [Pr 13:20](https://ref.ly/Prov13:20). The חכמים recall the Gk. σόφοι, having their schools, pupils (בֵן), discipline (מוּסָר), principles and collections of wisdom, דברי חכמים [Pr 1:6](https://ref.ly/Prov1:6), [22:17](https://ref.ly/Prov22:17) (cf. [24:23](https://ref.ly/Prov24:23)), [Ec 9:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:17), [12:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:11); God himself is חכם לבב [Jb 9:4](https://ref.ly/Job9:4). (3\) *the wise* are prosperous: true, in sceptical view of Ec., they have no advantage over the fool [Ec 6:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:8); but die as the fool [Ec 2:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:16), [2:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:16), [Psalm 49:11](https://ref.ly/Ps49:11); they will not be able to find the works of God [Ec 8:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:17); like others are in the hands of God [Ec 9:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:1); they do not secure bread [Ec 9:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:11); but elsewhere reverse is true: precious treasure is in his dwelling [Pr 21:20](https://ref.ly/Prov21:20); his lips preserve him [Pr 14:3](https://ref.ly/Prov14:3); they inherit glory [Pr 3:35](https://ref.ly/Prov3:35); wealth is their crown [Pr 14:24](https://ref.ly/Prov14:24); the wise man is strong [Pr 24:5](https://ref.ly/Prov24:5), [Ec 7:19](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:19); his eyes are in his head [Ec 2:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:14); his mind is at his right hand [Ec 10:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:2); he interprets things [Ec 8:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:1) (4\) the *wise* man is a blessing to others: he wins men [Pr 11:30](https://ref.ly/Prov11:30); scales the city of the mighty [Pr 21:22](https://ref.ly/Prov21:22); though poor he delivers the city [Ec 9:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:15); is a reproof to scorners [Pr 15:12](https://ref.ly/Prov15:12); his mind is in the house of mourning [Ec 7:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:4); injustice makes him foolish [Ec 7:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:7); Job finds no wise men among his cruel friends [Jb 17:10](https://ref.ly/Job17:10).

> \[Str2451,2454] † חָכְמה **n.f.** wisdom, ´ח [Ex 28:3](https://ref.ly/Exod28:3) \+ 106 t.; cstr. חָכְמַת [Ex 35:35](https://ref.ly/Exod35:35) \+ 15 t.; sf. חָכְמָתִי [Ec 2:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:9), etc. \+ 25 t. sf.; pl. abst. emph. חָכְמוֺת [Psalm 49:4](https://ref.ly/Ps49:4), [Pr 1:20](https://ref.ly/Prov1:20), [9:1](https://ref.ly/Prov9:1), [24:7](https://ref.ly/Prov24:7); חַכְמוֺת [Pr 14:1](https://ref.ly/Prov14:1) (in correctly pointed as adj. cstr. f.; rd. חָכְמוֺת De);—**1\.** *skill* in war [Is 10:13](https://ref.ly/Isa10:13); in technical work [Ex 28:3](https://ref.ly/Exod28:3), [31:3](https://ref.ly/Exod31:3), [31:6](https://ref.ly/Exod31:6), [35:26](https://ref.ly/Exod35:26), [35:31](https://ref.ly/Exod35:31), [35:35](https://ref.ly/Exod35:35), [36:1](https://ref.ly/Exod36:1), [36:2](https://ref.ly/Exod36:2) (P), cf. [1 K 7:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:14), [1 Ch 28:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:21); of sailors [Psalm 107:27](https://ref.ly/Ps107:27). **2\.** *wisdom*, in administration [Dt 34:9](https://ref.ly/Deut34:9), [Is 29:14](https://ref.ly/Isa29:14), [Je 49:7](https://ref.ly/Jer49:7), [49:7](https://ref.ly/Jer49:7), [2 S 14:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:20); of prince of Tyre Ez [28:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:4), [28:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:5), [28:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:7), [28:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:12), [29:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:12), [29:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:17); Sol.'s wisdom included this with other forms of cleverness and shrewdness [1 K 2:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:6), [3:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:28), [5:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:9), [5:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:10), [5:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:14), [5:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:14), [5:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:26), [10:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:4), [10:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:6), [10:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:7), [10:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:8), [10:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:23), [10:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:24) \= [2 Ch 9:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:3), [9:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:5), [9:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:6), [9:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:7), [9:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:22), [9:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:23), [1 K 11:41](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:41), [2 Ch 1:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:10), [1:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:11), [1:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:12); the Messiah is to have רוּחַ חָכְמָה וּבִינָה [Is 11:2](https://ref.ly/Isa11:2). **3\.** *shrewdness, wisdom*, [2 S 20:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:22), [1 K 5:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:10), [5:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:10), [Je 9:22](https://ref.ly/Jer9:22); withheld by God from the ostrich [Jb 39:17](https://ref.ly/Job39:17); of magicians and prophets [Is 47:10](https://ref.ly/Isa47:10), [Dn 1:4](https://ref.ly/Dan1:4), [1:17](https://ref.ly/Dan1:17), [1:20](https://ref.ly/Dan1:20). **4\.** *wisdom, prudence* in religious affairs [Dt 4:6](https://ref.ly/Deut4:6), [Psalm 37:30](https://ref.ly/Ps37:30), [51:8](https://ref.ly/Ps51:8), [90:12](https://ref.ly/Ps90:12), [Pr 10:31](https://ref.ly/Prov10:31), [Is 33:6](https://ref.ly/Isa33:6), [Je 8:9](https://ref.ly/Jer8:9) **5\.** *wisdom*, ethical and religious: **a.** of God, as a divine attribute or energy; his wisdom is in the skies [Jb 38:36](https://ref.ly/Job38:36); by it he numbers the clouds [Jb 38:37](https://ref.ly/Job38:37); founded the earth [Pr 3:19](https://ref.ly/Prov3:19); and made all things [Je 10:12](https://ref.ly/Jer10:12) \= [51:15](https://ref.ly/Jer51:15), [Psalm 104:24](https://ref.ly/Ps104:24); it is with him [Jb 12:13](https://ref.ly/Job12:13); not to be found by the most persevering human search [Jb 28:12](https://ref.ly/Job28:12), [28:20](https://ref.ly/Job28:20); he alone knows it [Jb 28:23](https://ref.ly/Job28:23); gives it [Pr 2:6](https://ref.ly/Prov2:6); and shows its secrets [Jb 11:6](https://ref.ly/Job11:6). **b.** the divine wisdom is personified: she was begotten before all things to be the architect and counsellor of God in the creation ([Pr 8:22–31](https://ref.ly/Prov8:22-Prov8:31)); she builds a palace and spreads a feast for those who will receive her instruction [9:1](https://ref.ly/Prov9:1) (cf. v:[2–5](https://ref.ly/Prov9:2-Prov9:5)); she teaches in public [1:20](https://ref.ly/Prov1:20), [8:1](https://ref.ly/Prov8:1), [8:5](https://ref.ly/Prov8:5), [8:11](https://ref.ly/Prov8:11), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Prov8:12) (v. context); gives her pupils the divine spirit [1:23](https://ref.ly/Prov1:23); by her discipline simple become wise, rulers rule wisely, and those seeking her are richly rewarded [8:14](https://ref.ly/Prov8:14) (cf. v:[1–21](https://ref.ly/Prov8:1-Prov8:21)). **c.** of man: to get wisdom is principal thing [Pr 4:7](https://ref.ly/Prov4:7), [4:7](https://ref.ly/Prov4:7); its fundamental principle is to fear God [Psalm 111:10](https://ref.ly/Ps111:10), [Pr 15:33](https://ref.ly/Prov15:33), [Jb 28:18](https://ref.ly/Job28:18) (‖סוּר מֵרַע בִּינָה); and know ´י, the All Holy [Pr 9:10](https://ref.ly/Prov9:10), [30:3](https://ref.ly/Prov30:3); it is of inestimable worth [Jb 28:18](https://ref.ly/Job28:18), [Pr 8:11](https://ref.ly/Prov8:11), [16:16](https://ref.ly/Prov16:16), [17:16](https://ref.ly/Prov17:16), [23:23](https://ref.ly/Prov23:23), [24:7](https://ref.ly/Prov24:7); beyond the reach of scorners [Pr 14:6](https://ref.ly/Prov14:6); God giveth it to the good [Ec 2:26](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:26); men are to incline the ear [Pr 2:2](https://ref.ly/Prov2:2); attend unto wisdom [Pr 5:1](https://ref.ly/Prov5:1); seek [Ec 7:25](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:25); know [Pr 24:14](https://ref.ly/Prov24:14), [Ec 1:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:17), [8:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:16); behold [Ec 2:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:12), [9:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:13); get her [Pr 4:5](https://ref.ly/Prov4:5), [4:7](https://ref.ly/Prov4:7); treat her as sister [Pr 7:4](https://ref.ly/Prov7:4); happy the man who finds her [Pr 3:13](https://ref.ly/Prov3:13); he who has wisdom is אִישׁ תְּבוּנָה [Pr 10:23](https://ref.ly/Prov10:23); נָבוֺן [Pr 10:13](https://ref.ly/Prov10:13), [14:33](https://ref.ly/Prov14:33); מֵבִין [Pr 17:24](https://ref.ly/Prov17:24); cf. also [Psalm 49:4](https://ref.ly/Ps49:4), [Jb 4:21](https://ref.ly/Job4:21), [12:2](https://ref.ly/Job12:2), [12:12](https://ref.ly/Job12:12), [13:5](https://ref.ly/Job13:5), [15:8](https://ref.ly/Job15:8), [26:3](https://ref.ly/Job26:3), [32:7](https://ref.ly/Job32:7), [32:13](https://ref.ly/Job32:13), [33:33](https://ref.ly/Job33:33), [Pr 1:2](https://ref.ly/Prov1:2), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Prov1:7), [2:10](https://ref.ly/Prov2:10), [4:11](https://ref.ly/Prov4:11), [11:2](https://ref.ly/Prov11:2), [13:10](https://ref.ly/Prov13:10), [14:1](https://ref.ly/Prov14:1), [14:8](https://ref.ly/Prov14:8), [18:4](https://ref.ly/Prov18:4), [21:30](https://ref.ly/Prov21:30), [24:3](https://ref.ly/Prov24:3), [28:26](https://ref.ly/Prov28:26), [29:3](https://ref.ly/Prov29:3), [29:15](https://ref.ly/Prov29:15), [31:26](https://ref.ly/Prov31:26), [Ec 1:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:13), [1:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:16), [1:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:16), [1:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:18), [2:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:3), [2:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:9), [2:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:13), [2:21](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:21), [7:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:10), [7:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:11), [7:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:12), [7:19](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:19), [7:23](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:23), [8:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:1), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:10), [9:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:15), [9:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:16), [9:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:16), [9:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:18), [10:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:1), [10:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:10)—on solomon's wisdom v. supr.

## I חָלָא (id: BDB03042)

\[Str2456] † I. \[חָלָא] **vb.** be sick, diseased (\= חלה, v. Ges§ 75, 22\)—**Qal** *Impf.* בְּרַגְלָיו…וַיֶּחֱלֶא אָסָא [2 Ch 16:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:12) (\= חלה [1 K 15:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:23)).

> \[Str8463] † תַּחֲלֻאִים **n.pl.m.**:[2 Ch 21:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:19) diseases, abs. ´ת [Je 16:4](https://ref.ly/Jer16:4), [2 Ch 21:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:19); cstr. תַּחֲלוּאֵי [Je 14:18](https://ref.ly/Jer14:18); תַּחֲלוּאָ֫יְכִי [Psalm 103:3](https://ref.ly/Ps103:3); תַּחֲלֻאַ֫יהָ [Dt 29:21](https://ref.ly/Deut29:21); —*diseases* [Dt 29:21](https://ref.ly/Deut29:21), [2 Ch 21:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:19), [Psalm 103:3](https://ref.ly/Ps103:3); מְמוֺתֵי תחלאים יָמֻתוּ [Je 16:4](https://ref.ly/Jer16:4); תחלואי רעב [Je 14:18](https://ref.ly/Jer14:18) *diseases of famine* (‖חַלְלֵיחֶחֶרֶב).

## II חלא (id: BDB03044)

II. חלא (√of foll.; DHM in MV comp. Sab. פחַטאת ותחלאן *she sinned and defiled herself*).

> \[Str2457] † I. \[חֶלְאָה] **n.f.** rust (perh. as *filth* v. supr.)—חֶלְאָתָהּ Ez [24:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:6), [24:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:12) \+ v:[11](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:11), v:[12](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:12) (del. Co intern. grounds) \+ v:[6](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:6) חֶלְאָתָהַ (cf. Ges§ 91, 1; R. 2\) *rust* on metal pot, symbol of impurity of Jerusalem.

> \[Str2458] † II. חֶלְאָה **n.pr.f.** (meaning dub.)—wife of Ashḫur (of Judah) ‘father’ of Tekoah [1 Ch 4:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:5), [4:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:7) LXX Αωδα, Αοαδα, Αλαα, Ελαα, Ελεα.

## חלב (id: BDB03049)

חלב (√ of foll.; meaning unknown).

> \[Str2461] † חָלָב **n.m.** milk (NH *id.* (and vb. denom.); Aram. חַלָבָּא, ܚܰܠܒܳܐ, Ph. חלב; Ar. حَليبٌ n. *fresh milk*, حَلَبَ vb. *milk;* As. *alibu, milk*, v. DlPr 174; Eth. ሐሊብ፡)—´ח abs. [Gn 18:18](https://ref.ly/Gen18:18) \+ 35 t. \+ Ez [34:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:3) (v. infr.); cstr. חֲלֵב [Ex 23:19](https://ref.ly/Exod23:19) \+ 5 t.; sf. חֲלָבִי Ct [5:1](https://ref.ly/Song5:1), חֲלָבֵח Ez [25:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:4); —*milk:* **a.** as common food [Gn 18:8](https://ref.ly/Gen18:8) (J; distinct from חֶמְאָה *curd*, q.v.), חֲלֵב צאֹן [Dt 32:14](https://ref.ly/Deut32:14) (poet.; distinct fr. חֶמְאַת בָּקָר); in Ju [5:25](https://ref.ly/Judg5:25) חָלָב is used parallel with חֶמְאָה; elsewhere חֶמְאָה seems to be produced fr. חָלָב:—מִיץ חָלָב יוֺצִיא חֶמְאָה [Pr 30:33](https://ref.ly/Prov30:33) *a pressing* (*squeezing*) *of milk produces curd;* מֵרֹב עֲשוֺת חָלָב יאֹכַל חֶמְאָה [Is 7:22](https://ref.ly/Isa7:22) *because of abundant yield of milk he shall eat curd;* cf. also חֲרִצֵי הֶחָלָב [1 S 17:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:18) *cuts of milk*, i.e. *cheeses;* it was received in buckets or pails (עֲטִינָיו) [Jb 21:24](https://ref.ly/Job21:24) (v. Ew De Di VB), and kept in skins (´נזֹוד ח) Ju [4:19](https://ref.ly/Judg4:19); men drank it (שׁתה) Ez [25:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:4) (cf. השׁקה Ju [4:19](https://ref.ly/Judg4:19)), but also ate it (אכל), if emend. הֶחָלָב Ez [34:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:3) (for הַחֵלֶב) is right, LXX Vulgate Bo Hi\-Sm Co, al.; it was poured out, v. sim. of formation of the embryo תַּתִּיכֵנִי ´הֲלאֹ כֶח [Jb 10:10](https://ref.ly/Job10:10) *hast thou not poured me out like milk*? specif. (1\) חֲלֵב עִזִּים לְלַחְמְךָ [Pr 27:27](https://ref.ly/Prov27:27) *milk of goats for thy food* (RS Sem. i. 204\). (2\) c. אֵם *mother's milk:* of kid לאֹ־תְבַשֵּׁל גְּדִי בַּחֲלֵב אִמּוֺ [Ex 23:19](https://ref.ly/Exod23:19) \= [34:26](https://ref.ly/Exod34:26) (JE) \= [Dt 14:21](https://ref.ly/Deut14:21), cf. ´טְלֵה ח [1 S 7:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:9) *sucking lamb* (for sacrif.); but also of human beings ´גְּמוּלֵי מֵח [Is 28:9](https://ref.ly/Isa28:9) *weaned from milk* (‖עַתִּיקֵי מִשָּׁדָ֫יִם); fig. in promise to Zion וְיָנַקְתְּ חֲלֵב גּוֺיִם [Is 60:16](https://ref.ly/Isa60:16) *and thou shalt suck the milk of nations* (‖תִּיָ\+נקִי וְשֹׁד מְלָכִים). (3\) ´ח with wine, as esp. delicacy Ct [5:1](https://ref.ly/Song5:1) (v. De), fig. of ´י's choicest blessings [Is 55:1](https://ref.ly/Isa55:1). (4\) oft. in phr. of productiveness of land of Canaan (דְּבַשׁ) זָבַת חָלָב וּדְבָ֫שׁ *flowing with milk and honey* [Ex 3:8](https://ref.ly/Exod3:8), [3:17](https://ref.ly/Exod3:17), [13:5](https://ref.ly/Exod13:5), [33:3](https://ref.ly/Exod33:3), [Nu 13:27](https://ref.ly/Num13:27), [14:8](https://ref.ly/Num14:8), [16:13](https://ref.ly/Num16:13), [16:14](https://ref.ly/Num16:14) (all JE), [Lv 20:24](https://ref.ly/Lev20:24) (H), [Dt 6:3](https://ref.ly/Deut6:3), [11:9](https://ref.ly/Deut11:9), [26:9](https://ref.ly/Deut26:9), [26:15](https://ref.ly/Deut26:15), [27:3](https://ref.ly/Deut27:3), [31:20](https://ref.ly/Deut31:20), [Jos 5:6](https://ref.ly/Josh5:6), [Je 11:5](https://ref.ly/Jer11:5), [32:22](https://ref.ly/Jer32:22), Ez [20:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:6), [20:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:15); in Messian. time the hills ´תֵּלַכְנָה ח [Jo 4:18](https://ref.ly/Joel4:18) (‖יִטְּפוּ הֶהָרִים עָסִיס); fig. of charms of loved one תַּחַת לְשׁוֺנֵח ´דְּבַשׁ וְח Ct [4:12](https://ref.ly/Song4:12) *honey and milk are under thy tongue.* **b.** *milk* as white ´לְבֶן־שִׁנַּיִם מֵח [Gn 49:12](https://ref.ly/Gen49:12) *white of teeth, from milk;* ´צַחוּ מֵח [La 4:7](https://ref.ly/Lam4:7) *they were whiter than milk* (‖זַכּוּ מִשֶּׁלֶג); so also prob. of eyes (iris) ´רֹחֲצוֺת בֶּח Ct [5:12](https://ref.ly/Song5:12) *bathed in milk*, i.e. the white of the eye.—On milk as not used by Israel in sacrifice v. RS Sem. 204\.

PS1274) orig. *diaphragm, midriff;* Ar. خِلْبٌ, incl. *midriff\-fat*, RS Sem. i. 360\);—´ח abs. [Lv 3:16](https://ref.ly/Lev3:16) \+ 40 t.; cstr. [Lv 8:16](https://ref.ly/Lev8:16), [8:25](https://ref.ly/Lev8:25) supr.);—**1\.** *fat* of human body Ju [3:22](https://ref.ly/Judg3:22) (covering intestines); גִּבּוֺרִים´ח [2 S 1:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:22) as smearing warrior's shield; of grossness of wicked כִּסָּה פָּנָיו בְּחֶלְבּוֺ [Jb 15:27](https://ref.ly/Job15:27); *fat* (of midriff) fig. of unreceptive heart סָֽגְרוּ ´ח [Psalm 17:10](https://ref.ly/Ps17:10), v. also [73:7](https://ref.ly/Ps73:7) (rd. מֵחֵלֶב עֲוֺנָמוֺ for מחלב עֵינֵמוֺ LXX SyrVer Hi Ew Ol De Che Bae al.); ´טָפַשׁ כַּח לִבָּם [Psalm 119:70](https://ref.ly/Ps119:70) i.e. their heart is as unresponsive as the midriff\-fat near it. **2\.** *fat* of beasts: **a.** as rich food (poet.) Lv, [Nu 18:17](https://ref.ly/Num18:17) (all P), [Lv 17:6](https://ref.ly/Lev17:6) (H), [1 S 15:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:22), [Is 1:11](https://ref.ly/Isa1:11), [43:24](https://ref.ly/Isa43:24), Ez [44:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:7), [44:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:15); v. (for [Je 4:31](https://ref.ly/Jer4:31) v. Qal; *my sickness*, so BI Che (others der. fr. חלל *my wounding*, v. De).—For other forms of Pi. v. II. חלה. **Pu.** *Pf. be made weak*, 2 ms. חֻלֵּיתָ [Is 14:10](https://ref.ly/Isa14:10). **Hithp.** *Impf.* וַיִּתְחָל [2 S 13:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:6); *Imv.* הִתְחָל [2 S 13:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:5); *Inf.* הִתְחַלּוֺת [2 S 13:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:2); —*make oneself sick*, of Ammon's morbid passion for his sister [2 S 13:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:2), sq. בַּעֲבוּר; of his pretended sickness of body [2 S 13:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:5), [13:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:6). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הֶחֱלִי [Is 53:10](https://ref.ly/Isa53:10); 1 s. הֶחֱלֵתִי [Mi 6:13](https://ref.ly/Mic6:13); pl. הֶחֱלוּ [Ho 7:5](https://ref.ly/Hos7:5) (on txt. v. infr.); *Pt.* מַחֲלָה [Pr 13:12](https://ref.ly/Prov13:12); —**1\.** *make* (*sick*, i.e.) *sore thy smiting* הַכּוֺתֶָךָ [Mi 6:13](https://ref.ly/Mic6:13) (cf. [Na 3:19](https://ref.ly/Nah3:19), Niph.); obj. (implic.) a person, חָפֵץ דַּכְּאוֺ ´וי הֶחֱלִי [Is 53:10](https://ref.ly/Isa53:10) *it pleased* ´י *to bruise him, making him sick* \= to bruise him *sorely,* v. further Di. **2\.** *make sick,* obj. לֵב (of hope deferred) [Pr 13:12](https://ref.ly/Prov13:12). **3\.** *shew* (signs of) *sickness, become sick,* only שרים חֲמַת מִָ\+יּיִן ´הֶח [Ho 7:5](https://ref.ly/Hos7:5) *princes are become sick with fever of wine* (Now Che VB; \> Vrss Hi\-St who rd. הֵחֵלּוּ *they begin* the day with wine\-fever). **Hoph.** *Pf. be made sick \= wounded* הָֽחָלֵיתִי [1 K 22:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:34) \= [2 Ch 18:33](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:33), [2 Ch 35:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:23).

## II חָלָה (id: BDB03070)

\[Str2470] † II. \[חָלָה] **vb.** only **Pi.** sq. פְּנֵי, פָּנֶיךָ, פָּנַיִךְ \= mollify, appease, entreat the favour of, lit. *make the face* of any one *sweet* or *pleasant* (cf. Ar. حَلَا, حَلِىَ, *be sweet, pleasant*, Aram. ܚܠܻܝ, חֲלִי, *id.*, adj. חָלֵי *sweet*);—**Pi.** *Pf.* חִלָּה [2 Ch 33:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:12), חִלִּיתִי [1 S 13:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:12), [Psalm 119:58](https://ref.ly/Ps119:58), וְחִלּוּ consec. [Jb 11:19](https://ref.ly/Job11:19), חִלִּינוּ [Dn 9:13](https://ref.ly/Dan9:13); *Impf.* וַיְחַל [Ex 32:11](https://ref.ly/Exod32:11) \+ 3 t., יְחַלּוּ [Psalm 45:13](https://ref.ly/Ps45:13), [Pr 19:6](https://ref.ly/Prov19:6); *Imv.* חַל־נָא [1 K 13:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:6), חַלּוּ־נָא [Mal 1:9](https://ref.ly/Mal1:9); *Inf. cstr.* לְחַלּוֺת [Zc 7:2](https://ref.ly/Zech7:2) \+ 2 t.;—**1\.** ´את־פני־י ´ח: **a.** *mollify, pacify, appease* ´י, i.e. induce him to shew favour in place of wrath and chastisement [Ex 32:11](https://ref.ly/Exod32:11) (JE), [1 K 13:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:6) ( \+ הִתְפַּלֵּל), v:[6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:6), [2 K 13:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:4), [Je 26:19](https://ref.ly/Jer26:19) (\+ וַיִּנָּ֫חֶם), [2 Ch 33:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:12) (‖וַיִּכָּנַע מאד מִלִּפְנֵי), [Dn 9:13](https://ref.ly/Dan9:13); also פני־אֵל ´ח [Mal 1:9](https://ref.ly/Mal1:9) (sq. וִיחָנֵּ֫נוּ *that he may be gracious to us*). **b.** *entreat the favour of* ´י, i.e. aim at success, prosperity, etc., through his favour, [1 S 13:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:12) (in anticipation of war), [Zc 8:21](https://ref.ly/Zech8:21), [8:22](https://ref.ly/Zech8:22) (‖´לְבַקֵּשׁ אֶת־י; of cities and nations assembling at Jerusalem for worship), [Zc 7:2](https://ref.ly/Zech7:2) (‖´לְבַקֵּשׁ אֶת־י) of worship at Jerusalem; quite gen., as habit of God\-fearing man, [Psalm 119:58](https://ref.ly/Ps119:58) (\+ בְּכָל־לַב). **2\.** *entreat favour* of men (in sense of **1 b**)—´רַבִּים יְח פני־נָדִיב [Pr 19:6](https://ref.ly/Prov19:6) *many entreat a prince's favour* (‖וְכָל־הָרֵעַ לְאִישׁ מַתָּן); פָּנָיִךְ ´יח [Psalm 45:13](https://ref.ly/Ps45:13) of favour of king's bride; פָּנַיִךְ רַבִּים ´וְח [Jb 11:19](https://ref.ly/Job11:19) favour of Job when absolved and restored.

> \[Str4257] † מַחֲלַת **n.f.** only in Psalm titles ´עַל־מ [Psalm 53:1](https://ref.ly/Ps53:1), [88:1](https://ref.ly/Ps88:1); appar. a catchword in a song, giving name to tune; mng. dub.; Aq. Symm. Vulgate מְחֹלֽת *dance;* v. OlPsalmen, p. 27 BaePsalmen, p. xviii.

## III חלה (id: BDB03072)

III. חלה (*adorn;* cf. Ar. حَلَى *adorn*, حَلْىٌ (neck\-) *ornament*).

> \[Str2481] † I. חֲלִי **n.m.** ornament, נֶזֶם זָחָב וַחֲלִי־כָּתֶם [Pr 25:12](https://ref.ly/Prov25:12) *a* (nose\-or\-ear\-) *ring of gold and* (neck\-or breast\-) *ornament of fine gold;* pl. חֲלָאִים Ct [7:2](https://ref.ly/Song7:2), in sim. of grace and beauty.

> \[Str2482] † II. חֲלִי **n.pr.loc.** in Asher [Jos 19:25](https://ref.ly/Josh19:25), site unknown (v. conject. in Di).

> \[Str2484] † \[חֶלְיָה] **n.f.** jewelry, נִזְמָהּ וְחֶלְיָתָהּ [Ho 2:15](https://ref.ly/Hos2:15) (v. Now Che).

> \[Str2477] † חֲלַח **n.pr.loc.** a city or district under Assyr. control, whither Isr. captives were taken [2 K 17:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:6) \= [18:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:11), [1 Ch 5:26](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:26), cf. *Ḫalaḫḫu* in Mesopotamia, SchrCOT on [2 K 17:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:8); \> HalMêl. Epigr. 1874, 70 *Cilica* (\= Ph. חלך, LagM. i. 211, As. *Ḫilakku*, LyonSargontexte) LagBN 57\.

> \[Str2478] † חַלְחוּל **n.pr.loc.** town in Judah, [Jos 15:58](https://ref.ly/Josh15:58) LagOnom. 119\. 7, 2nd ed. 152 Elul in tribu Iuda, cf. Alula juxta Chebron; mod. Ḥalḥûl, 1 1/2 hour (3 1/2 miles) N. fr. Hebron, v. Di (and reff.) Bla RobBR iii. 281 f. GuêrinJudêe iii. 284 ff.

> \[Str2479] חַלְחָלָה **n.f.** v. I. חול

## חָלַט (id: BDB03079)

\[Str2480] † \[חָלַט] **vb.** only וַיַּחְלְטוּ הֲ מִמֶּנּוּ [1 K 20:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:33), rd. prob. וַיַּחְלְטוּהָ מִמֶּנּוּ (cf. LXX ἀνέλεξ́αν τὸν λόγον ἀπὸ τοῦ στόματος ά̓τοῦ and StaG i. 445f.) *and they snatched it from him*, caught the word fr. his lips; Sta§ 529 a as **Qal;** Köi. 251 Ges§ 53\. 3 R 4 and most as **Hiph.,** with —ִ om.

## חלך (id: BDB03080)

חלך (√ of foll.; poss. (si vera l.) cf. Ar. حَلَكَ *be black* (passing into fig. sense, *unfortunate*, in Heb.), v. Bae:[Psalm 10:8](https://ref.ly/Ps10:8); cf. Aram. חֲשׁוֺח).

> \[Str2489] † חֵֽלְכָה rd. חַלְכֶּה, חֶלְכֶּה, or חָלְכֶּה, **adj.** helpless, unfortunate, only as subst. *hapless, luckless one(s)*—לְחֵֽלְכָה [Psalm 10:8](https://ref.ly/Ps10:8), LXX εἰς τὸν πένητα, SyrVer Targum; חֵלֶָכָה [10:14](https://ref.ly/Ps10:14) LXX ὁ πτωχός; חלכאים [10:10](https://ref.ly/Ps10:10) Kt, rd. pl. חַלְכָּאִים (´חֶל, ´חָל); Qr חֵל כָּאִים LXX τῶν πενμ́των. In all txt. and mng. dub. (MT rds. חֵלְכָה \= חֵילְךָ *thine army;* and חֵל כָּאִים v:[10](https://ref.ly/Ps10:10) *army of dejected ones*, but this against usage of חֵל, and no such fig. sense of חַיִל, v. O1 Hup De Bae Che, and crit. n.)

\[Str2490] † I. חָלַל **vb.** bore, pierce (Ar. خَلَّ *perforate, pierce through, transfix*, Eth. ኅለት፡ (hollow) *read;* Aram. חֲלַל *hollow out*, חֲלִילָא *pipe;* ܚܠܻܝܠܴܐ adj. *hollow*, ܚܠܳܠܴܐ *cave*, ܚܶܠܬܳܐ *sheath*, etc.; NH in deriv. חָלָל n. *hollow*, adj. *slain*, חָלִיל *pipe*);—**Qal** *Pf.* לִבִּי חָלַל בְּקִרְבִּי *my heart is pierced* (wounded) *within me* [Psalm 109:22](https://ref.ly/Ps109:22) (? lit. *one has pierced my heart;* or rd. **Pu.** חֻלַּל ?); *Inf. cstr.* חַלּוֺתִי הִיא [Psalm 77:11](https://ref.ly/Ps77:11) *it is my piercing, my wound* (my woe, my cross; so Ew Hi De Bae MV SS Köi. 341, but Hu Pe BI Che rd. חֲלוֺתִי *my sickness*). **Pi.** *Pt.* pl. ## III חָלַל (id: BDB03089)

Hiph. transit.), cf. ## I חָלַם (id: BDB03094)

\[Str2492] † I. \[חָלַם] **vb.** be healthy, strong (NH *id.;* Aram. ܚܠܰܡ Ethp. *be made healthy, strong,* Aph. causat., also deriv.; חֲלַם *press firmly together*)—**Qal** *Impf.* יַחְלְמוּ בְנֵיהֶם [Jb 39:4](https://ref.ly/Job39:4) *their young are healthy.* **Hiph.** *Impf.* 2 ms. וְתַחֲלִימֵנִי [Is 38:16](https://ref.ly/Isa38:16) *and restore me to health* (‖וְהַחֲיֵ֫ינִי).

> \[Str2494] † חֵ֫לֶם **n.pr.m.** (*strength*)—a returned exile [Zc 6:14](https://ref.ly/Zech6:14) \= חֶלְדַּי v:[10](https://ref.ly/Zech6:10).

* **Associated Passages:** Job 39:4; Zechariah 6:10; Isaiah 38:16; Zechariah 6:14

[Dn 2:1](https://ref.ly/Dan2:1), [2:3](https://ref.ly/Dan2:3), old men in latter days [Jo 3:1](https://ref.ly/Joel3:1). **c.** of dreams of false prophets [Dt 13:2](https://ref.ly/Deut13:2), [13:4](https://ref.ly/Deut13:4), [13:6](https://ref.ly/Deut13:6), [Je 23:25](https://ref.ly/Jer23:25), [23:25](https://ref.ly/Jer23:25). **Hiph.** *Pt,* מַחְלְמִים *dream* (of false prophets, c. acc. cogn.) [Je 29:8](https://ref.ly/Jer29:8) (but rd. חֹלְמִים \[מ by dittogr. fr. אַתֶּם], Hi Gf Gie Gr).

> \[Str2495] חַלָּמוּת **n.f.** name of a plant, with thick, slimy juice, *purslain,* [Jb 6:6](https://ref.ly/Job6:6) RVm, so Thes Rob Ges al.; SyrVer ܚܠܰܡܬܳܐ, *anchusa* PSi, 1284; on this, and later interpr. v. Bö Di Löwpp. 165, 361; only אִם־יֶשׁ־טַעַם בְּרִיר חַלָּמוּת [Jb 6:6](https://ref.ly/Job6:6) *is there any taste in the juice of* ´ח (fig. of insipid and dull discourse); \> AV RV Ew Hi SS after Targum Saad Rabb. *in the white of an egg*.
> 
> 321:b. חַלָּמוּת, 1\. 6: the ref. is to Job’s sufferings (from which as little joy comes as from eating unsavoury food, so now Comm. generally), rather than to the unpalatable words of his friends. It is doubtful whether Di’s reason for preferring *purslain* to Vulgate חֶלְמוֺנָא *yolk* of egg (´רִיר ח: *slime of yolk*, i.e. the white of the egg) is convincing, viz. that ancient Hebrews did not keep hens, or that of De, viz. that white of egg is not *slime*, and is not unpalatable; mng. *yolk* preferred also by Da Bu Du.

## חָלַף (id: BDB03102)

\[Str2498] † חָלַף **vb.** pass on or away, pass through (mostly poet.) (NH *id., pass by* or *away, change;* Ph. חלפת *equivalent,* ReinachRÉJ 1888, 276; HoffmG. G. Abh. xxxvi. 1890, 20; Aram. חֲלֵף *pass away, change;* ܚܠܰܦ *change, substitute;* Nab. חלף *change* (?) Lane792–799; so also ויחלפה MI6 *and* his son *succeeded him;* Eth. ኀለፈ፡ *pass by, across, through*)—**Qal** *Pf.* חָלַף Ct [2:11](https://ref.ly/Song2:11) Hb [1:11](https://ref.ly/Hab1:11) etc.; *Impf.* יַחֲלֽף [Jb 4:15](https://ref.ly/Job4:15) \+ 4 t.; 3 fs. sf. תַּחְלְפֵָהוּ [Jb 20:24](https://ref.ly/Job20:24); יַחֲלָֽפוּ [Psalm 102:27](https://ref.ly/Ps102:27); *Inf. cstr.* לַחֲלוֺף [Is 21:1](https://ref.ly/Isa21:1); —**1\. a.** *pass on quickly* [1 S 10:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:3); elsewh. only poet., *move* or *sweep on,* of a flood [Is 8:8](https://ref.ly/Isa8:8), or wind [21:1](https://ref.ly/Isa21:1), [Jb 4:15](https://ref.ly/Job4:15), Hb [1:11](https://ref.ly/Hab1:11), of God [Jb 9:11](https://ref.ly/Job9:11), [11:10](https://ref.ly/Job11:10). **b.** *pass away* (*vanish*) [Jb 9:26](https://ref.ly/Job9:26) (of days); Ct [2:11](https://ref.ly/Song2:11) (of rain ‖עבר), of the heavens [Psalm 102:27](https://ref.ly/Ps102:27), of idols Is 2:l8, (but perhaps gloss JBLix. 1890, 86\). **2\.** of grass, *come on anew,* i. e. *sprout again* (cf. **Hiph. 2**) [Psalm 90:5](https://ref.ly/Ps90:5), [90:6](https://ref.ly/Ps90:6), so Thes AV De Hi Che Bae al.; less suitably in context, LXX Vulgate Ew *pass away*, supr. **1 b. 3\.** trans. **a.** *pass through*, i.e. pierce, sq. acc. Ju [5:26](https://ref.ly/Judg5:26), [Jb 20:24](https://ref.ly/Job20:24). **b.** *overstep, transgress* [Is 24:5](https://ref.ly/Isa24:5) (‖עבר), (cf. Ar. IV. to *break* a promise). **Pi.** *Impf.* וַיְחַלֵּף (*cause to pass*) *change,* sq. acc. garment [Gn 41:14](https://ref.ly/Gen41:14) (E) [2 S 12:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:20). **Hiph.** *Pf.* חֶחֱלִף [Gn 31:7](https://ref.ly/Gen31:7); *Impf.* יַחֲלִיף [Jb 14:7](https://ref.ly/Job14:7); sf. יַהֲּלִיפֶנּוּ [Lv 27:10](https://ref.ly/Lev27:10); 3 fs. תַּחֲלִיף [Jb 29:20](https://ref.ly/Job29:20); 2 ms. וַתַּחֲלֵף [Gn 31:41](https://ref.ly/Gen31:41); sf. תַּחֲלִיפֵם; יַחֲלִָיפוּ [Is 40:31](https://ref.ly/Isa40:31), [41:1](https://ref.ly/Isa41:1); נַחֲלִיף [Is 9:9](https://ref.ly/Isa9:9); —**1\.** *change* (trans.) garments [Gn 35:2](https://ref.ly/Gen35:2) (E) [Psalm 102:27](https://ref.ly/Ps102:27), wages [Gn 31:7](https://ref.ly/Gen31:7), [31:41](https://ref.ly/Gen31:41) (both E) ; no obj. expr. [Lv 27:10](https://ref.ly/Lev27:10) (H); *substitute* i. e. *cause to succeed* [Is 9:9](https://ref.ly/Isa9:9); *change* for better, *renew,* obj. כֹּחַ [Is 40:31](https://ref.ly/Isa40:31), [41:1](https://ref.ly/Isa41:1). **2\.** *shew newness,* of tree, putting forth fresh shoots [Jb 14:7](https://ref.ly/Job14:7), of bow [29:20](https://ref.ly/Job29:20).

Pa. etc.; Ph. n.pr. חלצבעל *Baal has rescued*, and חלץ alone; Ar. خَلَصَ *withdraw, retire*)—**Qal** *Pf.* [Ho 5:6](https://ref.ly/Hos5:6); וְחָֽלְצָה consec. [Dt 25:9](https://ref.ly/Deut25:9); חָֽלְצוּ [La 4:3](https://ref.ly/Lam4:3); *Impf.* 2 ms. תַּחֲלֽץ; *Pt. pass.* cstr. חֲלוּץ [Dt 25:10](https://ref.ly/Deut25:10); —**1\.** *draw, draw off,* c. acc. of sandal [Dt 25:9](https://ref.ly/Deut25:9), [Is 20:2](https://ref.ly/Isa20:2) (both sq. רֶגֶל c. מִן), cf. בֵּית חֲלוּץ הַָ\+נּעַל [Dt 25:10](https://ref.ly/Deut25:10); *draw out, present* the breast, of animal giving suck [La 4:3](https://ref.ly/Lam4:3). **2\.** intrans. *withdraw* (himself) sq. מִן [Ho 5:6](https://ref.ly/Hos5:6). **Niph.** *Pf. be delivered* נֶחֱלָץ [Pr 11:8](https://ref.ly/Prov11:8) (sq. מִן); *Impf.* יֵחָלֵָצוּ [Pr 11:9](https://ref.ly/Prov11:9); יֵחָֽלְצוּן [Psalm 60:7](https://ref.ly/Ps60:7) \= [108:7](https://ref.ly/Ps108:7) (Ges§ 51\. 2\. R. 2\). **Pi.** *Pf.* חִלֵּץ [Lv 14:43](https://ref.ly/Lev14:43) etc.; *Impf.* יְחַלֵּץ [Jb 36:15](https://ref.ly/Job36:15); וָאֲחַלְּצָה [Psalm 7:5](https://ref.ly/Ps7:5) (but on text vid. infr. ad fin.); sf. אֲחַלָּצְךָ [Psalm 50:15](https://ref.ly/Ps50:15); אֲחַלְּצֶ֫ךָּ [Psalm 81:8](https://ref.ly/Ps81:8), etc.; *Imv.* חַלְּצָה [Psalm 6:5](https://ref.ly/Ps6:5); חַלְּצֵנִי [Psalm 119:153](https://ref.ly/Ps119:153), [140:2](https://ref.ly/Ps140:2); —**1\.** *pull out, tear out* [Lv 14:40](https://ref.ly/Lev14:40), [14:43](https://ref.ly/Lev14:43) (P; stones fr. infested house). **2\.** *rescue, deliver,* c. obj. Niph. v:[20](https://ref.ly/Num32:20)) [2 Ch 20:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:21), [28:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:14), c. art. ´הֶח [Jos 6:7](https://ref.ly/Josh6:7), [6:9](https://ref.ly/Josh6:9), [6:13](https://ref.ly/Josh6:13) (JE); חֲלוּץ צָבָא [Nu 32:27](https://ref.ly/Num32:27) *men equipped for war* (JE), so הַמִּלְחָמָה ´ח [32:29](https://ref.ly/Num32:29) (P); לַצָּבָא ´הֶחָ [1 Ch 12:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:24) (v:[23](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:23) van d. H); pl. חֲלוּצֵי צָבָא [Nu 31:5](https://ref.ly/Num31:5) (P) [1 Ch 12:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:25) (v:[24](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:24), van d. H) [2 Ch 17:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:18); ´הַצּ ´ח [Jos 4:13](https://ref.ly/Josh4:13) (P); note חֲלֻצֵי מוֺאָב [Is 15:4](https://ref.ly/Isa15:4) *warriors* (*equipped ones,* Che *men\-at\-arms*) *of Moab.* **Niph.** *Impf.* 2 mpl. תֵּחָֽלְצוּ [Nu 32:20](https://ref.ly/Num32:20), 1 pl. נֵחָלֵץ [Nu 32:17](https://ref.ly/Num32:17); *Imv.* הֵחָֽלְצוּ [Nu 31:3](https://ref.ly/Num31:3); *be,* or *go equipped,* sq. לִפְנֵי [Nu 32:17](https://ref.ly/Num32:17), [32:20](https://ref.ly/Num32:20) (both JE ; v. Qal Pt. v:[21](https://ref.ly/Num32:21)); sq. לַצָּבָא [31:3](https://ref.ly/Num31:3) (P; Niph. Imv. here surprising with לממֵֶאִתְּכֶם, v. Di). **Hiph.** *Impf.*) עַצְמֹתֶיךָ יַחֲלִיץ [Is 58:11](https://ref.ly/Isa58:11) *he* (´י) *will brace up, in\-vigorate, thy bones* (MV De Di; Hup Che crit. n. Du al. rd. יִחֲלִיף *renew, rejuvenate*).

> \[Str2504] † \[חָלָץ] **n.\[f.]** only dual, loins (as seat of strength, vigour; cf. Aram. חֲרְצָא *hip, loin,* Syr. ܚܰܨܳܐ, NöZMG 1886, 741\)—חֲלָצָיִם [Is 32:11](https://ref.ly/Isa32:11); sf. חֲלָצֶיךָ [Gn 35:11](https://ref.ly/Gen35:11) \+ 4 t.; חֲלָצָיו [Is 5:27](https://ref.ly/Isa5:27) \+ 2 t.; חלצו Kt [Jb 31:20](https://ref.ly/Job31:20), Qr חֲלָצָיו;—*loins:* **1\.** as seat of virility ´יצא מן ח [Gn 35:11](https://ref.ly/Gen35:11) (P), [1 K 8:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:19), [2 Ch 6:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:9). **2\.** as girded אֱזָ־ר ´נָא כְּגֶבֶר ח [Jb 38:3](https://ref.ly/Job38:3), [40:7](https://ref.ly/Job40:7); אֵזוֺר חֲלָצָיו [Is 5:27](https://ref.ly/Isa5:27) (v. אֵזוֺר), וַחֲג֫וֺרָה עַל חֲלָצָיִם [32:11](https://ref.ly/Isa32:11), cf. [Jb 31:20](https://ref.ly/Job31:20) where the clothed loins are conceived as blessing charitable giver; metaph. [Is 11:5](https://ref.ly/Isa11:5) faithfulness waist\-cloth of his loins (‖מתניו). **3\.** as seat of pains, like a woman's in travail, [Je 30:6](https://ref.ly/Jer30:6).

## I חָלַק (id: BDB03116)

\[Str2505] † I. חָלַק **vb.** divide, share (NH *id.;* Aram. חֲלַק *field*, חוּלְּקָא *portion*, ܚܠܰܩ *divide, determine, decree;* Ar. خَلَقَ *measure, measure off;* As. *eḳlu, possession, field*, JägerBAS ii. 296; Eth. ኄለቄ፡: 1\. 2, is *enumerate,* ኁልቁ፡ ኁልቁ፡ *enumeration, number*)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ה [Dt 4:19](https://ref.ly/Deut4:19) \+ 5 t.; חָֽלְקוּ [Jos 18:2](https://ref.ly/Josh18:2); *Impf.* יַחֲלֽק [Jb 27:17](https://ref.ly/Job27:17), [Pr 17:2](https://ref.ly/Prov17:2); 2 ms. sf. וַתַּחְלְקֵם [Ne 9:22](https://ref.ly/Neh9:22); 3 pl. וַיַּחְלְקוּ [Jos 14:5](https://ref.ly/Josh14:5); יַחֲלָֽקוּ [1 S 30:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:24); וַיַּחְלְקוּם [1 Ch 24:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:4), [24:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:5); תַּחְלְקוּ [2 S 19:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:30); *Imv.* חִלְקוּ [Jos 22:8](https://ref.ly/Josh22:8); *Inf.* לַחֲלֽק [Ne 13:13](https://ref.ly/Neh13:13); *Pt.* חוֺלֵק [Pr 29:24](https://ref.ly/Prov29:24); —**1\.** *divide, apportion*, sq. acc. (of gods) and לְ Pers. [Dt 4:19](https://ref.ly/Deut4:19), [29:25](https://ref.ly/Deut29:25); acc. not expressed [Ne 13:13](https://ref.ly/Neh13:13) (ref. to offerings and tithes); c. acc. (of land) [Jos 14:5](https://ref.ly/Josh14:5) (P), [18:2](https://ref.ly/Josh18:2) (JE), [2 S 19:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:30): obj. people [Ne 9:22](https://ref.ly/Neh9:22); עִם ´ח *divide with* others [Jos 22:8](https://ref.ly/Josh22:8) (D ?). **2\.** *assign, distribute :* Levites over (על) temple [2 Ch 23:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:18), cf. [2 Ch 28:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:21) (v. Be). **Niph.** *Impf.* יֵחָלֵק [Nu 26:55](https://ref.ly/Num26:55), [1 K 16:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:21); יֵחָ֫לֶק [Jb 38:24](https://ref.ly/Job38:24); וַיֵּחָלֵק [Gn 14:15](https://ref.ly/Gen14:15); 3 fs. תֵּחָלֵק [Nu 26:53](https://ref.ly/Num26:53), [26:56](https://ref.ly/Num26:56); וַיֶּֽחָלְקֵם (Baer) [1 Ch 23:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:6), [24:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:3) cf. Be \& infr. sub **Pi.**;—**1\.** reflex., *divide oneself* [Gn 14:15](https://ref.ly/Gen14:15) (i.e. one's men). **2\.** pass. *be divided:* **a.** *apportioned,* the land [Nu 26:53](https://ref.ly/Num26:53), [26:55](https://ref.ly/Num26:55) (by lot) v:[56](https://ref.ly/Num26:56) (*id.*); all P. **b.** of people, *divided into* two factions [1 K 16:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:21). **c.** of light, *parted, spread abroad* [Jb 38:24](https://ref.ly/Job38:24). **3\.** trans. *assign, distribute* [1 Ch 23:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:6), [24:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:3), but trans. [1 Ch 23:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:6), [24:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:3) (v. supr.) **3\.** *scatter*, sq. acc. [Gn 49:7](https://ref.ly/Gen49:7) (poem) (‖הפיץ), [La 4:16](https://ref.ly/Lam4:16). **Pu.** *Pf.* הֻלַּק [Is 33:23](https://ref.ly/Isa33:23); וְחֻלַּק consec. [Zc 14:1](https://ref.ly/Zech14:1); *Impf.* 3 fs. תְּחֻלָּק [Am 7:17](https://ref.ly/Amos7:17); *be divided*, of land [Am 7:17](https://ref.ly/Amos7:17); spoil [Is 33:23](https://ref.ly/Isa33:23), [Zc 14:1](https://ref.ly/Zech14:1). **Hithp.** *Pf.* וְהִתְחַלְּקוּ consec. > \[Str2506] † I. חֵ֫לֶק **n.m.**:[Jos 18:5](https://ref.ly/Josh18:5) portion, tract, territory—´ח abs. [Gn 31:14](https://ref.ly/Gen31:14) \+; cstr. [Gn 14:24](https://ref.ly/Gen14:24) \+; sf. חֶלְקִי [Psalm 16:5](https://ref.ly/Ps16:5) \+ 6 t.; חֶלְקְךָ [Nu 18:20](https://ref.ly/Num18:20), [Ec 9:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:9); חֶלְקֶָךָ [Psalm 50:18](https://ref.ly/Ps50:18); חֶלְקֵח [Is 57:6](https://ref.ly/Isa57:6); חֶלְקוֺ Hb [1:16](https://ref.ly/Hab1:16) \+ 5 t.; חֶלְקָם [Gn 14:24](https://ref.ly/Gen14:24) \+ 3 t.; pl. חֲלָקִים [Jos 18:5](https://ref.ly/Josh18:5) \+ 5 t.; sf. חֶלְקֵיהֶם [Ho 5:7](https://ref.ly/Hos5:7); —**1\.** *portion, share:* **a.** of booty [Gn 14:24](https://ref.ly/Gen14:24), [14:24](https://ref.ly/Gen14:24), [Nu 31:36](https://ref.ly/Num31:36) (P) [1 S 30:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:24), [30:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:24). **b.** of food [Lv 6:10](https://ref.ly/Lev6:10) (P); יזכלי ´כּח ´ח [Dt 18:8](https://ref.ly/Deut18:8); \= food Hb [1:16](https://ref.ly/Hab1:16) (‖מַאֲכָל); so (of unrestricted charity) [Ec 11:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:2) v. Now. **c.** sq. בְּ, *share* or *interest in* [Ec 9:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:6), with idea of *obligation to* [Gn 31:14](https://ref.ly/Gen31:14) (E; in father's house), [2 S 20:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:1) \= [1 K 12:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:16) \= [2 Ch 10:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:16) (in royal line; all ‖נַחֲלָה); of *right* (*privilege*) *in* ביהוה ´ח [Jos 22:25](https://ref.ly/Josh22:25), [22:27](https://ref.ly/Josh22:27) (P); [Ne 2:20](https://ref.ly/Neh2:20) (in Jerus.; \+ צְדָקָה וְזִכָּרוֺן), **d.** proper *share* or *part* אַעֲנֶה חֶלְקִי Jb 32:l7 i.e. I will do my part in replying. Esp. **2\.** *portion, tract,* of land: **a.** as distrib. at conquest [Jos 19:9](https://ref.ly/Josh19:9), seven *portions* [18:5](https://ref.ly/Josh18:5), [18:6](https://ref.ly/Josh18:6), [18:9](https://ref.ly/Josh18:9) (all JE) v:[7](https://ref.ly/Josh18:7) (JE; \+ נחלה in ‖ cl.), [15:13](https://ref.ly/Josh15:13), [19:9](https://ref.ly/Josh19:9) (both P); none for tribe of Levi [Dt 10:9](https://ref.ly/Deut10:9), [12:12](https://ref.ly/Deut12:12), [14:27](https://ref.ly/Deut14:27), [14:29](https://ref.ly/Deut14:29), [18:1](https://ref.ly/Deut18:1), [Jos 18:7](https://ref.ly/Josh18:7) (all D; ‖נחלה), [Jos 14:4](https://ref.ly/Josh14:4) (P), none for priests [Nu 18:20](https://ref.ly/Num18:20) (P; ‖ vb. נחל). **b.** of land possessed by people [Ho 5:7](https://ref.ly/Hos5:7), [Mi 2:4](https://ref.ly/Mic2:4). **c.** portion to be assigned after exile [Is 61:7](https://ref.ly/Isa61:7), Ez [45:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:7), [48:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:8), [48:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:21), חֶלְקוֺ עַל־אַדְמַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ [Zc 2:16](https://ref.ly/Zech2:16). **d.** specif. of *town\-land,* district about a town, יִזְרְעֵאל ´ח [2 K 9:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:10), [9:36](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:36) \+ [1 K 21:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:23) (MT חֵל), ´יִזְ ´הַשָּדֶה בְּח v:[37](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:37). **e.** הַחֵלֶק [Am 7:4](https://ref.ly/Amos7:4) (opp. תְּהוֺם) appar. of *land* of Isr. **3\.** hence *portion,* i.e. (acquired) *possession:* **a.** of people as ´י's possession [Dt 32:9](https://ref.ly/Deut32:9) (‖חֶבֶל נחלתו); but esp. **b.** of ´י as possession of his servants: of priests, as enjoying perquisites of altar [Nu 18:20](https://ref.ly/Num18:20) (P; ‖נחלה), in spiritual sense [Psalm 73:26](https://ref.ly/Ps73:26), [119:57](https://ref.ly/Ps119:57), [La 3:24](https://ref.ly/Lam3:24); מְנָת חֶלְקִי ´י [Psalm 16:5](https://ref.ly/Ps16:5); בְּאֶרֶץ הַחַיִּים ´ח [142:6](https://ref.ly/Ps142:6); hence יעקב ´ח of ´י [Je 10:16](https://ref.ly/Jer10:16) \= [51:19](https://ref.ly/Jer51:19) (שֵׁבֶט נַחֲלָתוֺ in ‖ cl.) **4\.** chosen *portion,* habitual mode of life ´עִם־מְנָאֲפִים ח [Psalm 50:18](https://ref.ly/Ps50:18) *with adulterers is thy chosen life* (companionship, shewing character); בְּחַלְּקֵי־נַחַל חֶלְקֵח [Is 57:6](https://ref.ly/Isa57:6) *among smooth* (things i.e. stones) *of a brook\-bed is thy chosen life* (of idolatrous habit; v. חָלָק); חֶלְקָם בַּחַיִּים [Psalm 17:14](https://ref.ly/Ps17:14). **5\.** *portion, award,* from God אֱלוֺהַּ ´ח [Jb 31:2](https://ref.ly/Job31:2) (‖נחלה); specif. of punishment of wicked [Is 17:14](https://ref.ly/Isa17:14) (‖גּוֺרָל), עִם־אֵל [Jb 27:13](https://ref.ly/Job27:13), מֵאֱלֽהִים [20:29](https://ref.ly/Job20:29) (‖נחלה); \= *profit, reward* חֶלְקִי מִכָּל־עֲמָלִי [Ec 2:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:10), cf. v:[21](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:21), [3:22](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:22), [5:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:17), [5:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:18), [9:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:9).—לְחֵלָק [Jb 17:5](https://ref.ly/Job17:5) is dub.: *a share* of feast, or of booty, Kmp Hi; *a prey, spoil,* Ew Di Da; AV *flattery* does not suit context.

> \[Str2513] † I. חֶלְקָה **n.f.** portion of ground;—abs. ´ח [Am 4:7](https://ref.ly/Amos4:7), [4:7](https://ref.ly/Amos4:7) \+ 8 t.; cstr. חֶלְקַת [Gn 33:19](https://ref.ly/Gen33:19) \+ 12 t.; sf. חֶלְקָתִי [Je 12:10](https://ref.ly/Jer12:10); חֶלְקָתָם [Jb 24:18](https://ref.ly/Job24:18); *portion* of ground, **1\.** esp. **a.** הַשָּרֶה ´ח \= the clearly divided *field* [Gn 33:19](https://ref.ly/Gen33:19), [Jos 24:32](https://ref.ly/Josh24:32) (both E), [2 S 23:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:11), [Ru 2:3](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:3), [4:3](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:3), [2 K 9:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:25), [1 Ch 11:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:13). > \[Str4256] מַחֲלֽ֫קֶת **n.f.** division, course (almost wholly Chr.)—´מ abs. [1 Ch 27:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:1), [27:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:2); cstr. [1 Ch 27:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:4); sf. מַחֲלֻקְתּוֺ [1 Ch 27:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:2) \+ 14 t. 1 Ch 27; abs. מַחְלְקוֺת [1 Ch 23:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:6) \+ 6 t. Ch \+ [Ne 11:36](https://ref.ly/Neh11:36); sf. מַחְלְקוֺתֵיכֶם [2 Ch 35:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:4); מַחְלְקוֺתֵיהֶם [2 Ch 31:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:16), [31:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:17); מַחְלְקוֺתָם [1 Ch 24:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:1) \+ 3 t. in Ch; מַחְלְקֹתָם [Jos 11:23](https://ref.ly/Josh11:23), [12:7](https://ref.ly/Josh12:7), [18:10](https://ref.ly/Josh18:10), Ez [48:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:29); —**1\.** *division, part,* of land assigned to the several tribes of Isr. [Jos 11:23](https://ref.ly/Josh11:23), [12:7](https://ref.ly/Josh12:7) ( both D) [18:10](https://ref.ly/Josh18:10) (JE; others, divisions of Isr., but v.) Ez [48:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:29). **2\.** techn. term of organization of priests and Levites (late), [1 Ch 23:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:6), [24:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:1), [26:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:1), [26:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:12), [26:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:19), [2 Ch 8:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:14), [8:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:14), [31:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:2); [31:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:2), [35:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:4), [35:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:10), [Ne 11:36](https://ref.ly/Neh11:36) \+ 26 t. in Ch.—סֶלַע הַמַּחְלְקוֺת n.pr. [1 S 23:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:28) was popularly connected with this √, \= *rock of divisions,* where Saul and David parted from neighbourhood of each other (Dr) so Vrss Th al.; Bö Klo, where their forces lay divided from each other; orig. connex. was perh. with II. חלק, *smooth, slippery;* Ges De W Ke al. expl. here as *rock of escapes,* but this meaning for חלק dubious.

## II חָלַק (id: BDB03127)

\[Str2505] II. חָלַק **vb.** be smooth, slippery (NH adj. חָלָק *smooth, empty,* Ar. خَلَقَ *make smooth, lie, forge, fabricate*)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ח [Ho 10:2](https://ref.ly/Hos10:2); 3 mpl. חָֽלְקוּ [Psalm 55:22](https://ref.ly/Ps55:22); —*smooth, slippery,* only fig.: [Ho 10:2](https://ref.ly/Hos10:2) of heart of faithless Isr. (so Ew Ke Wü Now RVm), We *false;* \>Vrss (not LXX) Hi St who rd. חֻלַּק, *divided* is their heart; LXX ἐμέρισαν; of words, *smooth* [Psalm 55:22](https://ref.ly/Ps55:22) i.e. deceptive (‖רַכּוּ דְבָרָיו מִשֶּׁמֶן). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הֶחֱלִיק [Psalm 36:3](https://ref.ly/Ps36:3), הֶחֱלִיקָה [Pr 2:16](https://ref.ly/Prov2:16), [7:5](https://ref.ly/Prov7:5); *Impf.* יַחֲלִיקוּן [Psalm 5:10](https://ref.ly/Ps5:10); *Pt.* מַחֲלִיק [Pr 28:23](https://ref.ly/Prov28:23) \+ 2 t.; *make smooth:* **1\.** lit. of idol\-maker [Is 41:7](https://ref.ly/Isa41:7) c. acc. instr. פַּטִּישׁ *hammer.* **2\.** fig. *the tongue,* i.e. flatter with it [Psalm 5:10](https://ref.ly/Ps5:10), [Pr 28:23](https://ref.ly/Prov28:23), words [Pr 2:16](https://ref.ly/Prov2:16), [7:5](https://ref.ly/Prov7:5) אֲמָרֶיהָ הֶחֱלִיקָה; abs. *deal smoothly,* sq. אֶ־ל [Psalm 36:3](https://ref.ly/Ps36:3), עַל [Pr 29:5](https://ref.ly/Prov29:5).

> \[Str2506] † III. חֵ֫לֶק **n.\[m.]** smoothness, seductiveness of speech, only sg. cstr. בְּחֵלֶק שְׁפָתֶיהָ תַּדִּיחֶָנּוּ [Pr 7:21](https://ref.ly/Prov7:21) *with the seductiveness of her lips she impelleth him* (‖הִטַּתּוּ בְּרֹב לִקְחָהּ).—I. and II. חֵלָק v. sub I. חלק.

> \[Str2509–10,2511] † חָלָק **adj.** smooth—**1\.** lit. אִישׁ חָלָק [Gn 27:11](https://ref.ly/Gen27:11) (J), *smooth man* (opp. אישׁ שָעִר); הָהָר הֶחָלָק [Jos 11:17](https://ref.ly/Josh11:17), [12:7](https://ref.ly/Josh12:7) *the bald mt.* (both D); southern limit of Jos.'s conquest, identif. dub.; (1\) white cliffs 8 m. S. of Dead Sea, and (2\) Mt. Mâdara SW. fr. Pass es\-Ṣafâ are proposed, v. Di.; as subst. חַלְּקֵי־נַחַל [Is 57:6](https://ref.ly/Isa57:6) (v. I. חֵלָק, **4**) *smooth* (stones) of a brook\-bed (ravine, wady), i.e. thou worshippest common round stones (contempt., v. Che Di). **2\.** fig. of mouth uttering smooth words וְחָלָק מִשֶּׁמֶן חִכָּהּ [Pr 5:3](https://ref.ly/Prov5:3) cf. [26:28](https://ref.ly/Prov26:28). **3\.** as subst. \= *flattery* מִקְסָם חָלָק Ez [12:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:24) *divination of flattery*.

> \[Str2513] † II. \[חֶלְקָה] **n.f.** smooth part, smoothness, flattery;—cstr. חֶלְקַת [Gn 27:16](https://ref.ly/Gen27:16), [Pr 6:24](https://ref.ly/Prov6:24); pl. חֲלָקוֺת [Psalm 12:3](https://ref.ly/Ps12:3) \+ 3 t.;—**1\.** *smoothness, smooth part* חֶלְקַת צַוָּארָיו [Gn 27:16](https://ref.ly/Gen27:16). **2\.** pl. *slippery places* [Psalm 73:18](https://ref.ly/Ps73:18) fig. of situation of wicked. **3\.** *smoothness* \= *flattery* ´שְפַת חִ [Psalm 12:3](https://ref.ly/Ps12:3), cf. v:[4](https://ref.ly/Ps12:4); חֶלְקַת לָשׁוֺן [Pr 6:24](https://ref.ly/Prov6:24); \= subst. pl. *smooth things,* i.e. *agreeable, flattering things* [Is 30:10](https://ref.ly/Isa30:10).—1\. חֶלְקָה v. sub I. חלק.

> \[Str2512] † \[חַלֻּק] **adj.** smooth, only pl. cstr. חַלֻּקֵי אֲבָנִים [1 S 17:40](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:40) *smooth ones of* (*among*) *stones* \= *smooth* (or *smoothest* ?) *stones* Ges§§ 132 R 2\. 133, 3 DrSm.

> \[Str2514] † \[חֲלַקָּה] **n.f.** smoothness, flattery, only pl. abs.: fine promises, יַחֲנִיף בַּחֲלַקָּוֺת [Dn 11:32](https://ref.ly/Dan11:32) (perhaps directly from חָלָק, exceptional pl., cf. גְּמַלִּים, קְטַנָּה, קְטַנִּים, קְטַנּוֺת).

> \[Str2519] † חֲלַקְלַקּוֺת **n.pl.f.abstr.** smoothness, slipperiness, flattery, fine promises; **1\.** *slipperiness* יהי־דרכם חשׁך וַחֲלַקְלַקֹּת [Psalm 35:6](https://ref.ly/Ps35:6); יהיה דרכם להם כַּחֲלַקְלַקּוֺת [Je 23:12](https://ref.ly/Jer23:12). **2\.** *fine promises* והחזיק מלכוּת בַּחֲלַקְלַקּוֺה [Dn 11:21](https://ref.ly/Dan11:21); cf. [11:34](https://ref.ly/Dan11:34).

> \[Str5555] † II. מַחְלְקוֺת **n.f.pl.** smoothness, ´סֶלַע הַמּ [1 S 23:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:28) *rock of smoothness,* i.e. *slippery rock,* perhaps original meaning of this n.pr., v. further I. מחלקוֺת sub I. חלק.

## חָלַשׁ (id: BDB03135)

\[Str2522] † \[חָלַשׁ] **vb. 1\.** be weak, prostrate. **2\.** (si vera l.) weaken, disable, prostrate (Aram. חֲלַשׁ *be weak;* ܚܰܠܳܫ, חַלָּשׁ *weak,* as in Heb.; Ar. مُحْلِس *poor* (Frey))—**Qal** *Impf.* וַיַּחֲלשׁ [Ex 17:13](https://ref.ly/Exod17:13), וַיֶּחֱלָשׁ [Jb 14:10](https://ref.ly/Job14:10); *Pt.* חוֺלֵשׁ [Is 14:12](https://ref.ly/Isa14:12); **1\.** ´ונבר ימות ויח [Jb 14:10](https://ref.ly/Job14:10) *but man dieth and is prostrate.* **2\.** *disable, prostrate,* sq. acc. Pers. [Ex 17:13](https://ref.ly/Exod17:13) (E; point as **Hiph.** ?); sq. עַל Pers. (strangely) [Is 14:12](https://ref.ly/Isa14:12).

> \[Str2523] † חַלָּשׁ **adj.** weak, only c. art. as subst., הַחַלָּשׁ יאמר גבור אני [Jo 4:10](https://ref.ly/Joel4:10) *the weak, let him say, I am a mighty man.*

> \[Str2476] † חֲלוּשָׁה **n.f.** weakness, prostration, אֵין ´קוֺל עֲנוֺת ח [Ex 32:18](https://ref.ly/Exod32:18) *it is not the sound of the answering of weakness* (JE; opp. גְּבוּרָה).

> \[Str2526] † I. חָם **n.pr.Pers. m.** and **loc.** (cf. Sab. חם Hal243)—LXX Χαμ:—**1\.** Ham, 2nd son of Noah [Gn 5:32](https://ref.ly/Gen5:32), [6:10](https://ref.ly/Gen6:10), [7:13](https://ref.ly/Gen7:13), [9:18](https://ref.ly/Gen9:18), [9:18](https://ref.ly/Gen9:18), [1 Ch 1:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:4); called father of Canaan [Gn 9:22](https://ref.ly/Gen9:22) and of various peoples [10:1](https://ref.ly/Gen10:1), [10:6](https://ref.ly/Gen10:6), [10:20](https://ref.ly/Gen10:20), [1 Ch 1:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:8), cf. מִן־חָם הַיּשְׁבִים שָׁם [4:40](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:40); these peoples were inhabitants of southern lands, or related historically or politically to such inhabitants; v. כּוּשׁ, כְּנַעַן, מִצְרַיִם, פּוּט, etc. **2\.** in late PsalmPsalm a name (coll.) for Egyptians:—´אָֽהֳלֵיאח [Psalm 78:51](https://ref.ly/Ps78:51); ´אֶרֶץ־ח [105:23](https://ref.ly/Ps105:23), [105:27](https://ref.ly/Ps105:27), [106:22](https://ref.ly/Ps106:22) (all ‖מִצְרַיִם, exc. [105:27](https://ref.ly/Ps105:27)).—On historical significance, and attempts at etymol. v. DIGen. ed. 6, chap.10, esp. pp. l65, 168 f., HomSV i. 427 BuUrg. 323 WiedemannÄgypt. Gesch. i. 22\.

## חמא (id: BDB03142)

חמא (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. خَمَا *be hard,* used of curdled milk, Frey).

> \[Str2529] † חֶמְאָהֹ **n.f.** curd;—´ח [Gn 18:8](https://ref.ly/Gen18:8) \+ 6 t.; \= חֵמָה [Jb 29:6](https://ref.ly/Job29:6); cstr. חֶמְאַה [Dt 32:14](https://ref.ly/Deut32:14); *curd, curdled milk,* mod. *labben* ´מִיץ חָלָב יוֺצְיא ח [Pr 30:33](https://ref.ly/Prov30:33), elsewhere joined with חָלָב [Gn 18:8](https://ref.ly/Gen18:8) (J) [Dt 32:14](https://ref.ly/Deut32:14) (poem ; תֶמְאַת בָּקָר וַחֲלֵב צאֹן), Ju [5:25](https://ref.ly/Judg5:25) (where served בְּסֵפֶל אַדִּירִים, v. ספל); with דְּבַשׁ [2 S 17:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:29), [Is 7:15](https://ref.ly/Isa7:15), [7:22](https://ref.ly/Isa7:22), [7:22](https://ref.ly/Isa7:22); cf. ´נַחֲלֵי דבשׁ וח fig. of abundance [Jb 20:17](https://ref.ly/Job20:17); בִּרְחֹץ חֲלִיכַי בְּחֵמָה [Jb 29:6](https://ref.ly/Job29:6) (‖צוּר יָצוּק :פַּלְגֵי־שָֽׁמֶן).
> 
> 326:a. חֶמְאָה: on mode of making *lebben*, by shaking milk in sour milk\-skin, v. DoughtyArab. Des. i. 221, 263; ii. 304, etc. ThomsonLand and Book ii. 418 See also ARSKEncy. Bib. MILK.

(incl. [Ex 20:17](https://ref.ly/Exod20:17), [20:17](https://ref.ly/Exod20:17) (E) \= [Dt 5:18](https://ref.ly/Deut5:18) (v:[18](https://ref.ly/Deut5:18) b has תִּתְאַוֶּה), [Ex 34:24](https://ref.ly/Exod34:24) (JE) [Dt 7:25](https://ref.ly/Deut7:25), [Jos 7:21](https://ref.ly/Josh7:21) (JE) [Mi 2:2](https://ref.ly/Mic2:2), [Pr 12:12](https://ref.ly/Prov12:12); of lustful desire [Pr 6:25](https://ref.ly/Prov6:25). **b.** \= *take pleasure in,* of idolatrous tendency [Is 1:29](https://ref.ly/Isa1:29), so pt. pl. חֲמוּדֵיהֵם [Is 44:9](https://ref.ly/Isa44:9) *their delightful things* (things in which they delight, v. also v11and BevJPh. xvii. 1888\. p. 126\); לֵצִים לָצוֺן חָֽמְדוּ [Pr 1:22](https://ref.ly/Prov1:22) how long do *scorners delight in scoring.* **c.** less often in good sense, said of God לְשִׁבְתּוֺ ´הָהָר חָמַד א, poet. [Psalm 68:17](https://ref.ly/Ps68:17); obj. the suffering servant of ´י, [Is 53:2](https://ref.ly/Isa53:2) no beauty in him, *that we should desire him* (choose him, be drawn toward him); pt. pass. coll. חֲמוּדוֺ [Psalm 39:12](https://ref.ly/Ps39:12) *his desired* things, i. e. chosen, choice, desirable (v. ## I חמה (id: BDB03152)

I. חמה (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. حَمَى *protect, guard,* RSK 136 LagBN 156; DlPr 91; BAS ii. 43 comp. an As. *ê mû, surround, guard,* cf. Mod. Syr. ܚܡܐ *id.;* Eth. ሐመወ፡ III. 1 means *contract affinity, be joined by affinity,* prob. denom.)

> \[Str2524] † II. \[חָם] **n.m.** husband's father (NH *id.,* of husband's or wife's father, so Aram. חֲמָא; Ar.حَمٌّ *husband's male relation* (father, brother, paternal uncle), but also *wife's father,* etc.; Eth. ሐም፡ As. *êmû*, ZimBP 48\);—only sf. חָמִ֫יךָ [Gn 38:13](https://ref.ly/Gen38:13), [38:25](https://ref.ly/Gen38:25), of Judah as Tamar's father\-in\-law; חָמִיהָ [1 S 4:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:19), [4:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:21) of Eli, father\-in\-law of Phinehas' wife.

> \[Str2545] † \[חָמוֺת] **n.f.** husband's mother (NH חָמוֺת, Aram. חֲמָתָא; Ar. حَمَاةٌ *husband's mother*; As. *êmêtu*, ZimBP 48; Eth. ሐምኣት፡)—only sf. חֲמוֺתֵךְ [Ru 2:11](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:11), [3:17](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:17); חֲמוֺתָהּ [Ru 1:14](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:14) \+ 7 t.; חֲמֹתָהּ [Mi 7:6](https://ref.ly/Mic7:6); —of Naomi [Ru 1:14](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:14), [2:11](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:11), [2:18](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:18), [2:19](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:19), [2:19](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:19), [2:23](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:23), [3:1](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:1), [3:6](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:6), [3:16](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:16), [3:17](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:17); בַּת קָמָ֫ה בְאִמָּ֔הּ כַּלָּה בַּחֲמֹתָהּ [Mi 7:6](https://ref.ly/Mic7:6).

O1:§ 113 a);—**1\.** usu. term for *wall* of city [Dt 28:52](https://ref.ly/Deut28:52), [Jos 2:15](https://ref.ly/Josh2:15), [6:5](https://ref.ly/Josh6:5), [6:20](https://ref.ly/Josh6:20) (all JE), [1 S 31:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:10), [31:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:12), [2 S 11:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:20), [11:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:21), [11:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:21), [2 K 18:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:26), [18:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:27) \= [Is 36:11](https://ref.ly/Isa36:11), [36:12](https://ref.ly/Isa36:12), [Am 1:7](https://ref.ly/Amos1:7), [1:10](https://ref.ly/Amos1:10), [1:14](https://ref.ly/Amos1:14), [Is 2:15](https://ref.ly/Isa2:15), [20:10](https://ref.ly/Isa20:10), [20:11](https://ref.ly/Isa20:11), [25:12](https://ref.ly/Isa25:12), [Na 2:6](https://ref.ly/Nah2:6), [3:8](https://ref.ly/Nah3:8), Ez [26:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:9) \+, [Je 1:15](https://ref.ly/Jer1:15) \+, [Jo 2:7](https://ref.ly/Joel2:7), [2:9](https://ref.ly/Joel2:9); בֵּין הַחֹמֹתַיִם (of Jerus.) i.e. *between the two walls* of the Ophel and the SW. hill respectively, of a reservoir [Is 22:11](https://ref.ly/Isa22:11), a gate [2 K 25:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:4) \= [Je 34:4](https://ref.ly/Jer34:4) \= [52:7](https://ref.ly/Jer52:7); symbol of Isr., [Am 7:7](https://ref.ly/Amos7:7); [2 Ch 8:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:5), [14:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:6), [25:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:23), [26:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:6), [26:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:6), [26:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:6), [Psalm 51:20](https://ref.ly/Ps51:20), [Ne 1:3](https://ref.ly/Neh1:3) \+ 31 t. in Ne; חוֺמַת אֲנָךְ [Am 7:7](https://ref.ly/Amos7:7) (lit. *wall of a plummet*) is of doubtful mng.: *a wall built plumb,* or *by means of a plumb\-line ?* We thinks unintelligible; עִיר חוֺמָה \= *walled city,* [Lv 25:29](https://ref.ly/Lev25:29), ´עִיר אשׁר לאֹ ח v:[30](https://ref.ly/Lev25:30) (לאֹ \= לוֺ), opp. ´הַחֲצֵרִים אשׁר אין להם ח v:[31](https://ref.ly/Lev25:31) (all H); more elaborately גְבֹהָה דְּלָתַיִם וּבְרִָיחַ ´עָרִים בְּצֻרֹת ח [Dt 3:5](https://ref.ly/Deut3:5), cf.2 Ch [8:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:5); גְּדֹלוֺת חוֺמָה וּבְרִיחַ נְחשֶׁת ´ע [1 K 4:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:13); ´בְצוּרָה ח [Is 2:15](https://ref.ly/Isa2:15); ´ח sg. of wall of Jerus. 1 K 3:l \+ oft.; contempt. חוֺמַת אַבְנֵיהֶם [Ne 3:35](https://ref.ly/Neh3:35); less oft. pl. [Ne 2:13](https://ref.ly/Neh2:13) \+ ; of specific portions of wall הָעֹפֶל ´ח [2 Ch 27:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr27:3), [Ne 3:27](https://ref.ly/Neh3:27); בְּרֵבַת הַשֶּׁלַח ´ח [Ne 3:15](https://ref.ly/Neh3:15); ´הַח הָֽרְחָבָה [3:8](https://ref.ly/Neh3:8), [12:38](https://ref.ly/Neh12:38); also (generally) of Babylon [Je 51:58](https://ref.ly/Jer51:58).—Men *build,* בנה, the ´ח [1 K 9:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:15) \+, esp. (of rebuilding), [Ne 2:17](https://ref.ly/Neh2:17) \+; fortify it בִּצֵּר [Is 22:10](https://ref.ly/Isa22:10); it is *joined together* קשׁר [Ne 3:38](https://ref.ly/Neh3:38); is *repaired* ´עָֽלְתָה אֲרוּכָה לְח Ne 4:l; it *falls* נפל [Jos 6:5](https://ref.ly/Josh6:5), [6:20](https://ref.ly/Josh6:20), [1 K 20:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:30), Ez [38:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:20); the enemy *makes it fall* הִפִּיל [2 S 20:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:15) (preceded by מַשְׁחִית si vera l., v. Dr); *destroys* it הִשְׁחִית [La 2:8](https://ref.ly/Lam2:8); שִׁהֵת Ez [26:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:4); *makes a breach in it* ´פָּרַץ בּ [2 K 14:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:13) \= [2 Ch 25:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:23), cf. פרץ c. acc. [2 Ch 26:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:6), [Ne 3:35](https://ref.ly/Neh3:35), and ´וֶיִּבֶן אֶת־כל־הח הַפְּרוּחָה [2 Ch 32:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:5); also (in sim.) ´עִיר פְּרוּחָה ואין ח [Pr 25:28](https://ref.ly/Prov25:28); and Pu. pt. מְפֹרָצֶת [Ne 1:3](https://ref.ly/Neh1:3); one *breakes it down* נָתַץ [2 K 25:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:10), [2 Ch 36:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:19), [Je 39:8](https://ref.ly/Jer39:8), [52:14](https://ref.ly/Jer52:14); *tears it down* הָרַס Ez [26:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:12), cf. pass. [Je 50:15](https://ref.ly/Jer50:15); Amos predicts that ´י will *send fire* into wall (of Gaza, etc.): ´וְשִׁלַּחְתִּי אֵשׁ בְּח [Am 1:7](https://ref.ly/Amos1:7), [1:10](https://ref.ly/Amos1:10); or *kindle fire in* ´וְהִצַּתִּי אֵשׁ בְּח v:[14](https://ref.ly/Amos1:14), so [Je 49:27](https://ref.ly/Jer49:27). **2\.** *wall* of a building: **a.** citadel, fortress; ארמנותיה ´ח [La 2:7](https://ref.ly/Lam2:7). **b.** surrounding new temple Ez [40:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:5), [42:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:20). **3\.** fig. of waters of Red Sea [Ex 14:22](https://ref.ly/Exod14:22), [14:29](https://ref.ly/Exod14:29) (P), cf. [Na 3:8](https://ref.ly/Nah3:8); of David's men as protectors of Nabal's shepherds [1 S 25:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:16); of prophet as object of assault חוֺמַת נְחשֶׁת [Je 1:18](https://ref.ly/Jer1:18); נחשׁת בְּצוּרָה ´ח [Je 15:20](https://ref.ly/Jer15:20); of strong, virtuous woman Ct [8:9](https://ref.ly/Song8:9), [8:10](https://ref.ly/Song8:10); of salvation, יְשׁוּעָה, as defence Is 26:l (‖חֵל); of ´י himself ´ח אֵשׁ [Zc 2:9](https://ref.ly/Zech2:9); a dangerous polit. scheme is called breach in נִשְׁגָּבָה ´ח [Is 30:13](https://ref.ly/Isa30:13); נִשְגָּבָה ´ח in sim. of wealth [Pr 18:11](https://ref.ly/Prov18:11) (‖קִרְיַת עֻוּוֺ); a reckless man is ´עִיר פְּרוּחָה אֵין ח [Pr 25:28](https://ref.ly/Prov25:28).

## חמט (id: BDB03161)

חמט (√ of foll.; cf. As. *ḫamâ ṭu, hasten,* PräZMG 1874, 88 ff., *ḫamṭu,* swift, ZimBP 84 n.; Aram. חֲמַט, חֲמֵיט is *sink* or *fall to the ground, kneel*).

> \[Str2546] † חֹ֫מֶט **n.\[m.]** a kind of lizard, only in list of unclean creeping animals [Lv 11:30](https://ref.ly/Lev11:30) וְהָאֲנָקָה :וְהַכֹּחַ וְהַלְּטָאָה וְהַחֹמֶט וְהַתִּנְשָֽׁמֶת (Aram. ܚܽܘܠܡܳܛܳܐ is *chameleon*).

> \[Str2547] † חֻמְטָה **n.pr.loc.** city in Judah [Jos 15:54](https://ref.ly/Josh15:54), LXX Εὐμα, LXXL Αμματα; site unknown.

> \[Str2537] חֲמִיטַל v. חמוטל supr. p. 327

## חָמַם (id: BDB03171)

\[Str2552] † \[חָמַם] **vb.** be or become warm (NH *id.,* Pi., *make warm,* Aram. ܚܰܡ, חֲמַם *be warm*; Ar. حَمَّ *become hot,* of water; also *heat, kindle fire in*)—**Qal** *Pf.* חַם [Psalm 39:4](https://ref.ly/Ps39:4), [Ex 16:21](https://ref.ly/Exod16:21); וְחַם consec. [1 K 1:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:2), [Ec 4:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:11); חַמּוֺתִ֫י [Is 44:16](https://ref.ly/Isa44:16); *Impf.* יָחֹם [Is 44:16](https://ref.ly/Isa44:16); וַיָּ֫חָם [2 K 4:34](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:34), [Is 44:15](https://ref.ly/Isa44:15); also יִחַם [1 K 1:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:1); יֵחַם [Dt 19:6](https://ref.ly/Deut19:6); יֵחָם [Ec 4:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:11); 3 fs. תֵּחַם Ez [24:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:11); יֵחַמּוּ [Ho 7:7](https://ref.ly/Hos7:7); וַיֶּחֱמוּ [Gn 30:39](https://ref.ly/Gen30:39); וַיֵּחַמְנָה v:[38](https://ref.ly/Gen30:38) (last seven forms could be also from יחם q.v.; but no decisive evidence that this √ is used in Qal; they are placed under חמם by Thes Ew§ 193 a Ol:§ 243 b; v. also Köi. 365, 417 ff., who on account of יחם Pi. Inf. [Gn 30:41](https://ref.ly/Gen30:41), [31:10](https://ref.ly/Gen31:10), der. last two, needlessly, fr. יחם); *Inf.* לַחְמָם [Is 47:14](https://ref.ly/Isa47:14), cf. [Jb 30:4](https://ref.ly/Job30:4) (where, however, ´ל is fr. לֶחֶם Mich Di unless (Luzz Che) we read לְחַמֵּם Pi. Inf. in both); חֹם [1 S 11:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:9), [Hg 1:6](https://ref.ly/Hag1:6), [Ne 7:3](https://ref.ly/Neh7:3); sf. חֻמּוֺ [Jb 6:17](https://ref.ly/Job6:17); חֻמָּם [Je 51:39](https://ref.ly/Jer51:39); —**1\.** lit., *be* or *grow warm* [Ex 16:21](https://ref.ly/Exod16:21) (P; subj. שֶׁמֶשׁ), cf. [1 S 11:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:9), [Ne 7:3](https://ref.ly/Neh7:3) and Jb 6:l7 (sf. of impers. subj.); from fire [Is 44:15](https://ref.ly/Isa44:15), [44:16](https://ref.ly/Isa44:16), [44:16](https://ref.ly/Isa44:16), [47:14](https://ref.ly/Isa47:14) (cf. supr.); natural heat [1 K 1:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:1), [1:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:2) (by personal contact), so [2 K 4:34](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:34), [Ec 4:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:11), [4:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:11), fr. clothing [Hg 1:6](https://ref.ly/Hag1:6); of Jerusalem under fig. of pot or caldron Ez [24:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:11). **2\.** fig., subj. לִבִּי [Psalm 39:4](https://ref.ly/Ps39:4) (‖בַהֲגִיגִי תִבְעַר־אֵשׁ), cf. [Dt 19:6](https://ref.ly/Deut19:6); of conspirators [Ho 7:7](https://ref.ly/Hos7:7); Chaldeans [Je 51:39](https://ref.ly/Jer51:39). **3\.** of heat in conception (animals) [Gn 30:38](https://ref.ly/Gen30:38), [30:39](https://ref.ly/Gen30:39) (cf. יחם). **Niph.** *Pt.* נֵחָמִים באלים [Is 57:5](https://ref.ly/Isa57:5) (Köi. 371\), *inflame oneself with,* of idolatry. **Pi.** *Impf.* 3 fs. תְּחַמֵּם [Jb 39:14](https://ref.ly/Job39:14) *keep* eggs *warm* (of ostrich). **Hithp.** *Impf.* יִתְחַמָּם *warm oneself* [Jb 31:20](https://ref.ly/Job31:20) מִגֵּז כְּבָשִים *by means of fleece of sheep.*

> \[Str2527] † חֹם **n.m.** [Je 17:8](https://ref.ly/Jer17:8) heat;—חֹם abs. [Gn 8:22](https://ref.ly/Gen8:22) \+ 3 t. \+ [1 S 21:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:7) (v. infr.); cstr. [Gn 18:1](https://ref.ly/Gen18:1)\+ 3 t.; of heat of (mid\-) day חֹם היום [Gn 18:1](https://ref.ly/Gen18:1) (J), [1 S 11:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:11), [2 S 4:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:5); in promise of regular seasons [Gn 8:22](https://ref.ly/Gen8:22) (opp. קֹר); cf. [Is 18:4](https://ref.ly/Isa18:4), [18:4](https://ref.ly/Isa18:4) (קָצִיר ´ח); but as dangerous to plant\-life [Je 17:8](https://ref.ly/Jer17:8); it melts snow Jb 24:l9; לֶחֶם חֹם [1 S 21:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:7) *bread of heat* \= *hot bread,* rd. perhaps חָם ´ל (as [Jos 9:12](https://ref.ly/Josh9:12)).

> \[Str2525,2526,2524] † III. חָם **adj.** hot;—´לחם ח [Jos 9:12](https://ref.ly/Josh9:12) *hot bread* (JE), i.e. freshly baked; בגדים חַמִּים [Jb 37:17](https://ref.ly/Job37:17) of garments heated by south wind.—1\. חָם v. supr. p. 325\. II. חָם v. sub חמה. p. 327

> \[Str2535] † חַמָּה **n.f.** heat, sun (poet.)—חַמָּה [Is 24:23](https://ref.ly/Isa24:23) \+ 4 t..; חַמָּתוֺ [Psalm 19:7](https://ref.ly/Ps19:7); —**1\.** *heat* of sun [Psalm 19:7](https://ref.ly/Ps19:7). **2\.** \= *sun* [Jb 30:28](https://ref.ly/Job30:28); ‖לְבָנָה *moon* [Is 24:23](https://ref.ly/Isa24:23) \= [30:26](https://ref.ly/Isa30:26), [30:26](https://ref.ly/Isa30:26), Ct [6:10](https://ref.ly/Song6:10).

> \[Str2553] † \[חַמָּן] **n.m.** sun\-pillar, used in idolatrous worship (v. Thes489 ff. RS Sem. i, 469, LagM i, 228 Now: Arch. ii, 302; \= Palm. חמנא VogNo. 123 a; Ph. לבעל חמן oft. as epithet of solar Baal);—only pl. abs. חַמָּנִים [Is 27:9](https://ref.ly/Isa27:9) \+ 4 t.; sf. חַמָּנֵיכֶם [Lv 26:30](https://ref.ly/Lev26:30) \+ 2 t.; ‖בָּמוֺת [Lv 26:30](https://ref.ly/Lev26:30) (H), [2 Ch 14:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:4), cf. Ez [6:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:4), [6:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:6); ‖מזבחות הבעלים [2 Ch 34:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:4); ‖אשׁרים [Is 17:8](https://ref.ly/Isa17:8), [27:9](https://ref.ly/Isa27:9); ‖אשׁרים and פְּסִלִים [2 Ch 34:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:7).

> \[Str2540] † חַמּוֺן **n.pr.loc.** (*hot spring* ?)—**1\.** town in Asher [Jos 19:28](https://ref.ly/Josh19:28). **2\.** in Naphtali [1 Ch 6:61](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:61) (perh. \= 1\. חַמַּת, חַמַּת דּאֹר, v. Be and Di [Jos 19:35](https://ref.ly/Josh19:35)).

> \[Str2575,2576,2540] † I. חַמַּת **n.pr.loc.** (*hot* spring)—town in Naphtali [Jos 19:35](https://ref.ly/Josh19:35) (perh. \= חַמּוֺן **2** v. Di); LXX Ωμαθαδακεθ (\= חַמַּת רַקַּת), A Αμαθ, LXXL Αμμαθ; JosAnt. xviii, 2\. 3 Αμμαθους (ed. Niese), cf. Id. BJ. iv, 1\.3 GutheZPV 1891, xiii, 284; prob. \= Talm. *Ḥamata* NbrGêogr. du Talm. 207 (with hot springs), 35 minutes south of Tiberias, v. RobBR ii, 385 BuhlZPV xiii, 1890, 39 f. Guthel. c. GASmGeogr, 450 (FurrerZPV ii, 1878, 55; xiii, 1890, 194 ff. al. say north of Tiberias); v. חַמֹּת דּאֹר, חַמּוֺן below \& above

> \[Str2576] † חַמֹּת דּאֹר **n.pr.loc.** in Naphtali, Levitical city [Jos 21:32](https://ref.ly/Josh21:32) (perh. \= 1\. חַמַּת, חַמּוֺן **2** v. Di), LXX Νεμμαθ, A Εμαθδωρ, LXXL Αμαθδωρ.
