## חָמַס (id: BDB03181)

\[Str2554] † \[חָמַס] **vb.** treat violently, wrong (NH *id.,* *act violently, treat violently;* Aram. חֲמַס (rare) *violently seize;* Ar. حَمِسَ is *be hard, strict, rigorous*)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. חָֽמְסוּ Ez [22:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:26), [Zp 3:4](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:4); *Impf.* יַחְמֹס [Jb 15:33](https://ref.ly/Job15:33), וַיַּחְמֹס [La 2:6](https://ref.ly/Lam2:6); תַּחְמָֹסוּ [Jb 21:27](https://ref.ly/Job21:27), [Je 22:3](https://ref.ly/Jer22:3); *Pt.* חֹמֵס [Pr 8:36](https://ref.ly/Prov8:36); —*treat violently, wrong*;—**1\.** of physical wrong: ´תַּחְ [Je 22:3](https://ref.ly/Jer22:3) (‖תֹּנוּ) of wrong to widows and orphans; כַּגַּן שֻבּוֺ ´וַיַּח [La 2:6](https://ref.ly/Lam2:6) *and hath done violence to his pavilion as to a garden* (‖שִׁחֵת מֹעֲדוֺ); so, fig., of vine, *wrong,* i.e. fail to nourish, kill כַּגֶּפֶן ´יַח בִּסְרוֺ [Jb 15:33](https://ref.ly/Job15:33). **2\.** of ethical wrong, obj. תּוֺרָה [Zp 3:4](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:4), [Ex 22:26](https://ref.ly/Exod22:26) (both ‖חִלֵּל קֹדֶשׁ); cf. מְזִמּוֺת עָלֵי תַּחְמָֹסוּ [Jb 21:27](https://ref.ly/Job21:27) *the devices* (wherewith) *ye do me violence.* **3\.** both physical and ethical חֹטְאִי חֹמֵס נַפְשׁוֺ [Pr 8:36](https://ref.ly/Prov8:36). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. נֶחְמְסוּ [Je 13:22](https://ref.ly/Jer13:22) thy heels *suffer violence* (‖נִגְלוּ שׁוּלַיִח).

> \[Str2555] † חָמָס **n.m.**:[Psalm 7:17](https://ref.ly/Ps7:17) violence, wrong—abs. ´ח [Gn 6:11](https://ref.ly/Gen6:11)\+ 44 t.; cstr. חֲמַס Ju [9:24](https://ref.ly/Judg9:24) \+ 7 t.; sf. חֲמָסִי [Gn 16:5](https://ref.ly/Gen16:5), [Je 51:35](https://ref.ly/Jer51:35) (in both \= wrong *done to me*); חֲמָסוֺ [Psalm 7:17](https://ref.ly/Ps7:17); pl. חֲמָסִים [2 S 22:49](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:49) \+ 3 t.;—*violence,* specif. of physical violence Ju [9:24](https://ref.ly/Judg9:24), [2 S 22:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:3) (not ‖ [Psalm 18:3](https://ref.ly/Ps18:3)), Ob:[10](https://ref.ly/Obad1:10), Hb [1:9](https://ref.ly/Hab1:9), [Je 51:35](https://ref.ly/Jer51:35) (of Chaldeans), Hb [2:8](https://ref.ly/Hab2:8), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Hab2:17), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Hab2:17), [Jo 4:19](https://ref.ly/Joel4:19), [Psalm 72:14](https://ref.ly/Ps72:14); but also *wrong,* incl. injurious language, harsh treatment, etc. [Gn 16:5](https://ref.ly/Gen16:5) (J, of wrong done to Sarah by, Hagar), [Jb 19:7](https://ref.ly/Job19:7); [Mal 2:16](https://ref.ly/Mal2:16); in gen. of rude wickedness of men, their noisy, wild, ruthlessness [Mi 6:12](https://ref.ly/Mic6:12), Hb [1:2](https://ref.ly/Hab1:2), [Zp 1:9](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:9), [Pr 10:6](https://ref.ly/Prov10:6), [10:11](https://ref.ly/Prov10:11), [13:2](https://ref.ly/Prov13:2), [26:6](https://ref.ly/Prov26:6) \+ Ez [7:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:11) (si vera l., v. Co), ‖שֹׁד [Am 3:10](https://ref.ly/Amos3:10), Hb [1:3](https://ref.ly/Hab1:3), [Je 6:7](https://ref.ly/Jer6:7), [20:8](https://ref.ly/Jer20:8), Ez [45:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:9), [Is 60:18](https://ref.ly/Isa60:18), ‖רִיב [Psalm 55:10](https://ref.ly/Ps55:10), ‖עָמָל [7:17](https://ref.ly/Ps7:17), ‖גַּאֲיָה [73:6](https://ref.ly/Ps73:6), ´אֹהֵב ח [11:5](https://ref.ly/Ps11:5) (‖רָשָׁע), denied, of servant of ´י [Is 53:9](https://ref.ly/Isa53:9), בארץ ´ח [Je 51:46](https://ref.ly/Jer51:46), cf. [Gn 6:11](https://ref.ly/Gen6:11), [6:13](https://ref.ly/Gen6:13) (P), Ez [8:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:17), [12:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:19); מָֽלְאוּ ´מַחֲשַׁכֵּי־אֶרֶץ נְאוֺת ח [Psalm 74:20](https://ref.ly/Ps74:20), ´הָעִיר מָֽלְאָה ח Ez [7:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:23), [28:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:16), ´שֶׁבֶת ח [Am 6:3](https://ref.ly/Amos6:3) is (prob.) *enthronement of violence*; ´כְּלֵי ח [Gn 49:5](https://ref.ly/Gen49:5) (poem) *instrument, weapons, of violence*;—other phrases are: בְּיָדַיִם ´ח [Jon 3:8](https://ref.ly/Jonah3:8), [1 Ch 12:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:17), cf. [Jb 16:17](https://ref.ly/Job16:17), יְדֵיכֶם ´ח [Psalm 58:3](https://ref.ly/Ps58:3) and בְּכַפֵּיהֶם ´פֹּעַל ח [Is 59:6](https://ref.ly/Isa59:6); ´עֵד ח i.e. a witness that promotes violence and wrong Ex 23:l (JE), [Dt 19:16](https://ref.ly/Deut19:16), ´עֵדֵי ח [Psalm 35:11](https://ref.ly/Ps35:11); עֵדֵי שֶׁקֶר וִיפֵהַ חָמָס [Psalm 27:12](https://ref.ly/Ps27:12); ´שנאת ח [Psalm 25:19](https://ref.ly/Ps25:19) \= hatred characterized by violence; אִישׁ חָמָס \= *violent man* [Psalm 18:49](https://ref.ly/Ps18:49) ([2 S 22:49](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:49) has the later חמסים ´א, cf. infr.) [140:12](https://ref.ly/Ps140:12), [Pr 3:31](https://ref.ly/Prov3:31), [16:29](https://ref.ly/Prov16:29); אישׁ חמסים (later) [Psalm 140:2](https://ref.ly/Ps140:2), [140:5](https://ref.ly/Ps140:5), [2 S 22:49](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:49); יֵין חֲמָסִים [Pr 4:17](https://ref.ly/Prov4:17) i.e. wine gained by violence (‖לֶחֶם רֶשַׁע).

> \[Str8464] † תַּחְמָס **n.\[m.]** name of male ostrich, acc. to Boii.830 Thes (Thes der. fr. *violence* of this bird, cf. Ar. ظَلِيمٌ *violence,* also *ostrich;* other conject. are: *owl* LXX Vulgate; *swallow,* Saad: v. also Kn in Di)—mentioned as unclean [Lv 11:16](https://ref.ly/Lev11:16) (P) [Dt 14:15](https://ref.ly/Deut14:15).

## I חָמֵץ (id: BDB03184)

\[Str2556] † I. חָמֵץ **vb.** be sour, leavened (NH *id.,* Pi. Hiph. *make sour, leaven;* Ar. حَمُضَ *be sour;* Aram. חֲמַע *be sour, leavened,* ܚܡܰܥ *be leavened*)—**Qal** *Pf.* חָמֵץ [Ex 12:39](https://ref.ly/Exod12:39); *Impf.* יֶחְמָץ [Ex 12:34](https://ref.ly/Exod12:34); *Inf.* sf. חֻמְצָתוֺ [Ho 7:4](https://ref.ly/Hos7:4) \+;—*be leavened,* of dough (בָּצֵק), [Ex 12:34](https://ref.ly/Exod12:34), [12:39](https://ref.ly/Exod12:39) (E), cf. [Ho 7:4](https://ref.ly/Hos7:4). **Hitph.** *be soured, embittered,* [Psalm 73:21](https://ref.ly/Ps73:21) (‖כִּלְיוֺתַי אֶשְׁתּוֺנָן).

> \[Str2557] † חָמֵץ **n.m.**:[Ex 13:3](https://ref.ly/Exod13:3) that which is leavened—´ח [Ex 12:15](https://ref.ly/Exod12:15) \+ 10 t.—forbidden at Passover [Ex 12:15](https://ref.ly/Exod12:15) (P), [13:3](https://ref.ly/Exod13:3), [13:7](https://ref.ly/Exod13:7) (JE), [Dt 16:3](https://ref.ly/Deut16:3), in all sacrifices [Ex 23:18](https://ref.ly/Exod23:18), [34:25](https://ref.ly/Exod34:25) (both JE); cf. [Am 4:5](https://ref.ly/Amos4:5); [Lv 2:11](https://ref.ly/Lev2:11), [6:10](https://ref.ly/Lev6:10) (P; appos.); exceptions are ´לֶחֶם ח of peace\-offering [Lv 7:13](https://ref.ly/Lev7:13) (P), and the wave\-loaves (´ח appos.) [Lv 23:17](https://ref.ly/Lev23:17) (H).—v. RS Sem 208 OTJC 2, 345\.

> \[Str2558] † חֹ֫מֶץ **n.m.** vinegar—´ח abs. [Pr 10:26](https://ref.ly/Prov10:26) \+ 3 t.; cstr. [Nu 6:3](https://ref.ly/Num6:3), [6:3](https://ref.ly/Num6:3); —a common condiment [Ru 2:14](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:14), forbidden to Nazirite (יין ´ח and שֵׁכָר ´ח) [Nu 6:3](https://ref.ly/Num6:3), [6:3](https://ref.ly/Num6:3) (P), offered (in cruelty) to a thirsty man [Psalm 69:22](https://ref.ly/Ps69:22) (fig. for harshness lack of sympathy); in sim. of sluggard כַּחֹמֶץ לַשִּׁנַּיִם [Pr 10:26](https://ref.ly/Prov10:26), עַ־לָ\+נתֶר ´ח [Pr 25:20](https://ref.ly/Prov25:20) *vinegar on nitre* (sim. of merry songs for the heavy\-hearted).

Hb [3:15](https://ref.ly/Hab3:15) (for MT חֹמֶר q.v.) the *great waters foamed*; *Impf.* 3 mpl. יֶחֱמוּ יֶחְמְרוּ מֵימָיו [Psalm 46:4](https://ref.ly/Ps46:4) *let its water roar and foam*. **Pe‘ al‘ al** *Pf. pass.* 3 pl. מֵעַי חֳמַרְמָרוּ [La 1:20](https://ref.ly/Lam1:20) *my bowels are in a ferment* (‖נֶהְפַּךְ לִבִּי), of distressat calamities of Jerus.; so חֳמַרְמְרוּ מֵעַי [La 2:11](https://ref.ly/Lam2:11).—[Jb 16:16](https://ref.ly/Job16:16) v. IV. חמר, with ref. to reddish\-brown colour)—חֵמָר [Gn 11:3](https://ref.ly/Gen11:3) \+ 2 t.; used for cement in building Babel [Gn 11:3](https://ref.ly/Gen11:3) (J); abounding in vale of Siddim ´בֶּאֱרֹת בֶּאֱרֹת ח [Gn 14:10](https://ref.ly/Gen14:10); used in coating Moses’ ‘ark’ of bulrushes [Ex 2:3](https://ref.ly/Exod2:3) (E; ‖ָ\+זפֶת).

[Is 10:6](https://ref.ly/Isa10:6) \+ 2 t.;—**1\.** *mortar, cement* for holding building\-stones (brick) together [Gn 11:3](https://ref.ly/Gen11:3) (J), [Ex 1:14](https://ref.ly/Exod1:14) (P; both ‖לְבֵנִים); [Na 3:14](https://ref.ly/Nah3:14) (‖טיט). **2\.** *clay:* **a.** as material of vessels [Je 18:4](https://ref.ly/Jer18:4), [18:6](https://ref.ly/Jer18:6), היצר ´ח [Is 29:16](https://ref.ly/Isa29:16), in sim. of God's fashioning man [Is 45:9](https://ref.ly/Isa45:9), [64:7](https://ref.ly/Isa64:7), [Jb 10:9](https://ref.ly/Job10:9); as material of human bodies ´בָּתֵּי ח [Jb 4:19](https://ref.ly/Job4:19) cf. [33:6](https://ref.ly/Job33:6); as material of bulwarks (disparagingly) [Jb 13:12](https://ref.ly/Job13:12); חותם ´ח \= *seal\-clay* i.e. clay upon which seal is pressed. **b.** \= *mire* חוצות ´ח [Is 10:6](https://ref.ly/Isa10:6); ´הֹרָנִי לַח [Jb 30:19](https://ref.ly/Job30:19) *he hath cast me into the mire,* i.e. deeply humiliated me (עָפָר and אֵפֶר in ‖ cl.); as sim. of commonness, abundance [Jb 27:16](https://ref.ly/Job27:16) (‖עָפָר).

III. חמר (√ of foll.; NH חָמַר, *heap up*; Aram. חַמֵּר *make a* ruin\-*heap*; Ar. حِمَارَةٌ *great mass of stone, heap of stones*).

[Is 5:10](https://ref.ly/Isa5:10) \+ 5 t. Ez; cstr. [Ho 3:2](https://ref.ly/Hos3:2) \+ 3 t.; pl. חֳמָרִים [Nu 11:32](https://ref.ly/Num11:32); —´ח of barley [Ho 3:2](https://ref.ly/Hos3:2), Ez [45:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:13), cf. שְעֹרִים ´זֶרַע ח [Lv 27:16](https://ref.ly/Lev27:16) i.e. land on which a ´ח of barley seed was sown (taxed at 50 shekels); ´ח of wheat Ez [45:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:13); in gen. ´זֶרַע ח [Is 5:10](https://ref.ly/Isa5:10); \= 10 ephahs (or baths, v. בַּת sub בתת) Ez [45:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:11), [45:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:11), [45:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:11), [45:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:14), [45:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:14) (in v:[14](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:14) del. Co); on actual size of ´ח, \= 393\.9 litres, v. HultzschMetrol. 2nd ed. 448, 452f. See further II. בַּת and reff., BenzArch 183 f Nowarch. i, 203 f.

> \[Str2569] † I. חֹ֫מֶשׁ **n.\[m.]** fifth part—only לְפַרְעֹה לַהֹמֶשׁ [Gn 47:26](https://ref.ly/Gen47:26), but connex. with preceding awk\-ward; rd. perh. הַחֹמֶשׁ (SyrVer), or לְחַמֵּשׁ LXX, v. Di.

> \[Str2567] † II. \[חמשׁ] **vb.denom.** only **Pi.** וְחִמֵּשׁ אֶ־ת אֶרֶץ מִצְרַיִם [Gn 41:34](https://ref.ly/Gen41:34) (E) *and he shall fifth* (take the fifth part of) *the land of Egypt,* i.e. the fifth part of the produce (cf. Ar. خَمَسَ *take a fifth part;* Eth. ኀመሰ፡ 1\. 2 *quinque facere*).

## III חמשׁ (id: BDB03216)

III. חמשׁ (√ of foll.; meaning dubious).

> \[Str2570] † II. חֹ֫מֶשׁ **n.m.** belly (Aram. ܚܽܘܡܫܳܐ)—הַחֹמֶשׁ [2 S 2:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:23) \+ 3 t.; ´אל־הח…וַיַּכֵּהוּ [2 S 2:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:23) *and he smote him in the belly;* [2 S 20:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:10) \+ [4:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:6), rd. וַתָּ֫נָם וַתִּישָׁן *and she slumbered and slept* LXX We Dr.; yet corruption difficult to explain, v. Klo; ´וַיַּכֵּהוּ שָׁם הח [2 S 3:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:27) (where prob. ins. א־ל cf. Dr).

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Samuel 4:6; 2 Samuel 3:27; 2 Samuel 2:23; 2 Samuel 20:10


## IV חמשׁ (id: BDB03218)

IV. חמשׁ (√ of foll.; mng. dub.; perh. cf. Ar. خَمِيسٌ *army,* Sab. חַמס men of a tribe who can bear arms; √ then poss. \= I. חמשׁ (e.g. army as composed of *five parts*) v. Lane Frey; also Sab. Denkm.:24, which cities *tribus (fr. tres), quartier* (fr. *quartus, quatuor*); \> Thes MV al. who comp. Ar. حَمَسَ *be firm strong,* حَمِسَ *be courageous,* etc.)

> \[Str2571] † חֲמֻשִׁים **adj.pl.** in battle array, alw. this form, [Ex 13:18](https://ref.ly/Exod13:18) (E), [Jos 1:14](https://ref.ly/Josh1:14), [4:12](https://ref.ly/Josh4:12) (both D), Ju [7:11](https://ref.ly/Judg7:11) \+ [Nu 32:17](https://ref.ly/Num32:17) (JE), v. חושׁ.

* **Associated Passages:** Joshua 4:12; Exodus 13:18; Joshua 1:14; Judges 7:11; Numbers 32:17


## חמת (id: BDB03220)

חמת (√ of foll.; mng. unknown; Mv al., very improbably, fr. Ar. حَمِتَ *grow rancid, putrid;* see on formation, LagBN 154\).

> \[Str2573] † חֵ֫מֶת, חֶָמֶת **n.\[m.]** waterskin (NH *id.*)—הַחֶָמֶת [Gn 21:15](https://ref.ly/Gen21:15); הַחֵ֫מֶת [Gn 21:19](https://ref.ly/Gen21:19); וְחֵמַ֙ת מַ֝יִם [Gn 21:14](https://ref.ly/Gen21:14) (v. Di), \[Hb [2:5](https://ref.ly/Hab2:5) חֲמָתְךָ v. חֵמָה].

> \[Str2574,2579,2578] † חֲמָת, חֲמַת **n.pr.loc.** (√ dub.; Thes MV al. der. fr. חָמָה, חֲמָת \= *fortress*, or (v. RS Sem. i, 140\), *sacred enclosure, temenos, ḥimā*, cf. HalJAS 7 ême Sêr. x, 392, LagBN 82, 156; but no certainty that name is Shemitic; Ar. حَمَاة, As. *Amattu*, DlPA 275 ff. Pr 174\);—mod. Ḥamâ, on *el\-‘A‘ṣi* (Orontes), 38 h. (c. 115 m.) N. of Damascus, v. BDPal 398 f. It had a king, תֹּעִי, in David's time [2 S 8:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:9) \= [1 Ch 18:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:9), and in 8th cent. B.C. 2 K [19:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:13) \= [Is 37:13](https://ref.ly/Isa37:13); gods of its own [2 K 17:30](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:30), [18:34](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:34) \= [Is 36:19](https://ref.ly/Isa36:19); gave its name to land (limits unknown) ´רִבְלָה בְּאֶרֶץ ח [2 K 23:33](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:33), [25:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:21), [Je 39:5](https://ref.ly/Jer39:5), [52:9](https://ref.ly/Jer52:9), [52:27](https://ref.ly/Jer52:27), cf. [2 Ch 8:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:4); contained Isr. exiles [Is 11:11](https://ref.ly/Isa11:11); furnished colonists for Samaria [2 K 17:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:24) (cf. v:[30](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:30) supr.) after being conquered by Sargon, (COT [2 K 17:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:24), [18:34](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:34) cf. [Is 10:9](https://ref.ly/Isa10:9)). It is called *great Hamath* by [Am 6:2](https://ref.ly/Amos6:2) (חֲמַת רַבָּה, —חֲמַת only here). ´ח is mentioned with דַּמֶּשֶק, צֹר, צִידוֺן in prophecy against land Hadrach (חַדְרָךְ) [Zc 9:2](https://ref.ly/Zech9:2), cf. [Je 49:23](https://ref.ly/Jer49:23). Often in phr. ´לְבאֹ ח (עַ־ד) \= *the entrance to H.*, i.e. *the approach to H.*, as territorial limit Ju [3:3](https://ref.ly/Judg3:3); usu. as denoting (ideal) northern limit of Isr. territory [Jos 13:5](https://ref.ly/Josh13:5), [Nu 13:21](https://ref.ly/Num13:21), [34:8](https://ref.ly/Num34:8), [Am 6:14](https://ref.ly/Amos6:14), [1 K 8:65](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:65) \= [2 Ch 7:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:8), [2 K 14:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:25), [1 Ch 13:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:5); חֲמָתָה [1 Ch 18:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:3); צובָה ´ח [2 Ch 8:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:3), and הֵשִׁיב לִיחוּדָה ´אֶת־דַּמֶּשֶק וְאֶת־ח [2 K 14:28](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:28); so also Ez 47, 48: ´גְּבוּל ח Ez [47:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:17), cf. v:[16](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:16) b \+ v:[16](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:16) a (del. Co); ´לְבוֺא ח [48:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:1), cf. [47:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:20); ´אֶל־יַד ח [48:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:1).

> \[Str2577] † חֲמָתִי **adj. gent.** c. art. \= subst. הַהֲמָתִָי, as descendants of Canaan [Gn 10:18](https://ref.ly/Gen10:18) \= [1 Ch 1:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:16).

> \[Str2575] חַמַּת **n.pr.loc.** v. sub חמס. p. 329

> \[Str2580–82,2584] חֵן, תֵנָדָד, חַנָּה v. sub חנן. p. 336–37

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Chronicles 18:9; Isaiah 10:9; Joshua 13:5; Ezekiel 47:16; Zechariah 9:2; Genesis 21:15; 2 Kings 14:25; 2 Kings 14:28; Jeremiah 39:5; 2 Chronicles 8:4; Ezekiel 48:1; Ezekiel 47:20; Habakkuk 2:5; Isaiah 11:11; 1 Chronicles 1:16; Numbers 34:8; Genesis 10:18; Jeremiah 49:23; Amos 6:14; Genesis 21:19; 1 Kings 8:65; 2 Kings 17:30; Numbers 13:21; Jeremiah 52:27; Isaiah 36:19; 2 Kings 19:13; 1 Chronicles 18:3; Judges 3:3; 2 Kings 23:33; Isaiah 37:13; 1 Chronicles 13:5; 2 Samuel 8:9; Amos 6:2; Ezekiel 47:17; 2 Chronicles 8:3; 2 Kings 17:24; Jeremiah 52:9; 2 Chronicles 7:8; 2 Kings 25:21; 2 Kings 18:34; Genesis 21:14


## חָנָה (id: BDB03226)

\[Str2583] חָנָה:143 **vb.** decline, bend down, encamp (Aram. ܚܢܳܐ *to aim at, incline towards, reach;* Zinj. מחנת cstr. *camp*, Inscr. Panammu13. 16\. 17; Ar. حَنَا *bend, curve, bend down;* As. *mûnu, mênu* and *tênû, couch, mânîtu, house*, ZimBP 44, 45 Dl in Zim117)—**Qal** *Pf.* חָנָה [1 S 26:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:5), [Is 29:1](https://ref.ly/Isa29:1); וְחָנִ֫יתִי consec. [Is 29:3](https://ref.ly/Isa29:3), [Zc 9:8](https://ref.ly/Zech9:8); חָנוּ [Nu 2:34](https://ref.ly/Num2:34) \+ 2 t.; וְחָנוּ consec. [Nu 1:52](https://ref.ly/Num1:52); *Impf.* וַיִּחַן [Gn 26:17](https://ref.ly/Gen26:17) \+ 9 t.; 3 fs. תַּחֲנֶה [Psalm 27:3](https://ref.ly/Ps27:3); יַחֲנוּ [Nu 1:50](https://ref.ly/Num1:50) \+ 13 t. Nu \+ [Ex 14:2](https://ref.ly/Exod14:2): וַיַּחֲנוּ [Ex 13:26](https://ref.ly/Exod13:26) \+ 79 t. (of which 42 in Nu 33); וַיַּחֲנוּן Ju [11:18](https://ref.ly/Judg11:18); 2 mpl. תַּחֲנוּ [Ex 14:2](https://ref.ly/Exod14:2); 1 pl. וַנַּחֲנֶה [Ezr 8:15](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:15); *Imv.* חֲנֵה [2 S 12:28](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:28); חֲנוּ [Nu 31:19](https://ref.ly/Num31:19), [Je 50:29](https://ref.ly/Jer50:29); *Inf.* חֲנוֺת Ju [19:9](https://ref.ly/Judg19:9); חֲנֹת [Nu 1:51](https://ref.ly/Num1:51); sf. חֲנֹתֵנוּ [Nu 10:31](https://ref.ly/Num10:31); חֲנֹחְכֶם [Dt 1:33](https://ref.ly/Deut1:33); *Pt.* חֹנֶה [Ex 18:5](https://ref.ly/Exod18:5), [Psalm 34:8](https://ref.ly/Ps34:8); f. חֹנָה [2 S 23:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:13), [1 Ch 11:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:15); חָֹ\+נךְ [Psalm 53:6](https://ref.ly/Ps53:6); pl. חֹנִיס [Ex 14:9](https://ref.ly/Exod14:9) \+ 11 t.; חוֺנִים [Nu 2:2](https://ref.ly/Num2:2), [Na 3:17](https://ref.ly/Nah3:17); —**1\.** *decline*, of day drawing to its close חֲנוֺת הַיּוֺם Ju [19:9](https://ref.ly/Judg19:9) (‖´רָפָה הַיּ לַעֲרוֺב; also ´נְטוֺת הַיּ v:[8](https://ref.ly/Judg19:8); הַיּוֺם רַד מְאֹד v:[11](https://ref.ly/Judg19:11)). **2\.** *encamp* (prop. *verge toward, incline to, settle at*, of goal of day's march):—**a.** of single nomad [Gn 26:17](https://ref.ly/Gen26:17) (J; sq. ב), [33:18](https://ref.ly/Gen33:18) (E; sq. אֶת־פני). **b.** of Israel at Exod. and in wilderness [Ex 13:20](https://ref.ly/Exod13:20) (ב) [14:2](https://ref.ly/Exod14:2) (לִפְנֵי), v:[2](https://ref.ly/Exod14:2) (עַל), \+ c. 80 t. (Hex. chiefly P); also (abs.) [Nu 9:18](https://ref.ly/Num9:18), [9:20](https://ref.ly/Num9:20), [9:22](https://ref.ly/Num9:22), [9:23](https://ref.ly/Num9:23) cf. [Ezr 8:15](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:15); [Ne 11:30](https://ref.ly/Neh11:30) *so they encamped* (i.e. *settled*, or *were settled*) from Beersheba to the valley of Hinnom (‖ישׁבו v:[25](https://ref.ly/Neh11:25)). **c.** of armies: (1\) Israel (conquering Canaan, etc.) [Jos 4:19](https://ref.ly/Josh4:19), [5:10](https://ref.ly/Josh5:10) \+ oft.; (2\) Philistines [1 S 4:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:1), [13:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:16), [2 S 23:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:23) \+; usu. sq. ב loc. [Nu 10:31](https://ref.ly/Num10:31), [1 S 4:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:1), [13:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:16), [Jos 4:19](https://ref.ly/Josh4:19), [1 S 13:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:5) \+ 63 t. (וַיַּחֲנוּ בְ [2 S 24:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:5) rd. וַיָּחֵלּוּ מִן LXXL We Dr v. חלל); so of locusts in hedges (in sim.) [Na 3:17](https://ref.ly/Nah3:17); also sq. עַל \= *by, near* [Ex 14:2](https://ref.ly/Exod14:2), [14:9](https://ref.ly/Exod14:9), [15:27](https://ref.ly/Exod15:27), [Nu 1:52](https://ref.ly/Num1:52), [2:2](https://ref.ly/Num2:2), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Num2:5), [2:12](https://ref.ly/Num2:12), [2:27](https://ref.ly/Num2:27), [3:29](https://ref.ly/Num3:29), [3:35](https://ref.ly/Num3:35) (על ירך), [33:10](https://ref.ly/Num33:10), Ju [7:1](https://ref.ly/Judg7:1), [1 S 4:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:1); על־פני השדה (על in diff. sense) [2 S 11:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:11); sq. עַל also \= *against, encamp* with hostile purpose [Jos 10:5](https://ref.ly/Josh10:5), [10:31](https://ref.ly/Josh10:31), [10:34](https://ref.ly/Josh10:34), Ju [6:4](https://ref.ly/Judg6:4), [20:19](https://ref.ly/Judg20:19), [1 S 11:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:1), [2 S 12:28](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:28), [1 K 16:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:15), [2 K 25:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:1), [2 Ch 32:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:1), [Je 50:29](https://ref.ly/Jer50:29), [52:4](https://ref.ly/Jer52:4), [Psalm 27:3](https://ref.ly/Ps27:3); sq. אֶ־ל in same sense [Jos 11:5](https://ref.ly/Josh11:5); metaph. of God's hostility to apostate Jerusalem (על) [Is 29:3](https://ref.ly/Isa29:3); sq. also לְ \= *at, by* [Nu 2:34](https://ref.ly/Num2:34), [Zc 9:8](https://ref.ly/Zech9:8); שָׁס…אשׁר [Ex 18:5](https://ref.ly/Exod18:5), [1 S 26:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:5); sq. סביב לְ for protection [Nu 1:50](https://ref.ly/Num1:50), [1:53](https://ref.ly/Num1:53), cf. metaph. [Psalm 34:8](https://ref.ly/Ps34:8), but also hostility [Jb 19:12](https://ref.ly/Job19:12); also sq. other prep. and prep. phrases, e.g. מֵעֵבֶר [Nu 21:13](https://ref.ly/Num21:13); מחוץ [Nu 31:19](https://ref.ly/Num31:19); בין [1 S 17:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:1); נגד [1 K 20:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:27) etc.; sq. acc. loc. [2 S 17:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:26); sq. sf. acc. with hostile sense חָֹ\+נךָ [Psalm 53:6](https://ref.ly/Ps53:6); without prep. or noun foll., in sense of *encamp, be* or *abide in encampment* [Nu 9:18](https://ref.ly/Num9:18), [9:20](https://ref.ly/Num9:20), [9:22](https://ref.ly/Num9:22), [9:23](https://ref.ly/Num9:23), [Ezr 8:15](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:15), [2 S 11:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:11) \+; poet. of David in Jerusalem, [Is 29:1](https://ref.ly/Isa29:1) *city in which David fixed his camp.*

## חָנַט (id: BDB03235)

\[Str2590] † חָנַט **vb.** spice, make spicy, embalm (NH *id., bud, blossom;* Ar. حَنَطَ *become mature*, II. *prepare for burial*, حَنُوطٌ *spices* for a corpse; حَنَّاطٌ *embalmer*, Dozyi, 322 after PS1320; Aram. ܚܢܰܛ, חֲנַט *embalm;* so Eth. ሐነጠ፡ (loan\-word DI110));—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. חָֽנְטָה Ct [2:13](https://ref.ly/Song2:13); *Impf.* 3 mpl. וַיַּחַנְטוּ [Gn 50:2](https://ref.ly/Gen50:2), [50:26](https://ref.ly/Gen50:26); *Inf. cstr.* לַחֲנֹט [Gn 50:2](https://ref.ly/Gen50:2); —**1\.** *spice, make spicy* פַגֶּיהָ ´הַתְּאֵנָה ח Ct [2:13](https://ref.ly/Song2:13) *the fig\-tree spiceth its figs*, so VB De in transl. (Ew De in notes Öt al. prefer *reddeneth*, on account of spring season, cf. the less common and perhaps secondary sense in Ar. *become red* (of leather) Lane657 a). **2\.** *embalm*, sq. acc. Pers. I. חנך (√ of foll. (see Ar. Syr.); meaning unknown).

> \[Str2441] † חֵךְ **n.m.** palate, roof of mouth, gums (NH *id.;* Aram. ܚܶܢܟܳܐ חִכָּא Ar. حَنَكٌ *palate, roof of mouth, jaws, lower part of mouth, lower jaw* of horse, *mouth,* etc. Lane659 Dozyi. 332\);—abs. ´ח [Jb 12:11](https://ref.ly/Job12:11) [34:3](https://ref.ly/Job34:3); sf. חִכִּי [Pr 8:7](https://ref.ly/Prov8:7) \+ 6 t., חִכְּךָ [Ho 8:1](https://ref.ly/Hos8:1), חִכֶָּךָ Ez [3:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:26), [Pr 24:13](https://ref.ly/Prov24:13); chiefly poet., esp. WisdLt:—חִכֵח Ct [7:10](https://ref.ly/Song7:10), חִכּוֺ [La 4:4](https://ref.ly/Lam4:4) \+ 2 t., חִכָּהּ [Pr 5:3](https://ref.ly/Prov5:3), חִכָּם [Jb 29:10](https://ref.ly/Job29:10); —*palate, roof of mouth:* **a.** אֶל־חִכְּךָ שֹׁפָר [Ho 8:1](https://ref.ly/Hos8:1) *a trumpet to thy palate!* (or *gums;* as in Eng., *to thy lips*); oft. c. לָשׁוֺן, *tongue clings* (דבק) *to* הך, expression for speechlessness Ez [3:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:26), [Jb 29:10](https://ref.ly/Job29:10), as imprecation [Psalm 137:6](https://ref.ly/Ps137:6); for the parching of extreme thirst [La 4:4](https://ref.ly/Lam4:4) (בַּצָּמָא), cf. [Psalm 22:16](https://ref.ly/Ps22:16) MT יָבֵּשׁ כַּחֶרֶש כֹּחִי rd. חִכִּי for כחי v. Checrit. n. (‖לְשׁוֺנִי מֻדְבָּק מַלְקוֺחָי). **b.** as organ of speech [Pr 5:3](https://ref.ly/Prov5:3), [8:7](https://ref.ly/Prov8:7) (both ‖שְפָתַיִם), [Jb 31:30](https://ref.ly/Job31:30), [33:2](https://ref.ly/Job33:2) דִּבְּרָה לְשׁוֺנִי בְחִכִּי (‖פָּתַחְתִּי פִי). **c.** as organ of taste [Jb 12:11](https://ref.ly/Job12:11), [34:3](https://ref.ly/Job34:3), פִּרְיוֺ מָתוֺק לְחִכִּי Ct [2:3](https://ref.ly/Song2:3) *his fruit was sweet to my palate,* [Pr 24:13](https://ref.ly/Prov24:13) (implied sim. of wisdom as sweet to the soul); fig. of God's words as sweet ´לְח [Psalm 119:103](https://ref.ly/Ps119:103) (‖פִּי); of taste as distinguishing misfortunes [Jb 6:30](https://ref.ly/Job6:30) (‖לָשׁוֺן; \> others of speech); of keeping wickedness ´בְּתוֺח ח [Jb 20:18](https://ref.ly/Job20:18) (‖תַּחַת לָשׁוֺן), i.e. delighting in and prolonging the taste of it. **d.** nearly \= *mouth,* as an element in personal sweetness and beauty Ct [5:16](https://ref.ly/Song5:16), [7:10](https://ref.ly/Song7:10).

* **Associated Passages:** Job 29:10; Psalms 22:16; Ezekiel 3:26; Hosea 8:1; Job 31:30; Proverbs 8:7; Proverbs 5:3; Job 20:18; Job 12:11; Psalms 119:103; Proverbs 24:13; Song of Songs 2:3; Job 34:3; Song of Songs 7:10; Job 6:30; Song of Songs 5:16; Job 33:2; Lamentations 4:4; Psalms 137:6


## II חִָנִךְ (id: BDB03242)

\[Str2596] †II. חִָנִךְ **vb.** train up, dedicate (cf. Ar. حَنَكَ appar. denom. fr. حَنَكٌ *rub palate* of child with chewed dates, Lane659 a, of midwife *rub palate* of new\-born child with oil, etc., before it begins to suck, Dozyii, 332; also *make experienced, submissive,* etc. (as one does a horse by a rope in its mouth) Lane; v. also WeSkizzen iii, 154; NH חִנֵּךְ *accustom;* Aram. חֲנַךְ *dedicate,* חַנֵּיךְ as NH; Eth. II. ሐነከ፡ *perceive, understand* (DI108, ህንክኣ፡ *initiatio* is loan\-word Id.:ib.))—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. חֲנָכוֺ [Dt 20:5](https://ref.ly/Deut20:5); *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יַחְנְכֶנּוּ [Dt 20:5](https://ref.ly/Deut20:5), 3 mpl. וַיַּחְנְכוּ [1 K 8:63](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:63), [2 Ch 7:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:5); *Imv.* חֲנֹךְ [Pr 22:6](https://ref.ly/Prov22:6); —**1\.** *train, train up a* (the) youth (לַנַּעַר) [Pr 22:6](https://ref.ly/Prov22:6) (cf. NH חִנּוּךְ). **2\.** *dedicate,* of formal opening of a new house [Dt 20:5](https://ref.ly/Deut20:5), [20:5](https://ref.ly/Deut20:5); *dedicate, consecrate* temple [1 K 8:63](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:63) \= [2 Ch 7:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:5) (all sq. acc.)

> \[Str2585] † חֲנוֺךְ **n.pr.m. 1\.** son of Cain [Gn 4:17](https://ref.ly/Gen4:17), [4:17](https://ref.ly/Gen4:17), [4:18](https://ref.ly/Gen4:18) LXX Ενωχ, LXXL Ενως. **2\.** son of Jered (line of Seth), the pious Enoch, who walked with God and was taken by him [Gn 5:18](https://ref.ly/Gen5:18), [5:19](https://ref.ly/Gen5:19), [5:21](https://ref.ly/Gen5:21), [5:22](https://ref.ly/Gen5:22), [5:23](https://ref.ly/Gen5:23), [5:24](https://ref.ly/Gen5:24), [1 Ch 1:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:3) LXX Ενωχ. **3\.** חֲנֹךְ a son of Midian [Gn 25:4](https://ref.ly/Gen25:4), [1 Ch 1:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:33), LXX Ενωχ. **4\.** חֲנוֺךְ a son of Reuben [Gn 46:9](https://ref.ly/Gen46:9), [Ex 6:14](https://ref.ly/Exod6:14), [Nu 26:5](https://ref.ly/Num26:5), [1 Ch 5:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:3), LXX Ενωχ.

> \[Str2599] † חֲנֹכִי **adj.gent.** of חֲנוֺךְ **4\.** only c. art. ´הַח \= subst. coll. [Nu 26:5](https://ref.ly/Num26:5).

> \[Str2593] † \[חָנִיךְ] **adj.** trained, tried, experienced, only pl. sf. חֲנִיכָיו יְלידֵי בֵיתוֺ, [Gn 14:14](https://ref.ly/Gen14:14) i.e. his tried and trusty men, born in his house.

> \[Str2598] † חֲנֻכָּה **n.f.** dedication, consecration, as a matter of usage only P and late (NH חֲנוּכָּה Feast of Dedication; so Aram. חֲנוּכְּתָא)—abs ´ח [Ne 12:27](https://ref.ly/Neh12:27); cstr. חֲנֻכַּת [Nu 7:10](https://ref.ly/Num7:10) \+ 6 t.; dedication of wall of Jerus. O1 Bae al. (opp. De), and esp. CheOP 234\. 247; \= *dedication\-offering* for altar in tabernacle [Nu 7:10](https://ref.ly/Num7:10) (acc. c. וַיַּקְרִיבוּ), cf. יַקְרִיבוּ אֶת־קָרְבָּנָם לַחֲנֻכַּת הַמִּזְבֵּחַ v:[11](https://ref.ly/Num7:11), also v:[84](https://ref.ly/Num7:84), v:[88](https://ref.ly/Num7:88) (all P; v Di [Nu 7:20](https://ref.ly/Num7:20)).

words [Ec 10:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:12). **2\.** *favour, acceptance:* **a.** *with men* [Pr 13:15](https://ref.ly/Prov13:15), [22:1](https://ref.ly/Prov22:1), [Ec 9:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:11). **b.** *with God* [Zc 4:7](https://ref.ly/Zech4:7), [4:7](https://ref.ly/Zech4:7), [12:10](https://ref.ly/Zech12:10); chiefly in phrases: מצא חן בעיני *find favour in the eyes of:* (*I*) *men* [Gn 30:27](https://ref.ly/Gen30:27), [32:6](https://ref.ly/Gen32:6), [33:8](https://ref.ly/Gen33:8), [33:10](https://ref.ly/Gen33:10), [33:15](https://ref.ly/Gen33:15), [34:11](https://ref.ly/Gen34:11), [39:4](https://ref.ly/Gen39:4), [47:25](https://ref.ly/Gen47:25), [47:29](https://ref.ly/Gen47:29), [50:4](https://ref.ly/Gen50:4), [Nu 32:5](https://ref.ly/Num32:5) (all J); [Dt 24:1](https://ref.ly/Deut24:1), [Ru 2:2](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:2), [2:10](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:10), [2:13](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:13), [1 S 1:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:18), [16:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:22), [20:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:3), [20:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:29), [25:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:8), [27:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:5), [2 S 14:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:22), [16:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:4), [1 K 11:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:19), [Est 5:8](https://ref.ly/Esth5:8), [7:3](https://ref.ly/Esth7:3); (*2*) *of God* [Gn 6:8](https://ref.ly/Gen6:8), [18:3](https://ref.ly/Gen18:3), [19:19](https://ref.ly/Gen19:19), [Ex 33:12](https://ref.ly/Exod33:12), [33:13](https://ref.ly/Exod33:13), [33:13](https://ref.ly/Exod33:13), [33:16](https://ref.ly/Exod33:16), [33:17](https://ref.ly/Exod33:17), [34:9](https://ref.ly/Exod34:9), [Nu 11:11](https://ref.ly/Num11:11), [11:15](https://ref.ly/Num11:15) (all J); Ju [6:17](https://ref.ly/Judg6:17), [2 S 15:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:25), [Pr 3:4](https://ref.ly/Prov3:4); abs. מצא חן (with man) [Pr 28:23](https://ref.ly/Prov28:23); (with God) [Je 31:2](https://ref.ly/Jer31:2); מֹצא חן לפניו [Est 8:5](https://ref.ly/Esth8:5) (i.e. of the king); נתן חן בעיני *give favour in the eyes of* (I) man [Gn 39:21](https://ref.ly/Gen39:21), [Ex 3:21](https://ref.ly/Exod3:21), [11:3](https://ref.ly/Exod11:3), [12:36](https://ref.ly/Exod12:36) (J); abs. נתן חן of man [Psalm 84:12](https://ref.ly/Ps84:12); (*2*) of God [Pr 3:34](https://ref.ly/Prov3:34); נשא חֵן בְּעֵינֵי *obtain favour in the eyes of* [Est 2:15](https://ref.ly/Esth2:15); of the king [5:2](https://ref.ly/Esth5:2); so וַתִּשָּא־חֵן וָחֶסֶד לְפָנָיו [Est 2:17](https://ref.ly/Esth2:17).

> \[Str2600] חִנָּם **subst.,** used chiefly in the accus. as **adv.** (cf. As. *annáma, in vain,* DlK 7, Pr 44; from הֵּן, with aff. ם—ָ, which is sometimes found in substantives proper, as סֻלָּם, and pr. names, but is more partic. used with substs. applied adverbially, as יוֺמָם אָמְנָם, דּוּמָם, רֵיקָם: Sta§ 203 BaNB § 216\)—lit. out of favour; i.e. **a.** *gratis, gratuitously, for nothing,* עָבַד חִנָּם to serve *for nought* [Gn 29:15](https://ref.ly/Gen29:15), [Jb 1:9](https://ref.ly/Job1:9), [Is 52:3](https://ref.ly/Isa52:3) to be sold (fig.) *for nought* v:[5](https://ref.ly/Isa52:5); [Ex 21:2](https://ref.ly/Exod21:2), [21:11](https://ref.ly/Exod21:11) יָצָא חִנָּם to go out(from slavery) *freely, for nothing,* [Nu 11:5](https://ref.ly/Num11:5) which we used to eat in Eg. *for nought,* [Je 22:13](https://ref.ly/Jer22:13), [1 Ch 21:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:24); in the genitive [2 S 24:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:24) עֹלוֺת חִנָּם i.e. burnt\-offerings *which cost nothing* (in the ‖ [1 Ch 21:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:24) the constr. is changed). **b.** *for no purpose, in vain* [Pr 1:17](https://ref.ly/Prov1:17), [Mal 1:10](https://ref.ly/Mal1:10): once אֶל חִנָּם (cf. אֶל **7**) Ez [6:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:10). **c.** *gratuitously, without cause, undeservedly,* esp. of groundless hostility or attack, [1 S 19:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:5) לְהָמִית חִנָּם to slay David *without cause,* [25:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:31), [Psalm 35:7](https://ref.ly/Ps35:7), [35:7](https://ref.ly/Ps35:7), [109:3](https://ref.ly/Ps109:3), [119:161](https://ref.ly/Ps119:161) רְדָפוּנִי חִנָּם, [Pr 1:11](https://ref.ly/Prov1:11), [3:30](https://ref.ly/Prov3:30), [23:29](https://ref.ly/Prov23:29), [Jb 2:3](https://ref.ly/Job2:3) ´לְבַלְּעוֺ ח [9:17](https://ref.ly/Ps9:17), [22:6](https://ref.ly/Ps22:6), Ez [14:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:23), [Psalm 35:19](https://ref.ly/Ps35:19) and [69:5](https://ref.ly/Ps69:5) שנְאַי חִנָּם my haters *without cause,* [La 3:52](https://ref.ly/Lam3:52) אֹיְבַי חִנָּם; in the genitive [1 K 2:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:31) דְּמֵי חִנָּם blood shed *without cause* (cf. [1 S 25:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:31)), [Pr 24:28](https://ref.ly/Prov24:28) ´אֲל־תְּהִי עֵד ח בְּרֵעֶָךָ, [26:2](https://ref.ly/Prov26:2) ´קִלְלַת ח the *causeless* curse.

> \[Str2581] †II. חֵן **n.pr.m.**(*favour*)—contemp. of Zerubbabel [Zc 6:14](https://ref.ly/Zech6:14), so RV SS We, but RVm Ew Hi Ke Or *grace, favour, kindness.*

> \[Str2433] † חִין **n.\[m.]** חִין עֶרְכּוֺ [Jb 41:4](https://ref.ly/Job41:4) the *grace* of his proportions ( \= חֵן Aram. חִנָּא) so AV RV Thes De Volck Da al.; meaning not very appropr. in context (description of crocodile); but nothing better has been proposed; v. suggestions in Di.

> \[Str2584] † חַנָּה **n.pr.f.** LXX Ἄννα (cf. Nab. חנה EutNo. 20\), mother of Samuel [1 S 1:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:2), [1:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:2), [1:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:5), [1:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:5), [1:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:8), [1:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:9), [1:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:13), [1:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:15), [1:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:19), [1:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:20), [1:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:22), [2:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:1), [2:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:21).

> \[Str2605] † חָנָן **n.pr.m.** (*gracious;* cf. Sab. חנן, Ph. חנא)—**1\.** one of the warriors of David [1 Ch 11:43](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:43). **2\.** one of the heads of families of Nethinim [Ezr 2:46](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:46) \= [Ne 7:49](https://ref.ly/Neh7:49). **3\.** names of Levites [Ne 8:7](https://ref.ly/Neh8:7), [10:11](https://ref.ly/Neh10:11), [13:3](https://ref.ly/Neh13:3). **4\.** chiefs of the people: **a.** [Ne 10:23](https://ref.ly/Neh10:23). **b.** [Ne 10:27](https://ref.ly/Neh10:27). **5\.** Benjamite name [1 Ch 8:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:23), [8:38](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:38), [9:44](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:44) (cf. n.pr.loc. בֵּית חָנָן, Mish. כְּעַר חָנָן). **6\.** אִישׁ הָאֱלֽהִים head of a prophetic guild, בְּנֵי חָנָן [Je 35:4](https://ref.ly/Jer35:4).

> \[Str2586] † חָנוּן (*favoured;* cf. As *Ḫanunu,* king of Gaza COT on [2 K 15:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:29));—**1\.** an Ammonite king [2 S 10:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:1), [10:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:2), [10:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:3), [10:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:4), [1 Ch 19:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:2), [19:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:2), [19:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:3), [19:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:4), [19:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:6). **2\.** Jewish chiefs, contemporaries of Nehemiah: **a.** [Ne 3:18](https://ref.ly/Neh3:18). **b.** [Ne 3:30](https://ref.ly/Neh3:30).

> \[Str2587] † חַנּוּן **adj.** gracious, only used as an attribute of God, as hearing the cry of the vexed debtor [Ex 22:26](https://ref.ly/Exod22:26) (covt. code), ‖ מְרַחֵם [Psalm 116:5](https://ref.ly/Ps116:5); elsewhere in the earlier phrase רֵחוּס וְחַנּוּן [Ex 34:6](https://ref.ly/Exod34:6) (JE) \= [Psalm 86:15](https://ref.ly/Ps86:15), [103:8](https://ref.ly/Ps103:8), or the later חַנּוּן וְרַחוּס [2 Ch 30:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:9), [Ne 9:17](https://ref.ly/Neh9:17), [9:31](https://ref.ly/Neh9:31), [Psalm 111:4](https://ref.ly/Ps111:4), [112:4](https://ref.ly/Ps112:4), [145:8](https://ref.ly/Ps145:8), [Jo 2:13](https://ref.ly/Joel2:13), [Jon 4:2](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:2).

> \[Str2594] † חֲנִינָה **n.f.** favour לא־אֶתֵּן לָכֶם חֲנִינָה *I will shew you no favour* [Je 16:13](https://ref.ly/Jer16:13) (v. on form Ba: NB 136\).

> \[Str2592] † חַנִּיאֵל **n.pr.m.** (*favour of Ēl;* cf. Ph. חנבעל, חנמלקרת (Hamilcar) *favour of Melqart* Nab. חנאל VogNo. 10\)—**1\.** prince of tribe of Manasseh [Nu 34:23](https://ref.ly/Num34:23). **2\.** a chief of the tribe of Asher [1 Ch 7:89](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:89).

> \[Str2606] † חֲנַנְאֵל **n.pr.m.** (*Ēl; is gracious;* cf. Ph. חננבעל, אלחנן, etc.)—name of a tower at Jerusalem [Je 31:38](https://ref.ly/Jer31:38), [Zc 14:10](https://ref.ly/Zech14:10), [Ne 3:1](https://ref.ly/Neh3:1), [12:39](https://ref.ly/Neh12:39).

> \[Str2582] † חֵנָדָד **n.pr.m.** (חֵן \+ הָדָד *favour of Hadad*)—Levite chief [Ezr 3:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:9), [Ne 3:18](https://ref.ly/Neh3:18), [3:24](https://ref.ly/Neh3:24), [10:10](https://ref.ly/Neh10:10).

> \[Str2607] † חֲנָנִי **n.pr.m.** (perh. Abbrev. From foll.);—**1\.** father of the prophet Jehu [1 K 16:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:1), [16:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:7), [2 Ch 16:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:7), [19:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:2), [20:34](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:34). **2\.** brother of Nehemiah [Ne 1:2](https://ref.ly/Neh1:2), [7:2](https://ref.ly/Neh7:2). **3\.** a chief musician of David [1 Ch 25:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:4), [25:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:25). **4\.** a chief musician in time of Nehemiah [Ne 12:36](https://ref.ly/Neh12:36). **5\.** a priest of Ezra's time [Ezr 10:20](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:20).

> \[Str2608] † חֲנַנְיָ֫הוּ, חֲנַנְיָה **n.pr.m.** (´י *hath been gracious*)—LXX Ἀνανίας; חֲנַנְיָהוּ: (cf. חנניהו on Israel. Seal, GanneauJas Fev.\-Mars 1883, 128, No.1\)—**1\.** father of a prince under Jehoiakim [Je 36:12](https://ref.ly/Jer36:12). **2\.** captain of Uzziah's army [2 Ch 26:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:11). **3\.** chief of one of the divisions musicians of David [1 Ch 25:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:23) \= חֲנַנְיָה v:[4](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:4).—חֲנַנְיָה: **4\.** a false prophet of Jeremiah's time [Je 28:1](https://ref.ly/Jer28:1), [28:5](https://ref.ly/Jer28:5), [28:10](https://ref.ly/Jer28:10), [28:11](https://ref.ly/Jer28:11), [28:12](https://ref.ly/Jer28:12), [28:13](https://ref.ly/Jer28:13), [28:15](https://ref.ly/Jer28:15), [28:15](https://ref.ly/Jer28:15), [28:17](https://ref.ly/Jer28:17). **5\.** one of the three companions of Daniel [Dn 1:6](https://ref.ly/Dan1:6), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Dan1:7), [1:11](https://ref.ly/Dan1:11), [1:19](https://ref.ly/Dan1:19). **6\.** grandfather of an officer of the guard in Jeremiah's time [Je 37:13](https://ref.ly/Jer37:13). **7\.** a son of Zerubbabel 1 Ch [3:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:19), [3:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:21). **8\.** a Benjamite [1 Ch 8:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:24). **9\.** various postexilic persons. **a.** [Ezr 10:28](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:28). **b.** [Ne 3:8](https://ref.ly/Neh3:8). **c.** [Ne 3:30](https://ref.ly/Neh3:30). **d.** [Ne 7:2](https://ref.ly/Neh7:2). **e.** [Ne 10:24](https://ref.ly/Neh10:24). **f.** [Ne 12:12](https://ref.ly/Neh12:12), [12:41](https://ref.ly/Neh12:41).

> \[Str2615] † חַנָּתֹן **n.pr.loc** place in the tribe of Zebulun [Jos 19:14](https://ref.ly/Josh19:14); LXX Αμωθ, Αενναθωθ, LXXL Αναθωθ; site unknown; acc. to Conder \= Talm, Caphar Hanania (NbrGeogr. 176\. 226\) on the border of Upper and Lower Galilee, mod. Kefr’ Anân, SurveyWP i. 205, 207\.

> \[Str8467] † תְּחִנּה **n.f.** favour, supplication for favour;—תְּחִנַּת [Jos 11:20](https://ref.ly/Josh11:20) \+ 4 t.; cstr. תְּחִנָּתִי [1 K 8:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:30), [8:52](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:52), [8:52](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:52); תְּחִנֹּתֵיהֶס [Je 37:20](https://ref.ly/Jer37:20) \+ 5 t.; תְּחִנֹּתֵיהֶם [2 Ch 6:39](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:39) etc. \+ 10 t. sf.;—**1\.** *favour,* shewn by Israel [Jos 11:29](https://ref.ly/Josh11:29) (D); מאת יהוה [Ezr 9:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:8). **2\.** *supplication for favour,* from God [1 K 8:52](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:52), [8:52](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:52), [2 Ch 33:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:13), [Psalm 119:170](https://ref.ly/Ps119:170); ‖ תְּפִלָּה [1 K 8:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:28) \= [2 Ch 6:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:19), [Psalm 6:10](https://ref.ly/Ps6:10), [55:2](https://ref.ly/Ps55:2); תחנה (ו) תפלה [1 K 8:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:38), [8:45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:45), [8:49](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:49), [8:54](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:54) \= [2 Ch 6:29](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:29), [6:35](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:35), [6:39](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:39), [1 K 9:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:3); אשׁר התפלל ´ת [1 K 8:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:30) (\= תַּחֲנוּן [2 Ch 6:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:21)); לִפְנֵי ´תִּפֹּלּ ת *let the supplication fall before,* Yahweh [Je 36:7](https://ref.ly/Jer36:7), the king [Je 37:20](https://ref.ly/Jer37:20), Jeremiah [Je 42:2](https://ref.ly/Jer42:2); לפני ´הִפִּיל ת *present supplication before,* Yahweh [Je 42:9](https://ref.ly/Jer42:9), [Dn 9:20](https://ref.ly/Dan9:20), the king [Je 38:26](https://ref.ly/Jer38:26).

> \[Str8468] † II. תְּחִנָּה **n.pr.m.** one in the line of Judah [1 Ch 4:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:12).

> \[Str8469] † תַּחֱנוּן **n.\[m]** only **pl.abstr.** supplication for favour;—abs. תַּחֲנוּנִים [Pr 18:23](https://ref.ly/Prov18:23) \+ 4 t.; cstr. תַּחֲנוּנֵי [Je 3:21](https://ref.ly/Jer3:21), [2 Ch 6:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:21); sf. 1 S. תַּחֲנוּנַי [Psalm 28:2](https://ref.ly/Ps28:2) etc., \+ 9 t. sf.; also תַּחֲנוּנוֺתָי [Psalm 86:6](https://ref.ly/Ps86:6); —**1\.** made to men: ´דִּבֶּר ת (a poor man to the rich) [Pr 18:23](https://ref.ly/Prov18:23); אֵל ´ת *supplication unto* (crocodile to man) [Jb 40:27](https://ref.ly/Job40:27); elsewhere **2\.** to God: ‖ תפלה [Psalm 143:1](https://ref.ly/Ps143:1), [Dn 9:3](https://ref.ly/Dan9:3), [9:17](https://ref.ly/Dan9:17); אשׁר התפלל ´ת [2 Ch 6:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:21) (\= ´קוֺל ת [1 K 8:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:30)); *voice of supplication* [Psalm 28:2](https://ref.ly/Ps28:2), [28:6](https://ref.ly/Ps28:6), [31:23](https://ref.ly/Ps31:23), [86:6](https://ref.ly/Ps86:6), [130:2](https://ref.ly/Ps130:2), [140:7](https://ref.ly/Ps140:7), also [116:2](https://ref.ly/Ps116:2) (for קוֺלִי is old case\-ending, and cstr. as LXX Vulgate Che Bae al.); ‖ בְּכִי [Je 31:9](https://ref.ly/Jer31:9); ´בְּכִּי ת [Je 3:21](https://ref.ly/Jer3:21); ´רוח חן ות [Zc 12:10](https://ref.ly/Zech12:10); לְפָנֶיךָ ´אֲנַחְנוּ מַפִּילִים ת [Dn 9:18](https://ref.ly/Dan9:18) *we are presenting our supplications before thee;* ´בִּתְחִלִּת ת [Dn 9:23](https://ref.ly/Dan9:23) *at the beginning of thy supplication*.

## II חָנַן (id: BDB03270)

\[Str2603] † II. \[חָנַן] be loathsome (cf. Ar. خَنَّ x. *foetorem emisit* (puteus); Syr. ܚܰܢܻܝܢܳܐ *rancid*);—only וְחַנֹּתִ֫י לִבְנֵי בִטְנִי [Jb 19:17](https://ref.ly/Job19:17) *and I am loath\-some* to the sons of my womb (‖ זָרָה v. II. זור), so RVm Ew De Di al. (On the tone v. De.)

> \[Str2609] † חָנֵס **n.pr.loc.** [Is 30:4](https://ref.ly/Isa30:4) in Lower Egypt, on island in Nile, S. of Memphis (‖ צֹּעַן) \= Egypt. *Ḥnnstn* \[*\*Ḥnênĕ* ], As. *Ḫininši;* Herodotii. 137  Ανυσις, afterward *Heracleopolis magna,* now *Ahnâs;* v. SteindorffBAS i. 602\.

* **Associated Passages:** Job 19:17; Isaiah 30:4
* **Associated Articles:** II זוּר (ID: `BDB02586@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## חָנֵף (id: BDB03272)

\[Str2610] † \[חָנֵף] **vb.** polluted, profane (Ar. حَنَفَ *incline, decline,* hence حَنِيفٌ *inclining to a right state,* but in Heb. of *inclining away from right, irreligion, profaneness,* cf. هَ.نِفَ *have a* حَنَفٌ i.e. *distortion of foot;* Syr. in deriv. *be profane,* etc.; NH Hiph., Aram. Aph. *act falsely toward, flatter,* חֲנוּפָה *hypocrisy;* As. *hanpu, ruthlessness; hanâpu, exercise ruthlessness toward,* Tel Amarna Zim: ZA vi, 1891, 256\)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. חָֽנְפָה [Is 24:5](https://ref.ly/Isa24:5); 3 pl. חָנֵפָוּ [Je 23:11](https://ref.ly/Jer23:11); *Impf.* 3 fs. תֶּחְֶנַף [Je 3:1](https://ref.ly/Jer3:1); תֱּהֶָנָף [Mi 4:11](https://ref.ly/Mic4:11); וֵתֶּחֱנַף [Je 3:9](https://ref.ly/Jer3:9), [Psalm 106:38](https://ref.ly/Ps106:38); *Inf. abs.* חָנוֺף [Je 3:1](https://ref.ly/Jer3:1); —**1\.** *be polluted:* of land [Is 24:5](https://ref.ly/Isa24:5), [Je 3:1](https://ref.ly/Jer3:1), [3:1](https://ref.ly/Jer3:1), [Psalm 106:38](https://ref.ly/Ps106:38); of Zion [Mi 4:11](https://ref.ly/Mic4:11). **2\.** of prophet and priest \= be חָנֵף (cf. infr.) i.e. *profane, godless* [Je 23:11](https://ref.ly/Jer23:11).—appar.trans וַתַּחֲנֵף את הארץ, Gf del. את, Gie cites Ges: § 121, 1, but rd. rather Hiph. וַתַּחֲנֵף with Codd. LXX Ewal. (v. also Gf.Gie). **Hiph.** *Impf.* יַחֲנַיף [Nu 35:33](https://ref.ly/Num35:33), [Dn 11:33](https://ref.ly/Dan11:33); 2 fs. וַתַּחֲנִ֫יפִי [Je 3:2](https://ref.ly/Jer3:2); 2 mpl. תַּחֲנִ֫יפוּ;—**1\.** *pollute:* the land [Nu 35:33](https://ref.ly/Num35:33), [35:33](https://ref.ly/Num35:33) (by blood; P), [Je 3:2](https://ref.ly/Jer3:2) (by idolatry, etc.) \+ [Je 3:9](https://ref.ly/Jer3:9) (v. supr.) **2\.** *make profane, godless* [Dn 11:32](https://ref.ly/Dan11:32) c. acc. Pers.

> \[Str2612] † חֹ֫נֶף **n.\[m.]** profaneness—´לִעֲשוֺת ח [Is 32:6](https://ref.ly/Isa32:6) *to practise profaneness* (\+ נְבָלָה, אָוֶַן, תּוֺעָה).

> \[Str2611] † חָנֵף **adj.** profane, irreligious (Syr. ܚܰܢܦܳܐ *profane, hence oft. Healthen, apostate;* Ar. حَنِيفٌ *inclining to a right state,* esp. *the true religion a Muslim*)—abs. ´ח [Jb 8:13](https://ref.ly/Job8:13) \+ 10 t.; pl. חֲנֵפִים [Is 33:14](https://ref.ly/Isa33:14); חַנְפֵי [Jb 36:13](https://ref.ly/Job36:13), [Psalm 35:16](https://ref.ly/Ps35:16); —*profane, godless:* of persons, ´אָדָם ח [Jb 34:30](https://ref.ly/Job34:30); ´גּוֺי ח [Is 10:6](https://ref.ly/Isa10:6); as subst. *godless man* [Is 9:16](https://ref.ly/Isa9:16) (‖ מרע) [Jb 8:13](https://ref.ly/Job8:13), [13:16](https://ref.ly/Job13:16), [17:8](https://ref.ly/Job17:8), [20:5](https://ref.ly/Job20:5), [27:8](https://ref.ly/Job27:8), [Pr 11:9](https://ref.ly/Prov11:9); coll. ´עֲדַת ח [Jb 15:34](https://ref.ly/Job15:34); pl. [Is 33:14](https://ref.ly/Isa33:14); חַנְפֵי־לֵב [Jb 36:13](https://ref.ly/Job36:13); בְּחַנְפֵי לֵעֲגֵי מָעוֺנ [Psalm 35:16](https://ref.ly/Ps35:16) *as profane men, mockers for cake* (i.e. table\-jesters; on cstr. v. Ges: § 130\. 5\), but txt. Dub. (v. Che: crit.n. Bae).

> \[Str2613] † חֲנֻפָּה (so Baer; van d. H חֲנֻפָךְ) **n.f.** profaneness, pollution, [Je 23:15](https://ref.ly/Jer23:15).

## חָנַק (id: BDB03276)

\[Str2614] † \[חָנַק] **vb.** strangle (NH *id.,* Aram. חֲנַק, ܚܢܰܩ; Ar. خَنَقَ);—**Niph.** *Impf.* וַיֵּחָנַָק [2 S 17:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:23) *strangled himself* (\+ וַיָּ֫מָת). **Pi.** *pt.* מְחַנֵּק [Na 2:13](https://ref.ly/Nah2:13) *strangle,* of lion strangling prey, fig. of Ninevitish king (object not expressed).

> \[Str4267] † מַחֲנָק **n.\[m.]** strangling, suffocation, as a mode of death, וַתִּבְחַר מַחֲנָק נַפְשִׁי [Jb 7:15](https://ref.ly/Job7:15)  *and my soul chooseth strangling* (‖ מָוֶת).

> \[Str2615] חַנָּתֹן v. sub. חִנן p. 337

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Samuel 17:23; Nahum 2:13; Job 7:15


## I חָסַד (id: BDB03279)

\[Str2616] † I. \[חָסַד] **vb. 1\.** be good, kind (NH חָסַד in deriv. חָסִיד *pious;* Aram חֲסַד *be kind, mild* (then *beg*), chiefly in deriv. חִסְדָּא etc.; cf. perh. Ar. حَشَدَ usu. pl. *they assembled,* sq. لَهُ *they combined for him, and took pains to shew him courtesy* Lane: 574 c, also RS: Proph. iv. n. 9 (and Schu AE v. in Thes); \>Thes al. Find primary meaning in *eager zeal* or *desire* (‖ קנא), whence develop *kindness* (as above), and *envy,* Ar. حَسَدَ, حَسَدٌ *envy* (vb. \& n.), Aram. חֲסַד *be put to shame,* ܚܰܣܶܕ, חַסֵּד *reproach, revile,* v, II. חסד)—only **Hithp.** *Impf.* עִס־חָסִיד תִּחְַסָּד *thou shewest thyself kind* [2 S 22:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:26) \= [Psalm 18:26](https://ref.ly/Ps18:26).—On **Pi.** [Pr 25:10](https://ref.ly/Prov25:10) v. II. חסד.

> \[Str2617] † חֶ֫סֶד: 247 **n.m.**: [2 S 16:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:17) goodness, kindness;—abs. ´ח [Gn 24:12](https://ref.ly/Gen24:12) \+ 85 t.; חָסֶד [Gn 39:21](https://ref.ly/Gen39:21) \+ 12 t.; cstr. חֶסֶד [1 S 20:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:14) \+ 8 t.; sf. חַסְדִּי [Psalm 59:18](https://ref.ly/Ps59:18) \+ 120 t. sfs.; pl. חֲסָדִיס [Gn 32:11](https://ref.ly/Gen32:11); cstr. חַסֵדֵי [Is 55:3](https://ref.ly/Isa55:3) \+ 5 t. (Baer: Jes p. 79 Ges: § 93, R, 1\. F.); sf. חֲסָדַי [Ne 13:14](https://ref.ly/Neh13:14) \+ 1o t. sfs.; (not in H or P). **I.** *of man:* **1\.** *kindness* of men towards men, in doing favours and benefits [1 S 20:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:15), [2 S 16:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:17), [Psalm 141:5](https://ref.ly/Ps141:5), [Pr 19:22](https://ref.ly/Prov19:22), [20:6](https://ref.ly/Prov20:6); יהוה ´ח [1 S 20:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:14) *the kindness of* ´י (such as he shews, Thes MV; that sworn to by oath to Yahweh Mich Dathe; shewn out of reverence to Yahweh Th Ke); cf. אלהים ´ח [2 S 9:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:3); ´תּוֺרַתאח [Pr 31:26](https://ref.ly/Prov31:26) *instruction in kindness, kindly instruction* עָשָה חֶסֶד עִמָּדִי *do or shew kindness* (in dealing) *with me* [Gn 20:13](https://ref.ly/Gen20:13), [40:14](https://ref.ly/Gen40:14) (E), [1 S 20:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:14), [2 S 10:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:2) (עִמִּי in ‖ [1 Ch 19:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:2)); c. עִם [Gn 21:23](https://ref.ly/Gen21:23) (E), [24:12](https://ref.ly/Gen24:12), [24:14](https://ref.ly/Gen24:14), [Jos 2:12](https://ref.ly/Josh2:12), [2:12](https://ref.ly/Josh2:12), Ju [1:24](https://ref.ly/Judg1:24) (J), [8:35](https://ref.ly/Judg8:35), [1 S 15:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:6), [2 S 2:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:5), [3:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:8), [9:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:1), [9:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:3), [9:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:7), [10:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:2) a \= [1 Ch 19:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:2) a, [1 Ch 19:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:2) b, [2 Ch 24:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:22); c. עַל [1 S 20:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:8) c. לְ [1 K 2:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:7); לפני ´נשא ח *obtain kindness before* [Est 2:9](https://ref.ly/Esth2:9), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Esth2:17); ´היטיב ח [Ru 3:10](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:10). **2\.** *kindness* (especially as extended to the lowly, needy and miserable), *mercy* [Pr 20:28](https://ref.ly/Prov20:28), [Jb 6:14](https://ref.ly/Job6:14); אישׁ חסד *merciful man* [Pr 11:17](https://ref.ly/Prov11:17) (opp. אַכְזָרִי); ´מַלְכֵּי ח *merciful kings* [1 K 20:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:31); ´עשה ח [Psalm 109:16](https://ref.ly/Ps109:16); in this sense usu. with other attributes (v. also infr. II. **2\.**); ‖ אמת [Ho 4:1](https://ref.ly/Hos4:1), [Is 16:5](https://ref.ly/Isa16:5); ואמת ´ח [Pr 3:3](https://ref.ly/Prov3:3), [14:22](https://ref.ly/Prov14:22), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Prov16:6), [20:28](https://ref.ly/Prov20:28); ואמת ´עשה ח [Gn 24:49](https://ref.ly/Gen24:49), [47:29](https://ref.ly/Gen47:29), [Jos 2:14](https://ref.ly/Josh2:14) (J; RV gives thse under **1**); ‖ צדקה [Ho 10:12](https://ref.ly/Hos10:12); ´צדקה וח [Pr 21:21](https://ref.ly/Prov21:21); ‖ משׁפט [Mi 6:8](https://ref.ly/Mic6:8); ומשׁפט ´ח [Ho 12:7](https://ref.ly/Hos12:7); ‖ חוֺנֵן [Psalm 109:12](https://ref.ly/Ps109:12); ורחמים ´ח [Zc 7:9](https://ref.ly/Zech7:9), [Dn 1:9](https://ref.ly/Dan1:9).—(On [Ho 6:4](https://ref.ly/Hos6:4), [6:6](https://ref.ly/Hos6:6) v. **3** infr.) **3\.** (rarely) *affection* if Isr. to ´י *love to God, piety:* נְעוּרַיִךְ ´ח [Je 2:2](https://ref.ly/Jer2:2) *priety of thy youth* (‖ *love of thine espousals* to Yahweh); poss. also חַסְדְּכֶס כַּעֲנַן־בֹּקֶר [Ho 6:4](https://ref.ly/Hos6:4) *your piety is like a morning cloud* (fleeting), and כִּי חֶסֶד חָפַחְתִּיּי וְל־אָֹ\+זבַח [Ho 6:6](https://ref.ly/Hos6:6) *for piety I delight in and not in peace\-offering* (‖ דעת אלהים, cf. [1 S 15:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:22));—so Wü Now Hi (v:[4](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:4)) Che; Ke Hi (v:[6](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:6)) al. sub **2** (or **1**);—נַנְשֵׁי חֶסֶד *men of piety* [Is 57:1](https://ref.ly/Isa57:1) (‖צַדִּיק); pl. *pious acts* [2 Ch 32:32](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:32), [35:26](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:26), [Ne 13:14](https://ref.ly/Neh13:14). **4\.** *lovely appearance:* כָּל־חַסְדּוֺ כְּצִיץ הַשָֹּׁדֶה [Is 40:6](https://ref.ly/Isa40:6) *all its loveliness as the flower of the field* (so Thes Hi De Che Di al.; but δόξα LXX I Pet 1:24 \& *gloria* Vulgate favour an original reading הוֺדוֺ Lo or כְּבֹדוֺ Ew, see BrMP 375; Du הֲדָרוֺ). **II.** *of God: kindness, lovingkindness* in condescendig to the needs of his creatures. He is חַסְדָּס *their goodness favour* [Jon 2:9](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:9); חַסְדִּי [Psalm 144:2](https://ref.ly/Ps144:2); אֱלֽהֵי חַסְדִּי *God of my kindness* [Psalm 59:18](https://ref.ly/Ps59:18); in v:[11](https://ref.ly/Ps59:11) rd. אֱלֽהַי חַסְדּוֺ *my God with his kindness* LXX Vulgate Ew Hup De Pe Che Bae; his is the kindness [Psalm 62:13](https://ref.ly/Ps62:13); it is with him [Psalm 130:7](https://ref.ly/Ps130:7); he delights in it [Mi 7:18](https://ref.ly/Mic7:18). **1\.** specif. lovingkindness: **a.** *in redemption from enemies and troubles* [Gn 19:19](https://ref.ly/Gen19:19), [39:21](https://ref.ly/Gen39:21) (J), [Ex 15:13](https://ref.ly/Exod15:13) (song), [Je 31:3](https://ref.ly/Jer31:3), [Ezr 7:28](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:28), [9:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:9), [Psalm 21:8](https://ref.ly/Ps21:8), [31:17](https://ref.ly/Ps31:17), [31:22](https://ref.ly/Ps31:22), [32:10](https://ref.ly/Ps32:10), [33:22](https://ref.ly/Ps33:22), [36:8](https://ref.ly/Ps36:8), [36:11](https://ref.ly/Ps36:11), [42:9](https://ref.ly/Ps42:9), [44:27](https://ref.ly/Ps44:27), [48:10](https://ref.ly/Ps48:10), [59:17](https://ref.ly/Ps59:17), [66:20](https://ref.ly/Ps66:20), [85:8](https://ref.ly/Ps85:8), [90:14](https://ref.ly/Ps90:14), [94:18](https://ref.ly/Ps94:18), [107:8](https://ref.ly/Ps107:8), [107:15](https://ref.ly/Ps107:15), [107:21](https://ref.ly/Ps107:21), [107:31](https://ref.ly/Ps107:31), [143:8](https://ref.ly/Ps143:8), [143:12](https://ref.ly/Ps143:12), [Jb 37:13](https://ref.ly/Job37:13), [Ru 1:8](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:8), [2:20](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:20); men should trust in it [Psalm 13:6](https://ref.ly/Ps13:6), [52:10](https://ref.ly/Ps52:8); rejoice in it [Psalm 31:8](https://ref.ly/Ps31:8); hope in it [Psalm 33:18](https://ref.ly/Ps33:18); [147:11](https://ref.ly/Ps147:11). **b.** *in preservation of life from death* [Psalm 6:5](https://ref.ly/Ps6:5), [86:13](https://ref.ly/Ps86:13), [Jb 10:12](https://ref.ly/Job10:12). **c.** *in quickening of spiritual life* [Psalm 109:26](https://ref.ly/Ps109:26), [119:41](https://ref.ly/Ps119:41), [119:76](https://ref.ly/Ps119:76), [119:88](https://ref.ly/Ps119:88), [119:124](https://ref.ly/Ps119:124), [119:149](https://ref.ly/Ps119:149), [119:159](https://ref.ly/Ps119:159). **d.** *in redemption from sin* [Psalm 25:7](https://ref.ly/Ps25:7), [51:3](https://ref.ly/Ps51:3). **e.** *in keeping the covenants,* with Abraham [Mi 7:20](https://ref.ly/Mic7:20); with Moses and Israel חֶסֶד(הַ)שׁמר הַבְּרִית וְ *keep\-eth the covenant and the lovingkindness* [Dt 7:9](https://ref.ly/Deut7:9), [7:12](https://ref.ly/Deut7:12), [1 K 8:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:23) \= [2 Ch 6:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:14), [Ne 1:5](https://ref.ly/Neh1:5), [9:32](https://ref.ly/Neh9:32), [Dn 9:4](https://ref.ly/Dan9:4); with David and his dynasty [2 S 7:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:15) \= [1 Ch 17:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:13), [2 S 22:51](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:51) \= [Psalm 18:51](https://ref.ly/Ps18:51), [1 K 3:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:6), [3:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:6) \= [2 Ch 1:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:8), [Psalm 89:29](https://ref.ly/Ps89:29), [89:34](https://ref.ly/Ps89:34); with the wife Zion [Is 54:10](https://ref.ly/Isa54:10). **2\.** חֶסֶד is grouped with other divine attributes: חסד ואמת *kindness* (*lovingkindness*) and *fidelity* [Gn 24:27](https://ref.ly/Gen24:27) (J), [Psalm 25:10](https://ref.ly/Ps25:10), [41:11](https://ref.ly/Ps41:11), [40:12](https://ref.ly/Ps40:12), [57:4](https://ref.ly/Ps57:4), [61:8](https://ref.ly/Ps61:8), [85:11](https://ref.ly/Ps85:11), [89:15](https://ref.ly/Ps89:15), [115:1](https://ref.ly/Ps115:1), [138:2](https://ref.ly/Ps138:2); ואמת עם ´עשה ח [2 S 2:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:6), [15:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:20) (LXX, v. Dr); ואמת ´רַב ח [Ex 34:6](https://ref.ly/Exod34:6) (JE), [Psalm 86:15](https://ref.ly/Ps86:15); also ‖אמת [Mi 7:20](https://ref.ly/Mic7:20), [Psalm 26:3](https://ref.ly/Ps26:3), [117:2](https://ref.ly/Ps117:2); ‖אֱמוּנָה [Psalm 88:12](https://ref.ly/Ps88:12), [89:3](https://ref.ly/Ps89:3), [92:3](https://ref.ly/Ps92:3); ´אמונה וח [Psalm 89:25](https://ref.ly/Ps89:25); ואמונה ´ח [Psalm 98:3](https://ref.ly/Ps98:3); ‖רחמים [Psalm 77:9](https://ref.ly/Ps77:9); ורחמיס ´ח [Je 16:5](https://ref.ly/Jer16:5), [Ho 2:21](https://ref.ly/Hos2:21), [Psalm 103:4](https://ref.ly/Ps103:4); ומשׁפט ´ח [Je 9:23](https://ref.ly/Jer9:23), [Psalm 101:1](https://ref.ly/Ps101:1); ‖צדקה [Psalm 36:11](https://ref.ly/Ps36:11); ´טוב וח [Psalm 23:6](https://ref.ly/Ps23:6). **3\.** *the kindness* of God is **a.** *abundant:* רַב־חֶסֶד *abundant, plenteous in kindness* (*goodness*) [Nu 14:18](https://ref.ly/Num14:18) (J), [Ne 9:17](https://ref.ly/Neh9:17) (Qr), [Jo 2:13](https://ref.ly/Joel2:13), [Jon 4:2](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:2), [Psalm 86:5](https://ref.ly/Ps86:5), [103:8](https://ref.ly/Ps103:8) (cf. [Ex 34:6](https://ref.ly/Exod34:6) JE; [Psalm 86:15](https://ref.ly/Ps86:15)); רֹב חַסְדְּךָ [Ne 13:22](https://ref.ly/Neh13:22), [Psalm 5:8](https://ref.ly/Ps5:8), [69:14](https://ref.ly/Ps69:14), [106:7](https://ref.ly/Ps106:7) (LXX Vulgate Aq Targan, to be preferred to MT חֲסָדֶיךָ); רֹב חֲסָדָו֯ [La 3:32](https://ref.ly/Lam3:32), [Psalm 106:45](https://ref.ly/Ps106:45) (Kt LXX in both to be preferred). **b.** *great in extent:* ´נֹּדֶל חִ *greatness of thy mercy* [Nu 14:19](https://ref.ly/Num14:19) (J); ´נְּדָוֺ֯לאח [Psalm 145:8](https://ref.ly/Ps145:8); it is kept for thousands [Ex 34:7](https://ref.ly/Exod34:7) (JE), [Je 32:18](https://ref.ly/Jer32:18), esp. of those connected with lovers of ´י, [Ex 20:6](https://ref.ly/Exod20:6) \= [Dt 5:10](https://ref.ly/Deut5:10); for 1000 generations [Dt 7:9](https://ref.ly/Deut7:9); it is great as the heavens [Psalm 57:11](https://ref.ly/Ps57:11), [103:11](https://ref.ly/Ps103:11), cf. [36:6](https://ref.ly/Ps36:6), [108:5](https://ref.ly/Ps108:5); the earth is full of it [Psalm 33:5](https://ref.ly/Ps33:5), [119:64](https://ref.ly/Ps119:64). **c.** *everlasting:* לעוֺלם חסדוֺ [Je 33:11](https://ref.ly/Jer33:11), [1 Ch 16:34](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:34), [16:41](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:41), [2 Ch 5:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:13), [7:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:3), [7:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:6), [20:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:21), [Ezr 3:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:11), [Psalm 100:5](https://ref.ly/Ps100:5), [106:1](https://ref.ly/Ps106:1), [107:1](https://ref.ly/Ps107:1), [118:1](https://ref.ly/Ps118:1), [118:2](https://ref.ly/Ps118:2), [118:3](https://ref.ly/Ps118:3), [118:4](https://ref.ly/Ps118:4), [118:29](https://ref.ly/Ps118:29), [136:1–26](https://ref.ly/Ps136:1-Ps136:26) (26 t.); חסדךָ לעולם [Psalm 138:8](https://ref.ly/Ps138:8); מעולם ´ח ועד עולם [Psalm 103:17](https://ref.ly/Ps103:17); עולם ´ח [Is 54:8](https://ref.ly/Isa54:8); אל כּל ´ח היום [Psalm 52:3](https://ref.ly/Ps52:3). **d.** *good:* כִּי־טוֺב חַסְדְּךָ [Psalm 69:17](https://ref.ly/Ps69:17), [109:21](https://ref.ly/Ps109:21); כי טוב חסדךָ מהיים [Psalm 63:4](https://ref.ly/Ps63:4). **4\.** pl. *mercies, deeds of kindness,* the historic displays of lovingkindness to Israel: shewn to Jacob [Gn 32:11](https://ref.ly/Gen32:11)(R); but mostly late [Is 63:7](https://ref.ly/Isa63:7), [Psalm 25:6](https://ref.ly/Ps25:6), [89:2](https://ref.ly/Ps89:2); כְּרֹב חסִדיו [Is 63:7](https://ref.ly/Isa63:7), see **3 a;** promised in the Davidic covenant [Psalm 89:50](https://ref.ly/Ps89:50); חַסְדֵי דָיִיד *mercies to David* [Is 55:3](https://ref.ly/Isa55:3), [2 Ch 6:42](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:42); *mercies* in general [La 3:22](https://ref.ly/Lam3:22), [Psalm 17:7](https://ref.ly/Ps17:7), [107:43](https://ref.ly/Ps107:43) f.—חֶסֶד in n.pr.m. ´בן־ח v. sub בֵּן. On [Lv 20:17](https://ref.ly/Lev20:17), [Pr 14:34](https://ref.ly/Prov14:34) v. II. חֶסֶד sub II. חסד.

> \[Str2623] † חָסִיד **adj.m.** kind, pious (so, as denoting active practice of חֶסֶד, kindness, Thes MV De and most, cf. קָצִיר, פָּקִיד etc.; \> Hup on [Psalm 4:4](https://ref.ly/Ps4:4) RVm who expl. as passive *reception* of ´י's חֶסֶד, cf. שָֹכֹיר, אָסִיר etc.; its use as attribute of God [Je 3:12](https://ref.ly/Jer3:12), [Psalm 145:17](https://ref.ly/Ps145:17), and the context [Psalm 12:1](https://ref.ly/Ps12:1), [Mi 7:2](https://ref.ly/Mic7:2) etc., favour active sense)—´ח [Psalm 4:4](https://ref.ly/Ps4:4) \+ 9 t.; חֲסִידֶָךָ [Dt 33:8](https://ref.ly/Deut33:8), [Psalm 89:20](https://ref.ly/Ps89:20); חֲסִידְי֯ךָ [Psalm 16:10](https://ref.ly/Ps16:10); pl. חֲסִידִיס [Psalm 149:1](https://ref.ly/Ps149:1), [149:5](https://ref.ly/Ps149:5); sf. חֲסִידָי [Psalm 50:5](https://ref.ly/Ps50:5); חֲסִידָו֯ [1 S 2:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:9) \+ 15 t. sfs.;—**1\.** *kind:* **a.** of man תתחסד ´עם ח *with the kind thou shewest thyself kind* [2 S 22:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:26) \= [Psalm 18:26](https://ref.ly/Ps18:26). **b.** of wing of ostrich אִס אֶבְרָה חֲסִידָה [Jb 39:13](https://ref.ly/Job39:13) *is it a kindly pinion?* poss. with play on חֲסִידָה n.f. *strok* (is the ostrich kind like the stork?). **c.** of God, only [Je 3:12](https://ref.ly/Jer3:12), [Psalm 145:17](https://ref.ly/Ps145:17). **2\.** *pious, godly,* either as exhibition of ‘duteous love’ toward God (CheOP 378\), or (in view of rarity of such passages as [Ho 6:4](https://ref.ly/Hos6:4), [6:6](https://ref.ly/Hos6:6), [Je 2:2](https://ref.ly/Jer2:2), and their possible ambiguity) because *kindness,* as prominent in the godly, comes to imply other attributes, and to be a designation of the godly character, *piety:* **a.** as adj.—גּוֺי לא חסיד *a nation, not pious,* \= *ungodly* [Psalm 43:1](https://ref.ly/Ps43:1). **b.** elsewh. as subst.: sing., *a pious man, the godly* [Psalm 4:4](https://ref.ly/Ps4:4), [12:2](https://ref.ly/Ps12:2), [32:6](https://ref.ly/Ps32:6), [86:2](https://ref.ly/Ps86:2), ‖יָשָׁר [Mi 7:2](https://ref.ly/Mic7:2); (*thy*) *pious one*(*s*) [Psalm 16:10](https://ref.ly/Ps16:10) (Kt pl.), [1 S 2:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:9) (Qr pl.) [Pr 2:8](https://ref.ly/Prov2:8), אִישׁ חֲסִידֶָךָ *men of thy pious one* [Dt 33:8](https://ref.ly/Deut33:8) (Moses, v. Di; others, *the man, thy godly one,* i.e. Levi); pl. *the pious, godly,* those of the people who were faithful, devoted to God's service, only in Psalter and chiefly, if not entirely, in late Psalms [Psalm 149:1](https://ref.ly/Ps149:1), [149:5](https://ref.ly/Ps149:5); *his pious ones* [Psalm 30:5](https://ref.ly/Ps30:5), [31:24](https://ref.ly/Ps31:24), [37:28](https://ref.ly/Ps37:28), [85:9](https://ref.ly/Ps85:9), [97:10](https://ref.ly/Ps97:10), [116:15](https://ref.ly/Ps116:15), [148:14](https://ref.ly/Ps148:14), [149:9](https://ref.ly/Ps149:9); *thy pious ones* [Psalm 52:11](https://ref.ly/Ps52:11), [79:2](https://ref.ly/Ps79:2), [132:9](https://ref.ly/Ps132:9) \= [2 Ch 6:41](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:41), [Psalm 145:10](https://ref.ly/Ps145:10); *my pious ones* [Psalm 50:5](https://ref.ly/Ps50:5), *her* (Zion's) *pious* [Psalm 132:16](https://ref.ly/Ps132:16).—(In Maccab. age, συναγωγὴ Ἀσιδαίων denoted, technically, the party of *the pious,* who opposed the Hellenization of the Judaea, v. 1 Macc 2:42, 7:13, 2 Macc 14:6 and CheOP 48, 56; so perhaps [Psalm 116:15](https://ref.ly/Ps116:15), [149:1](https://ref.ly/Ps149:1), [149:5](https://ref.ly/Ps149:5), [149:9](https://ref.ly/Ps149:9).)

> \[Str2624] † I. חֲסִידָה **n.f.** stork (so called as kind and affectionate to its young)—[Lv 11:19](https://ref.ly/Lev11:19) \= [Dt 14:18](https://ref.ly/Deut14:18), [Psalm 104:17](https://ref.ly/Ps104:17), [Je 8:7](https://ref.ly/Jer8:7), [Zc 5:9](https://ref.ly/Zech5:9).

> \[Str2619] † חֲסַדְיָה **n.pr.m.** (*Yah is kind*) son of Zerubbabel [1 Ch 3:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:20).

## II חָסַד (id: BDB03284)

\[Str2616] † II. \[חָסַד] **vb.** be reproached, ashamed (Aramaism, v. RSProph. iv, n. 9; Aram. חֲסַד *be put to shame*, ܚܰܣܶܕ, חַסֵּד *reproach, revile;* חִסּוּדָא, ܚܶܣܕܳܐ *shame, reproach*, oft. in Targum LXX for חֶרְפָּה);—only **Pi.** *Impf.* פֶּן־וְחַסֶּדְךָ [Pr 25:10](https://ref.ly/Prov25:10) *lest he reproach thee, expose thee to shame.*

> \[Str2617] † II. חֶ֫סֶד **n.m.** shame, reproach, only abs.:—הוּא ´ח [Lv 20:17](https://ref.ly/Lev20:17) (H) *it is a shame* (shameful thing); לְאֻמִּים חַטָּאת ´ח [Pr 14:34](https://ref.ly/Prov14:34) *sin is a reproach to peoples.*

* **Associated Passages:** Proverbs 25:10; Proverbs 14:34; Leviticus 20:17


## חָסָה (id: BDB03286)

\[Str2620] † חָסָה **vb.** (mostly poet. and fig.) **seek refuge** (Ar. حَشِىَ III. is *set aside;* v. *go aside, apart;* حَشَني *shelter, protection;* but ? ش \= ס)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ח [Psalm 64:11](https://ref.ly/Ps64:11); 3 fs. חָסָ֫יָה [Psalm 57:2](https://ref.ly/Ps57:2), חָסִ֫יתִי [Psalm 7:2](https://ref.ly/Ps7:2) \+ 7 t.; pl. חָסוּ [Psalm 37:40](https://ref.ly/Ps37:40), [Zp 3:12](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:12), חָסָ֫יוּ [Dt 32:37](https://ref.ly/Deut32:37); *Impf.* יֶחְסֶּה [Psalm 34:9](https://ref.ly/Ps34:9), תֶחְסֶּה [Psalm 91:4](https://ref.ly/Ps91:4), אֶחְסֶּה [Psalm 57:2](https://ref.ly/Ps57:2) (see Baer [Psalm 34:9](https://ref.ly/Ps34:9)), אֶחֱסֶה [Psalm 18:3](https://ref.ly/Ps18:3) \+ 2 t.; *pt.* חוֺסֶה [Is 57:13](https://ref.ly/Isa57:13), חֹסֶה [Pr 14:32](https://ref.ly/Prov14:32), חוֺסִים [Psalm 17:7](https://ref.ly/Ps17:7) \+ 3 t.; חֹסְים [Pr 30:5](https://ref.ly/Prov30:5), [2 S 22:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:31) (see Baer [Pr 30:5](https://ref.ly/Prov30:5)), חוֺסֵי [Psalm 2:12](https://ref.ly/Ps2:12), [5:12](https://ref.ly/Ps5:12), חֹסֵי [Na 1:7](https://ref.ly/Nah1:7); —*seek refuge*, c. בְּ:בְּצֵל in the shadow of a tree Ju [9:15](https://ref.ly/Judg9:15), בְּצֵל מחריס [Is 30:2](https://ref.ly/Isa30:2); בָּהּ [Is 14:32](https://ref.ly/Isa14:32) in Zion, in gods [Dt 32:37](https://ref.ly/Deut32:37) (poem), elsewhere *in God* [2 S 22:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:3) \= [Psalm 18:3](https://ref.ly/Ps18:3), [Na 1:7](https://ref.ly/Nah1:7), [Psalm 2:12](https://ref.ly/Ps2:12), [5:12](https://ref.ly/Ps5:12), [7:2](https://ref.ly/Ps7:2), [11:1](https://ref.ly/Ps11:1), [16:1](https://ref.ly/Ps16:1), [25:20](https://ref.ly/Ps25:20), [31:2](https://ref.ly/Ps31:2), [31:20](https://ref.ly/Ps31:20), [34:9](https://ref.ly/Ps34:9), [34:23](https://ref.ly/Ps34:23), [37:40](https://ref.ly/Ps37:40), [57:2](https://ref.ly/Ps57:2), [64:11](https://ref.ly/Ps64:11), [71:1](https://ref.ly/Ps71:1), [118:8](https://ref.ly/Ps118:8), [118:9](https://ref.ly/Ps118:9), [141:8](https://ref.ly/Ps141:8), [144:2](https://ref.ly/Ps144:2), [Is 57:13](https://ref.ly/Isa57:13); Ges§ 45\. 1 a; *abs.* חָסוֺר [Gn 8:5](https://ref.ly/Gen8:5); *Pt.* חָסֵר [1 K 11:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:22), [Ec 10:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:3) (v. חָסֵר infr.);—**1\.** lack: **a.** c. acc. [Gn 18:28](https://ref.ly/Gen18:28) (J) *perchance the fifty righteous lack five*, [Dt 2:7](https://ref.ly/Deut2:7) *thou hast not lacked anything*, [8:9](https://ref.ly/Deut8:9), [Je 44:18](https://ref.ly/Jer44:18), [1 K 11:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:22), Ez [4:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:17), [Psalm 34:11](https://ref.ly/Ps34:11), [Pr 31:11](https://ref.ly/Prov31:11), cf. also בַּחֲסַ־לֵב [Pr 10:21](https://ref.ly/Prov10:21) *by lacking intelligence (sense)*, (Di puts here [Dt 15:8](https://ref.ly/Deut15:8), v. infr.) **b.** abs. *be in want, want* [Psalm 23:1](https://ref.ly/Ps23:1), [Pr 13:25](https://ref.ly/Prov13:25), [Ne 9:21](https://ref.ly/Neh9:21). **2\.** *be lacking,* לוֺ ´מַחְסֹרוֺ אֲשֶׁר יֶח [Dt 15:8](https://ref.ly/Deut15:8) *his lack* (i.e. thing needed), *which is lacking to him* (possible also is, *which he lacks for himself,* so Di, v. supr.); ´עַל־רֹאְשְׁךָ אַל־יֶח [Ec 9:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:8) *oil on thy head let it not be lacking;* v. also abs. [Is 51:14](https://ref.ly/Isa51:14), Ct [7:3](https://ref.ly/Song7:3), and לִבּוֺ חָסֵר [Ec 10:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:3) *his sense is lacking;* of jar of oil [1 K 17:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:14), [17:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:16) (abs.) by meton. for the oil itself (cf. ‖כלה *be consumed, exhausted*). **3\.** *diminish, decrease,* of waters [Gn 8:3](https://ref.ly/Gen8:3) (P), cf. הָלוֺךְ וְחָסוֺר v:[5](https://ref.ly/Gen8:5) (P), waters *continually diminished* (v. הלך supr. p. 233:b). **Pi.** *cause to lack,* c. acc. Pers., *Impf.* 2 ms. sf. וַתְּחַסְּרֵהוּ מעט מֵאֱלהים [Psalm 8:6](https://ref.ly/Ps8:6) *and thou didst make him lack little of God; Pt.* וּמְחַסֵּר את־נפשׁי מִטּוֺבָה [Ec 4:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:8) for whom am I labouring *and depriving myself of good things?* **Hiph.** *Pf.* הֶחֱסִיר [Ex 16:18](https://ref.ly/Exod16:18); *Impf.* יַחְסִיר [Is 32:6](https://ref.ly/Isa32:6); —*cause to be lacking, fail,* c. acc. rei ´מַשְׁקֵה צָמֵא יַח [Is 32:6](https://ref.ly/Isa32:6); *the drink of the thirsty he causeth to fail* (‖לְהָרִיק נפשׁ רָעֵב); abs. [Ex 16:18](https://ref.ly/Exod16:18) (P), *he that gathered little caused no lack*.

[Psalm 34:13](https://ref.ly/Ps34:13), [35:27](https://ref.ly/Ps35:27), [Mal 3:1](https://ref.ly/Mal3:1); c. inf. [Ne 1:11](https://ref.ly/Neh1:11); abs. הֶחָפֵץ *whosoever would* [1 K 13:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:33); אם חפץ אתה *if thou pleasest* [1 K 21:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:6); נֶפֶשׁ חֲפֵצָה *willing soul* [1 Ch 28:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:9); pl. cstr. before nouns abs. [Psalm 35:22](https://ref.ly/Ps35:22), [40:15](https://ref.ly/Ps40:15) \= [70:3](https://ref.ly/Ps70:3); דְּרוּשִׁים לְכָל־חֶפְצֵיהֶם *studied of all who take pleasure in them* [Psalm 111:2](https://ref.ly/Ps111:2). **2\.** *of God*, לאֹ אֵ־ל חָפֵץ רֶשַׁע אַתָּה *thou art not a God taking pleasure in wickedness* [Psalm 5:5](https://ref.ly/Ps5:5).

> \[Str2656] † חֵ֫פֶץ **n.m.**:[Pr 3:15](https://ref.ly/Prov3:15) delight, pleasure;—´ח [Is 54:12](https://ref.ly/Isa54:12) \+ 20 t.; sf. חֶפְצְךָ [Is 58:13](https://ref.ly/Isa58:13) etc. \+ 13 t. sfs.; pl. חֲפָצִים [Pr 8:11](https://ref.ly/Prov8:11); sf. חֲפָצֶיךָ [Pr 3:15](https://ref.ly/Prov3:15); חֲפָצֶ֭ךָ [Is 58:13](https://ref.ly/Isa58:13)—**1\.** *delight* ´אבני ח [Is 54:12](https://ref.ly/Isa54:12) *delightful stones;* ´אֶרֶץ ח [Mal 3:12](https://ref.ly/Mal3:12) *delightsome land;* ´דִּבְּרֵי ח [Ec 12:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:10); so perhaps also ´בִּגְדֵי ח Ez [27:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:20) *garments of delight*, i.e. of beauty and luxury (Gr; MT חֹפֶשׁ q.v.); c. בְּ of persons [Psalm 16:3](https://ref.ly/Ps16:3), [Ec 5:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:3), [Mal 1:10](https://ref.ly/Mal1:10); of things [1 S 15:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:22), [18:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:25), [Psalm 1:2](https://ref.ly/Ps1:2), [Jb 21:21](https://ref.ly/Job21:21), [Ec 12:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:1); כְּלִי אֵין חֵפֶץ בּוֺ *vessel wherein is no pleasure* [Je 22:28](https://ref.ly/Jer22:28), [48:38](https://ref.ly/Jer48:38), [Ho 8:8](https://ref.ly/Hos8:8); הַחֵפֶץ לְשַׁדַּי כִּי [Jb 22:3](https://ref.ly/Job22:3) *is it a pleasure to Shadday that ?* **2\.** *desire, longing* דלים ´מנע מח [Jb 31:16](https://ref.ly/Job31:16) *withhold the poor from* (*their*) *desire;* מְחוֺז חֶפְצָם *the city of their desire* [Psalm 107:30](https://ref.ly/Ps107:30); כל חפץ *all* (*one's*) *desire* [2 S 23:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:5), [1 K 5:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:22), [5:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:23), [5:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:24), [9:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:11), [10:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:13) \= [2 Ch 9:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:12); כל חפצים *all things to be desired* [Pr 3:15](https://ref.ly/Prov3:15), [8:11](https://ref.ly/Prov8:11). **3\.** *the good pleasure, will, purpose*, of Yahweh [Is 44:28](https://ref.ly/Isa44:28), [46:10](https://ref.ly/Isa46:10), [48:14](https://ref.ly/Isa48:14). **4\.** *that in* which one takes delight, his *business* (late), or *matter* (very late, cf. in Mish. \= *thing*) בְּיָדוֺ יִצְלַח ´חֵפֶץ י *the good pleasure* (*cause, business*) *of Yahweh will prosper in his hands* [Is 53:10](https://ref.ly/Isa53:10); ´מצא ח [Is 58:3](https://ref.ly/Isa58:3), [58:13](https://ref.ly/Isa58:13); עֲשוֺת חֲפָצֶךָ *doing thy affairs* [Is 58:13](https://ref.ly/Isa58:13) (see De in loco); בְּחֵפֶץ כַּפֶּיהָ *in the business of her hands* [Pr 31:13](https://ref.ly/Prov31:13); עֵתּ לְכָל־חֵפֶץ *time for every matter, affair* [Ec 3:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:1), [3:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:17), [8:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:6); אַ־ל תִּתְּמַהּ עַל־הַחֵפֶץ *marvel not at the matter* [Ec 5:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:7).
> 
>  *Note*.—חפץ is not used in any of its forms in E D2 H P of the Hexateuch.

> \[Str2657] † חֶפְצִי־בָהּ **n.pr.f.** (*my delight is in her;* cf. Ph. חפצבעל)—**1\.** mother of king Manasseh [2 K 21:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:1). **2\.** fig. name of Zion [Is 62:4](https://ref.ly/Isa62:4), here expl. by כִּי חפץ יהוה בָּח *for Yahweh delighteth in thee*.

## חָפַץ (id: BDB03328)

\[Str2654] † \[חָפַץ] **vb.** bend down (Ar. خَفَضَ *lower, depress*, as wings Qor 15:88\)—only **Qal** *Impf.* יַחְפֹּץ זְנָבוֺ כְמוֺ־אֶרֶז [Jb 40:17](https://ref.ly/Job40:17); *he bendeth down (extendeth down stiffly) his tail like a cedar*, v. Wetzst in DeJob 526 HiHiob 299\.

* **Associated Passages:** Job 40:17


## I חָפַר (id: BDB03329)

\[Str2658] † I. חָפַר **vb.** dig, search for (Ar. حَفَرَ *id.;* Aram. חֲפַר, ܚܦܰܪ, *id.;* NH חֵפֶר *act of digging*)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ח [Jb 39:29](https://ref.ly/Job39:29); וְחָפַרְתָּ֫ [Jb 11:18](https://ref.ly/Job11:18); וְחָפַרְתָּ֫ה [Dt 23:14](https://ref.ly/Deut23:14); חָפַרְתִּי [Gn 21:30](https://ref.ly/Gen21:30); חָֽפְרוּ [Gn 26:18](https://ref.ly/Gen26:18) \+ 2 t.; חָפָרוּ [Gn 26:32](https://ref.ly/Gen26:32); חֲפָרוּהָ [Nu 21:18](https://ref.ly/Num21:18); *Impf.* וַיַּחְפֹּר [Gn 26:18](https://ref.ly/Gen26:18), [26:22](https://ref.ly/Gen26:22); וַיַּחְפְּרֵהוּ [Psalm 7:16](https://ref.ly/Ps7:16); וָאֶחְפֹּר [Je 13:7](https://ref.ly/Jer13:7); יַחְפְּרוּ Jb [Jb 39:21](https://ref.ly/Job39:21) (but rd. יַחְפֹּר so LXX Vulgate SyrVer Di) [Dt 1:22](https://ref.ly/Deut1:22); וַיַּחְפְּרוּ [Gn 26:19](https://ref.ly/Gen26:19) \+ 2 t.; וַיַּחְפְּרֻהוּ [Jb 3:21](https://ref.ly/Job3:21); *Inf. cstr.* לַחְפֹּר [Jos 2:2](https://ref.ly/Josh2:2), [2:3](https://ref.ly/Josh2:3) \+ [Is 2:20](https://ref.ly/Isa2:20) (cf. infr.); *Pt.* חֹפֵר [Ec 10:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:8); —**1\.** *dig:* **a.** a well, c. acc. בְּאֵר [Gn 21:30](https://ref.ly/Gen21:30) (E), [26:15](https://ref.ly/Gen26:15), [26:18](https://ref.ly/Gen26:18), [26:18](https://ref.ly/Gen26:18), [26:19](https://ref.ly/Gen26:19), [26:21](https://ref.ly/Gen26:21), [26:22](https://ref.ly/Gen26:22), [26:32](https://ref.ly/Gen26:32) (J), [Nu 21:18](https://ref.ly/Num21:18) (E); cf. [Ex 7:24](https://ref.ly/Exod7:24) (E); *dig* (a hole, implied as obj.) [Dt 23:14](https://ref.ly/Deut23:14); a pit, with hostile purpose, as snare or trap, fig. of malicious plan, c. acc. בּוֺר [Psalm 7:16](https://ref.ly/Ps7:16) (‖כָּרָה); so c. acc. גומץ [Ec 10:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:8), cf. \[שַׁחַת]לְנַפְשִׁי ´ח [Psalm 35:7](https://ref.ly/Ps35:7); of horse, *dig*, i.e. *paw* the ground, יַחְפֹּר בָּעֵמֶק [Jb 39:21](https://ref.ly/Job39:21) (so rd., v. supr.) *he paweth in the valley* (cf. Ar. حَافِرٌ *hoof*). **b.** *dig* for something hidden, c. acc. [Jb 3:21](https://ref.ly/Job3:21) (fig. of longing for death), [Je 13:7](https://ref.ly/Jer13:7) (no obj. expr.) **2\.** *search, search out, explore*, c. acc. הָאָרֶץ [Jos 2:3](https://ref.ly/Josh2:3), [2:3](https://ref.ly/Josh2:3) (JE), [Dt 1:22](https://ref.ly/Deut1:22); of eagle, *search for* food, c. acc. אֹכֶל ´ח [Jb 39:29](https://ref.ly/Job39:29), with esp. reference to keen vision (‖מֵרָחוֺק עֵינָיו יַבִּיטוּ); so \= *search* or *look carefully about* before going to rest [Jb 11:18](https://ref.ly/Job11:18) (no obj.).—לַחְפֹּר [Is 2:20](https://ref.ly/Isa2:20) v. חֲפַרְפָּרָה infr.

> \[Str2660] † I. חֵ֫פֶר **n.pr.m. 1\.** a Manassite [Nu 26:32](https://ref.ly/Num26:32), [26:33](https://ref.ly/Num26:33), [27:1](https://ref.ly/Num27:1) [Jos 17:2](https://ref.ly/Josh17:2), [17:3](https://ref.ly/Josh17:3), LXX Οφερ. **2\.** a man of Judah [1 Ch 4:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:6); LXX Ηφαλ, LXXL Αφερ. **3\.** one of David's heroes, acc. to [1 Ch 11:36](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:36) (but on txt. v. Be VB and Dr [2 S 23:36](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:36)).

> \[Str2662] † חֶפְרִי **adj. gent.** of I. חֵפֶר **a**; only c. art. as n.pr. coll. ´הַח [Nu 26:32](https://ref.ly/Num26:32).

> \[Str2660] † II. חֵ֫פֶר **n.pr.loc. 1\.** Canaanitish town, with a king, named just before Aphek [Jos 12:17](https://ref.ly/Josh12:17), exact site unknown. **2\.** [1 K 4:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:10) in Judah (´ארץ ח);—on ´גִּ֫תָּה ח [Jos 19:13](https://ref.ly/Josh19:13), ´גַּת הַח [2 K 14:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:25), v. גַּת sub יגן.

> \[Str2663] † חֲפָרַיִם **n.pr.loc.** in Issachar [Jos 19:19](https://ref.ly/Josh19:19), LXX Αγειν, A Αφεραειμ, LXXL Αμφαραιμ; \= Egypt. Ḥa\-pu\-ru\-m\-â WMMAsien, 170; site dub.; 6 m. N. of *Legio* acc. to LagOnom. 223, 2nd ed. 241\.

> \[Str2661,6512] † \[חֲפַרְפָּרָה] **n.f.** mole (as *digger*)—only [Is 2:20](https://ref.ly/Isa2:20), rd. לַחֲפַרְפָּרוֺת (MT לַחְפֹּר פֵּרוֺת, meaning obscure, v. conject. in Thes GesComm. Di).

## II חָפֵר (id: BDB03335)

\[Str2659] † II. \[חָפֵר] **vb.** be abashed, ashamed (Targum חֲפַר [Pr 13:5](https://ref.ly/Prov13:5), מַחְפְּרָנָא *one causing shame* [Pr 19:26](https://ref.ly/Prov19:26); Syr. ܚܦܰܪ, esp. Aph. *be ashamed, put to shame;* Ar. خَفَرَ *be bashful*, خَفِرٌ *shy, bashful;* Eth. ኀፈረ፡ *be ashamed, blush*)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. חָֽפְרָה [Je 50:12](https://ref.ly/Jer50:12); וְחָֽפְרָה consec. [Is 24:23](https://ref.ly/Isa24:23); וְחָפֵָרָה [Je 15:9](https://ref.ly/Jer15:9); חָֽפְרוּ [Psalm 71:24](https://ref.ly/Ps71:24); וְחָֽפְרוּ consec. [Mi 3:7](https://ref.ly/Mic3:7); *Impf.* יַחְפְּרוּ [Psalm 35:4](https://ref.ly/Ps35:4) \+ 4 t.; יֶחְפָּרוּ [Psalm 34:6](https://ref.ly/Ps34:6); וַיֶּחְפָּרוּ [Jb 6:20](https://ref.ly/Job6:20); תַּחְפְּרוּ [Is 1:29](https://ref.ly/Isa1:29); —*be abashed, ashamed*, said of face [Psalm 34:6](https://ref.ly/Ps34:6) (avoided by looking to ´י); elsewhere always ‖בּוֺשׁ, of enemies of righteousness [Psalm 71:24](https://ref.ly/Ps71:24), [35:4](https://ref.ly/Ps35:4), [35:26](https://ref.ly/Ps35:26), [40:15](https://ref.ly/Ps40:15) \= [70:3](https://ref.ly/Ps70:3), [83:18](https://ref.ly/Ps83:18); of idolaters [Is 1:29](https://ref.ly/Isa1:29); diviners [Mi 3:7](https://ref.ly/Mic3:7); of distressed Jerusalem [Je 15:9](https://ref.ly/Jer15:9); so of Babylon [50:12](https://ref.ly/Jer50:12); of moon [Is 24:33](https://ref.ly/Isa24:33); *be put to shame* (disappointment), of caravans looking for water [Jb 6:20](https://ref.ly/Job6:20). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הֶחְפִּיר [Is 33:9](https://ref.ly/Isa33:9); *Impf.* יַחְפִּיר [Pr 13:5](https://ref.ly/Prov13:5); תַּחְפִּ֫ירִי [Is 54:4](https://ref.ly/Isa54:4); *Pt.* מַחְפִּיר [Pr 19:26](https://ref.ly/Prov19:26); —*display shame*, fig. of Lebanon [Is 33:9](https://ref.ly/Isa33:9) (‖קָמַָל ‘ dried up ’ Che); of Israel is [54:4](https://ref.ly/Isa54:4) (‖בּוֺשׁ Qal); of an unfilial son, *cause shame* [Pr 19:26](https://ref.ly/Prov19:26) (‖מֵבִישׁ); cf. [Pr 13:5](https://ref.ly/Prov13:5) of wicked (‖יבאישׁ: v. באשׁ **Hiph.** supr. p. 93\).

> \[Str6548] † חָפְרַע **n.pr.m.** Apries, reigned in Egypt alone, B.C. 589 – 570 and with Amasis 570–564; named as מֶלֶח־מִצְרַיִם ´פַּרְעֹה ח [Je 44:30](https://ref.ly/Jer44:30) *Pharaoh Hophraï king of Egypt* (v. פַּרְעֹה); 4th king of 26th dynasty; LXX Οὐαφρη; Manetho Οὔαφρις; Egypt. Monum. *Uaḥ\-ȧbra*, WiedemannÄgypt. Gesch. 602, 636 ff.; Gesch. Ägypt. 163 ff.; GK. Ἀπρίης Herodot.:ii. 161 etc. (v. WiedHerodot's 2tes Buch, 569\), Diodori. 68; Ἀπρίας Ctesias (Athen. 13:560\).

> \[Str2661] חֲפַרְפָּרֶה v. sub I. חפר. above

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 35:26; Micah 3:7; Isaiah 24:23; Psalms 83:18; Isaiah 54:4; Jeremiah 15:9; Psalms 35:4; Psalms 70:3; Jeremiah 50:12; Psalms 34:6; Jeremiah 44:30; Proverbs 13:5; Psalms 40:15; Proverbs 19:26; Isaiah 33:9; Isaiah 1:29; Isaiah 24:33; Job 6:20; Psalms 71:24
