## חָפַש (id: BDB03338)

\[Str2664] † \[חָפַש] **vb.** search (TargumJer חֲפַס *dig, seek;* Pal. Syr. *dig* (Schw); perh. As. *êppêšu, êtpêšu, sensible*, LyonSargontexte 65\)—**Qal** *Impf.* 2 ms. sf. תַּחְפְּשֶ֫נָּה [Pr 2:4](https://ref.ly/Prov2:4); יַחְפְּשוּ [Psalm 64:7](https://ref.ly/Ps64:7); נַחְפְּשָה [La 3:40](https://ref.ly/Lam3:40); *Pt.* חֹפֵש [Pr 20:27](https://ref.ly/Prov20:27); —*search, search out*, fig.; **1\.** *search for*, obj. בִּינָה etc. [Pr 2:4](https://ref.ly/Prov2:4) (‖בקשׁ). **2\.** \= *think out, devise*, c. acc. עולות unjust acts [Psalm 64:7](https://ref.ly/Ps64:7) (cf. also sub. **Pu.**) **3\.** *search* \= *test*, [La 3:40](https://ref.ly/Lam3:40) (obj. דרכינו; ‖חקר), [Pr 20:27](https://ref.ly/Prov20:27). **Niph.** *Pf.* נַחְפְּשׁוּ Ob:[6](https://ref.ly/Obad1:6) subj. עֵשָו coll.; *searched out* \= exposed and plundered (‖נִבְעוּ מַחְפּוּנָיו). **Pi.** *Pf.* וְחִפַּשְתִּ֫י [1 S 23:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:23); וְחִפְּשוּ [1 K 20:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:6); *Impf.* וַיְחַפֵּש [Gn 31:35](https://ref.ly/Gen31:35) \+ 2 t.; אֲחַפֵּש [Am 9:3](https://ref.ly/Amos9:3), [Zp 1:12](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:12); *Imv.* חַפְּשוּ [2 K 10:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:23); —**1\.** *search through*, c. acc., a house [1 K 20:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:6), Jerusalem (metaph.) [Zp 1:12](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:12); no obj. expr. [2 K 10:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:23). **2\.** *search for:* **a.** a person c. acc. [1 S 23:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:23). **b.** a thing, c. acc. [Gn 44:12](https://ref.ly/Gen44:12) (J; Joseph searching for his cup); [Gn 31:35](https://ref.ly/Gen31:35) (E; no obj. expr., Laban looking for his teraphim); [Am 9:3](https://ref.ly/Amos9:3) (´י searching for evildoers), [Psalm 77:7](https://ref.ly/Ps77:7) (soul searching to understand ´י's dealings with his servants). **Pu.** *Impf.* יְחֻפַּש [Pr 28:12](https://ref.ly/Prov28:12) *be searched for* \= be hidden; *Pt.* מְחֻפָּש [Psalm 64:7](https://ref.ly/Ps64:7) in ´חֵפֶש מ \= *a searched out search*, i.e. a device well thought out (cf. Che; ‖ **Qal** q.v.); v. also חֵ֫פֶש. **Hithp.** *Pf.* הִתְחַפֵּש [2 Ch 35:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:22) (but LXX הִתְחַזֵּק, cf. also Vulgate SyrVer 3 [Esdr 1:26](https://ref.ly/Esth1:26), so Be Öt); *Impf.* יִתְחַפֵּש [Jb 30:18](https://ref.ly/Job30:18); וַיִּתְחַפֵּש [1 S 28:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:8) \+ 3 t.; *Inf. abs.* הִתְחַפֵּש [1 K 22:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:30) \= [2 Ch 18:29](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:29); —*disguise oneself* (lit. *let oneself be searched for*) [1 S 28:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:8), [1 K 22:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:30), [22:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:30) \= [2 Ch 18:29](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:29), [18:29](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:29), [2 Ch 35:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:22) (but v. supr.), all of disguise by change of garments; [1 K 20:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:38) באפר i.e. with headgear over eyes; subj. לבוש [Jb 30:18](https://ref.ly/Job30:18), i.e. his garment is disguised, no longer looks like the mantle it is.

[Je 34:9](https://ref.ly/Jer34:9), [34:10](https://ref.ly/Jer34:10), [34:11](https://ref.ly/Jer34:11), [34:14](https://ref.ly/Jer34:14), [34:16](https://ref.ly/Jer34:16); of slave (male or female) set free on account of injury done [Ex 21:26](https://ref.ly/Exod21:26), [21:27](https://ref.ly/Exod21:27) (JE); מֵאֲדֹנָיו ´עֶבֶד ח [Jb 3:19](https://ref.ly/Job3:19) *a slave is free from his master* (i.e. in She’ôl); but בַּמֵּתִים חָפְשִׁי [Psalm 88:6](https://ref.ly/Ps88:6) *among the dead* I am *free* (i.e. adrift, cut off from Yahweh's remembrance); more gen., שַׁלַּח רְצוּצִים חָפְשִׁים [Is 58:6](https://ref.ly/Isa58:6) *to let oppressed ones go free*. **2\.** *free* from taxes, obligations, etc. אֵת בֵּית אָבִיו יַעֲשֶה חָפְשִׁי בְּיְשְרָאֵל [1 S 17:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:25) *his father's house will he make free in Israel*.

> \[Str2669] † חָפְשִׁית, חפשׁות **n.f.** freedom, separateness, only בֵּית הַחָפְשִׁית [2 K 15:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:5) \= [2 Ch 26:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:21) Qr (Kt החפשׁות) i.e. (si vera 1\.) a separate house—a house apart (on account of his disease).

## חצן (id: BDB03357)

חצן (√ of foll.; meaning dub.; denom. seem to be Ar. حَضَنَ, Eth. ሐፀነ፡ *carry in the arms* or *bosom, rear, foster;* perhaps also As. *êṣênu, bring together*, also *drink* or *draw in* sweet odours Flood Tabletiii, 49 Hpt in KAT2 Gloss i).

> \[Str2683] † \[חֵ֫צֶן] **n.m.** bosom of a garment (Ar. حِضْنٌ, Eth.ሕፅን፡ also Syr. ܚܰܢܳܐ *bosom* (\= ܚܥܢܐ \*), v. HoffmZMG 1878, 753 LagM ii. 361 ff.; BN 46 f.; on this word and foll. v. BaNB 120; ZMG 1889, 183\)—שֶׁלּאֹ מִלֵּא כַפּוֺ קוֺצֵר וְחִצְנוֺ מְעַמֵּר [Psalm 129:7](https://ref.ly/Ps129:7) (grass) *with which a reaper filleth not his hand, nor a binder his bosom*.

> \[Str2684] † חֹ֫צֶן **n.m.** bosom;—חָצְנִי נָעַרְתִּי [Ne 5:13](https://ref.ly/Neh5:13) *I shook out my bosom* (the bosom of my garment); וְהֵבִיאוּ בָּנַיִח בְּחֹ֫צֶן [Is 49:22](https://ref.ly/Isa49:22) *and they shall bring thy sons in the bosom* (i.e. in the arms, clasped to the bosom, like infants; ‖עַל־כָּתֵף).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 129:7; Isaiah 49:22; Nehemiah 5:13

## II חצר (id: BDB03368)

II. חחר (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. حَضَرَ *be present, settle* or *dwell*, v. LagBN 47 f.; Sab. חחַר *fixed abode* OS (Levy):ZMG l865, 185 CISiv, 1\. 2, No. 79\. 82\).

347:a. I. חָחֵר end: add ‘[42:6](https://ref.ly/Ps42:6) הַחִיצֹנוֺת for הַחֲצֵרוֺת.’

> \[Str2691,2699] II. \[חָצֵר]:46 **n.m.**:[Ne 11:25](https://ref.ly/Neh11:25) settled abode, settlement, village (Lagl. c.)—always pl., abs. חֲצֵרִים [Jos 19:8](https://ref.ly/Josh19:8) \+ 5 t.; cstr. חַצְרֵי [1 Ch 9:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:16), [Ne 12:28](https://ref.ly/Neh12:28); sf. חֲצֵרֶיהָ [Jos 21:12](https://ref.ly/Josh21:12) \+ 4 t.; חַצְרֵיהֶם [1 Ch 4:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:33) \+ 6 t.; חַצְרֵיהֶן [Jos 13:23](https://ref.ly/Josh13:23) \+ 24 t. Jos;—**a.** settlements of Ishmaelites [Gn 25:16](https://ref.ly/Gen25:16) (P; ‖טִירוֺת *circles* of tents); of עַוִּים [Dt 2:23](https://ref.ly/Deut2:23); of Kedar [Is 42:11](https://ref.ly/Isa42:11) (‖עָרִים); בָּתֵּי הַחֲצֵרִים אֲשֶׁר אֵין־לָהֶם חֹמָה סָבִיב [Lv 25:31](https://ref.ly/Lev25:31) (H), *houses of the villages which have no wall about them;* מַאֲרַב חֲצֵרִים [Psalm 10:8](https://ref.ly/Ps10:8) *lurking\-place(s) of villages* (i.e. where oppressor, as a wild beast, watches his opportunity to attack the defenceless). Usu. **b.** as dependencies of cities [Jos 15:46](https://ref.ly/Josh15:46), with בְּנֹתֶיהָ (v. supr. בַּת **4** p. 123\) [Jos 15:45](https://ref.ly/Josh15:45), [15:47](https://ref.ly/Josh15:47), [15:47](https://ref.ly/Josh15:47) (all JE ?); אֲשֶׁר סְבִיבוֺת הֶעָרִים ´וְכָל־הַח [19:8](https://ref.ly/Josh19:8) (P), also [21:12](https://ref.ly/Josh21:12) (P), \= [1 Ch 6:41](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:41), [Ne 12:29](https://ref.ly/Neh12:29); also [Jos 13:23](https://ref.ly/Josh13:23), [13:28](https://ref.ly/Josh13:28), [15:32](https://ref.ly/Josh15:32) \+ 23 t. Jos (all P), [Ne 11:25](https://ref.ly/Neh11:25), [11:30](https://ref.ly/Neh11:30) (‖בָּנוֺת v:[25](https://ref.ly/Neh11:25), v:[27](https://ref.ly/Neh11:27) etc.), [12:28](https://ref.ly/Neh12:28). **c.** villages, not contrasted with cities (cf. [Is 42:11](https://ref.ly/Isa42:11) supr.) [1 Ch 9:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:16), [9:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:22), [9:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:25) (all of Levites). **d.** ´ח appar. incl. all settlements outside of Jerusalem ´הַח בִּשְדֹתָם [Ne 11:25](https://ref.ly/Neh11:25).—´ח in n.pr.loc. v. infr. (and see Bla on [Jos 15:20](https://ref.ly/Josh15:20) – [32](https://ref.ly/Josh15:32)).

> \[Str2692] † חֲצַר־אַדָּר **n.pr.loc** place on southern border of Canaan (Judah) [Nu 34:4](https://ref.ly/Num34:4) LXX ἔπαυλις Αραδ; \= חֶצְרוֺן (q.v.) [Jos 15:3](https://ref.ly/Josh15:3) (\+ אַדָּ֫רָה) LXX Σαραδα, A LXXL Αδδαρα.

> \[Str2703–04] † חֲצַר עֵינוֺן, עֵינָן ´ח **n.pr.loc.** on NE. border of Canaan Ez [47:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:17); חֲצַר עֵינָן [48:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:1) (Co in both חָצֵ֫רָךְ עֵינוֺן); עֵינָן ´ח [Nu 34:9](https://ref.ly/Num34:9), [34:10](https://ref.ly/Num34:10); where it is said (v:[9](https://ref.ly/Num34:9)) זֶה יִהְיֶה לָכֶם גְּבוּל צָפוֺן; LXX Αρσεναιειμ, LXXL Ασερναιν;—v. also חָצֵר הַתִּיכוֺן infr.

> \[Str2705] † חֲצַר שׁוּעָל **n.pr.loc** **1\.** place in southern Judah [Jos 15:28](https://ref.ly/Josh15:28) \= [1 Ch 4:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:28), [Ne 11:27](https://ref.ly/Neh11:27) **2\.** place in Simeon [Jos 19:3](https://ref.ly/Josh19:3).—LXX Αρσωλα, Ξολασεωλα, Ασαρσουλα, Ασαρσουαρ, etc.

WinerBW *Hazor* Gf Gie.—´עֵין ח [Jos 19:37](https://ref.ly/Josh19:37), v. sub עַיִן.

> \[Str2681] † I. חָצִיר **n.\[m.]** settled abode or haunt (‖ form of חָצֵר *supr.*, or txt. err. for it);—נְוֵה תַנִּים חָצִיר לִבְנוֺת יַעֲנָה [Is 34:13](https://ref.ly/Isa34:13) *a dwelling of jackals (and) a hunt for ostriches;* also בִּנְוֵת תַנִּים רִבְצָהּ חָצִיר לְקָנֶה וָ\+גֹמֶא [35:7](https://ref.ly/Isa35:7) *an abode, home, of reeds and rushes* (so Che Di Du al.; others put this under II. חָצִיר); Che Du think the v. mutilated, and would emend this line acc. to [34:13](https://ref.ly/Isa34:13).

> \[Str2700] † חֲצַרְמָ֫וֶת **n.pr.m.** (Ar. حَضْرَمَوْت, Sab. חצַרמות, חצַרמת, OsZMG 1865, 289 ff. DHMZMG 1883, 18\. 412\)—a ‘ son ’ of יָקְטָן, 5th in order from Shem (שֵׁם, 1\. אַרְפַּכְשַׁד; 2\. שֶׁלַת; 3\. עֵבֶר; 4\. יָקְטָן; 5\. ´ח [Gn 10:26](https://ref.ly/Gen10:26) \= [1 Ch 1:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:20), LXX Ασαρ(α)μωθ; \= **n.pr.terr.** a district in southern Arabia, where dwelt the Ξατραμωτῖται (Straboxvi. 4\. 2\); mod. *Ḥaḍramaut* (or *Ḥaḍramût*, see MaltzanZMG 1871, 498 MordtZMG 1876, 323, van d. Bergp. 9, v. infr.) is same land, but not quite co\-extensive; v. Di [Gn 10:26](https://ref.ly/Gen10:26), RitterErdkunde xii (Arabien i) 609 ff., de GoejeḤadhr. 1886, van den BergḤadhramout, 1886, GlaserSkizze ii. 20, 428 ff. etc.; on its precise limits see esp. Ritter611 ff, de Goejel. c. van d. Bergch. i.

## III חצר (id: BDB03384)

III. חחר (√ of foll.; Ar. خَضِرَ *be green*).

> \[Str2682] † II. חָצִיר **n.m.**:[Is 15:6](https://ref.ly/Isa15:6) **green grass, herbage**, abs. חָצִיר [Nu 11:5](https://ref.ly/Num11:5) \+ 16 t.; cstr. חֲצִיר [Is 37:27](https://ref.ly/Isa37:27) \= [2 K 19:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:26), [Psalm 129:6](https://ref.ly/Ps129:6); —**1\.** *grass*, as food for animals [1 K 18:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:5), [Jb 40:15](https://ref.ly/Job40:15), [Psalm 104:14](https://ref.ly/Ps104:14), [147:8](https://ref.ly/Ps147:8), [Pr 27:25](https://ref.ly/Prov27:25) (‖דֶּשֶׁא and עֵשֶב), [Is 15:6](https://ref.ly/Isa15:6) (‖דֶּשֶׁא and יֶרֶק); spec. of *leeks* (as still sometimes in Aram., v. Löwpp. 226, 228\) [Nu 11:5](https://ref.ly/Num11:5) (v. Di); in sim. of abundant growth [Is 44:4](https://ref.ly/Isa44:4) (v. LXX Ew Che); on [Is 35:7](https://ref.ly/Isa35:7) v. 1\. חָצִיר *supr.* **2\.** as type of what is quickly perishing [Jb 8:12](https://ref.ly/Job8:12), hence fig. of perishing enemies חֲצִיר גַּגּוֺת [Is 37:27](https://ref.ly/Isa37:27) \= [2 K 19:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:26) (‖דֶּשֶׁא, עֵשֶב), [Psalm 129:6](https://ref.ly/Ps129:6), i.e. having no depth of root; of wicked, soon to be cut down [Psalm 37:2](https://ref.ly/Ps37:2); with special reference to Israel's oppressors [Is 40:6](https://ref.ly/Isa40:6) (‖\[השרה]ציץ), v:[7](https://ref.ly/Isa40:7) (‖ *id.*), v:[7](https://ref.ly/Isa40:7), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Isa40:8) (‖ *id.*), [51:12](https://ref.ly/Isa51:12); in sim. of frail man [Psalm 90:5](https://ref.ly/Ps90:5); man's days [Psalm 103:15](https://ref.ly/Ps103:15) (‖ציץ השדה).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 37:2; Isaiah 37:27; Isaiah 40:6; Job 8:12; Isaiah 44:4; Psalms 104:14; Job 40:15; Psalms 129:6; Psalms 90:5; 1 Kings 18:5; Isaiah 40:8; Isaiah 51:12; Proverbs 27:25; Isaiah 15:6; Psalms 147:8; 2 Kings 19:26; Isaiah 35:7; Psalms 103:15; Isaiah 40:7; Numbers 11:5


## IV חצר (id: BDB03386)

IV. חצר (assumed as √ for redupl. חצרצר whence foll.; mng. unknown; perh. onomatop., v. Thes Ol:§§ 82 c.188 a Sta:§124 b; see also LagOr ii.18\).

> \[Str2689] † חֲצֹצְרָה **n.f.** clarion (NH חֲצוֺצֶרֶת, Aram. חֲצוֺצַרְתָּצ)—mostly P and late;—abs. ´ח [Ho 5:8](https://ref.ly/Hos5:8); pl. abs. חֲצֹצְרוֺת [Nu 10:8](https://ref.ly/Num10:8) \+ 22 t.; חֲצֹצְרֹת [Nu 10:9](https://ref.ly/Num10:9), [10:10](https://ref.ly/Num10:10); cstr. *id.* [Nu 31:6](https://ref.ly/Num31:6), [2 Ch 13:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:12); חֲצוֺצְרֹת [Nu 10:2](https://ref.ly/Num10:2); *clarion:* **1\.** as secular instr. [Ho 5:8](https://ref.ly/Hos5:8) (‖שׁוֺפָר) [2 K 11:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:14), [11:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:14) \= [2 Ch 23:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:13), [23:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:13). **2\.** as sacred instr. [2 K 12:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:14), esp. for use by priests (only P, Psalm 98 and Chr). **a.** ´תקע בח (of blowing a single long blast) [Nu 10:3](https://ref.ly/Num10:3), [10:4](https://ref.ly/Num10:4), [10:7](https://ref.ly/Num10:7), [10:8](https://ref.ly/Num10:8), to gather congreg. or ´נשיא together, and, on festivals, over sacrif., ‘ to be remembered before ´י, ’ v:[10](https://ref.ly/Num10:10). **b.** ´תקע תרועה בח, or ´הריע בח (of sounding alarm,—a series of quick blasts) for camps to move [Nu 10:5](https://ref.ly/Num10:5), [10:6](https://ref.ly/Num10:6), also in battle, v:[9](https://ref.ly/Num10:9), ‘ to be remembered before ´י; ’ so [Nu 31:6](https://ref.ly/Num31:6), [2 Ch 13:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:12) (cf. v:[14](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:14)), both התרועה ´ח. **c.** esp. in Chr's descriptions of ceremonies at festivals, to express rejoicing: [1 Ch 13:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:8), [15:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:28) (‖קוֺל שׁוֺפָר), [16:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:6), [16:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:42), [2 Ch 15:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:14) (‖שׁוֺפָר), [20:28](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:28), [29:26](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:26), [29:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:27), [Ezr 3:10](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:10), [Ne 12:35](https://ref.ly/Neh12:35), [12:41](https://ref.ly/Neh12:41), [Psalm 98:6](https://ref.ly/Ps98:6) (‖קוֺל שׁוֺפָר); ´הָרִים קוֺל בח [2 Ch 5:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:13); ´מחצצרים בח [1 Ch 15:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:24), [2 Ch 5:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:12), [5:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:13), [13:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:14); in [2 Ch 29:28](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:28) this pt. agrees with noun in *sense*, and is masc.; *and the clarions* (\= players on the clarions) *sounded*.—The חֲצֹצְרָה, or (sacred) clarion, was a long, straight, slender metal tube, with flaring end, v. BenzArchäol. 277; distinguished thus from the שׁוֺפָר which was originally a ram's horn, and prob. always retained the horn\-shape; the שׁוֺפָר is mentioned constantly in the earlier lit., and was used by watchmen, warriors, etc., as well as priests (v. Benzib. 276 and שׁוֺפָר).

> \[Str2690] † \[חצצר] **vb.** Kt, \[חצר] Qr, denominat. from חצצרה \= *sound a clarion*—**Pi.** *Pt.* מְחַצְּצ֯רִים [2 Ch 5:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:13) (Qr מְחַצְּרִים) \= *players on clarions*. **Hiph.** *Pt.* מַחְצְצ֯רִים (Qr מַחְצְרִים as Hiph. v. Köii, 252\) [1 Ch 15:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:24) \+ 3 t. \+ [2 Ch 5:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:12) Baer, (van d. H מחצררים); *sound with clarions* ´מח בחצצרות [1 Ch 15:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:24), [2 Ch 5:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:12), [13:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:14); abs. *sounded (sounding)* [2 Ch 7:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:6), [29:28](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:28), cf. חצצרה *ad fin.* (Kt in all to be pronounced (prob.) מְחַצְצְרִים).

> \[Str2436,2706] חֵק v. חֵיק sub חוק; חֹק v. sub חקק. p. 300, 349

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Chronicles 20:28; Numbers 10:2; 2 Kings 12:14; Numbers 10:7; 1 Chronicles 13:8; 1 Chronicles 16:42; 1 Chronicles 15:24; Nehemiah 12:41; 2 Chronicles 29:28; 2 Chronicles 13:14; Nehemiah 12:35; Numbers 10:4; 2 Chronicles 5:13; Numbers 10:8; Psalms 98:6; Numbers 10:9; 1 Chronicles 16:6; 2 Chronicles 7:6; Numbers 10:5; 2 Chronicles 13:12; 2 Kings 11:14; 2 Chronicles 29:26; 2 Chronicles 29:27; Ezra 3:10; Numbers 31:6; Numbers 10:6; Hosea 5:8; 2 Chronicles 23:13; 2 Chronicles 15:14; 1 Chronicles 15:28; Numbers 10:10; 2 Chronicles 5:12; Numbers 10:3
* **Associated Articles:** חוק (ID: `BDB02917@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## חָקָה (id: BDB03390)

\[Str2707] † חָקָה **vb.** only *Pu.* cut in, carve (NH *represent, imitate;* ‖חקק)—**Pu.** *Pt.* מְחֻקֶּה [1 K 6:35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:35), Ez [8:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:10) \+ [23:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:14) (Co מְחֻקִּים, after LXX SyrVer Targum Vulgate), *carved* figure on wall Ez [8:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:10), [23:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:14) (where rd. אנשׁים מחקים, v. supr.) \= subst. *carved work* [1 K 6:35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:35) (on doors of temple). **Hithp.** *Impf.* 2 ms. על־שָׁרְשֵׁי רַגְלַי תִּתְחַקֶּה [Jb 13:27](https://ref.ly/Job13:27) *thou gravest thee a* *graving* (\= markest a line) *for* (i.e. about) *the soles of my feet*, fixest limits for them (v. Di).

> \[Str2708] חֻקָּה v. sub חקק. below

> \[Str2709] † חֲקוּפָא **n.pr.m.** head of a family of returning exiles [Ezr 2:51](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:51) \= [Ne 7:53](https://ref.ly/Neh7:53), LXX Αφεικα, Αχειφα, Ακουφα, etc.

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 8:10; Ezra 2:51; Job 13:27; Nehemiah 7:53; 1 Kings 6:35; Ezekiel 23:14


## חָקַק (id: BDB03393)

\[Str2710] † \[חָקַק] **vb.** cut in, inscribe, decree (NH *id.*, Aram. חֲקַק, Zinj. חקק; Ph. *id. Pt.* Hiph.; Ar. حَقَّ *be just, right, obligatory*, also *make* or *decide to be just*, etc.; حَقٌّ *justness, truth, necessity, obligation;* Eth. ሕቅ፡ adj. *moderate, sufficient;* cf. also חקה)—**Qal** וְחַקּוֺתָ֫ Ez [4:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:1); sf. חַקֹּתִיח [Is 49:16](https://ref.ly/Isa49:16); *Inf.* sf. בְּחֻקּוֺ [Pr 8:27](https://ref.ly/Prov8:27); בְּחוּקוֺ [Pr 8:29](https://ref.ly/Prov8:29) (assim. to בְּשוּמוֺ); חֻקָּהּ [Is 30:8](https://ref.ly/Isa30:8); *Pt.* חֹקְקִי [Is 22:16](https://ref.ly/Isa22:16) (archaic case ending Ges§ 90\. 3 a); pl. חֹקְקִים [Is 10:1](https://ref.ly/Isa10:1); cstr. חוֺקְקֵי Ju [5:9](https://ref.ly/Judg5:9) (poss. Poïel \= מחוקקי with מְ omitted, v. Ges§ 52 (2\), note 6\); *Pt. Pass.* pl. חֲקֻקִַים Ez [23:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:14) (Co חקוקים);—**1\.** *cut in*, with בְּ, [Is 22:16](https://ref.ly/Isa22:16), of a dwelling\-place \= tomb, in a rock. **2\.** *cut in* or *on, upon, engrave, inscribe*, c. על on roll of a book [Is 30:8](https://ref.ly/Isa30:8); representation of city on brick (as in Babylonia) Ez [4:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:1); images on a wall Ez [23:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:14); fig. of Zion's walls on palms of ´י [Is 49:16](https://ref.ly/Isa49:16). **3\.** *trace, mark out*, a circle, c. על over the face of deep [Pr 8:27](https://ref.ly/Prov8:27); c. acc., foundations of earth v:[29](https://ref.ly/Prov8:29). **4\.** of a law, *engrave, inscribe* (on a tablet), fig. for *enact, decree* חקקים חקקי און [Is 10:1](https://ref.ly/Isa10:1) (‖כתב עמל); חוֺקקֵי ישראל poet. \= *commanders* (v. **Poïel**) Ju [5:9](https://ref.ly/Judg5:9) (‖מחקקים v:[14](https://ref.ly/Judg5:14)). **Poïel** *Impf.* יְחוֺקְקוּ [Pr 8:15](https://ref.ly/Prov8:15); *Pt.* מְחֹקֵק [Gn 49:10](https://ref.ly/Gen49:10), [Nu 21:18](https://ref.ly/Num21:18), [Dt 33:21](https://ref.ly/Deut33:21); sf. מְחֹקְקִי [Psalm 60:9](https://ref.ly/Ps60:9), [108:9](https://ref.ly/Ps108:9); מְחֹקְקֵנוּ [Is 33:22](https://ref.ly/Isa33:22); pl. מְחֹקְקִים Ju [5:14](https://ref.ly/Judg5:14); —*inscribe* (as a law), *enact* (poet.): c. acc. צֶדֶק [Pr 8:15](https://ref.ly/Prov8:15) elsewh. pt. **a.** *prescriber* of laws, hence (as sovereign authority in a warlike clan) *commander* [Dt 33:21](https://ref.ly/Deut33:21) (of warlike tribe of Gad), Ju [5:14](https://ref.ly/Judg5:14), [Is 33:22](https://ref.ly/Isa33:22) (of ´י; ‖שֹׁפְטֵנוּ, מַלְכֵּנוּ). **b.** *commander's staff* [Gn 49:10](https://ref.ly/Gen49:10) (‖שֵׁבֶט), [Nu 21:18](https://ref.ly/Num21:18) (both poet.), [Psalm 60:9](https://ref.ly/Ps60:9) \= [108:9](https://ref.ly/Ps108:9). **Pu.** *Pt.* מְחֻקָּק *that which is decreed* [Pr 31:5](https://ref.ly/Prov31:5) (late). **Hoph.** *Impf.* יֻחָקַוּ [Jb 19:23](https://ref.ly/Job19:23) *inscribed* in (בְּ) a book.

sf. חֻקִּי [Pr 30:8](https://ref.ly/Prov30:8) \+ 3 t.; חָקְךָ [Lv 10:13](https://ref.ly/Lev10:13), [10:14](https://ref.ly/Lev10:14) etc. \+ 4 t. sfs.; pl. חֻקִּים [Dt 4:5](https://ref.ly/Deut4:5) \+ 31 t.; portion of oil for the תּרומה Ez [45:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:14). **5\.** *prescribed limit, boundary:* of sea [Je 5:22](https://ref.ly/Jer5:22), [Pr 8:29](https://ref.ly/Prov8:29), [Jb 26:10](https://ref.ly/Job26:10), [38:10](https://ref.ly/Job38:10); of heavens [Psalm 148:6](https://ref.ly/Ps148:6); of land of Israel [Mi 7:11](https://ref.ly/Mic7:11); of time [Jb 14:5](https://ref.ly/Job14:5), [14:13](https://ref.ly/Job14:13); so פָּֽעֲרָה פִיהָ לִבְלִי חָֹק [Is 5:14](https://ref.ly/Isa5:14) She’ôl *openeth wide her mouth, without limit*. **6\.** *enactment, decree, ordinance* of either God or man: **a.** *specific decree:* law of fifth in Egypt [Gn 47:26](https://ref.ly/Gen47:26) (J); of passover [Ex 12:24](https://ref.ly/Exod12:24) (J); lament for Jephthah's daughter Ju [11:39](https://ref.ly/Judg11:39); for Josiah [2 Ch 35:25](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:25); חק ומשׁפט [Ex 15:25](https://ref.ly/Exod15:25), [Jos 24:25](https://ref.ly/Josh24:25) (both E), [1 S 30:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:25); חק עולם [Ex 30:21](https://ref.ly/Exod30:21); ‖עֵדוּת, מִשְׁפָּט [Psalm 81:5](https://ref.ly/Ps81:5), [81:6](https://ref.ly/Ps81:6) (law of a festival). **b.** of ´י in nature [Jb 28:26](https://ref.ly/Job28:26). **c.** respecting Mess. king [Psalm 2:7](https://ref.ly/Ps2:7); day of ´י [Zp 2:2](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:2) MT, but on txt. v. We and טֶרֶם, infr.; covenant with Jacob [Psalm 105:10](https://ref.ly/Ps105:10) \= [1 Ch 16:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:17); destiny of man [Jb 23:14](https://ref.ly/Job23:14). **d.** *law in general* עלי חק *according to law, right* [Psalm 94:20](https://ref.ly/Ps94:20) (others *against law*); ומשׁפט ´לַמֵּד ח [Ezr 7:10](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:10); ‖בּרית and תורות [Is 25:5](https://ref.ly/Isa25:5); ‖עדות [Psalm 99:7](https://ref.ly/Ps99:7). **7\.** pl. חֻקִּים *enactments, statutes* of a law: **a.** of ´י in nature [Je 31:36](https://ref.ly/Jer31:36). **b.** of the prophets [Zc 1:6](https://ref.ly/Zech1:6). **c.** *conditions* of deed of purchase [Je 32:11](https://ref.ly/Jer32:11). **d.** *enactments* ‖מִשְׁפָּטִים: ancestral Ez [20:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:18); given by God as punishment for disobedience [20:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:25). **e.** *decrees* of unjust judges חִקֲקֵי־אָוֶן [Is 10:1](https://ref.ly/Isa10:1). **f.** *civil enactments prescribed by God:* חֻקֵּי האלהים [Ex 18:16](https://ref.ly/Exod18:16) (E; (usu. preceding): e.g. (ים)דבר [Psalm 147:19](https://ref.ly/Ps147:19); תורה [2 Ch 33:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:8), [Mal 3:22](https://ref.ly/Mal3:22); מצוה [Dt 5:28](https://ref.ly/Deut5:28), [6:1](https://ref.ly/Deut6:1), [7:11](https://ref.ly/Deut7:11); מצות [Ne 1:7](https://ref.ly/Neh1:7), [1 K 8:58](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:58); מצוה, תורה [2 Ch 19:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:10); עֵדֹת [Dt 4:45](https://ref.ly/Deut4:45), [6:20](https://ref.ly/Deut6:20); sts. foll.: e.g. תורות [Lv 26:46](https://ref.ly/Lev26:46); מצוה, תורה [2 K 17:37](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:37); or in different order, e.g. ותורות ´מ ומצות ´ח [Ne 9:13](https://ref.ly/Neh9:13); ´וח ´מצות ומ [Ne 10:30](https://ref.ly/Neh10:30); ומצות ´ח ´ומ [Dt 26:17](https://ref.ly/Deut26:17). Apart from משׁפטום it is combined with מצות, e.g. ´וח ´מ [Ex 15:26](https://ref.ly/Exod15:26) (R), [Dt 27:10](https://ref.ly/Deut27:10); ´ומ ´ח [Dt 4:40](https://ref.ly/Deut4:40), [1 K 3:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:14), [8:61](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:61); ותורה ´וח ´מ [Ne 9:14](https://ref.ly/Neh9:14); ´ועדות וח ´מ [Dt 6:17](https://ref.ly/Deut6:17), [1 Ch 29:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:19), [2 Ch 34:31](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:31); ´וח ´דברי מ [Ezr 7:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:11); it is used c. vbs.: נתן [Lv 26:46](https://ref.ly/Lev26:46) \+ 3 t.; צוּה [Nu 30:17](https://ref.ly/Num30:17) \+ 9 t.; למּד [Dt 4:1](https://ref.ly/Deut4:1) \+ 10 t.; שׁמר [Dt 26:17](https://ref.ly/Deut26:17) \+ 16 t.; עשה [Dt 6:24](https://ref.ly/Deut6:24) \+ 5 t.; שׁמר לעשות [Dt 11:32](https://ref.ly/Deut11:32) \+ 4 t.; הלך בְּ [1 K 8:61](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:61) \+ 3 t.—\[On usage of חֹק v. חֻקָּה ad fin.]

## חָקַר (id: BDB03399)

\[Str2713] † \[חָקַר] **vb.** search (NH *id.*, Niph. pass., Aram. חֲקַר)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. וַחֲקָרוֺ consec. [Pr 18:17](https://ref.ly/Prov18:17); חֲקָרָהּ [Jb 28:27](https://ref.ly/Job28:27); וְחָֽקַרְתָּ֫ [Dt 13:15](https://ref.ly/Deut13:15) etc.; *Impf.* יַחְקֹר [Jb 13:19](https://ref.ly/Job13:19); יַחֲקָ־ר [Psalm 44:22](https://ref.ly/Ps44:22); יַחְקְרֶנּוּ [Pr 28:11](https://ref.ly/Prov28:11); אֶחְקֹר [1 S 20:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:12); תַּחְקְרוּן [Jb 32:11](https://ref.ly/Job32:11); נַחְקָֹרָה [La 3:40](https://ref.ly/Lam3:40) etc.; *Imv.* חָקְרֵ֫נִי [Psalm 139:23](https://ref.ly/Ps139:23); חִקְרוּ Ju [18:2](https://ref.ly/Judg18:2); *Inf.* חֲקֹר [2 S 10:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:3) \+ 2 t.; לַחְקוֺר [Pr 23:30](https://ref.ly/Prov23:30); לְחָקְרָהּ Ju [18:2](https://ref.ly/Judg18:2); —**1\.** *search (for)*, sq. acc. [Pr 23:30](https://ref.ly/Prov23:30), [Jb 32:11](https://ref.ly/Job32:11); abs. וְדָרַשְׁתָּ֫ וְחָקַרְתָּ֫ וְשָׁאַלְתָּ֫ הֵיטֵיב [Dt 13:15](https://ref.ly/Deut13:15); lit. Ez [39:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:14) (i.e. for bones unburied). **2\. a.** *search through, explore*, lit., obj. הארץ Ju [18:2](https://ref.ly/Judg18:2) (‖רַגֵּל) v:[2](https://ref.ly/Judg18:2), [1 Ch 19:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:3) (‖ *id.*); city העיר [2 S 10:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:3) (‖ *id.*), of mining [Jb 28:3](https://ref.ly/Job28:3); *search out* a subject or matter [Jb 5:27](https://ref.ly/Job5:27), [28:27](https://ref.ly/Job28:27), [Pr 25:2](https://ref.ly/Prov25:2), [Psalm 44:22](https://ref.ly/Ps44:22). **b.** *search* a man, find out his sentiments (AV *sound* him) [1 S 20:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:12). **c.** of ´י *searching* man, c. acc. [Psalm 139:1](https://ref.ly/Ps139:1) (‖ידע), v:[23](https://ref.ly/Ps139:23) (‖ *id.*) [Jb 13:9](https://ref.ly/Job13:9), cf. [Je 17:10](https://ref.ly/Jer17:10) (obj. לֵב). **d.** of *examining thoroughly*, so as to expose weakness in a case [Jb 29:16](https://ref.ly/Job29:16), [Pr 18:17](https://ref.ly/Prov18:17), cf. [28:11](https://ref.ly/Prov28:11); so of self\-examination דרכים ´ח [La 3:40](https://ref.ly/Lam3:40) (‖חפש). **Niph.** *Pf.* נֶחְקַר [1 K 7:47](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:47), [2 Ch 4:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:18); *Impf.* יֵחָקֵר [Je 46:23](https://ref.ly/Jer46:23); יֵחָֽקְרוּ [Je 31:37](https://ref.ly/Jer31:37) *be searched out, found out, ascertained*, [1 K 7:47](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:47), [2 Ch 4:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:18) (weight of the bronze used in temple utensils), of forest [Je 46:23](https://ref.ly/Jer46:23); *searched out*, [Je 31:37](https://ref.ly/Jer31:37) subj. מוסד־י ארץ **Pi.** *Pf.* חִקֵּר [Ec 12:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:9) *he sought out* many proverbs (מְשָׁלִים; ‖אִזֵּן).

> \[Str2714] † חֵ֫קֶר **n.m.**:Ju [5:16](https://ref.ly/Judg5:16) searching, thing (to be) searched out;—abs. חֵ֫קֶר [Jb 5:9](https://ref.ly/Job5:9) \+ 8 t.; cstr. *id.* [Jb 8:8](https://ref.ly/Job8:8) \+ 2 t.; pl. cstr. חִקְרֵי Ju [5:16](https://ref.ly/Judg5:16); —חִקְרֵי לֵב Ju [5:16](https://ref.ly/Judg5:16) *searchings, questionings of heart;* [Jb 11:7](https://ref.ly/Job11:7) canst thou reach אֱלוֺהַּ ´ח i.e. what is to be explored in him, the whole range of his nature, so ´ח תהום [38:16](https://ref.ly/Job38:16) the range of the deep; esp. ´אֵין ח i.e. *it is unsearchable* [Jb 5:9](https://ref.ly/Job5:9), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Job9:10), [Psalm 145:3](https://ref.ly/Ps145:3), [Pr 25:3](https://ref.ly/Prov25:3), [Is 40:28](https://ref.ly/Isa40:28), cf. ´לא ח [Jb 34:24](https://ref.ly/Job34:24), [36:26](https://ref.ly/Job36:26); אבותם ´ח [8:8](https://ref.ly/Job8:8) i.e. thing searched out by their fathers.

> \[Str4278] † \[מֶחְקָר] **n.m.** range (as place to be explored), מֶחְקְרֵי־אָרֶץ [Psalm 95:4](https://ref.ly/Ps95:4) (cf. חֵקֶר [Jb 38:16](https://ref.ly/Job38:16)).

## חרא (id: BDB03403)

חרא, or חרה (√ of following; meaning unknown).

NoM 56\); Ar. خُفْ;ٌ *id.*, whence vb. خچَرِيءَ of act, and n. مَخْرَأَةٌ of place; Amh. ሕርኣ፡ (DILex. Aeth. 88\))—pl. sf. חַרְאֵיהֶם [Is 36:12](https://ref.ly/Isa36:12) Kt (Qr צוֺאָתָם); ‖ [2 K 18:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:27) has חֲרִיהֶם (v. sg. חרי [6:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:25); Qr as above); חֲרִי יוֺנִים *dove's dung* [2 K 6:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:25) Kt (Qr דִּבִיוֺנִים); v., further, GeiUrschrift, 409\.

## חָרֵב (id: BDB03406)

\[Str2717] † \[חָרֵב] **vb.** be dry, dried up (cf. יָבֵשׁ) (Aram. חֲרוֺב *be dry*, חוּרְבָּא *drought*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. חָֽרְבוּ [Gn 8:13](https://ref.ly/Gen8:13), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Gen8:13), [Is 19:6](https://ref.ly/Isa19:6); *Impf.* 3 ms. יֶחֱרַב [Ho 13:15](https://ref.ly/Hos13:15) \+ 2 t., וַיֶּחֱרָב [Psalm 106:9](https://ref.ly/Ps106:9); *Imv.* fs. חֳרָבִי [Is 44:27](https://ref.ly/Isa44:27); —*be dry, dried up:* **1\.** of ground, be freed from waters of flood פְּנֵי חָאֲדָמָה ´ח [Gn 8:13](https://ref.ly/Gen8:13) b (J). **2\.** of waters, *be dried up*, taken away; of waters of flood [Gn 8:13](https://ref.ly/Gen8:13) a (P); of Nile וְנָהָר יֶחֱרַב וְיָבֵשׁ [Is 19:5](https://ref.ly/Isa19:5) (‖וְנִשְּׁתוּ־מַיִם מֵהַיָּם), v. same combination [Jb 14:11](https://ref.ly/Job14:11) (‖אָֽזְלו מַיִם מִנִּי־יָם); [Is 19:6](https://ref.ly/Isa19:6) (‖דָּֽלֲלוּ) [44:27](https://ref.ly/Isa44:27) (‖הוֺבִישׁ); of Red Sea [Psalm 106:9](https://ref.ly/Ps106:9); fig. of Ephraim's freshness and vigour [Ho 13:15](https://ref.ly/Hos13:15). **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 pl. לאֹ חֹרָבוּ Ju [16:7](https://ref.ly/Judg16:7), [16:8](https://ref.ly/Judg16:8), of fresh bow\-strings (of gut, v. Bla). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הֶחֱרִיב [Na 1:4](https://ref.ly/Nah1:4), of ´י's *drying up* rivers (‖וַיַּבְּשֵׁהוּ), perh. also [Is 11:15](https://ref.ly/Isa11:15) (for MT החרים) v. LXX SyrVer Vulgate Targum; וְהַחֲרַבְתִּ֫י the Euphrates [Je 51:36](https://ref.ly/Jer51:36) (‖הוֺבַשְׁתִּי); *Impf.* אַחֲרִיב יָם [Is 50:2](https://ref.ly/Isa50:2), subj. ´י, *I dry up a sea* (‖אָשִים נְהָרוֺת מִדְבָּר); אַחְרִב [Is 37:25](https://ref.ly/Isa37:25) \= אַחֲרִב [2 K 19:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:24), of king of Assyria *drying up* rivers of Egypt; *Pt.* fs. הַמַּחֲרֶבֶת [Is 51:10](https://ref.ly/Isa51:10) of ´י's arm *drying up* (Red) Sea.

351:b. II. חֹרֶב, 1\. 3: after [Zp 2:14](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:14) add ‘(but rd. עֹרֵב, LXX We al.).’

> \[Str2720] † I. \[חָרֵב] **adj.** dry, only fs. חֲרֵבָה [Lv 7:10](https://ref.ly/Lev7:10) of meal\-offering not moistened (with oil; P); פַּ־ת חֲרֵבָה [Pr 17:1](https://ref.ly/Prov17:1) *a dry morsel*.

> \[Str2721] † I. חֹ֫רֶב **n.m.**:[Gn 31:40](https://ref.ly/Gen31:40) dryness, drought, heat, abs. ´ח [Gn 31:40](https://ref.ly/Gen31:40) \+ 11 t. \+ [Dt 28:22](https://ref.ly/Deut28:22) (for MT חֶרֶב; v. Di, sq. Vulgate; LXX and LXXL om.);—**1\.** *dryness* Ju [6:37](https://ref.ly/Judg6:37), [6:39](https://ref.ly/Judg6:39), [6:40](https://ref.ly/Judg6:40) (all of absence of dew); [Je 50:38](https://ref.ly/Jer50:38) (‖יָבֵשׁ); so rd. also [Zc 11:17](https://ref.ly/Zech11:17) (for MT חֶרֶב, see vb and StaZAW i. 1881, 29\), *a drying up upon his arm* (i.e. a *withering*) *and upon his right eye*. **2\.** *drought* [Hg 1:11](https://ref.ly/Hag1:11) \+ [Dt 28:22](https://ref.ly/Deut28:22) v. supr. **3\.** *parching heat* (of sun) [Gn 31:40](https://ref.ly/Gen31:40) (opp. קֶ֫רַח), [Je 36:30](https://ref.ly/Jer36:30) (opp. *id.*); [Is 4:6](https://ref.ly/Isa4:6), [25:4](https://ref.ly/Isa25:4), [25:5](https://ref.ly/Isa25:5), [25:5](https://ref.ly/Isa25:5); of fever [Jb 30:30](https://ref.ly/Job30:30).

> \[Str2724] † חָֽרָבָה **n.f.** dry ground;—abs. ´ח [Gn 7:22](https://ref.ly/Gen7:22) \+ 7 t.; opp. הַיָּם [Ex 14:21](https://ref.ly/Exod14:21) (J), [Hg 2:6](https://ref.ly/Hag2:6); cf. [Gn 7:22](https://ref.ly/Gen7:22) (J); opp. water of Jordan [Jos 3:17](https://ref.ly/Josh3:17), [3:17](https://ref.ly/Josh3:17) (J), [4:18](https://ref.ly/Josh4:18) (E), [2 K 2:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:8); of Nile\-arms ´וְנָתַתִּ֫י יְאֹרִים ח Ez [30:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:12).

> \[Str2725] † \[חֵרָבוֺן] **n.m.** drought, only pl. cstr. בְּחַרְבֹנֵי קַ֫יִץ [Psalm 32:4](https://ref.ly/Ps32:4) metaph. of fever heat.

## II חָרֵב (id: BDB03411)

\[Str2717] † II. \[חָרֵב] be waste, desolate (NH חָרֵב *id.;* Aram. חֲרוֺב ܚܪܰܒ *be laid waste;* Zinj. חרב adj.; Ar. خَرِبَ *be in ruins, waste, depopulated;* As. *ḫarâbu, be waste*, DlPr 175, *ḫuribtu, desert*, LotzTP);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 fs. תֶּחֱרַ֔ב [Je 26:9](https://ref.ly/Jer26:9), תֶּחֱרָ֔ב [Is 34:10](https://ref.ly/Isa34:10); 3 mpl. יֶחֶרְבוּ Ez [6:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:6), יֶחֱרָבוּ [Am 7:9](https://ref.ly/Amos7:9), [Is 60:12](https://ref.ly/Isa60:12); 3 fpl. תֶּחֱרַבְנָה Ez [6:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:6), [12:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:20); *Imv.* חָרְבוּ (so, not חָֽרְבוּ—Theile—see van d. H Hahn Köi. 244\) [Je 2:12](https://ref.ly/Jer2:12); *Inf. abs.* חָרֹב [Is 60:12](https://ref.ly/Isa60:12); —*be waste, desolate;* of sanctuaries of Isr. [Am 7:9](https://ref.ly/Amos7:9) (‖נָשַׁמּוּ), of altars Ez [6:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:6); of cities [Je 26:9](https://ref.ly/Jer26:9), Ez [6:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:6), [12:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:20) (‖שׁמם); of nations [Is 34:10](https://ref.ly/Isa34:10), חָרֹב יֶחֱרָבוּ [60:12](https://ref.ly/Isa60:12) (‖יאֹבֵדוּ); fig., in address to the heavens חָרְבוּ מְאֹד [Je 2:12](https://ref.ly/Jer2:12)—(‖שֹׁמּוּ, וְשַעֲרוּ), *be very desolate* (at sins of Isr.); others, *be amazed, astounded* (on relation of meanings v. שׁמם). **Niph.** *Pt.* f. נֶחֱרֶבֶת Ez [26:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:19) as adj., *desolate* city, i.e. uninhabited, v. context; נַחֲרָבוֺת [30:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:17) of *desolate* countries (‖נָשַׁמּוּ). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הֶחֱרִיב Ez 19:17; 1 S. הֶחֱרַבְתִּי [Zp 3:6](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:6); 3 pl. הֶחֱרִיבוּ [Is 37:18](https://ref.ly/Isa37:18), [2 K 19:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:17); *Impf.* 1 S. אַחֲרִיב [Is 42:15](https://ref.ly/Isa42:15); *Pt.* מַחֲרִיב Ju [16:24](https://ref.ly/Judg16:24); pl. c. sf. מַחֲרִיבַיִח [Is 49:17](https://ref.ly/Isa49:17); —*lay waste, make desolate*, human subj.; nations and lands [Is 37:18](https://ref.ly/Isa37:18) \= [2 K 19:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:17), [Is 42:15](https://ref.ly/Isa42:15), cf. pt. [Is 49:17](https://ref.ly/Isa49:17) (‖מְהָֽרְסַיִח); add prob. with We [Am 4:9](https://ref.ly/Amos4:9) (הֶחֱרַבְתִּי for the untranslateable הַרְבּוֺת) *I have devastated your gardens and vineyards;* pt. Ju [16:24](https://ref.ly/Judg16:24) *desolater of our country* (‖הִרְבָּה אֶת־חֲלָלֵ\+נוּ); obj. cities Ez [19:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:7); streets [Zp 3:6](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:6) (‖נָשַׁמּוּ). **Hoph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. הָחֳרָבָה Ez [26:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:2) *she is laid waste*, of a city; *Pt.* מָחֳרָבוֺת Ez [29:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:12) *laid waste*, of cities (‖שׁמם).

## III חָרַב (id: BDB03416)

\[Str2717] † III. \[חָרַב] **vb.** (Aram. and rare) attack, smite down (cf. Ar. حَرَبَ *plunder*, III. *wage war with*, VI. *fight together*, حَرْبٌ *war, battle;* Syr. ܚܪܰܒ *smite, slay*)—**Qal** *Imv.* ms. חֲרֹב [Je 50:21](https://ref.ly/Jer50:21) attack (\+ וְהַחֲרֵם אַחֲרֵיהֶם); mpl. חִרְבוּ v:[27](https://ref.ly/Jer50:27) obj. כָּל־פָּרֶיהָ, fig. of men of Babylon. **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. נֶחֶרְבוּ המלכים (v. **Hoph.**) הָחֳרֵב [2 K 3:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:23) *the kings have attacked one another, fought together* (‖וַיַּכּוּ אִישׁ אֶת־רֵעֵהוּ). **Hoph.** *Inf. abs.* [2 K 3:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:23), v. **Niph.,** but rd. perhaps הֵחָרֵב, cf. Dr [Lv 19:20](https://ref.ly/Lev19:20), note.

> \[Str2719] חֶ֫רֶב:411 **n.f.**:[Is 21:15](https://ref.ly/Isa21:15) sword (as weapon; Aram. חַרְבָּא, ܚܰܪܒܳܐ, Zinj. חרב; Ar. حَرْبَةٌ *dart, javelin*)—abs. חֶ֫רֶב [Gn 3:24](https://ref.ly/Gen3:24) \+; חָרֶב [Ex 17:13](https://ref.ly/Exod17:13) \+; cstr. חֶ֫רֶב [Dt 33:29](https://ref.ly/Deut33:29) \+; sf. חַרְבִּי [Ex 15:9](https://ref.ly/Exod15:9) \+ 13 t.; חַרְבְּךָ [Gn 27:40](https://ref.ly/Gen27:40) \+ 7 t.; חֵרְבֶָּךָ [1 S 17:33](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:33); [Psalm 17:13](https://ref.ly/Ps17:13); חַרְבּוֺ [Nu 22:23](https://ref.ly/Num22:23) \+ 24 t.; חַרְבְּכֶם [Je 2:30](https://ref.ly/Jer2:30), Ez [33:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:26); חַרְבָּם [Psalm 37:15](https://ref.ly/Ps37:15), [44:4](https://ref.ly/Ps44:4); pl. חֲרָבוֺת [Is 21:15](https://ref.ly/Isa21:15) \+ 5 t.; cstr. חַרְבוֺת [Jos 5:2](https://ref.ly/Josh5:2) \+ 2 t.; sf. חַרְבוֺתָיו Ez [26:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:9); חַרְבוֺתָם [Is 2:4](https://ref.ly/Isa2:4) \+ 5 t.; חַרְבֹתֵיהֶם [Mi 4:3](https://ref.ly/Mic4:3) \+ 2 t.;—**1\. a.** *sword*, as weapon of war [Gn 48:22](https://ref.ly/Gen48:22) (E), Ju [7:14](https://ref.ly/Judg7:14), [7:20](https://ref.ly/Judg7:20), [1 S 21:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:9), and so in all periods; *two\-edged* (short) *sword* וְלָהּ שְׁנֵי פֵיוֺ֭ת גֹּמֶד אָרְכָּהּ ´ח Ju [3:16](https://ref.ly/Judg3:16), cf. פִּיּוֺת ´ח [Pr 5:4](https://ref.ly/Prov5:4) (in sim.), פִּיפִיּוֺת ´ח [Psalm 149:6](https://ref.ly/Ps149:6); v. also ´צוּר ח *edge of sword* [Psalm 89:44](https://ref.ly/Ps89:44). **b.** *gird on sword* \= ´חָגַר הח [1 S 17:39](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:39), [25:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:13), [25:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:13), [25:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:13), [Psalm 45:4](https://ref.ly/Ps45:4) (´הח ´ח עַל יָרֵך), שִים על ירך [Ex 32:27](https://ref.ly/Exod32:27), cf. esp. WeSkizzen iii, 166\); Ez [5:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:1); [5:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:2) חַדָּה תַּעַר ´ח הַגַּלָּבִים (acc. to Co use of *sword* as razor is significant). **3\.** of tools used in hewing stone חַרְבְּךָ הֵנַפְתָּ עליה [Ex 20:25](https://ref.ly/Exod20:25); ´יִתֹּץ בח Ez [26:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:9), acc. to most \= *tools*, axes, ‘ steel ’ (Co *‘ Eisen ’*), but poss. *swords* as implement ready to hand; [2 Ch 34:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:6) Qr בְּחַרְבֹתֵיהֶם *with their tools;* rd. perh. בְּחָרְבֹתֵיהֶם *in their ruins*, Be, who comp. [Psalm 109:10](https://ref.ly/Ps109:10); v. חָרְבָּה sub II. חרב (LXX Vulgate om. חרב).

## חָרַג (id: BDB03420)

\[Str2727] † \[חָרַג] **vb.** quake (cf. (si vera 1\.) Ar. حَرِجَ *be straitened, unable to move for fear and rage* (Lane), also *put oneself in a rage* (Dozy); Targum חַרְגַת מוֺתָא [Dt 32:25](https://ref.ly/Deut32:25) *terror of death*)—only **Qal** *Impf.* 3 mpl. וְיַחְרְגוּ מִמִּסְגְּרוֺתֵיהֶם [Psalm 18:46](https://ref.ly/Ps18:46) pregn. *and come quaking out of their fortresses* (cf. יִרְגְּזוּ in like connex. [Mi 7:17](https://ref.ly/Mic7:17)); rd. the same also in ‖ [2 S 22:46](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:46) (for MT וְיַחְגְרוּ, v. חגר; possible would be also ויחרדו in both, as [Ho 11:10](https://ref.ly/Hos11:10), [11:11](https://ref.ly/Hos11:11) etc.)

* **Associated Passages:** Hosea 11:10; Psalms 18:46; Hosea 11:11; 2 Samuel 22:46; Micah 7:17; Deuteronomy 32:25

## חרגל (id: BDB03421)

חרגל (quadril. √ of foll.; cf. Ar. حَرْجَلَ *run right and left, run swiftly* (Frey)).

> \[Str2728] חַרְגֹּל **n.\[m.]** a kind of locust (NH *id.;* Aram. ܚܰܪܓܳܠܴܐ, חַרְגּוֺלָא; حَرْجَلٌ (Dozy), all \= *locust;* حَرْجَلَةٌ *swarm of locusts* (Frey))—only [Lv 11:22](https://ref.ly/Lev11:22) (P) in list of edible insects, ‖אַרְבֶּה, סָלְעָם, חָגָב.

* **Associated Passages:** Leviticus 11:22


## חָרַד (id: BDB03423)

\[Str2729] † חָרַד **vb.** tremble, be terrified (As. *ḫarâdu, id.*, acc. to DlHA 20, Pr 46; Ar. خَرِدَ *be bashful, shamefaced;* NH חֲרָדָה *a trembling*)—**Qal** *Pf.* וְחָרַד consec. [Is 19:16](https://ref.ly/Isa19:16), חָֽרְדָה [Is 10:29](https://ref.ly/Isa10:29), חָרַדְתְּ [2 K 4:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:13), חָרְדוּ [1 S 13:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:7), [14:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:15), וְחָֽרְדוּ consec. Ez [26:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:16), [32:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:10); *Impf.* יֶחֱרַד [Jb 37:1](https://ref.ly/Job37:1), וַיֶּחֱרַד [Gn 27:33](https://ref.ly/Gen27:33) \+ 5 t.; יֶחְרְדוּ [Ho 11:10](https://ref.ly/Hos11:10), Ez [26:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:18), יֶחֶֽרְדוּ [Ho 11:11](https://ref.ly/Hos11:11), יֶחֱרָדוּ [Am 3:6](https://ref.ly/Amos3:6), [Is 41:6](https://ref.ly/Isa41:6), וַיֶּחֶֽרְדוּ [Gn 42:28](https://ref.ly/Gen42:28) \+ 2 t.; *Imv.* חִרְדוּ [Is 32:11](https://ref.ly/Isa32:11); —**1\.** *tremble, quake*, of a mountain [Ex 19:18](https://ref.ly/Exod19:18)—so HCT, but rd. חָעָם, LXX v. Di (E); of isles Ez [26:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:18) (metaph., in fear); so [Is 41:5](https://ref.ly/Isa41:5) (קְצוֺת הארץ, ‖אִיִּים יראו). **2\.** *tremble*, of persons under supernat. infl. [1 S 14:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:15) (‖חֲרָדֶה, וַתִּרְגַּז הָאָרָץ); *start, start up* (out of sleep) [Ru 3:8](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:8); in mental disturbance [Gn 27:33](https://ref.ly/Gen27:33) (J; c. acc. cogn.) *tremble*, in terror [Ex 19:16](https://ref.ly/Exod19:16) (E), [Is 32:11](https://ref.ly/Isa32:11) (‖רגז), *be startled* (at sound of trumpet) [Am 3:6](https://ref.ly/Amos3:6) cf. [1 K 1:49](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:49) (\+ קום, הלך, ירא); *tremble* Ez [26:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:16), [32:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:10) sq. לְ \= *at*, [Jb 37:1](https://ref.ly/Job37:1) (לִבִּי), *be terrified*, of Egypt (personif.) [Is 19:16](https://ref.ly/Isa19:16) (‖פָּחַד), Ramah [10:29](https://ref.ly/Isa10:29), of Saul [1 S 28:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:5) subj. לִבּוֺ, ‖ירא). **3\.** *be anxiously careful* חָרַדְתְּ אֵלֵנוּ את־כל הַחֲרָדָה הַזּאֹת [2 K 4:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:13). **4\.** with preps. pregn. \= *go or come trembling* [1 S 13:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:7) (sq. מֵאחריו LXXL We Dr) [Gn 42:28](https://ref.ly/Gen42:28) (E; sq. אֶ־ל) [1 S 16:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:4) (sq. לִקְרָאתוֺ) so [21:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:2), [Ho 11:10](https://ref.ly/Hos11:10) (מִיָּם) v:[11](https://ref.ly/Hos11:11) (מִמִּצְרַיִם). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הֶחֱרִיד Ju [8:12](https://ref.ly/Judg8:12), וְּהַחֲרַדְתִּ֫י [2 S 17:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:2); *Pt.* מַחֲרִיד [Lv 6:26](https://ref.ly/Lev6:26) \+ 11 t.; *Inf. cstr.* הַחֲרִיד Ez [30:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:9), [Zc 2:4](https://ref.ly/Zech2:4); —*drive in terror, rout* an army, sq. acc. Ju [8:12](https://ref.ly/Judg8:12), Ez [30:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:9), [Zc 2:4](https://ref.ly/Zech2:4) cf. [2 S 17:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:2); (We prop. החרידו [Ho 5:8](https://ref.ly/Hos5:8) *‘ setzt Benj. in Schrecken, ’* for MT אַחֲרֶיךָ); in *Pt.* abs., only in ´וְאֵין מ; *and none shall terrify*, i.e. disturb the peace of those reposing—שׁכב, שׁקט, רבץ, ישׁב, ישׁב לָבֶטַחִ—in the promised land [Lv 26:6](https://ref.ly/Lev26:6), so after captivity [Je 30:10](https://ref.ly/Jer30:10) \= [46:27](https://ref.ly/Jer46:27), also Ez [34:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:28), [39:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:26), [Mi 4:4](https://ref.ly/Mic4:4), [Zp 3:13](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:13); of undisturbed peace of the righteous [Jb 11:19](https://ref.ly/Job11:19); peace of flocks in forsaken cities [Is 17:2](https://ref.ly/Isa17:2); undisturbed feeding on carrion by fowl and beast [Dt 28:26](https://ref.ly/Deut28:26), [Je 7:33](https://ref.ly/Jer7:33), undisturbed prowling of lions [Na 2:12](https://ref.ly/Nah2:12) (metaph. of Assyrians).

## חָרָה (id: BDB03429)

\[Str2734,8474] † חָרָה **vb.** burn, be kindled, of anger (Aram. חרי Pa. *cause*, fire *to burn* (rare); Zinj. חרא *anger*, NöZMG 1893, 98\. 103; Ar. حَرْوَةٌ *burning sensation, in throat,* etc., *fr. rage and pain*)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ח [Gn 4:6](https://ref.ly/Gen4:6) \+ 20 t.; *Impf.* יֶחֱרֶה [Ex 32:11](https://ref.ly/Exod32:11) \+ 2 t.; יִ֫חַר [Gn 18:30](https://ref.ly/Gen18:30) \+ 7 t.; וַיִּ֫חַר [Gn 4:5](https://ref.ly/Gen4:5) \+ 46 t.; *Inf. abs.* חָרֹה [1 S 20:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:7); cstr. חֲרוֺת [2 S 24:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:1), [Psalm 124:3](https://ref.ly/Ps124:3); —**1\.** of man: **a.** חרה אף (one's) *anger was kindled, burned* [Gn 39:19](https://ref.ly/Gen39:19), [Nu 22:27](https://ref.ly/Num22:27) (J), [Ex 32:19](https://ref.ly/Exod32:19), [32:22](https://ref.ly/Exod32:22) (JE), Ju [9:30](https://ref.ly/Judg9:30), [14:19](https://ref.ly/Judg14:19), [1 S 11:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:6), [Jb 32:2](https://ref.ly/Job32:2), [32:5](https://ref.ly/Job32:5); c. בְּ *against* [Gn 30:2](https://ref.ly/Gen30:2) (E), [44:18](https://ref.ly/Gen44:18) (J), [1 S 17:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:28), [20:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:30), [2 S 12:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:5), [2 Ch 25:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:10), [Psalm 124:3](https://ref.ly/Ps124:3), [Jb 32:2](https://ref.ly/Job32:2), [32:3](https://ref.ly/Job32:3); c. אֶל [Nu 24:10](https://ref.ly/Num24:10) (E). **b.** impers., אף omitted; חרה לְ *it was kindled for* (him) *he burned with anger* [Gn 31:36](https://ref.ly/Gen31:36) (E), [4:5](https://ref.ly/Gen4:5), [4:6](https://ref.ly/Gen4:6), [34:7](https://ref.ly/Gen34:7), [Nu 16:15](https://ref.ly/Num16:15) (J), [1 S 15:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:11) (rd. perh. וַיֵּחֶר, Weir in Dr), [18:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:8), [20:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:7), [20:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:7), [2 S 3:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:8), [6:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:8) \= [1 Ch 13:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:11), [2 S 13:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:21), [19:43](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:43), [Ne 3:33](https://ref.ly/Neh3:33), [4:1](https://ref.ly/Neh4:1), [5:6](https://ref.ly/Neh5:6), [Jon 4:1](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:1), [4:4](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:4), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:9), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:9); ins. ויחר לדוד [2 S 11:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:22) LXX We Dr; בְּעֵינֵי ´ח [Gn 31:35](https://ref.ly/Gen31:35), [45:5](https://ref.ly/Gen45:5) (E). **2\.** of God: **a.** אף ´ח [Ex 22:23](https://ref.ly/Exod22:23), [Nu 22:22](https://ref.ly/Num22:22) (E), [Nu 11:1](https://ref.ly/Num11:1), [11:10](https://ref.ly/Num11:10), [32:10](https://ref.ly/Num32:10) (J); c. בְּ [Ex 4:14](https://ref.ly/Exod4:14), [32:10](https://ref.ly/Exod32:10), [Nu 12:9](https://ref.ly/Num12:9), [Dt 31:17](https://ref.ly/Deut31:17) (J), [6:15](https://ref.ly/Deut6:15), [7:4](https://ref.ly/Deut7:4), [11:17](https://ref.ly/Deut11:17), [29:26](https://ref.ly/Deut29:26), [Jos 23:16](https://ref.ly/Josh23:16) (D), Ju [6:39](https://ref.ly/Judg6:39), [2 S 6:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:7) \= [1 Ch 13:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:10), [Ho 8:5](https://ref.ly/Hos8:5), [2 K 23:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:26), [2 Ch 25:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:15), [Jb 42:7](https://ref.ly/Job42:7); בְּעַם [Ex 32:11](https://ref.ly/Exod32:11), [Nu 11:33](https://ref.ly/Num11:33) (J), [Is 5:25](https://ref.ly/Isa5:25), [Psalm 106:40](https://ref.ly/Ps106:40); בישראל [Nu 25:3](https://ref.ly/Num25:3), [32:13](https://ref.ly/Num32:13) (JE); Ju [2:14](https://ref.ly/Judg2:14), [2:20](https://ref.ly/Judg2:20), [3:8](https://ref.ly/Judg3:8), [10:7](https://ref.ly/Judg10:7), [2 S 24:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:1), [2 K 13:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:3); בבני ישראל [Jos 7:1](https://ref.ly/Josh7:1) (R); בנהרים, אף omitted, Hb [3:8](https://ref.ly/Hab3:8); c. עַל [Zc 10:3](https://ref.ly/Zech10:3). **b.** חרה לְ [Gn 18:30](https://ref.ly/Gen18:30), [18:32](https://ref.ly/Gen18:32) (J) [2 S 22:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:8) \= [Psalm 18:8](https://ref.ly/Ps18:8).—*Note:* חרה not in HP (who use קצף); nor in Je Ez Is:2 Pr or poetry of Jb. **Niph.** *Pf.* נִחֲר־וּ sq. בְּ Ct [1:6](https://ref.ly/Song1:6) (acc. to Böii. 379 De Kö1. 551\) *be angry* with; *Pt.* ´כֹּל הַנֶּחֱרִים בְּ *all that are incensed against (thee)* [Is 41:11](https://ref.ly/Isa41:11), so [45:24](https://ref.ly/Isa45:24). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הֶחֱרָה הֶחֱזִיק [Ne 3:20](https://ref.ly/Neh3:20) *burned with zeal* (?) *in repairing*, but LXX om. החרה; prob. dittogr.; *Impf.* וַיַּחַר עָלַי אַפּוֺ [Jb 19:11](https://ref.ly/Job19:11) *and kindled his anger against me*. **Hithp.** *Impf.* אַל תִּתְחַר [Psalm 37:1](https://ref.ly/Ps37:1), [37:7](https://ref.ly/Ps37:7), [37:8](https://ref.ly/Ps37:8), [Pr 24:19](https://ref.ly/Prov24:19) *heat oneself in vexation*.—The foll. forms are somewhat dub.: *Impf.* 2 ms. אֵיח תְּתַחֲרֶה אֶת־הַסּוּסִים [Je 12:5](https://ref.ly/Jer12:5) *how canst thou hotly contend* (in a race) *with the horses ? Pt.* כִּי אַתָּה מְתַחֲרֶה בָאָרֶז [Je 22:15](https://ref.ly/Jer22:15) *because thou strivest eagerly* (to excel) *in cedar;* expl. as *Tiph‘ēl* by Thes Ges§ 55, 5 Ew§ 122 a Ol:§ 255 a Sta§ 159 b Köi. p. 557 al., and regarded by most as a rare *causative* stem; but ag. existence of *Tiph‘ēl* v. BaNB § 180 a; ZMG 1894, 20; Ba regards the form as denom. (fr. a noun formed by ת preform., cf. Aram. ܬܰܚܪܘܽܬܳܐ *contention*); SS would point as Hithp. \[cf. Syr. ܐܶܬܚܪܺܝ *contend with*, ܚܶܪܝܳܢ *contention*], although meaning of Hithp. is diff., v. supr.

Co), v:[14](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:14); pl. *bursts of burning anger* [Psalm 88:17](https://ref.ly/Ps88:17).—*Note:* כְּמוֺ־חָרוֺן [Psalm 58:10](https://ref.ly/Ps58:10) *burning anger* LXX Vulgate Jer AV Bae Che; but Thes MV SS DeW Ew Ol Pe, something burning, e.g. thorns, antith. *green* thorns; Hengst De *cooked* flesh, antith. *raw;* [Je 25:38](https://ref.ly/Jer25:38) הַיּוֺנָה ´מפני ח because of the *fierceness of the oppressor* (? AV) would then be the only use of the word of other than God's anger; here LXX Targum Ew Hi Gf Ke Che Gie RV rightly rd. חרב *oppressing sword* (as [46:16](https://ref.ly/Jer46:16), [50:16](https://ref.ly/Jer50:16)). חרון is not used in H P D2 or E (except song, [Ex 15:7](https://ref.ly/Exod15:7)) of the Hex; nor in Ez except [7:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:12) (del. Co), v:[14](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:14).

[Ezr 10:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:8)); a field consecrated to ´י becomes under certain conditions ´כִּשְדֵה הַח [Lv 27:21](https://ref.ly/Lev27:21); every ´ח is קֹדֶשׁ קָֽדָשִׁים ליהוה v:[28](https://ref.ly/Lev27:28), and no ´ח that a man may devote, whether man, beast or field, may be sold or redeemed v:[28](https://ref.ly/Lev27:28); every such ´ח (as in case of metals and metal objects [Jos 6:17](https://ref.ly/Josh6:17), [6:19](https://ref.ly/Josh6:19)) went to Aaron and his sons [Nu 18:14](https://ref.ly/Num18:14) (P), to Zadokite priests Ez [44:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:29). **3\.** *devotion, ban*, involving destruction; אִישׁ חֶרְמִי [1 K 20:42](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:42) *man under my* (´י's) *ban* (of Benhadad); עַם חֶרְמִי [Is 34:5](https://ref.ly/Isa34:5) (of Edom); ´נחן לַח [Is 43:28](https://ref.ly/Isa43:28) fig. of ´י's giving over Jud. to Chald.; ´ח not to be in future [Zc 14:11](https://ref.ly/Zech14:11); ´פֶּן־אָבוֺא וְהִכֵּיתִי אֶת־הָאָרֶץ ח [Mal 3:24](https://ref.ly/Mal3:24) *smite the land with a ban*, i.e. utterly destroy it.

> \[Str2765] † חֳרֵם **n.pr.loc.** (*sacred;* cf. Sab. n.pr.loc. אחרם DHMEpigr. Denkm. 43\)—a place in tribe of Naphtali [Jos 19:38](https://ref.ly/Josh19:38) (P); not identified.

> \[Str2766] † חָרִם **n.pr.m.** (*consecrated;* cf. Sab. n.pr.m. חרם, יחרמאל Ha1411. 504 DHM1. c.)—**1\.** priest of the third course, David's time, acc. to [1 Ch 24:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:8). **2\.** priest of time of Nehemiah [Ne 10:6](https://ref.ly/Neh10:6). **3\.** heads of families of returning exiles: **a.** [Ezr 2:39](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:39) \= [Ne 7:42](https://ref.ly/Neh7:42), [Ezr 10:21](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:21), [Ne 12:15](https://ref.ly/Neh12:15). **b.** [Ezr 2:32](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:32) \= [Ne 7:35](https://ref.ly/Neh7:35), [Ezr 10:31](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:31), [Ne 3:11](https://ref.ly/Neh3:11). **4\.** a prince [Ne 10:28](https://ref.ly/Neh10:28).

> \[Str2767] † חָרְמָה **n.pr.loc.** (*asylum*, cf. Ar. *id.*, WetzstZKW v. 1884, 115\)—a royal city of Canaanites, in the South, in tribe of Simeon [Nu 14:45](https://ref.ly/Num14:45) (JE; art. only here v. Di), [21:3](https://ref.ly/Num21:3) (J) where name expl. from Israel's *devoting* Canaanites of Arad to destruction; [Jos 15:30](https://ref.ly/Josh15:30), [19:4](https://ref.ly/Josh19:4) (P), [Dt 1:44](https://ref.ly/Deut1:44), [Jos 12:14](https://ref.ly/Josh12:14) (D), [1 S 30:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:30), [1 Ch 4:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:30); originally called צְפַת Ju [1:17](https://ref.ly/Judg1:17), where name is said to have been changed to Ḥormah because Judah and Simeon (after death of Joshua) *devoted* its inhabitants to destruction, v. Di [Nu 21:3](https://ref.ly/Num21:3).—On site v. צְפַת.

> \[Str2768,2769] † חֶרְמוֺן **n. pr. mont.** Hermon (*sacred* mountain, cf. Sab. מחרם, etc., *temple*, Ar. حَرَامٌ *interior of mosque*, حُرْمَةٌ *asylum*, WetzstZKW v. 1884, 115 RS Sem. i, 93; 2nd ed., 94\)—the highest peak of Anti\-Lebanon range, usually snow\-capped, commanding southern Syria \& northern Palestine; it is called שִיאֹן by the author of [Dt 4:48](https://ref.ly/Deut4:48); by the Amorites שְנִיר, by the Sidonians שִרְיֹן [Dt 3:9](https://ref.ly/Deut3:9). It has three peaks; and the names חרמון and שניר, distinguished in [1 Ch 5:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:23), Ct [4:8](https://ref.ly/Song4:8), may refer to two of these peaks; חֶרְמוֺנִים [Psalm 42:7](https://ref.ly/Ps42:7) prob. refers to these different peaks (see RobBR iii. 357 BädPal 301\). It is a northern boundary ´עד הר ח [Dt 3:8](https://ref.ly/Deut3:8), [Jos 12:1](https://ref.ly/Josh12:1); הר חרמוֺן *mount Hermon* is used also [Jos 11:17](https://ref.ly/Josh11:17), [12:5](https://ref.ly/Josh12:5), [13:5](https://ref.ly/Josh13:5), [13:11](https://ref.ly/Josh13:11), [1 Ch 5:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:23); but חרמון [Jos 11:3](https://ref.ly/Josh11:3), [Psalm 89:13](https://ref.ly/Ps89:13), [133:3](https://ref.ly/Ps133:3), Ct [4:8](https://ref.ly/Song4:8). Vid. בַּעַל חֶרְמוֺן Ju [3:3](https://ref.ly/Judg3:3), [1 Ch 5:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:23) (see Wetzstib. 115\).

> \[Str2769] חֶרְמוֺנִים v. foregoing.

Di (Kn).

> \[Str2771] † I. חָרָן **n.pr.loc.** city in northern Mesopotamia (As. *ḫarrânu* \= *road, path* COTGloss; cf. DlPa 185; JensenKosmologie 28 ‘ junction of trading\-routes ’ \= *cross\-roads*)—mentioned as city of Abram's sojourn [Gn 11:31](https://ref.ly/Gen11:31), where his father Teraḥ died v:[32](https://ref.ly/Gen11:32), and whence he departed for Canaan [12:4](https://ref.ly/Gen12:4), [12:5](https://ref.ly/Gen12:5); where Laban, Jacob's uncle, lived, and whither Jacob fled [27:43](https://ref.ly/Gen27:43), [28:10](https://ref.ly/Gen28:10) (both חָרָ֫נָה), [29:4](https://ref.ly/Gen29:4); as conquered by fathers of Sennacherib [2 K 19:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:12) \= [Is 37:12](https://ref.ly/Isa37:12); as place of trade Ez [27:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:23); As. *Ḫarrana* (*\-ni*), SchrCOT on [Gn 11:31](https://ref.ly/Gen11:31), [27:43](https://ref.ly/Gen27:43) LXX Ξαρραν. It was an ancient seat of worship of moon\-god (Sin), v. SchrKG 355, 596; see further ChwolsohnSsabier i, 303 ff. WüstenfeldZMG 1864, 443 f. SchrKG 355, 536 SachauReise 217 ff. MezGesch. d. Stadt Harran, 1892\.

LagOnom. 96, 2nd ed. 131\) so SS.—תִּמְנַת־חֶרֶס Ju [2:9](https://ref.ly/Judg2:9) v. sub תִּמְנַת, √ מנה.

\[Str2778] † I. \[חָרַף] **vb.** reproach (NH Pi. *id.;* Aram. חֲרַף *id.*, ܚܰܪܶܦ *sharpen*, ܚܰܪܺܝܦ *sharp, keen, acute*);—**Qal** *Impf.* לאֹ־יֶחֱרַף לְבָבִי מִיָּמָי [Jb 27:6](https://ref.ly/Job27:6); *Pt. sf.* חֹרְפִי [Psalm 119:42](https://ref.ly/Ps119:42), [Pr 27:11](https://ref.ly/Prov27:11); חוֺרְפֶיךָ [Psalm 69:10](https://ref.ly/Ps69:10). **Piel.** *Pf.* חֵרֵף Ju [5:18](https://ref.ly/Judg5:18) \+ 6 t.; חֵרַפְתָּ [Is 37:23](https://ref.ly/Isa37:23) \+ 4 t. etc. \+ 10 t. pf.; *Impf.* יְחָרֵף [2 S 21:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:21), [1 Ch 20:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:7); יְחָ֫רֶף [Psalm 74:10](https://ref.ly/Ps74:10) \+ 2 t. sf.; *Inf.* לְחָרֵף [Is 37:4](https://ref.ly/Isa37:4) \+ 5 t.; בְּחָֽרְפָם [2 S 23:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:9) (but v. infr.); *Pt.* מְחָרֵף [Psalm 44:17](https://ref.ly/Ps44:17); —*reproach*,—prop. *say sharp things against*,—*taunt*, c. acc. Ju [8:15](https://ref.ly/Judg8:15), [1 S 17:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:10), [17:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:25), [17:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:26), [17:36](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:36), [17:45](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:45), [2 S 21:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:21) \+[1 Ch 20:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:7); God, by injustice to the poor [Pr 14:31](https://ref.ly/Prov14:31), [17:5](https://ref.ly/Prov17:5); Yahweh, by idolatry [Is 65:7](https://ref.ly/Isa65:7); Yahweh as the God of Israel [2 K 19:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:4), [19:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:16), [19:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:22), [19:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:23) \= [Is 37:4](https://ref.ly/Isa37:4), [37:17](https://ref.ly/Isa37:17), [37:23](https://ref.ly/Isa37:23), [37:24](https://ref.ly/Isa37:24) \= [2 Ch 32:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:17) (c. לְ); c. בְּ, Philistines [2 S 23:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:9) MT (but rd. בְּפַס דַּמִּים *at Pas\-dammîm*, n.pr.loc., as [1 Ch 11:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:12) We Dr); *reproach*, [Ne 6:13](https://ref.ly/Neh6:13), Psalm [42:11](https://ref.ly/Ps42:11), [44:17](https://ref.ly/Ps44:17), [55:13](https://ref.ly/Ps55:13), [57:4](https://ref.ly/Ps57:4), [74:10](https://ref.ly/Ps74:10), [74:18](https://ref.ly/Ps74:18), [79:12](https://ref.ly/Ps79:12), [89:52](https://ref.ly/Ps89:52), [89:52](https://ref.ly/Ps89:52), [102:9](https://ref.ly/Ps102:9), [Zp 2:8](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:8), [2:10](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:10); poet. and fig., *despise, scorn*, נפשׁ למוּת ´ח Ju [5:18](https://ref.ly/Judg5:18).

† II. חרף (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. خَرَفَ *gather fruit, pluck*).

> \[Str2779] † חֹ֫רֶף **n.m.** harvest\-time, autumn (Ar. خَرِيفٌ *freshly gathered fruit, autumn* (also *rain of autumn* or *beginning of winter*) \= Sab. (ן)חַרפ DHMZMG 1875, xxix. 597; Sab. חַרף, חַרפם \= *year,* cf. Eth. ኀሪፍ፡ *annus currens* Os (Levy):ZMG 1865, 168, 174 DHMZMG 1883, 369; As *ḫarpu*, SchrJPTh 1875, 341 KAT: 2 53 n. \= COTi. 54 n.)—abs. ´ח [Zc 14:8](https://ref.ly/Zech14:8) \+ 5 t.; sf. חָרְפִּי [Jb 29:4](https://ref.ly/Job29:4); —קַיִץ וָחֹרֶף [Gn 8:22](https://ref.ly/Gen8:22) (J), [Psalm 74:17](https://ref.ly/Ps74:17), [Zc 14:8](https://ref.ly/Zech14:8); ´בֵּית הַח *autumn\-house* or *palace* [Am 3:15](https://ref.ly/Amos3:15), [Je 36:22](https://ref.ly/Jer36:22) (\+ בַּחֹדֶשׁ הַתְּשִׁיעִי *in the 9th month,* i.e. Nov.–Dec.); ´מח עָצֵל לאֹ יַחֲרשׁ [Pr 20:4](https://ref.ly/Prov20:4) *a sluggard ploughs not after harvest;* as implying maturity, בִּימֵי חָרְפִּי [Jb 29:4](https://ref.ly/Job29:4) *in the days of my autumn* (prime).

> \[Str2778] III. \[חָרֵף] **vb. denom.** remain in harvest\-time (so Ar. خَرَفَ; Eng. *to winter*)—only **Qal** *Impf.* 3 fs. כָּל־בֶּהֱמַת הָאָרֶץ עָלָיו תֶּחֱרָף [Is 18:6](https://ref.ly/Isa18:6) *all the beasts of the earth shall spend the harvest\-time upon it*.

> \[Str2780] † חָרֵף (dub. whether from I. or II.) **n.pr.m.** a chief of the line of Judah [1 Ch 2:51](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:51).

> \[Str2756] † חָרִף, חָרִיף **n.pr.m.** (cf. Ar. خَرِيفٌ *autumn,* v. חֹרֶף supr.)—**1\.** חָרִף head of a family of returned exiles [Ne 7:24](https://ref.ly/Neh7:24) (LXX Αρειφ, LXXL Ιωρηε) \= יוֺרָה (cf. יוֺרָה *early rain*) [Ezr 2:18](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:18) (LXX Ουρα, A Ιωρα, LXXL Ωραι). **2\.** חָרִיף one of those sealed [Ne 10:20](https://ref.ly/Neh10:20) LXX Αρειφ(α), LXXL Αρηφ.

> \[Str2741] † חֲרִיפִּי Qr, חרופי Kt, **adj. gent.** c. art.; ´שְׁפַטְיָהוּ הַח [1 Ch 12:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:6) (Baer, v5 van d. H), LXX Χαρα(ι)φ(ε)ι, A Αρουφι; perh., if Qr right, connected with חָרִף [Ne 7:24](https://ref.ly/Neh7:24).

Ez [24:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:11), חָ֫רוּ [Is 24:6](https://ref.ly/Isa24:6); —**1\.** *be hot, scorched,* Jerus., under fig. of caldron Ez [24:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:11). **2\.** *burn* \= *be burned,* fig. of men, in ´י's judgement, [Is 24:6](https://ref.ly/Isa24:6). **3\.** *burn,* of bones of sick men in fever מִנִּי־חֹרֶב ´ח [Jb 30:30](https://ref.ly/Job30:30). **Niph.** *Pf.* נָחַר [Je 6:29](https://ref.ly/Jer6:29), נָחָר Ez [15:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek15:4), נִחַר [Psalm 69:4](https://ref.ly/Ps69:4); 3 pl. נִחָרוּ [Psalm 102:4](https://ref.ly/Ps102:4) (Köi. 368\); *Impf.* וַיֵּחָר Ez [15:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek15:5) יֵחָרוּ Ez [24:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:10) (del. Co B al.) **1\.** *be scorched,* of bellows מַפֻּחַ in fierce fire [Je 6:29](https://ref.ly/Jer6:29) (fig.); *scorched, charred,* of the vine (as fuel) Ez [15:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek15:4), [15:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek15:5) middle part charred, the ends devoured (אכל) by fire (sim. of inhab. of Jerus.); bones (sim. *id.*) Ez [24:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:10) (v. supr.). **2\.** *burn,* of bones in fever [Psalm 102:4](https://ref.ly/Ps102:4) (כְּמוֺקֵד, cf. **Qal** [Jb 30:30](https://ref.ly/Job30:30)); *be parched,* of throat גרוני ´נח [69:4](https://ref.ly/Ps69:4).—[Is 41:11](https://ref.ly/Isa41:11), [45:24](https://ref.ly/Isa45:24), Ct [1:6](https://ref.ly/Song1:6) v. חרה. **Pilp.** *Inf.* לְחַרְחַר־רִיב [Pr 26:21](https://ref.ly/Prov26:21) *to kindle strife*.

359:a. I. \[חָרַר]: **Niph. 1\.** נָחַר [Je 6:29](https://ref.ly/Jer6:29) from √ נחר *snort, puff,* acc. to Mich Ew Gf Gie al.,—not Du.

## III חרר (id: BDB03502)

III. חרר (√ of foll.; v. As *ḫarâru, bore, pierce, ḫurru, hole, ravine* DlPr 150, 182; Ar. خُرٌْ *hole* or *mouth* of millstone; NH חִרְחֵר *bore pierce*).

> \[Str2356] † III. חֹר, חוֺר **n.\[m.]** hole;—abs. חֹר [2 K 12:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:10), Ez [8:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:7) (del. Co B etc.), חוֺר Ct [5:4](https://ref.ly/Song5:4), חֹרִים [1 S 14:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:11); cstr. חֹרֵי [Jb 30:6](https://ref.ly/Job30:6); sf. חֹרָיו [Na 2:13](https://ref.ly/Nah2:13), חֹרֵיהֶן [Zc 14:12](https://ref.ly/Zech14:12); —*hole* in lid of chest [2 K 12:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:10) (made by boring, נקב), in door Ct [5:4](https://ref.ly/Song5:4); in wall Ez [8:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:7) (v. supra); \= *eye\-socket* ´עיניו תִּמַּקְנָה בְּח [Zc 14:12](https://ref.ly/Zech14:12) (plague of enemies of Jerusalem); *holes* as hiding\-places for men [1 S 14:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:11), so also [13:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:6) Ew We Dr (for MT חוחים), dwelling of outcast people חרי עָפָר [Jb 30:6](https://ref.ly/Job30:6); of *dens* of lions [Na 2:13](https://ref.ly/Nah2:13).—I. חֹר, v. II. חור

> \[Str2352] † חֻר, חוּר **n.\[m.]** id.—coll. חוּר [Is 42:22](https://ref.ly/Isa42:22) as hiding\-places of men; cstr. חֻר [Is 11:8](https://ref.ly/Isa11:8) hole of asp (פָּחֶן).

> \[Str2752–53,2752,2753] † II. חֹרִי, חוֺרִי **adj.,** usu. **n.pr.gent.** et **Pers.** (prob. \= *cave\-dweller,* so Thes Rob Ges MV VB; v. also W Max MüllAsien u. Europa 136, 155, 156\)—**1\. adj. gent.** ´שֵעִיר הַח [Gn 36:20](https://ref.ly/Gen36:20) *Seir the Horite* (P); rd. ´הַחִ also v:[2](https://ref.ly/Gen36:2) for MT הַחִוִּי **2\. n.pr.gent.** alw. c. art.; usu. sg. coll. הַחֹרִיִ, ancient inhabitants of land of Edom [Gn 14:6](https://ref.ly/Gen14:6) (בְּהַרֲרָם שֵעִיר); in P called בְּנֵי שֵעִיר בְּאֶרֶץ אֱדוֺם [36:21](https://ref.ly/Gen36:21) (vid. v:[20](https://ref.ly/Gen36:20)); cf. v:[29](https://ref.ly/Gen36:29), v:[30](https://ref.ly/Gen36:30); acc. to [Dt 2:12](https://ref.ly/Deut2:12) (where alone הַחֹרִים), v:[22](https://ref.ly/Deut2:22) they were driven out by sons of Esau (yet v. [Gn 36:1](https://ref.ly/Gen36:1) c. and Di); LXX ὁ Χορραιος, οἱ Χορραιοι, ([Gn 36:29](https://ref.ly/Gen36:29), [36:30](https://ref.ly/Gen36:30) Χορρ(ε)ι). **3\. n. pr. Pers. m. a.** חֹרִי an Edomite [Gn 36:22](https://ref.ly/Gen36:22) \= [1 Ch 1:39](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:39); LXX Χορρ(ε)ι. **b.** חִוֺרי a Simeonite [Nu 13:5](https://ref.ly/Num13:5), but LXX Σουρ(ε)ι, LXXL Σουδρι.—I. חֹרִי v. sub I. חור.

## חרש (id: BDB03506)

חרש (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. خَرَشَ vb. *scratch, lacerate,* خِرَاشٌ *irritation,* etc.)

> \[Str2789] † חֶ֫רֶש **n.\[m.]** earthenware, earthen vessel, sherd, potsherd, P and late (NH חֶרֶס, Aram. חֲרַס)—abs. ´ח [Lv 6:21](https://ref.ly/Lev6:21) \+ 9 t., חָרֶש [Nu 5:17](https://ref.ly/Num5:17) \+ 4 t.; pl. cstr. חַרְשֵי [Is 45:9](https://ref.ly/Isa45:9); sf. חֲרָשֶיהָ Ez [23:34](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:34).—**1\.** *earthenware:* ´כְּלִיאח *earthen vessel* [Je 32:14](https://ref.ly/Jer32:14); esp. P [Lv 6:21](https://ref.ly/Lev6:21), [11:33](https://ref.ly/Lev11:33), [14:5](https://ref.ly/Lev14:5), [14:50](https://ref.ly/Lev14:50), [15:12](https://ref.ly/Lev15:12), [Nu 5:17](https://ref.ly/Num5:17); without כְּלִי [Pr 26:23](https://ref.ly/Prov26:23); cf. ´בַּקְבֻּק יוֺצֵר ח [Je 19:1](https://ref.ly/Jer19:1); ´לְנִבְלֵי ח [La 4:2](https://ref.ly/Lam4:2) they are reckoned *as earthen vessels,* sim. of sons of Zion; חֶרֶש אֶת־חַרְשֵי אֲדָמָה [Is 45:9](https://ref.ly/Isa45:9) *a potsherd* (perh. \= *earthen vessel,* v. [Pr 26:23](https://ref.ly/Prov26:23) supr.) *among earthen potsherds* (of men, over against ´י their potter); sim. of dryness [Psalm 22:16](https://ref.ly/Ps22:16). **2\.** *a fragment of earthenware, sherd* [Is 30:14](https://ref.ly/Isa30:14), Ez [23:34](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:34); לְהִתְגָּרֵד ´ח [Jb 2:8](https://ref.ly/Job2:8) *a sherd to scrape himself;* ´חַדּוּדֵי ח [Jb 41:22](https://ref.ly/Job41:22) *sharpest potsherds,* fig. of sharp scales on belly of crocodile.

> חֲרֶשֶת v. קִיר־חֲרֶשֶת.

> \[Str2775] † III. \[חֶ֫רֶס] **n.\[m.]** an eruptive disease, itch (Aram. חַרְסָא, ܚܶܪܣܳܐ; also ܚܰܪܘܽܣ *rough,* etc.)—וּבֶחָרֶס [Dt 28:27](https://ref.ly/Deut28:27) (‖עפלים Kt, גָּרָב).—I, II. חֶרֶם v. sub חרס. p. 357

> \[Str2777] † חרסות Kt, חַרְסִית Qr **n.f.coll.** potsherd—פֶּתַח שַׁעַר הַחַרְסִ֯ות [Je 19:2](https://ref.ly/Jer19:2), designation of a gate in Jerus.; opening of the *gate of potsherds,* i.e. where they were thrown (vid. v:[10](https://ref.ly/Jer19:10), v:[11](https://ref.ly/Jer19:11) and Gf); it led into the valley of Hinnom; LXX Χαρς(ε)ιθ favours Qr.

## I חָרַשׁ (id: BDB03511)

\[Str2790,2794] † I. \[חָרַשׁ] **vb.** cut in, engrave, plough, devise (NH *id., plough,* so Eth ሐረሰ፡ Ph. חרש, Aram. (rare) חֲרַת *engrave,* ܚܪܰܬ *cleave, plough,* Ar. حَرَثَ *plough*)—**Qal** *Pf.* חָֽרְשׁוּ [Psalm 129:3](https://ref.ly/Ps129:3), חֲרַשְׁתֶּם Ju [14:18](https://ref.ly/Judg14:18), [Ho 10:13](https://ref.ly/Hos10:13); *Impf.* יַחֲרשׁ [Is 28:24](https://ref.ly/Isa28:24), [Pr 20:4](https://ref.ly/Prov20:4); יַחֲרוֺשׁ [Ho 10:11](https://ref.ly/Hos10:11)\+ [Am 6:12](https://ref.ly/Amos6:12) (v. infr.); 2 ms. תַּחֲרשׁ [Dt 22:10](https://ref.ly/Deut22:10), [Pr 3:29](https://ref.ly/Prov3:29); *Inf. cstr.* חֲרשׁ [1 S 8:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:12); *Pt.* חֹרֵשׁ [Gn 4:22](https://ref.ly/Gen4:22) \+ 5 t.; חוֺרֵשׁ [Am 9:13](https://ref.ly/Amos9:13); pl. חֹרְשִׁים [Psalm 129:3](https://ref.ly/Ps129:3), חֹרְשֵׁי [Jb 4:8](https://ref.ly/Job4:8) \+ 3 t., fpl. abs. חֹרְשׁוֺת [Jb 1:14](https://ref.ly/Job1:14); *pass.* חֲרוּשָׁה [Je 17:1](https://ref.ly/Jer17:1):—**1\.** *cut in, engrave,* of worker in metals נְחשֶׁת וּבַרְזֶל ´ח [Gn 4:22](https://ref.ly/Gen4:22) (J), נחשׁת ´ח [1 K 7:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:14); fig. עַל־לוּחַ לִבָּם ´ח [Je 17:1](https://ref.ly/Jer17:1) *engraved on the tablet of their heart*. **2\.** *plough,* lit., human subj. (animal usu. c. בְּ), no obj. expr. [1 K 19:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:19), [Dt 22:10](https://ref.ly/Deut22:10), [Is 28:24](https://ref.ly/Isa28:24), so יַחֲרוֺשׁ בבקרים [Am 6:12](https://ref.ly/Amos6:12) (but rd. prob. יֵחָרֵשׁ בבקר יָם, v. We al.); c. acc. cogn. חֲרִישׁוֺ ´ח [1 S 8:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:12) *plough his ploughing* (\= *do his ploughing*), [Pr 20:4](https://ref.ly/Prov20:4); fig. of Judah [Ho 10:11](https://ref.ly/Hos10:11); with ethical ref. רֶשַׁע ´ח v:[13](https://ref.ly/Hos10:13) (‖קצר), אָיֶן ´ח [Jb 4:8](https://ref.ly/Job4:8) (‖זרע, קצר); עַל־גַּבִּי חָֽרְשׁוּ חֹרְשִׁים [Psalm 129:3](https://ref.ly/Ps129:3) *upon my back have ploughmen ploughed* (fig. of oppression by wicked); חֹרֵשׁ \= *ploughman* [Is 28:24](https://ref.ly/Isa28:24), [Am 9:13](https://ref.ly/Amos9:13) (‖קוֺצֵר); ´ח with oxen subj. only [Jb 1:14](https://ref.ly/Job1:14). **3\.** *devise* (as one who *works in, practises*), usu. bad sense, obj. רָעָה [Pr 3:29](https://ref.ly/Prov3:29), רַע [6:14](https://ref.ly/Prov6:14); מַחְשְׁבוֺת אָוֶן v:[18](https://ref.ly/Prov6:18), חֹרְשֵׁי רַע [Pr 12:20](https://ref.ly/Prov12:20), [14:22](https://ref.ly/Prov14:22); but also חֹרְשֵׁי טוֺב v:[22](https://ref.ly/Prov14:22). **Niph.** *Impf.* 3 fs. צִיּוֺן שָדֶה תֵּחָרֵשׁ [Mi 3:12](https://ref.ly/Mic3:12) Zion, as a field she *shall be ploughed* \= [Je 26:18](https://ref.ly/Jer26:18). **Hiph.** *Pt.* מַחֲרִישׁ רָעָה עַ־ל [1 S 23:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:9) *fabricating mischief against* (v. Dr, and cf. We).

> \[Str2796,1516,2798] † חָרָשׁ **n.m.**:[Ex 38:23](https://ref.ly/Exod38:23) graver, artificer (Ph. חרש)—abs. ´ח (\= …חַרָּשׁ) [Ex 35:35](https://ref.ly/Exod35:35) \+ 14 t.; cstr. חָרַשׁ [Ex 28:11](https://ref.ly/Exod28:11) \+ 2 t.; pl. חָֽרָשִׁים [Ho 13:2](https://ref.ly/Hos13:2) \+ 7 t.; חֲרָשִׁים [1 Ch 4:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:14), [4:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:14), [Ne 11:35](https://ref.ly/Neh11:35) (v. infr.); cstr. חָֽרָשֵׁי [2 S 5:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:11) \+ 6 t.;—**1\.** *graver, artificer:* **a.** worker in metal [1 S 13:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:19), [Ho 8:6](https://ref.ly/Hos8:6), [13:2](https://ref.ly/Hos13:2), [Dt 27:15](https://ref.ly/Deut27:15), [Je 10:9](https://ref.ly/Jer10:9) (‖צֹרֵף), [Is 40:19](https://ref.ly/Isa40:19) (‖ *id.*), [54:16](https://ref.ly/Isa54:16), [1 Ch 29:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:5); בַּרְזֶל ´ח [Is 44:12](https://ref.ly/Isa44:12), [2 Ch 24:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:12); perh. also [Is 41:7](https://ref.ly/Isa41:7) (\+ צֹרֵף); appar. \= hammer [Zc 2:3](https://ref.ly/Zech2:3) (symbol.). **b.** worker in wood עֵץ ´ח [2 S 5:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:11) \= [1 Ch 14:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr14:1) (עֵצִים), [2 K 12:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:12), [22:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:6) \= [2 Ch 34:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:11), [Je 10:3](https://ref.ly/Jer10:3), [Is 40:20](https://ref.ly/Isa40:20), [44:13](https://ref.ly/Isa44:13) (v. also [1 Ch 22:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:15) infr.); perh. also [Ezr 3:7](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:7), [2 Ch 24:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:12) (both ‖חצב). **c.** worker in stone אֶבֶן קִיר ´ח [2 S 5:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:11) \= [1 Ch 14:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr14:1) (om. אבן); also of engraving on gems אבן ´ח [Ex 28:11](https://ref.ly/Exod28:11) (P; ‖פִּתּוּחֵי חֹתָם); see also foll. **d.** in gen. אֶבֶן וָעֵץ ´ח [1 Ch 22:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:15); *idol\-maker* צִירִים ´ח [Is 45:16](https://ref.ly/Isa45:16), cf. LagBN 120\)—**Qal** *Impf.* יֶחֱרָשׁ [Psalm 50:3](https://ref.ly/Ps50:3); 2 ms. תֶּחֱרַשׁ [Psalm 28:1](https://ref.ly/Ps28:1), [83:2](https://ref.ly/Ps83:2); תֶּחֱרַָשׁ [Psalm 35:22](https://ref.ly/Ps35:22), [109:1](https://ref.ly/Ps109:1); תֶּחֱרָשׁ [Psalm 39:13](https://ref.ly/Ps39:13); 3 fpl. תֶּחֱרַָשְׁנָה [Mi 7:16](https://ref.ly/Mic7:16); —**1\.** *be silent,* alw. of God's keeping silence when men pray [Psalm 35:22](https://ref.ly/Ps35:22), [50:3](https://ref.ly/Ps50:3), [83:2](https://ref.ly/Ps83:2), [109:1](https://ref.ly/Ps109:1); sq. אֶ־ל [39:13](https://ref.ly/Ps39:13). **2\.** *be deaf,* subj. ´י, sq. מִמֶּנִּי [Psalm 28:1](https://ref.ly/Ps28:1); subj. אָזְנַיִם [Mi 7:16](https://ref.ly/Mic7:16). **Hiph.** *Pf.* חֶחֱרִשׁ [Nu 30:15](https://ref.ly/Num30:15), [Gn 34:5](https://ref.ly/Gen34:5); יְחֶחֱרִישׁ consec. [Nu 30:12](https://ref.ly/Num30:12); הֶחֱרַ֫שְׁתִּי [Est 7:4](https://ref.ly/Esth7:4) \+ 2 t. etc.; *Impf.* יַחֲרִישׁ [Nu 30:15](https://ref.ly/Num30:15) \+ 2 t. (incl. [1 S 10:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:27) v. infr.); מַחֲרִישִׁים [2 S 19:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:11); —**1\.** *be silent* (\= *exhibit silence*) **a.** almost alw. of men [Gn 24:21](https://ref.ly/Gen24:21), [34:5](https://ref.ly/Gen34:5), [Ex 14:14](https://ref.ly/Exod14:14) (all J), Ju [18:19](https://ref.ly/Judg18:19), [2 S 13:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:20), [19:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:11), [2 K 18:36](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:36) \= [Is 36:21](https://ref.ly/Isa36:21), [Je 4:19](https://ref.ly/Jer4:19), [Jb 6:24](https://ref.ly/Job6:24), [13:5](https://ref.ly/Job13:5), [13:5](https://ref.ly/Job13:5), [13:19](https://ref.ly/Job13:19), [33:31](https://ref.ly/Job33:31), [33:33](https://ref.ly/Job33:33), [Pr 11:12](https://ref.ly/Prov11:12), [17:28](https://ref.ly/Prov17:28), [Psalm 32:3](https://ref.ly/Ps32:3), [Ne 5:8](https://ref.ly/Neh5:8), [Est 4:14](https://ref.ly/Esth4:14); [4:14](https://ref.ly/Esth4:14), [7:4](https://ref.ly/Esth7:4); also וַיְהִי כְּמַחֲרִישׁ [1 S 10:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:27) (but rd. rather ויהי כְּמֵחֹדֶשׁ *and it came to pass after about a month,* LXX Dr); יחרישׁ באהבתו [Zp 3:17](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:17) (LXX SyrVer יְחַדֵּשׁ he *will renew* his love, v. esp. BuhlZAW v. 1885, 183\); ´ח c. לְ Pers. *keep silence at* one, i.e. fail to make objection at proper time [Nu 30:5](https://ref.ly/Num30:5), [30:8](https://ref.ly/Num30:8), [30:12](https://ref.ly/Num30:12), [30:15](https://ref.ly/Num30:15), [30:15](https://ref.ly/Num30:15), [30:15](https://ref.ly/Num30:15) (all P); אלי ´הח [Is 41:1](https://ref.ly/Isa41:1) pregn. \= *come silently unto me;* sq. מִן Pers. *cease to speak with* [Je 38:27](https://ref.ly/Jer38:27); *be silent about, pass by in silence,* sq. acc. בַּדָּיו ´אח [Jb 41:4](https://ref.ly/Job41:4). **b.** rarely of God, permitting evil in silence Hb [1:13](https://ref.ly/Hab1:13), [Is 42:14](https://ref.ly/Isa42:14), [Psalm 50:21](https://ref.ly/Ps50:21). **2\.** once causat. *make silent,* c. acc. ## IV חרשׁ (id: BDB03521)

IV. חרשׁ (√ of foll.; meaning unknown; suggestions on etym. v. infr.)

> \[Str2791] † II. חֶ֫רֶשׁ **n.\[m.]** magic art, or perh. magic drug (Aram. ܚܰܪܶܫ *practise magic,* חָרָשׁ, ܚܰܪܳܫܳܐ *magician,* ܚܶܪܫܳܐ *incantation, magic art, preparation of magic potion,* so Eth. ሐሪሴ፡ *one using incantations;* ሐረስ, ሐርስ, ሐራስ፡ *incantation, magic*; comp. perh. Ar. خُرْسٌ, خُرْسَةٌ a medicinal broth given to women in childbed; חֶרֶשׁ perh. magical drug, v. RSJPh xiv. 1885, 125\)—only חֲכַם חֲרָשִׁים [Is 3:3](https://ref.ly/Isa3:3) *skilled in magic arts,* or *drugs* (‖נְבוֺן לָחַשׁ), ( \> others gen. \= *handicraft*).

ThomsonLand and Book; Central Palestine, 1883, 215 ff. BDPal 241 Be Bla CookeHist. Deb. 3 GASmGeog. 393\.

> \[Str1516,2791,2796,2798] חֲרָשִׁים v. חָרָשׁ and גַּיְא; also II. חֶרֶשׁ. above \& p. 360

> \[Str2759] † \[חֲרִישִׁי] **adj.** meaning wholly dub.; only רוּחַ קָדִים חֲרִישְׁית [Jon 4:8](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:8); *a silent east wind* is not suitable in context; *still* \= *sultry* is mere conject.; Hi *autumnal* (√ I. חרשׁ); St. prop. חרישית \= חריסית, fr. חֶרֶס *sun* (or √ whence חֶרֶס comes) *hot east wind;* We makes no attempt to explain.
