## חָשַךְ (id: BDB03530)

\[Str2820] † חָשַךְ **vb.** withhold, refrain (NH חָסַח, Aram. חֲסַח, ܚܣܰܟ; Palm. חסך *remit, spare,* VogNos. 6:15; Sab. משחכן *weaned* (child) DHMZMG 1875, 608\)—**Qal** *Pf.* חָשַךְ [Gn 39:9](https://ref.ly/Gen39:9) \+ 4 t. (\+ Ez [30:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:18) Ba; v. חשׁך; [Is 38:17](https://ref.ly/Isa38:17) v. infr.), חָשָךְ [Is 14:6](https://ref.ly/Isa14:6), חָֽשְכוּ [Jb 30:10](https://ref.ly/Job30:10), חָשָכוּ [Je 14:10](https://ref.ly/Jer14:10), etc.; *Impf.* יַחְשח [Jb 16:5](https://ref.ly/Job16:5) \+ 2 t.; 2 ms. תַּחְשח [Is 58:1](https://ref.ly/Isa58:1), תַּחְשוֺח [Pr 24:11](https://ref.ly/Prov24:11); 2 fs. תַּחְשָכִי [Is 54:2](https://ref.ly/Isa54:2), etc.; *Imv.* חֲשח [Psalm 19:14](https://ref.ly/Ps19:14); *Pt.* חֹשֵח [Pr 10:19](https://ref.ly/Prov10:19), [11:24](https://ref.ly/Prov11:24); חוֺשֵח [Pr 13:24](https://ref.ly/Prov13:24), [17:27](https://ref.ly/Prov17:27); sq.—**1\. a.** *withhold, keep back, keep for oneself*, acc., [Gn 22:12](https://ref.ly/Gen22:12) (E), v:[16](https://ref.ly/Gen22:16), [39:9](https://ref.ly/Gen39:9) (sq. מִמֶּגִּי; both J); abs. [Pr 21:26](https://ref.ly/Prov21:26) (opp. נָתַן); *withhold the rod* (שׁבט) in discipline, [Pr 13:24](https://ref.ly/Prov13:24); no obj. expr. [11:24](https://ref.ly/Prov11:24), [24:11](https://ref.ly/Prov24:11). **b.** *keep* one from evil, calamity, She’ôl, etc., c. acc. and מִן [Gn 20:6](https://ref.ly/Gen20:6) (E), [1 S 25:39](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:39), [Psalm 19:14](https://ref.ly/Ps19:14); מִמָּוֶת נַפְשָׁם ´ח [Psalm 78:50](https://ref.ly/Ps78:50) cf. [Jb 33:18](https://ref.ly/Job33:18), [Is 38:7](https://ref.ly/Isa38:7) (rd. חָשַכְתָּ or חֲשח for MT חָשַׁקְתָּ, v. חשׁק); abs. *hinder* (i.e. calamities fr. coming) [Is 14:6](https://ref.ly/Isa14:6); לְמַטָח מֵעֲוֺנֵנוּ ´ח [Ezr 9:13](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:13) thou hast *kept back, downward* (\= and kept down), *part of our iniquity,* i.e. hast not punished us according to our full desert. **c.** *hold in check* c. acc. [2 S 18:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:16) (or *spare* v. infr.); obj. רַגְלֵיהֶם [Je 14:10](https://ref.ly/Jer14:10); esp. of keeping silence, obj. פִּי [Jb 7:11](https://ref.ly/Job7:11) *restrain my mouth,* שפתים [Pr 10:19](https://ref.ly/Prov10:19), אמרים [17:27](https://ref.ly/Prov17:27); רֹק ´מִפָּנַי לאֹ ח [Jb 30:10](https://ref.ly/Job30:10) *from my face they have not withheld spittle* (\= they have spit in my face). **d.** *refrain* (fr. doing what is mentioned in the context), abs. [Is 54:2](https://ref.ly/Isa54:2), [58:1](https://ref.ly/Isa58:1). **e.** *spare,* c. acc. Pers. [2 K 5:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:20) *he hath spared* Naaman מִקַּחַת *so as not to take* (\= and hath not taken anything) out of his hand; perh. also [2 S 18:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:16) Joab *spared* the people (v. supr.). **f.** *reserve* for, c. acc. \+ לְ [Jb 38:23](https://ref.ly/Job38:23). **2\.** (abs.) *restrain, check* (pain) i.e. *assuage,* so appar. MT [Jb 16:5](https://ref.ly/Job16:5) (v. Di Kau; *I would not restrain condolence of my lips,* LXX SyrVer Me, rdg. לא אחשך for יחשך).—Ez [30:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:18) v. חשׁך. **Niph.** *Impf.* לְיוֺם אֵיד יֵחָשֶח־רָע [Jb 21:30](https://ref.ly/Job21:30) *at the day of calamity the wicked is spared* (pass. of **Qal 1 e**); יֵחָשֵח [Jb 16:6](https://ref.ly/Job16:6) *be assuaged,* of pain (pass of **Qal 2**).

* **Associated Passages:** Ezra 9:13; Job 16:6; Genesis 20:6; Proverbs 10:19; Jeremiah 14:10; Psalms 78:50; Genesis 22:16; Ezekiel 30:18; Proverbs 17:27; Job 38:23; 2 Kings 5:20; Proverbs 13:24; Genesis 22:12; Job 30:10; Isaiah 38:17; Job 33:18; Proverbs 11:24; Genesis 39:9; Job 21:30; 1 Samuel 25:39; Proverbs 24:11; Job 16:5; Isaiah 14:6; Psalms 19:14; Job 7:11; 2 Samuel 18:16; Isaiah 58:1; Isaiah 54:2; Isaiah 38:7; Proverbs 21:26


## חָשַף (id: BDB03531)

\[Str2834] † חָשַף **vb.** strip off, strip, make bare—**Qal** *Pf.* חָשַף [Is 52:10](https://ref.ly/Isa52:10), חֲשָפָהּ [Jo 1:7](https://ref.ly/Joel1:7), חָשַפְתִּי [Je 13:26](https://ref.ly/Jer13:26), [49:10](https://ref.ly/Jer49:10); *Impf.* וַיֶּחֱשף [Psalm 29:9](https://ref.ly/Ps29:9); *Imv.* fs. חֶשְפִּ־י [Is 47:2](https://ref.ly/Isa47:2); *Inf. abs.* חָשף [Jo 1:7](https://ref.ly/Joel1:7); *cstr.* לַחְשף [Is 30:14](https://ref.ly/Isa30:14), [Hg 2:16](https://ref.ly/Hag2:16); *Pt. pass.* חֲשוּפָה Ez [4:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:7), חֲשוּפַי [Is 20:4](https://ref.ly/Isa20:4) (pl. cstr. ? cf. *infr.*).—**1\.** *strip off,* expose oneself by removing (obj. שֹׁבֶל, flowing skirt, train) [Is 47:2](https://ref.ly/Isa47:2) of Babylon, personified as queen (‖גַּלִּי שׁוֺק), שׁוּלַיִח עַל־פָּנַיִח ´ח [Je 13:26](https://ref.ly/Jer13:26). **2\.** *strip, lay bare,* fig. of ´י [Is 52:10](https://ref.ly/Isa52:10) obj. זְרוֺעַ קדשׁו; of prophet, Ez [4:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:7) (but Co del. v. as interpol.); obj. Pers. [Je 49:10](https://ref.ly/Jer49:10) (‖גִּלֵּיתִי); חָשף חשפהּ [Jo 1:7](https://ref.ly/Joel1:7) of locusts stripping fig\-tree; חֲשוּפַי שֵׁת [Is 20:4](https://ref.ly/Isa20:4) (pt. either sg. coll., with formative ending י—ַ, De LagSe i. 19 (cf. 69\), BN 192; or pl. cstr., v. Ges§ 87, 1 c.); יְעָרוֺת ´ח [Psalm 29:9](https://ref.ly/Ps29:9) of voice of ´י (i.e. a storm) stripping forests. **3\.** *draw* (water) [Is 30:14](https://ref.ly/Isa30:14); (wine) [Hg 2:16](https://ref.ly/Hag2:16); properly *take from the surface, skim*.

> \[Str2835] † \[חָשִיף] **n.m.** only pl. cstr. כִּשְׁנֵי חֲשִפֵי עִוִּים [1 K 20:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:27) LXX Vulgate Targum and most, *two little flocks of goats* (חשיף \= *segregatum,* strictly *what is stripped off*), but this without other evidence than authority of the Vrss; Klo proposes בַּשְּׁפִי מִשְׁפַּט עִזִּיס *on the bare height, after the manner of goats*.

> \[Str2817] † חֲשוּפָא, חֲשֻׁפָא **n.pr.m.** head of a family of Nethinim among the returning exiles חֲשוּפָא [Ezr 2:43](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:43) \= חֲשֻפָא [Ne 7:46](https://ref.ly/Neh7:46).

> \[Str2834] חֲשוּפַי [Is 20:4](https://ref.ly/Isa20:4) v. חשף. above

> \[Str4286] † מַחְשף **n.m.** a laying bare, stripping—only cstr. מַחְשף הַלָּבָן [Gn 30:37](https://ref.ly/Gen30:37) (J) *a stripping of the white,* i.e. so as to shew wood under the bark.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 29:9; Ezra 2:43; Ezekiel 4:7; Haggai 2:16; Isaiah 30:14; Isaiah 20:4; Joel 1:7; Isaiah 47:2; Jeremiah 49:10; Genesis 30:37; Jeremiah 13:26; 1 Kings 20:27; Nehemiah 7:46; Isaiah 52:10

## חָשַׁב (id: BDB03536)

We Dr; in this case לבלתי carries on לאֹ יִשָּא). **3\.** *charge, impute* ´עון ל ´ח, [2 S 19:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:20) *impute iniquity to*. **4\.** *esteem, value, regard,* silver [Is 13:17](https://ref.ly/Isa13:17), a man [Is 33:8](https://ref.ly/Isa33:8), the servant of ´י [Is 53:3](https://ref.ly/Isa53:3). **5\.** *invent* ingenious and artistic things, להם כלי שׁיר ´ח [Am 6:5](https://ref.ly/Amos6:5) *invent* for themselves instruments of music; מחשׁבת (כל) ´ח *invent cunning work* (of artistic devices in constr. of tabern.) [Ex 35:35](https://ref.ly/Exod35:35), [38:23](https://ref.ly/Exod38:23) (P). **II.** of God: **1\.** *think,* c. acc. pers \+ לְ indirect obj. *account one* לאויב, *for an enemy* [Jb 13:24](https://ref.ly/Job13:24), [33:10](https://ref.ly/Job33:10); לוֺ כצריו ´ח [Jb 19:11](https://ref.ly/Job19:11) *he accounted me unto him as his adversaries*. **2\.** *devise, plan, mean,* c. acc. \+ ל indirect obj. לטבה *for good* [Gn 50:20](https://ref.ly/Gen50:20) (E); c. ל Pers. לִי *devise for me* [Psalm 40:18](https://ref.ly/Ps40:18); acc. rei \+ עַל, *devise something against a person* [Mi 2:3](https://ref.ly/Mic2:3), [Je 18:11](https://ref.ly/Jer18:11); *towards* one [Je 29:11](https://ref.ly/Jer29:11), c. אל against [Je 49:20](https://ref.ly/Jer49:20), [50:45](https://ref.ly/Jer50:45); sq. inf. [Je 26:3](https://ref.ly/Jer26:3), [36:3](https://ref.ly/Jer36:3), [La 2:8](https://ref.ly/Lam2:8). **3\.** *impute, reckon,* c. acc. rei \+ ל Pers., the habit of believing in ´י *he reckoned* to Abram as righteousness [Gn 15:6](https://ref.ly/Gen15:6) (JE; cf. **Niph. 3**); not *impute iniquity to* one [Psalm 32:2](https://ref.ly/Ps32:2). **Niph.** *Pf.* נֶחְשַׁב [Nu 18:27](https://ref.ly/Num18:27), [18:30](https://ref.ly/Num18:30), נֶחְשַׁבְתִּי [Psalm 88:5](https://ref.ly/Ps88:5) etc. \+ 10 t. *Pf.; Impf.* יֵחָשֵׁב [Lv 7:18](https://ref.ly/Lev7:18) \+ 7 t., etc., \+ 6 t. *Impf.; Pt.* נֶחְשָׁב [Is 2:22](https://ref.ly/Isa2:22), [1 K 10:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:21), [2 Ch 9:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:20):—**1\.** *be accounted, thought, esteemed,* c. כְּ *as* [Ho 8:12](https://ref.ly/Hos8:12), [Is 5:28](https://ref.ly/Isa5:28), [29:16](https://ref.ly/Isa29:16), [40:15](https://ref.ly/Isa40:15), [Psalm 44:23](https://ref.ly/Ps44:23), [Jb 18:3](https://ref.ly/Job18:3), [41:21](https://ref.ly/Job41:21); c. לְ [Is 29:17](https://ref.ly/Isa29:17) \= [32:15](https://ref.ly/Isa32:15), [La 4:2](https://ref.ly/Lam4:2); עִם *with, among* [Psalm 88:5](https://ref.ly/Ps88:5), בַּמֶּה [Is 2:22](https://ref.ly/Isa2:22) *at what* (value) ? (v. prob. interpol.; om. LXX); c. acc. [Dt 2:11](https://ref.ly/Deut2:11), [2:20](https://ref.ly/Deut2:20), [Pr 17:28](https://ref.ly/Prov17:28), [Ne 13:13](https://ref.ly/Neh13:13); נָכְרִיּוֺת נֶחְשַׁבְנוּ לוֺ [Gn 31:13](https://ref.ly/Gen31:13) (E; LXX Sam ´כְּנֶח) [Is 40:17](https://ref.ly/Isa40:17). **2\.** *be computed, reckoned,* c. לְ [Jos 13:3](https://ref.ly/Josh13:3) (D), עַל [Lv 25:31](https://ref.ly/Lev25:31) (P), [2 S 4:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:2): abs. כסף *was not counted* (so plentiful was it) [1 K 10:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:21) \= [2 Ch 9:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:20), [2 K 22:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:7). **3\.** *be imputed to any one,* c. לְ [Lv 7:18](https://ref.ly/Lev7:18), [Nu 18:27](https://ref.ly/Num18:27), [18:30](https://ref.ly/Num18:30) (all P), [Lv 17:4](https://ref.ly/Lev17:4) (H) [Pr 27:14](https://ref.ly/Prov27:14); the interposition of Phinehas לוֺ לצדקה ´נח [Psalm 106:31](https://ref.ly/Ps106:31) *was imputed to him for righteousness* (cf. **Qal II 3**). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 ms. חִשַּׁב [Lv 25:27](https://ref.ly/Lev25:27) \+ 4 t.; f. חִשְּׁבָה [Jon 1:4](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:4); 1 s. חִשַּׁבְתִּי [Psalm 77:6](https://ref.ly/Ps77:6), [119:59](https://ref.ly/Ps119:59); *Impf.* יְחַשֵּׁב [Pr 16:9](https://ref.ly/Prov16:9), [Dn 11:24](https://ref.ly/Dan11:24), תְּחַשְּׁבוּן [Na 1:9](https://ref.ly/Nah1:9), etc., \+ 4 t. *Impf.; Pt.* מְחַשֵּׁב [Pr 24:8](https://ref.ly/Prov24:8):—**1\.** *think upon, consider, be mindful of,* c. acc. [Psalm 77:6](https://ref.ly/Ps77:6), [119:59](https://ref.ly/Ps119:59), בֶּן־אֱנוֺשׁ וַתְּחַשְּׁבֵהוּ (מה) [Psalm 144:3](https://ref.ly/Ps144:3) *what* (is) *man's son, and thou thinkest upon him* (‖תֵּדָעֵהוּ). **2\.** *think* to do, *devise, plan,* c. acc. [Pr 16:9](https://ref.ly/Prov16:9), אֶל of persons against whom [Ho 7:15](https://ref.ly/Hos7:15), [Na 1:9](https://ref.ly/Nah1:9), עַל [Dn 11:24](https://ref.ly/Dan11:24); c. inf. [Psalm 73:16](https://ref.ly/Ps73:16), [Pr 24:8](https://ref.ly/Prov24:8); so of inanim. object הָאֳנִיָּה חִשְּׁבָה לְהִשָּׁבֵר [Jon 1:4](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:4) *the ship was about to* (minded to) *be broken up*. **3\.** *count, reckon,* the years since a sale of land [Lv 25:27](https://ref.ly/Lev25:27) (H); c. לְ Pers. ## חָשָׁה (id: BDB03548)

\[Str2814] † \[חָשָׁה] **vb.** be silent, inactive, still (chiefly poet. and late) (NH *id.,* Aram. in deriv.)—**Qal** *Impf.* 2 ms. תֶּחֱשֶׁה [Psalm 28:1](https://ref.ly/Ps28:1), [Is 64:11](https://ref.ly/Isa64:11); אֶחֱשֶׁה [Is 62:1](https://ref.ly/Isa62:1), [65:6](https://ref.ly/Isa65:6); יֶחֱשׁוּ [Is 62:6](https://ref.ly/Isa62:6); וַיֶּחֱשׁוּ [Psalm 107:29](https://ref.ly/Ps107:29); —*be silent* [Ec 3:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:7) (opp. לְדַבֵּר); [Is 62:1](https://ref.ly/Isa62:1), [62:6](https://ref.ly/Isa62:6) (\= neglect to speak); of ´י i.e. be unresponsive [Psalm 28:1](https://ref.ly/Ps28:1) (‖חרשׁ); [Is 64:11](https://ref.ly/Isa64:11) (‖הִתְאַפַּק); but [65:6](https://ref.ly/Isa65:6) of ´י's keeping silence at iniquity, i.e. overlooking it (cf. [57:11](https://ref.ly/Isa57:11) Hiph.); of waves, *be still* [Psalm 107:29](https://ref.ly/Ps107:29) (subj. גַּלֵּיהֶם). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הֶחֱשֵׁיתִי [Psalm 39:3](https://ref.ly/Ps39:3), [Is 42:14](https://ref.ly/Isa42:14); *Imv.* הֶחֱשׁוּ [2 K 2:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:3), [2:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:5); *Pt.* מַחְשֶׁה [Is 57:11](https://ref.ly/Isa57:11); pl. מַחְשִׁים Ju [18:9](https://ref.ly/Judg18:9) \+ 3 t.;—**1\.** *exhibit silence, be silent* [2 K 2:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:3), [2:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:5), [7:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:9), [Psalm 39:3](https://ref.ly/Ps39:3) (sq. מִטּוֺב, ‖נֶאֱלַמְתִּי), fig. [Is 42:14](https://ref.ly/Isa42:14) (‖אחרישׁ); of ´י's *being silent* at iniquity [Is 57:11](https://ref.ly/Isa57:11) (i.e. overlooking it, cf. Qal [65:5](https://ref.ly/Isa65:5)), poss. also is *be silent,* opp. *rescue,* Ges Che Di Du. **2\.** *shew inactivity* Ju [18:9](https://ref.ly/Judg18:9), מִקַּחַת ´הח [1 K 22:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:3) Gilead is ours, and we *shew inactivity so as not to take it*. **3\.** causat. *make still, quiet,* sq. לְ, of direct obj. [Ne 8:11](https://ref.ly/Neh8:11) (vb הַסּוּ q.v.)

## חָשַׁל (id: BDB03555)

\[Str2826] † \[חָשַׁל] **vb.** shatter (BAram. חֲשַׁל *shatter*; NH Pi., ‖משׁבר; Syr. ܚܫܠ *forge* a metal, Ar. حَسَلَ *drive* cattle *violently*; As. *ḫašâlu, shatter, destroy* ZimBP 12 DlPr 42\)—**Niph.** *Pt.* fig. כָּ־ל הַנֶּחֱשָׁלִים [Dt 25:18](https://ref.ly/Deut25:18) *all the shattered ones,* i.e. those broken down, worn out, sq. וְאַתָּה עָיֵף וְיָגֵעַ; others think \= חלשׁ.

> \[Str2828] † חָשֻׁם **n.pr.m.** head of a family of returned exiles [Ezr 2:19](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:19) \= [Ne 7:22](https://ref.ly/Neh7:22), [Ezr 10:33](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:33) cf. [Ne 8:4](https://ref.ly/Neh8:4), [10:19](https://ref.ly/Neh10:19).

> \[Str2830] † חַשְׁמַל **n.\[m.]** etym. and exact mng. dub.; evidently some *shining* substance; AV amber; supposed by Thes (q. v.) and most to be a brilliant amalgam of gold and silver, LXX ἤλεκτρον (v. Liddell \& Scott s. v. **2**), Vulgate *electrum;* v. also Dl in BaerEzech. xii; only in the combination ´כְּעֵין ח *like the appearance of* ´ח Ez [1:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:4), [1:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:27); בְּעֵין הַחַשְׁמַָלָה Ez [8:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:2) (on ending ה—ָ v. Ges§ 90, 2 R a; Co del.)

> \[Str2831] † \[חַשְׁמַן] **n.m.** only pl. חַשְׁמַגִּים [Psalm 68:32](https://ref.ly/Ps68:32); mng. unknown, LXX SyrVer Vulgate *ambassadors,* Rabb. *nobles,* conject. from context; doubtless txt. err.; NesJBL 1891, 152 prop. בשׁמנים they shall come *with oils, ointments,* Hilg Che (after Aq Jer) חָשִׁם *hasting*.

> \[Str2829] † חֶשְׁמוֺן **n.pr.loc.** town in southern Judah, site unknown [Jos 15:27](https://ref.ly/Josh15:27).

> \[Str2832] † חַשְׁמֹנָה **n.pr.loc.** a station of Isr. in wilderness [Nu 33:29](https://ref.ly/Num33:29), [33:30](https://ref.ly/Num33:30), site unknown.

## חשׁן (id: BDB03561)

חשׁן (√ of foll.; meaning not certain; Ar. حَسُنَ is *be excellent, beautiful;* حُسْنٌ *beauty, all excellence*; hence poss. חשֶׁן either as chief ornament of ephod, or as the most excellent, precious article of high priest's attire).

> \[Str2833] † ח֫שֶׁן **n.m.** the breast\-piece or sacred pouch, containing the אורים ותמים, worn on the breast of the high priest when he ministered in the Holy Place to bring the tribes for memorial. before ´י. It was made of the same material as the ephod (v. אֵפוֺד), a span square; set in front with twelve jewels in four rows, engraved with the names of the twelve tribes. It was firmly fastened to the shoulders of the ephod by gold chains passing through gold rings, and to the lower part of the ephod just above the girdle by a blue ribbon passing through other gold rings. It was מִשְׁפָּט(ה)חשֶׁן ( [Ex 28:15](https://ref.ly/Exod28:15), [28:29](https://ref.ly/Exod28:29), [28:30](https://ref.ly/Exod28:30)) *pouch of judgment,* because of the decision given by the אוּרִים וְתֻמִּים. The name occurs only in P, [Ex 25:7](https://ref.ly/Exod25:7), [28:4](https://ref.ly/Exod28:4), [28:15](https://ref.ly/Exod28:15), [28:22](https://ref.ly/Exod28:22), [28:23](https://ref.ly/Exod28:23), [28:23](https://ref.ly/Exod28:23), [28:24](https://ref.ly/Exod28:24), [28:26](https://ref.ly/Exod28:26), [28:28](https://ref.ly/Exod28:28), [28:28](https://ref.ly/Exod28:28), [28:29](https://ref.ly/Exod28:29), [28:30](https://ref.ly/Exod28:30), [29:5](https://ref.ly/Exod29:5), [35:9](https://ref.ly/Exod35:9), [35:27](https://ref.ly/Exod35:27), [39:8](https://ref.ly/Exod39:8), [39:9](https://ref.ly/Exod39:9), [39:15](https://ref.ly/Exod39:15), [39:16](https://ref.ly/Exod39:16), [39:17](https://ref.ly/Exod39:17), [39:19](https://ref.ly/Exod39:19), [39:21](https://ref.ly/Exod39:21), [39:21](https://ref.ly/Exod39:21), [Lv 8:8](https://ref.ly/Lev8:8), [8:8](https://ref.ly/Lev8:8).—Vid. NowArch. ii. 119\.

## I חָשַׁק (id: BDB03563)

\[Str2836] † I. חָשַׁק **vb.** be attached to, love (NH *press together, desire* (rare); Aram. חֲשַׁק *bind,* *saddle* (an ass))—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ח [Dt 7:7](https://ref.ly/Deut7:7) \+ 4 t.; חָֽשְׁקָה [Gn 34:8](https://ref.ly/Gen34:8); חָשַׁקְתָּ [Is 38:17](https://ref.ly/Isa38:17) (but v. infr.); וְחָֽשַׁקְתָּ֫ [Dt 21:11](https://ref.ly/Deut21:11); —*be attached to,* only fig. \= *love,* a woman sq. בְּ [Gn 34:8](https://ref.ly/Gen34:8) (P) [Dt 21:11](https://ref.ly/Deut21:11); elsewh. of ´י's; love for Israel [Dt 7:7](https://ref.ly/Deut7:7), [10:15](https://ref.ly/Deut10:15), and of love to ´י [Psalm 91:14](https://ref.ly/Ps91:14); sq. acc. cogn.\+ לְ and inf. ´כל־חֵשֶׁק שְׁלֽמה אשׁר ח לִבְנוֺת [1 K 9:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:19) \= [2 Ch 8:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:6); חָשַׁקְתָּ נַפְשִׁי מִשַּׁחַת [Is 38:17](https://ref.ly/Isa38:17) lit. *thou hast loved my soul out of the pit,* i.e. lovingly delivered it; but rd. חָשַכְתָּ *thou hast held back, kept,* from LXX Vulgate Lo Ew Che Di, or חֲשח *hold back* (Imv.), so Du.

> \[Str2837] † חֵ֫שֶׁק **n.m.** desire \= thing desired—cstr. חֵשֶׁק שְׁלֽמֹה [1 K 9:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:19) \= [2 Ch 8:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:6), [1 K 9:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:1); sf. נֶשֶׁף חִשְׁקִי [Is 21:4](https://ref.ly/Isa21:4) *the twilight of my pleasure* .

> \[Str2838] † \[חָשׁוּק] **n.\[m.]** fillet or ring clasping (*binding*) a pillar of the tabernacle, only pl. sf. חֲשֻׁקֵיהֶם [Ex 27:10](https://ref.ly/Exod27:10) \+ 5 t.; חֲשׁוּקַיהֶם [Ex 38:12](https://ref.ly/Exod38:12), [38:17](https://ref.ly/Exod38:17); those of the pillars at door of tabernacle (הָאֹהֶל) were overlaid with gold [Ex 36:38](https://ref.ly/Exod36:38); those of the pillars of the court with silver [27:10](https://ref.ly/Exod27:10), [27:11](https://ref.ly/Exod27:11), [38:10](https://ref.ly/Exod38:10), [38:11](https://ref.ly/Exod38:11), [38:12](https://ref.ly/Exod38:12), [38:17](https://ref.ly/Exod38:17), [38:19](https://ref.ly/Exod38:19) (all P), v. Di on [Ex 27:10](https://ref.ly/Exod27:10); \> Thes and most who understand of *connecting\-rods,* joining tops of pillars, from which curtains were hung.

> \[Str2836] † II. \[חשׁק] **vb. denom.** only **Pi.** furnish with fillets or rings, and **Pu.** pass.; **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 ms. חִשַּׁק [Ex 38:28](https://ref.ly/Exod38:28) (P), subj. Bezaleel. **Pu.** *Pt.* מְחֻשָּׁקִים כֶּסֶף [Ex 27:17](https://ref.ly/Exod27:17), [38:17](https://ref.ly/Exod38:17) *finished with silver fillets,* in agreement with הָעַמּוּדִים (both P).

> \[Str2839] † \[חִשּׁוּק] **n.\[m.]** spoke of a wheel (as *binding* felloe to nave)—pl. sf. חִשֻּׁקֵיהֶם [1 K 7:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:33).

## חשׁר (id: BDB03568)

חשׁר (√ of foll.; cf. As. *ašâru, collect gather* ZimBP 39\. In Ar., *collect* is حَشَرَ, but ش not usu. \= שׁ).

> \[Str2841] † \[חַשְׁרָה] **n.f.** collection, mass, only (si vera 1\.) חַשְׁרַת־מַיִם [2 S 22:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:12) (חֶשְׁכַּת in ‖ [Psalm 18:12](https://ref.ly/Ps18:12)).

> \[Str2840] † \[חִשּׁוּר] **n.\[m.]** nave, hub of a wheel (which *gather in* the spokes)—pl. sf. חִשֻּׁרֵיהֶם [1 K 7:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:33).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:12; 1 Kings 7:33; 2 Samuel 22:12


## חשׁשׁ (id: BDB03571)

חשׁשׁ (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. حَتَّ *hasten, hurry* (trans.), حُتٌّ *particles of straw, sand, dust,* as flying quickly about; v. LagBN 40\).

> \[Str2842] † חֲשַׁשׁ **n.m.** [Is 59:24](https://ref.ly/Isa59:24) chaff;—abs. תֵלְדוּ ´תַּהֲרוּ ח קַשׁ [Is 33:11](https://ref.ly/Isa33:11) *ye conceive chaff, ye bring forth stubble* (fig. of vain attempt of Assyr.); cstr. לֶהָבָה ´ח יִרְפֶּה [Is 5:24](https://ref.ly/Isa5:24) (‖קַשׁ) *as flaming chaff sinketh down* (sim. of perishing of heedless Judahites).

> \[Str2843] חֻשָׁתִי v. חושׁ. p. 302

> \[Str2844,2847] חַת, חִתָּה v. חתת

> \[Str2845] † חֵת **n.pr.m.** appar. represented as ancestor of the Hittites (etym. and mng. unknown; pronounced as fr. √ ע“ע in Heb. \& As., but not Egypt.; v. חִתִּי infr.)—‘begotten’ by Canaan [Gn 10:15](https://ref.ly/Gen10:15) (J) \= [1 Ch 1:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:13); elsewh. only in combin. with בְּנֵי, בְּנוֺת; **a.** בְּנֵי־חֵת acc. to P lived at Mamre (Hebron), and one of them sold Abraham the cave of Machpelah for a sepulchre [Gn 23:3](https://ref.ly/Gen23:3), [23:5](https://ref.ly/Gen23:5), [23:10](https://ref.ly/Gen23:10), [23:10](https://ref.ly/Gen23:10), [23:16](https://ref.ly/Gen23:16), [23:18](https://ref.ly/Gen23:18), [23:20](https://ref.ly/Gen23:20), [25:10](https://ref.ly/Gen25:10), [49:32](https://ref.ly/Gen49:32). **b.** חֵת(־)בְּנוֺת only of wives of Esau [Gn 27:46](https://ref.ly/Gen27:46), [27:46](https://ref.ly/Gen27:46) (P; LXX om. vb; ‖בְּנוֺת הָאָרֶץ \= בְּנוֺת כְָּ\+נעַן [28:1](https://ref.ly/Gen28:1)).

> \[Str2850] † חִתִּי **adj.** et **n. gent.** Hittite(s) (Egypt. *Ḫ\-tá, Ḫetá* W Max MüllAsien u. Europa, 319 ff.; As. *Ḫatti,* SchrKG 192 ff. COT on [Gn 10:19](https://ref.ly/Gen10:19) DlPa, 269 ff.; Telel\-Amarna *Ḫatti, Ḫatta* BezTel el Amarn. Brit. Mus. 150\)—m. alw. c. art. הַחִתִּי [Gn 23:10](https://ref.ly/Gen23:10), [Ex 3:8](https://ref.ly/Exod3:8) \+; f. חִתִּית Ez [16:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:3), [16:45](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:45); mpl. הַחִתִּים [Jos 1:4](https://ref.ly/Josh1:4) \+ 3 t.; fpl. חִתִּיֹּת [1 K 11:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:1); —**1\. adj.,** of seller of Machpelah to Abr., עֶפְרוֺן הַחִתִּי [Gn 23:10](https://ref.ly/Gen23:10), [49:29](https://ref.ly/Gen49:29), [49:30](https://ref.ly/Gen49:30), [50:13](https://ref.ly/Gen50:13); עֶפְרֹן בֶּן־צֹחַר ´הַח [25:9](https://ref.ly/Gen25:9) (all P); of fathers of Esau's wives בְּאֵרִי ´הַח [Gn 26:34](https://ref.ly/Gen26:34); ´אֵילוֺן הַח v:[34](https://ref.ly/Gen26:34), [36:2](https://ref.ly/Gen36:2) (all P); also of warriors of David, ´אֲחִימֶלֶח הַח [1 S 26:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:6), and esp. ´אוּרִיָּה הַח [2 S 11:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:3), [11:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:6), [11:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:17), [11:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:21), [11:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:24), [12:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:9), [12:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:10), [23:39](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:39), [1 K 15:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:5), [1 Ch 11:41](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:41); fs. \= subst., only of (religious) ancestry of Jerus. Ez [16:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:3) *thy father was the Amorite, and thy mother was a Hittite woman* חִתִּית, similarly v:[45](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:45); fpl. \= subst. of Sol.'s foreign wives, חִתִּיֹּת [1 K 11:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:1) *Hittite women* (\+ מוֺאֲבִיּוֺת, עַמּוֺנִיּוֺת, אֲדֹמִיֹּת, צֵדְנִיֹּת). **2\. n.** usu. **a.** coll. הַחִתִּי *the Hittites:* in lists of Canaanitish peoples [Gn 15:20](https://ref.ly/Gen15:20) (JE), [Ex 3:8](https://ref.ly/Exod3:8), [3:11](https://ref.ly/Exod3:11) (both J), [13:5](https://ref.ly/Exod13:5), [23:23](https://ref.ly/Exod23:23), [23:28](https://ref.ly/Exod23:28), [33:2](https://ref.ly/Exod33:2), [34:11](https://ref.ly/Exod34:11), [Nu 13:29](https://ref.ly/Num13:29), [Jos 3:10](https://ref.ly/Josh3:10) (all JE), [Dt 7:1](https://ref.ly/Deut7:1), [20:17](https://ref.ly/Deut20:17), [Jos 9:1](https://ref.ly/Josh9:1), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Josh12:8), [24:11](https://ref.ly/Josh24:11) (all D); rd. also for הַחִוִּי Ju [3:3](https://ref.ly/Judg3:3) (so We Mey BuUrg 350\); in [Jos 11:3](https://ref.ly/Josh11:3) (D) del. הַחִתִּי We Mey Bu1. c., and rd. then הַחִתִּי for הַחִוִּי vb (so LXX, not LXXL); further [1 K 9:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:20) \= [2 Ch 8:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:7), [Ezr 9:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:1) [Ne 9:8](https://ref.ly/Neh9:8). **b.** pl. הַחִתִּים: כֹּל אֶרֶץ הַחִתִּים [Jos 1:4](https://ref.ly/Josh1:4) (D) nearly \= land of Canaanites; ´ארץ הַח specif. of northern home of Hittites Ju [1:26](https://ref.ly/Judg1:26); so also [2 S 24:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:6) where rd. אֶרֶץ הַחִתִּים קֵ֫דְשָׁה (for MT תחתים חָדְשִׁי, v. חָדְשִׁי, קֵדֶשׁ); מַלְכֵי הַחִתִּים [1 K 10:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:29) \= [2 Ch 1:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:17) (both \+ מַלְכֵי אֲרָם), [2 K 7:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:6) (\+ מַלְכֵי מִצְרַיִם, both feared by אֲרָם). Hence it appears that (ם)הַחִתִּי had their proper seat in the north—where also they were encountered by Assyrians from time of TP I (v. Schr: Dli. c.), and by Egyptians from time of Tutmes III (v. W Max Müll.:1\. c.)—(cf. also Ju [3:3](https://ref.ly/Judg3:3), [Jos 11:3](https://ref.ly/Josh11:3) supr.), but that individual Hittites were known in Isr. (cf., besides [1 S 2:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:6), [2 S 11:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:3) etc., [1 K 9:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:20) supr.); that the Hittites were regarded (by JED) as one of the peoples of Canaan, and that the name even came to be used in more gen. sense for Canaanites. Only in P do they appear as having a definite settlement in the south, and are designated by בְּנֵי־חֵת (v. חֵת), as well as by the adj. הַחִתִּי.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:24; Exodus 3:11; Joshua 24:11; Isaiah 5:24; Ezra 9:1; Joshua 1:4; 2 Samuel 12:10; 1 Kings 15:5; 2 Chronicles 8:7; Genesis 49:30; Exodus 23:23; 2 Samuel 24:6; Genesis 28:1; Genesis 10:15; 2 Samuel 11:6; Genesis 23:3; Numbers 13:29; Exodus 33:2; 2 Samuel 11:21; Joshua 9:1; 2 Chronicles 1:17; Joshua 12:8; Genesis 26:34; Genesis 25:10; Genesis 50:13; Exodus 13:5; Genesis 36:2; 2 Samuel 11:17; 1 Kings 9:20; 1 Samuel 26:6; Ezekiel 16:3; Judges 1:26; Exodus 23:28; Genesis 10:19; Joshua 11:3; Genesis 23:5; 1 Samuel 2:6; Joshua 3:10; Genesis 23:10; Deuteronomy 7:1; Nehemiah 9:8; Genesis 15:20; 1 Kings 10:29; Genesis 49:32; Genesis 23:16; 2 Samuel 23:39; 2 Kings 7:6; 1 Chronicles 11:41; Judges 3:3; Exodus 3:8; Genesis 23:18; 1 Chronicles 1:13; Genesis 23:20; Genesis 27:46; 2 Samuel 12:9; Ezekiel 16:45; Genesis 49:29; 2 Samuel 11:24; 2 Samuel 11:3; Deuteronomy 20:17; Genesis 25:9; 1 Kings 11:1; Isaiah 33:11; Exodus 34:11

## חָתָה (id: BDB03577)

(in sim. of adulterer); pregn. *seize* (and put) *upon* על־ראֹשׁוֺ ´גְּחָלִים אַתָּה ח [Pr 25:22](https://ref.ly/Prov25:22); יַחְתְּךָ וְיִסָּֽחֲךָ מֵאָֹהֶל [Psalm 52:7](https://ref.ly/Ps52:7) God…*shall snatch thee away,* and pluck thee up tent\-less.

[Nu 4:14](https://ref.ly/Num4:14) (all P); *fire\-pans* of gold [1 K 7:50](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:50) \= [2 Ch 4:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:22), [2 K 25:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:15) \= [Je 52:19](https://ref.ly/Jer52:19). **3\.** *censer,* [Lv 10:1](https://ref.ly/Lev10:1), [16:12](https://ref.ly/Lev16:12), [Nu 16:6](https://ref.ly/Num16:6), [16:17](https://ref.ly/Num16:17), [16:17](https://ref.ly/Num16:17), [16:17](https://ref.ly/Num16:17), [16:17](https://ref.ly/Num16:17), [16:18](https://ref.ly/Num16:18), [17:2](https://ref.ly/Num17:2), [17:3](https://ref.ly/Num17:3), [17:4](https://ref.ly/Num17:4) (of bronze), v:[11](https://ref.ly/Num17:11) (all P).

NH חוֺתָל *woven date\-basket*)—**Pu.** *Pf.* 2 fs. חֻתַּלְתְּ and **Hoph.** *Inf. abs.* הָחְתֵּל *be swathed, swaddled;*—וְהָמְלֵחַ לאֹ הֻמְלַ֫חַתְּ וְהָחְתֵּל לאֹ חֻתַּלְתְּ Ez [16:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:4) *and not at all wast thou rubbed with salt, and not at all wast thou swaddled,* of Jerusalem under fig. of infant.

## חָתַם (id: BDB03587)

\[Str2856] † חָתַם **vb.** seal, affix seal, seal up (NH *id.;* Aram. חֲתַם, ܚܬܰܡ; Eth. ኀተመ፡ Ar. خَتَمَ (whence خَاتَمٌ) is loan\-word acc. to Frä252)—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. יַחְתֹּם [Jb 9:7](https://ref.ly/Job9:7), [33:6](https://ref.ly/Job33:6), יַחְתּוֺם [Jb 37:7](https://ref.ly/Job37:7), וַיַּחְתֹּם [Est 8:10](https://ref.ly/Esth8:10), etc.; *Imv.* חֲתֹם [Dn 12:4](https://ref.ly/Dan12:4), חֲתוֺם [Is 8:16](https://ref.ly/Isa8:16), חִתְמוּ [Est 8:8](https://ref.ly/Esth8:8); *Inf. abs.* חָתוֺם [Je 32:44](https://ref.ly/Jer32:44); *cstr.* לַחְתֹּם [Dn 9:24](https://ref.ly/Dan9:24), \+ v:[24](https://ref.ly/Dan9:24) Kt (Qr לְהָתֵם); *Pt. act.* חוֺתֵם Ez [28:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:12); *pass*. חָתוּם [Dt 32:24](https://ref.ly/Deut32:24) \+ 2 t., etc.;—**1\.** *seal, affix one's seal,* in attestation; seal with king's seal (בְּחֹתָמוֺ) [1 K 21:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:8), בְּטַבַּעַת המלך [Est 8:8](https://ref.ly/Esth8:8), [8:10](https://ref.ly/Esth8:10); of covenant with ´י, *attested by seal* (pass.) [Ne 10:1](https://ref.ly/Neh10:1), [10:2](https://ref.ly/Neh10:2); fig., of ´י putting his seal upon (בְּ) discipline, i.e. ratifying it, [Jb 33:16](https://ref.ly/Job33:16); perh. also upon hand of man (that man may know ´י's ways) [37:7](https://ref.ly/Job37:7) v. De; Di sub **2\.** *seal up* hand of man, so that he cannot work with it (in winter). **2\.** *seal up, fasten up by sealing,* a deed of sale [Je 32:10](https://ref.ly/Jer32:10), [32:11](https://ref.ly/Jer32:11)(opp. הַגָּלוּי *that which was left open,*), v:[14](https://ref.ly/Jer32:14), v:[44](https://ref.ly/Jer32:44), a book of prophecy [Dn 12:4](https://ref.ly/Dan12:4) (‖סְתֹם הַדְּבָרים), cf. v:[9](https://ref.ly/Dan12:9) (‖ *id.*), [9:24](https://ref.ly/Dan9:24) b; so in sim.of unintelligible prophecy [Is 29:11](https://ref.ly/Isa29:11), [29:11](https://ref.ly/Isa29:11); חֲתֹם תּוֺרָה בְּלְמֻּדָי [Is 8:16](https://ref.ly/Isa8:16) *seal up the teaching among my disciples,* i.e. keep it securely (‖צוֺר); of ´י's remembrance of offences חָתוּם בְּאוֺצְרֹתָי [Dt 32:34](https://ref.ly/Deut32:34) (‖כָּמֻס עְמָּרִי), so חָתוּם בִּצְרוֺר פִּשְׁעִי [Jb 14:17](https://ref.ly/Job14:17) (‖וַתִּטְפֹּל עַל־עֲוֺנִי); מַעְיָן חָתוּם Ct [4:12](https://ref.ly/Song4:12) *a fountain sealed up,* metaph. of chaste woman (‖נָעוּל (גַּל)גַּן); ´וּבְעַד כּוֺכָבִים יַח [Jb 9:7](https://ref.ly/Job9:7) *and about the stars he putteth a seal* (‖ of forbidding sun to rise).—אַתָּה חוֺתֵם תָּכְנִית Ez [28:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:12) is obscure: *thou* wast one *sealing proportion,* i.e. *perfection,*—wast complete perfection, Ges Sm.; wast *a sealer of symmetry* v. Da; wast a *seal (ring)* —i.e. חוֺתַם cstr.—of proportion Codd. LXX Vulgate Hi cf. Ew; txt. perh. corrupt, v. suggestions in Co.—[Dn 9:24](https://ref.ly/Dan9:24) a rd. Qr לְהָתֵם (v. תמם). [Jb 33:16](https://ref.ly/Job33:16), [37:7](https://ref.ly/Job37:7) v. sub **1\. Niph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. \[or *Pt.?*] נִכְתָּ֔ב וְנֶחְתָּ֭ם [Est 3:12](https://ref.ly/Esth3:12) (it was) *written and sealed* בְּטַבַּעַת הַמֶּלֶח; *Inf. abs.* וְנַחְתּוֺם [Est 8:8](https://ref.ly/Esth8:8) (continuing pt. נִכְתָּ֣ב) also \+ ´בְּטַבַּעַת הַמּ; both pass. of **Qal 1\. Pi.** *Pf.* 3 pl. יוֺמָם חִתְּמוּ־לָמוֺ [Jb 24:16](https://ref.ly/Job24:16) lit. *by day they seal up for themselves,* i.e. acc. to most, they shut themselves up, do not shew themselves (‖לאֹ יָדְעוּ אוֺר); LXX C has ἡμέρας ἐσφράγισαν ἐαυτοῖ ς (B al. ἑαυτους), whence Siegf conj. plausibly ´ל ´יָמִים ח *they seal up the days unto themselves,* i.e. daytime is for them sealed up and unused. **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. אוֺ־הֶחְתִּים בְּשָׁרוֺ מִוּוֺבוֺ [Lv 15:3](https://ref.ly/Lev15:3) *or hath his flesh* (genital organ) *shewn stoppage by reason of his flux.*

> \[Str2368] † I. חֹתָם, חוֺתָם **n.m.**:[Jb 41:7](https://ref.ly/Job41:7) seal, signetring (Aram. ܚܳܬܡܳܐ; Eth. ምኣኅተም፡—v. LagBN 116\)—abs. חֹתָם [Ex 28:11](https://ref.ly/Exod28:11) \+ 2 t.; חוֺתָם [Je 22:24](https://ref.ly/Jer22:24) \+ 8 t.; sf. חֹתָֽמְךָ [Gn 38:18](https://ref.ly/Gen38:18), חֹתָמוֺ [1 K 21:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:8); —on supposed cstr. חוֺתַם v. foregoing;—*seal, signet\-ring,* hung by a cord (פָּתִיל) about the neck [Gn 38:18](https://ref.ly/Gen38:18) (J; \= חֹתֶמֶת v:[25](https://ref.ly/Gen38:25)), or (later ?) worn on (finger of) right hand [Je 22:24](https://ref.ly/Jer22:24), (BenzArchäol. 106\); the two customs appar. combined Ct [8:6](https://ref.ly/Song8:6) (עַל־לִבֶֹּךָ, עַל־זְרוֺעֶךָ); used to attest a royal missive [1 K 21:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:8) (instr. of vb. חתם); a precious article [Hg 2:23](https://ref.ly/Hag2:23) (sim. of Zerubbabel); ´פִּתּוּחֵי ח *engravings of a seal,* as model for cutting names and inscr. on precious stones and gold plate [Ex 28:11](https://ref.ly/Exod28:11), [28:21](https://ref.ly/Exod28:21), [28:26](https://ref.ly/Exod28:26), [39:6](https://ref.ly/Exod39:6), [39:14](https://ref.ly/Exod39:14), [39:30](https://ref.ly/Exod39:30) (all P; v. Benz258 ff.); ´חֹמֶר ח [Jb 38:14](https://ref.ly/Job38:14) \= *clay of* (under) *a signet;* צָר ´ח [41:7](https://ref.ly/Job41:7) *a close signet,* i.e. one that is closely pressed down, sim. of closely joined (סָגוּר) scales of crocodile.

> \[Str2369] † II. חוֺתָם **n.pr.m.1\.** man of Asher [1 Ch 7:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:32) LXX Χωθαν(μ) (\= הֶלֶם v:[35](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:35); A Ελαμ, LXXL Λασουλ). **2\.** father of two of David's heroes [1 Ch 11:44](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:44), LXX Κωθαν, LXXL Χωθαν.

> \[Str2858] חֹתֶ֫מֶת **n.f.** signet\-ring—only ´הַח [Gn 38:25](https://ref.ly/Gen38:25) (J; for I. חֹתָם v:[18](https://ref.ly/Gen38:18)) *the signet\-ring* (acc. to BöNÄ i. 23 fem. coll. *the sealing\-apparatus*).

## I חתן (id: BDB03591)

I. חתן (√ of foll.; prob. *circumcise*; cf. Ar. خَتَنَ *circumcise,* خِتَانٌ *circumcision, circumcision\-feast*).

> \[Str2859] † חֹתֵן **n. verb. 1\. m.** wife's father (Ar. خَاتِنٌ *a circumciser,* hence father\-in\-law, with ref. to circumcision performed on young men just before marriage; خَتَنٌ *relation on wife's side;* v. WeProl. 1886, 355 Anm, 1; Skizzen iii, 154 StaZAW 1886\. 143 Aum. NöZMG 1886, 187; otherwise DlPr 91 LagBN 116\)—cstr. חֹתֵן [Ex 18:1](https://ref.ly/Exod18:1) \+ 9t., חֹתֶנְךָ [Ex 18:6](https://ref.ly/Exod18:6), חֹתְנוֺ [Ex 3:1](https://ref.ly/Exod3:1)\+ 9 t.;—usu. of Moses’ *wife's father* [Ex 3:1](https://ref.ly/Exod3:1), [4:18](https://ref.ly/Exod4:18), [18:1](https://ref.ly/Exod18:1), [18:2](https://ref.ly/Exod18:2), [18:5](https://ref.ly/Exod18:5), [18:6](https://ref.ly/Exod18:6), [18:7](https://ref.ly/Exod18:7), [18:8](https://ref.ly/Exod18:8), [18:12](https://ref.ly/Exod18:12), [18:12](https://ref.ly/Exod18:12), [18:14](https://ref.ly/Exod18:14), [18:15](https://ref.ly/Exod18:15), [18:17](https://ref.ly/Exod18:17), [18:24](https://ref.ly/Exod18:24), [18:27](https://ref.ly/Exod18:27) (a11 E), [Nu 10:29](https://ref.ly/Num10:29) (J), Ju [1:16](https://ref.ly/Judg1:16), [4:11](https://ref.ly/Judg4:11); of a Levite Ju [19:4](https://ref.ly/Judg19:4), [19:7](https://ref.ly/Judg19:7), [19:9](https://ref.ly/Judg19:9). **2\. f.** *wife's mother,* only sf. חֹתַנְתּוֺ [Dt 27:23](https://ref.ly/Deut27:23).

> \[Str2860] † חָתָן **n.m.** daughter's husband, bride\-groom (as one who undergoes circumcision, v. supr.; NH *id.;* Aram. חַתְנָא, and ܚܰܬܢܳܐ (also sister's husband, etc.); vulg. Ar. خَتَنٌ *daughter's husband,* then more gen. wife's and husband's relations; Sab. (Liḥyân) חַתן *daughter's husband* DHMEpigr. Denkm. 87; As. *ḫatanu, daughter's husband* COTGloss)—abs. ´ח [1 S 18:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:18) \+ 10 t. \+ [Gn 19:12](https://ref.ly/Gen19:12) (where rd. חֲחָנֶיךָ LXX Ol Di); cstr. חֲתַן [Ex 4:23](https://ref.ly/Exod4:23) \+ 4 t.; sf. חֲתָנוֺ Ju [19:5](https://ref.ly/Judg19:5); חֲתָנָיו [Gn 19:14](https://ref.ly/Gen19:14), [19:14](https://ref.ly/Gen19:14); —**1\.** in relation to a father, *daughter's husband, or bridegroom* [Gn 19:12](https://ref.ly/Gen19:12), [19:14](https://ref.ly/Gen19:14) (לֽקְחֵי בְּנֹתָיו ´ח), v:[14](https://ref.ly/Gen19:14) (all J), Ju [15:6](https://ref.ly/Judg15:6), [19:5](https://ref.ly/Judg19:5), [1 S 18:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:18), [22:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:14), [Ne 6:18](https://ref.ly/Neh6:18), [13:28](https://ref.ly/Neh13:28); v. also חֲתַן בֵּית אַחְאָב [2 K 8:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:27) *son\-in\-law of the house of Ahad,* said of Ahaziah, whose mother Athaliah was the daughter of Ahab. **2\.** in relation to the bride, *bridegroom* דָּמִים אַתָּה לִי ´ח [Ex 4:25](https://ref.ly/Exod4:25) *a bloody bridegroom art thou to me,* cf. v:[26](https://ref.ly/Exod4:26) (J; on this v. WeProl. 1886, 355\); ‖כַּלָּה [Je 7:34](https://ref.ly/Jer7:34), [16:9](https://ref.ly/Jer16:9), [25:10](https://ref.ly/Jer25:10), [33:11](https://ref.ly/Jer33:11), [Jo 2:16](https://ref.ly/Joel2:16); in sim. of ´י's work for and delight in his people [Is 61:10](https://ref.ly/Isa61:10), [62:5](https://ref.ly/Isa62:5); of sun [Psalm 19:6](https://ref.ly/Ps19:6).

> \[Str2859] † II. חתן **vb. denom.** only **Hiphp.** make oneself a daughter's husband (NH Hithp. *id.,* Aram. Ithpa. *id.*)—*Pf.* 2 mpl. וְהִתְחַתַּנְתֶּם consec. [Jos 23:12](https://ref.ly/Josh23:12); *Impf.* וַיִּתְחַתֵּן [1 K 3:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:1), [2 Ch 18:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:1); 2 m. תִּתְחַתֵּן [Dt 7:3](https://ref.ly/Deut7:3), [1 S 18:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:21); *Imv.* הִתְחַתֵּן [1 S 18:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:22), הִתְחַתְּנוּ [Gn 34:9](https://ref.ly/Gen34:9); *Inf.* הִתְחַתֵּן [1 S 18:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:23) \+ 3 t.;—**1\.** *make oneself daughter's husband* (son\-in\-law) *to, c.* בְּ [1 S 18:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:21), [18:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:22), [18:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:23), [18:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:26), [18:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:27); c. אֶת [1 K 3:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:1), [Gn 34:9](https://ref.ly/Gen34:9) (P); c. לְ [2 Ch 18:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:1), of marriage of Jehoshaphat's son and Ahab's daughter (cf. [2 K 8:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:18) \= [2 Ch 21:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:6)). **2\.** in gen. *form marriage\-alliance with,* c. בְּ [Dt 7:3](https://ref.ly/Deut7:3) [Jos 23:12](https://ref.ly/Josh23:12) (D), [Ezr 9:14](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:14).

> \[Str2861] † \[חֲתֻנָּה] **n.f.** marriage, wedding (NH חִיתּוּן *id.*; Aram. חִיתּוּנָא *id.;* Ar. خُتُونَةٌ *marriage\-alliance, marriage*)—only בְּיוֺם חֲתֻנָּתו Ct [3:11](https://ref.ly/Song3:11) *on the day of his marriage*.

## חָתַף (id: BDB03596)

\[Str2862] † \[חָתַף] vb. seize, snatch away (cf. Aram. ܚܰܬܶܦ Pa. *break in, pieces;* Ar. حَتْفٌ *death*)—**Qal** *Impf.* no obj. expr. הֵן יַחְתֹּף וּמִי יְשִׁיבֶנּוּ [Jb 9:12](https://ref.ly/Job9:12) *lo ! he seizeth, and who shall turn him back ?*

> \[Str2863] † חֶ֫תֶף **n.\[m.]** prey—only תֶּאֱרֹב ´אַף־חִיא כְּחֶ [Pr 23:28](https://ref.ly/Prov23:28) *she also lieth in wait as* for *prey* (said of strange woman).

* **Associated Passages:** Proverbs 23:28; Job 9:12


## חָתַר (id: BDB03598)

\[Str2864] † חָתַר **vb.** dig, row (NH *id., bore* (through), so Aram. חֲתַר Targum (rare))—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ח [Jb 24:16](https://ref.ly/Job24:16), חָתַרְתִּי Ez [12:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:7); *Impf.* וָאֶחְתֹּר Ez [8:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:8), יַחְתְּרוּ Ez [12:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:12), [Am 9:2](https://ref.ly/Amos9:2), וַיַּחְתְּרוּ [Jon 1:13](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:13); *Imv.* חֲתָ־ר Ez [8:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:8), [12:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:5); —**1\.** *dig into* houses, c. acc. בָּתִּים [Jb 24:16](https://ref.ly/Job24:16) (of burglary); sq. בְּ into or through a wall Ez [8:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:8), [8:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:8) (Co del. on intern. grounds), [12:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:5), [12:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:7), [12:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:12); metaph. sq. בשׁאול [Am 9:2](https://ref.ly/Amos9:2) *dig into She’ôl,* i.e. as a refuge. **2\.** *row* (as *digging* into the water) [Jon 1:13](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:13).

> \[Str4290] † מַחְתֶּ֫רֶת **n.m.** breaking in, burglary—יִמָּצֵא הַגַּנָּב ´אִם־בַּמּ Ex 22:l i.e. caught in the act (JE); so מְצָאתִים ´לאֹ בַּמּ [Je 2:34](https://ref.ly/Jer2:34), *not in the act of breaking in didst thou find them* (2 fs. sf. Ges§ 44, 2, R 4\), i.e. those whom thou hast slain were not detected in crime.

(of ´י's foes in gen.) [1 S 2:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:10) (song;—others render *dismayed* in all exc. [Is 7:8](https://ref.ly/Isa7:8)); fig. of ´י's righteousness [Is 51:6](https://ref.ly/Isa51:6) (\= be abolished, annihilated); lit. of bows [Je 51:56](https://ref.ly/Jer51:56), acc. to Gie's rdg. חַתָּה, v. **Pi. 2\.** *be dismayed*, usu. **a.** abs.: ‖יָרֵא [Dt 1:21](https://ref.ly/Deut1:21), [31:8](https://ref.ly/Deut31:8), [Jos 8:1](https://ref.ly/Josh8:1), [10:25](https://ref.ly/Josh10:25) (all D), [1 S 17:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:11), [Je 23:4](https://ref.ly/Jer23:4), [30:10](https://ref.ly/Jer30:10), [46:27](https://ref.ly/Jer46:27), Ez [2:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek2:6), [3:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:9), [1 Ch 22:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:13), [28:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:20), [2 Ch 20:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:15), [20:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:17), [32:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:7); ‖בּוֺשׁ [Is 20:5](https://ref.ly/Isa20:5), [37:27](https://ref.ly/Isa37:27) \= [2 K 19:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:26), [Je 8:9](https://ref.ly/Jer8:9), [17:18](https://ref.ly/Jer17:18), [17:18](https://ref.ly/Jer17:18), [48:1](https://ref.ly/Jer48:1), [48:20](https://ref.ly/Jer48:20), [48:39](https://ref.ly/Jer48:39), [50:2](https://ref.ly/Jer50:2), [50:2](https://ref.ly/Jer50:2) (others assign. [48:20](https://ref.ly/Jer48:20), [48:39](https://ref.ly/Jer48:39), [50:2](https://ref.ly/Jer50:2), [50:2](https://ref.ly/Jer50:2) to **1**); fig. of the ground הָאֲדָמָה, *dismayed* for lack of rain [Je 14:4](https://ref.ly/Jer14:4) (‖בּוֺשׁ of the husband\-men); no ‖ [Je 50:36](https://ref.ly/Jer50:36), Ob:[9](https://ref.ly/Obad1:9), [Jb 39:22](https://ref.ly/Job39:22). **b.** *be dismayed at, by reason of*, sq. מִן [Is 31:4](https://ref.ly/Isa31:4), [31:9](https://ref.ly/Isa31:9), [51:7](https://ref.ly/Isa51:7) (‖יָרֵא) [Je 1:17](https://ref.ly/Jer1:17), [10:2](https://ref.ly/Jer10:2), [10:2](https://ref.ly/Jer10:2). **Niph.** *Pf.* only 3 ms. וּמִפְּנֵי שְׁמִי נִחַת [Mal 2:5](https://ref.ly/Mal2:5) and at my name *he is put in awe* (‖יָרֵא). **Pi.** *Pf. dismay, scare*, 2 ms. sf. וְחִתַּתַּ֫נִי בַחֲלֽמוֺת (Köi. 372\) consec. [Jb 7:14](https://ref.ly/Job7:14) *and thou scarest me with dreams* (‖בִּעֵת); 3 fs. חִתְּתָה [Je 51:56](https://ref.ly/Jer51:56) (of bows) is intrans. \[inchoat. Ew§ 120, 2 d], Gf. al., *be shattered*, but txt. prob. erron. Gie חַתָּה (v. **Qal 1**). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 2 ms. הַחִתֹּתָ [Is 9:3](https://ref.ly/Isa9:3); 1 s. וְהַחְתַּתִּ֫י [Je 49:37](https://ref.ly/Jer49:37) (Köi. 372\); *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יְחִתֵּנִי [Jb 31:34](https://ref.ly/Job31:34), sf. 3 fpl. יְחִיתַָן Hb [2:17](https://ref.ly/Hab2:17) (Köi. 374; but LXX Targum SyrVer Ew Ol Sta We יְחִתֶָּךָ); 1 s. sf. אֲחִתְּךָ [Je 1:17](https://ref.ly/Jer1:17) (sq. לפניהם)—**1\.** *shutter* c. acc. [Is 9:3](https://ref.ly/Isa9:3). **2\. a.** *cause to be dismayed* [Je 49:37](https://ref.ly/Jer49:37) sq. לִפְנֵי. **b.** *dismay, terrify*, sq. sf. [Jb 31:34](https://ref.ly/Job31:34), Hb [2:17](https://ref.ly/Hab2:17).

369:a. \[חָתַת]: יֵחַת [Je 21:13](https://ref.ly/Jer21:13) V. נָחֵת.

> \[Str2844] † I. \[חַת] **n.m.**:[Gn 9:2](https://ref.ly/Gen9:2) terror, fear;—חָת [Jb 41:25](https://ref.ly/Job41:25), חִתְּכֶם [Gn 9:2](https://ref.ly/Gen9:2); —c. sf. as obj. gen., [Gn 9:2](https://ref.ly/Gen9:2) *terror of you* (‖מוֺרַאֲכֶם); in description of crocodile c. neg., הֶעָשוּ לִבְלִי־חָת [Jb 41:25](https://ref.ly/Job41:25) *one made for fearlessness*.

> \[Str2844] † II. \[חַת] **adj.** shattered, dismayed—mpl. חַתִּים:—**1\.** *shattered* ´קֶשֶׁת גִּבֹּרִים ח [1 S 2:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:4) (song; on pl. חַתִּים v. Dr); so fig. Ez [32:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:30) acc. to LXX Codd. Co (חַתִּים for MT חִתִּיתָם) *broken in their might*, of Sidonians in She’ôl. **2\.** *dismayed* [Je 46:5](https://ref.ly/Jer46:5) of Egyptians defeated by Nebuchadrezzar.

> \[Str2866] † I. חֲתַת **n.\[m.]** terror—only וַתִּירָאוּ ´חִרְאוּ ח [Jb 6:21](https://ref.ly/Job6:21) (\> Baer תִּֽירְאוּ for תִּרְאוּ) *ye see a terror, and fear* (note paronomasia in Heb.; v. Di).

> \[Str2847] † \[חִתָּה] **n.f.** terror—חִתַּת אלהים עַל־הֶעָרִים [Gn 35:5](https://ref.ly/Gen35:5) *a terror of* (\= from) *God was upon the cities*.

> \[Str2867] † II. חֲתַת **n.pr.m.** son of Othniel and grandson of Kenaz (brother of Caleb), חֲתַָת [1 Ch 4:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:13).

> \[Str2849] † \[חַתְחַת] **n.\[m.]** terror, only pl. חַתְחַתִּים בַּדֶּרֶח [Ec 12:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:5) *terrors* are *in the way* (‖ירא).

> \[Str2851] חִתִּית **n.f.** terror, only Ezek.; abs. ´ח Ez [32:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:23), but Co rds. חִתִּיתָם (with LXX and v:[24](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:24), v:[26](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:26)); cstr. *id.* [32:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:27); sf. חתיתו [32:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:32) Kt (\> חִתִּיתִי Qr); חִתִּיתָם [32:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:24) \+ 2 t.\+ [26:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:17) (rd. חִתִּיתָהּ, with LXX Ew Co, see VB), [32:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:30) (rd. חַתִּים, v. II. חת);—*terror*, caused by powerful cities, nations, kings, etc.: usu. c. sf. obj. genit. Ez [26:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:17) (of Tyre); elsewh. only Ez 32: v:[27](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:27) (גִּבּוֺרִים ´ח, SyrVer Co גבור תם ´ח), v:[30](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:30) (חִתִּיתָם, but rd. חַתִּים, v. supr.); esp. in phr. בארץ ´נָתַן ח חַיִּים *cause one's terror* (terror of oneself) *in the land of the living* v:[23](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:23), v:[24](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:24), v:[25](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:25) (del. LXX Co), v:[26](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:26), v:[32](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:32).—חִתִּית Ez [16:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:3), [16:45](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:45) v. חִתִּי sub חֵת supr. p. 366\.

> \[Str4288] † מְחִתָּה **n.f.** terror, destruction, ruin, poet. (esp. Pr)—abs. ´מ [Pr 10:14](https://ref.ly/Prov10:14) \+ 8 t.; cstr. מְחִתַּת [Pr 10:15](https://ref.ly/Prov10:15), [14:28](https://ref.ly/Prov14:28); —**1\. a.** *terror*, [Is 54:14](https://ref.ly/Isa54:14) (‖עשֶׁק), [Je 17:17](https://ref.ly/Jer17:17); \= obj. of terror, [48:39](https://ref.ly/Jer48:39) (‖שְחֹק). **b.** *dismay*, [Pr 21:15](https://ref.ly/Prov21:15) (opp. שִמְחָה). **2\.** *ruin*, of strongholds [Psalm 89:41](https://ref.ly/Ps89:41); רָזוֺן ´בְּאֶפֶס לְאֹם מ [Pr 14:28](https://ref.ly/Prov14:28) *without people is ruin to a prince* (opp. הַדְרַת־מֶלֶח); דַּלִּים ´מ רֵישָׁם [10:15](https://ref.ly/Prov10:15) *the ruin of the poor* (is) *their poverty;* as consequence of evil\-doing לְפֹעֲלֵי אָוֶן ´מ [Pr 10:29](https://ref.ly/Prov10:29) (opp. מָעוֺז); of fools ־לוֺ´פִּי כְסִיל מ [18:7](https://ref.ly/Prov18:7), cf. [13:3](https://ref.ly/Prov13:3) (opp. שֹׁמֵר נַפְשׁוֺ); קְרֹבָה ´פִּי אֱוִיל מ [10:14](https://ref.ly/Prov10:14) *the mouth of a fool is imminent ruin.*

> \[Str2845,2850] חֵת, חִתִּי v. supr. p. 366
